### [1] [Preference-Augmented Distributional Reinforcement Learning for Risk-Sensitive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09932)
*Zhaofan Zhang,Yue Chang,Rufeng Chen,Hechang Chen,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出在分布式强化学习中使用分位数权重分布表示风险偏好变化，通过偏好引导探索覆盖多样偏好，并在部署时针对最坏情况偏好分布优化以获得保守策略，从而提升不确定环境下导航的安全性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于IQN的导航方法在训练时使用固定或随机采样的CVaR水平，部署时依赖手工调参或外部传感器信号调整风险水平，标量CVaR表述限制了学习到的尾部风险偏好范围，导致训练与部署阶段风险偏好不匹配。

Method: 将风险偏好的变化建模为分位数权重分布的偏移，通过偏好引导探索让智能体接触多样化的风险偏好；部署阶段针对最坏情况偏好分布进行优化，得到兼顾多种风险特征的保守策略，减少超参数调优和对有限传感器信号的依赖。

Result: 在多种仿真环境和车辆平台上的实验表明，该方法减少了碰撞并提高了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 以分位数权重分布刻画风险偏好并结合最坏情况偏好优化，可在部分可观测和不确定条件下实现更安全可靠的自主导航。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and reliable navigation under diverse sources of uncertainty remains a central challenge for autonomous systems. Within distributional reinforcement learning (DistRL), Implicit Quantile Networks (IQN) have attracted interest for navigation under uncertainty because they enable risk adaptation through flexible quantile sampling. Existing approaches, however, typically use a fixed or randomly sampled Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) level during training and adjust the risk level during deployment using hand-tuned hyperparameters or external sensor signals. This scalar-CVaR formulation confines learning to a narrow family of tail-risk preferences, creating a mismatch between training-time preference exposure and deployment-time risk adjustment. We instead represent variations in risk preference as shifts in quantile-weight distributions and expose the agent to diverse preferences through preference-guided exploration. At deployment, we optimize against the worst-case preference distribution to derive a conservative policy that accounts for multiple risk profiles. This reduces hyperparameter tuning and reliance on limited sensor signals under partial observability. Experiments across simulated environments and vehicle platforms show that our method reduces collisions while improving task success.

</details>


### [2] [Selective Cooperative Perception via Blind Spot Reasoning and Agentic Dialogue with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10819)
*Chengyuan Ma,Shixiao Liang,Hangyu Li,Zheng Li,Hang Zhou,Keke Long,Bofeng Cao,Zhaohui Liang,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由视觉语言模型（VLM）驱动的智能体框架，用于盲区推理和基于对话的协同感知（CP），通过混合架构与思维链提示实现安全关键场景下的选择性CP，在真实车联数据上盲区检测准确率超80%、对话驱动感知共享成功率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体交通环境中的实时协同感知资源消耗大，难以大规模部署；仅在安全关键场景和关键遮挡对象上选择性执行CP可显著提升效率，而VLM适合动态不确定环境下的复杂推理，是完成该选择性CP任务的有力工具。

Method: 设计混合模型架构，集成传统目标检测实现稳定感知、视觉语言推理识别有影响的遮挡、基于物理的几何模块将推理与真实可见性约束对齐；引入思维链（CoT）提示机制增强可解释性与意图感知决策；采集城市交通中网联车辆的真实数据，比较四种先进VLM在驾驶关键盲区识别与对话方面的表现。

Result: 所提方法在驾驶关键盲区检测中准确率超过80%，在对话驱动的感知共享中成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 该工作为传统CP流水线提供了一种可扩展、可解释且带宽高效的替代方案，并为未来网联交通系统的智能体通信设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Real-time cooperative perception (CP) in multi-agent traffic environments is highly resource-intensive, which posing scalability challenges for large-scale deployment. Efficiency can be significantly improved by selectively performing CP only in safety-critical scenarios and for key occluding objects. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are well-suited for complex reasoning in dynamic and uncertain environments, making them a promising tool for this selective CP task. This study proposes a VLM-driven agentic framework for blind spot reasoning and dialogue-based CP. A mixed-model architecture is designed that integrates conventional object detection for stable perception, vision-language reasoning for identifying impactful occlusions, and a physics-based geometric module to align reasoning with real-world visibility constraints. The framework also incorporates a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting mechanism to enhance interpretability and intent-aware decision-making. We collect real-world data from connected vehicles operating in urban traffic and compare the driving-critical blind spot identification and dialogue performance of four state-of-the-art VLMs. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves over 80% accuracy in driving-critical blind spot detection and over 90% success in dialogue-driven perception sharing. This work provides a scalable, interpretable, and bandwidth-efficient alternative to conventional CP pipelines, and offers new insights into agentic communication design for future connected traffic systems.

</details>


### [3] [Passivity-Guaranteed Variable Impedance and Compliance Control via Online Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10778)
*Huseyin Tugcan Dinc,Ersin Daş,Nikolas Thelenberg,Christian Ott*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于二次规划的变阻抗参数优化框架，通过调制期望惯量补偿刚度变化注入的能量，保证无源性并保持临界阻尼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变阻抗控制在线调整阻抗参数时，时变参数可能向系统注入能量而破坏无源性，尤其任务需要快速刚度变化时；现有方法通过增大阻尼或限制刚度曲线来解决，但会降低动态响应。

Method: 针对对角阻抗矩阵，以无源性约束构建凸二次规划，优化惯量与刚度的变化率；将框架扩展到柔顺控制，其中期望惯量与机器人构型相关的惯量矩阵耦合，使用基于Cholesky分解的特征分解优化谱权重并保持无源性。

Result: 在Kinova Gen3七自由度机械臂上结合人类交互扰动验证了两种方法；与能量罐方法和非无源性方法相比，所提框架实现了更少保守性的刚度跟踪，且无需能量储池或额外阻尼。

Conclusion: 所提二次规划参数优化框架可在保持临界阻尼的同时保证无源性，避免能量罐和阻尼开销，实现更优的刚度跟踪性能。

Abstract: Variable impedance control enhances the interaction capability of robot manipulators by adapting impedance parameters online. However, time-varying impedance parameters may inject energy into the system and violate passivity, particularly when tasks demand rapid stiffness changes. Existing methods address this by increasing damping or limiting the stiffness profile, which often degrades the dynamic response. In this work, we propose a quadratic programming-based parameter optimization framework that guarantees passivity by modulating the desired inertia to compensate for energy injected by stiffness variations, while preserving a critically damped system. For the case of diagonal impedance matrices, we optimize the rates of change of inertia and stiffness subject to a passivity constraint formulated as a convex quadratic program. We extend the framework to compliance control, where the desired inertia is coupled to the robot's configuration-dependent inertia matrix. We use an eigendecomposition based on Cholesky factorization to optimize the spectral weights while maintaining passivity. Experiments on the Kinova Gen3 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator validate both methods with human interaction perturbations. Comparisons with an energy tank approach and a nonpassivity-based method demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves less conservative stiffness tracking without requiring an energy reservoir or damping overhead.

</details>


### [4] [REINS: A Self-Evolving Agent Harness for Real-Time Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10748)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Haoran Tang,Shijun Liu,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REINS 是一种运行时 harness，将 LLM 规划器与学习到的动力学模型、校准谓词验证和索引规则记忆耦合，在战术决策层实现实时且可自演化的轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的轨迹规划方法要么让 LLM 留在决策回路中，超出毫秒级控制预算，要么依赖离线规则或训练时监督，导致运行时规划器无法演化；已有折中方案也缺乏使 harness 可靠的验证与固化回路。

Method: 提出 REINS，基于三种 LLM-规划器耦合：将 LLM 输出（i）接地到学习到的变量级动力学模型，（ii）通过校准谓词与前向推演进行验证，（iii）固化到索引规则记忆中，以 O(log n) 服务未来场景而无需再次调用 LLM；该 harness 形成自演化闭环，熟悉场景通过记忆查找解决，新场景调用 LLM 并将验证后的输出充实记忆。

Result: 在不修改 LLM 权重的情况下，REINS 在仿真与真实基准的战术决策层达到 98% 以上合规率、碰撞减少近 50%、延迟 1.9–4.6 ms，且随着记忆成熟 LLM 调用率单调下降。

Conclusion: REINS 表明合适的运行时 harness 可使 LLM 规划器在满足实时约束的同时自演化，成为一种可部署的规划方案。

Abstract: Agent harnesses -- runtime scaffolds that wrap an LLM's reasoning loop with tool dispatch, verification, and persistent memory -- have become the standard substrate for deploying LLMs as autonomous agents, as demonstrated by Claude Code and Codex in software engineering. In trajectory planning, however, this paradigm has not taken hold: existing LLM-based trajectory planning methods either keep the LLM in the decision loop, exceeding the millisecond-level control budget, or offload it to offline rules or training-time supervision -- leaving the runtime planner non-evolving. Intermediate hybrids still lack the verification-and-consolidation loop that makes harnesses reliable. We ask: what runtime harness would let an LLM planner meet real-time constraints while self-evolving into a deployable one? We propose REINS, a harness built on three LLM-planner couplings: LLM outputs are (i) grounded to a learned variable-level dynamics model, (ii) verified via calibrated predicates and forward rollout, and (iii) consolidated into an indexed rule memory that serves future scenes in O(log n) without re-invoking the LLM. The harness closes a self-evolution loop: familiar scenes resolve by memory lookup; novel scenes invoke the LLM, whose verified outputs enrich the memory. Without modifying LLM weights, REINS attains 98%+ compliance, nearly 50% collision reduction, and 1.9-4.6 ms latency on simulated and real-world benchmarks at the tactical decision layer, with LLM invocation rate monotonically declining as memory matures. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [5] [Burden, Bounty, or Bliss? Muscle-Aware Retargeting and Null-Space Exploration for Whole-Body Musculoskeletal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10682)
*Ruiyang Kong,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出运动到策略流水线，结合肌肉感知重定向与零空间探索，从单目视频训练控制700块肌肉的全身模仿策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人体运动学习全身肌肉骨骼控制对人形机器人、辅助设备和康复系统很重要，但存在参考轨迹运动学可行却难以肌肉驱动复现、以及700维高度冗余动作空间下无结构探索易破坏目标运动两大挑战。

Method: 两阶段利用肌肉冗余：肌肉感知重定向（MAR）在语义、解剖和接触硬约束下对运动学轨迹进行有界适应，并通过分析参考轨迹伪影对肌肉驱动跟踪的影响来设定约束边界；零空间探索（NSE）估计肌肉激励到广义力的一控制帧映射，并沿其零空间向值引导流策略的动作注入有界、解剖结构化的扰动。

Result: 在24段动作捕捉基准上，MAR与NSE共同求解所有参考轨迹，以基线流水线一半样本达到完整时间跨度，并将关节中心误差降低44%且无后期崩溃。

Conclusion: 据所知，这是首个将单目视频端到端转换为控制700块肌肉的全身模仿策略的流水线。

Abstract: Learning whole-body musculoskeletal control from human motion is important for developing human-like robots, assistive devices, and rehabilitation systems. This task presents two main challenges, namely reference trajectories that are kinematically plausible but difficult to reproduce through muscle-driven dynamics, and a highly redundant 700-dimensional action space in which unstructured exploration can easily disrupt the target motion. In this paper, we propose a motion-to-policy pipeline that exploits muscle redundancy in two complementary stages. First, muscle-aware retargeting (MAR) performs bounded adaptation of kinematic trajectories subject to hard semantic, anatomical, and contact constraints. We motivate the constraint bounds by analyzing how reference-trajectory artifacts affect muscle-driven tracking. Second, null-space exploration (NSE) estimates a one-control-frame mapping from muscle excitations to generalized forces and injects bounded, anatomically structured perturbations along its null space into the actions of a value-guided flow policy. On a 24-clip motion-capture benchmark, MAR and NSE together solve every reference, reach the full horizon with half the samples of the baseline pipeline, and lower joint-center error by 44% without late collapse. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end pipeline that transforms monocular video into a trained whole-body imitation policy controlling 700 muscles.

</details>


### [6] [Task-Critical Part Alignment Improves Imitation of Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10664)
*Denis Hadjivelichkov,Sebastian Ægidius,Valerio Modugno,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对DINOBot模仿学习框架进行两项改进：引入RANSAC剔除离群点以提高对齐鲁棒性，并聚焦任务相关交互区域优化语义对应，在真实操作任务中任务成功率提升13.8%且对齐收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINOBot在模仿学习中面临深度数据噪声和虚假特征匹配影响对齐精度，且语义对应未聚焦任务关键区域，导致迁移到真实场景时鲁棒性和效率不足。

Method: 在DINOBot对齐阶段集成RANSAC进行离群点剔除以抑制噪声深度数据和错误特征匹配；同时改进语义对应过程，聚焦任务相关的交互区域，使学习到的表示优先关注影响操作成功的关键物体特征。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务（抓取、插入、摇晃、倾倒）上评估，相比基线任务成功率总体提升13.8%，对齐过程收敛更快。

Conclusion: 上述增强有效提升了机器人物体-物体交互模仿学习的精度与效率。

Abstract: This paper presents enhancements to the DINOBot framework for imitation learning of object-object interactions. We introduce two key contributions aimed at improving the accuracy and efficiency of alignment in imitation-based robotic tasks. First, we integrate RANdom SAmple Consensus (RANSAC) for outlier rejection during the alignment phase, effectively mitigating the impact of noisy depth data and spurious feature matches. This improves robustness when transferring demonstrations to real-world applications. Second, we refine the semantic correspondence process by focusing on task-relevant interaction regions, ensuring that the learned representation prioritizes critical object features that influence successful manipulation. We evaluated our approach in a range of real-world manipulation tasks, including picking, insertion, shaking, and pouring, using a robotic system. The experimental results demonstrate an overall improvement of 13.8% in task success rates compared to the baseline, along with faster convergence in the alignment process. These results highlight the effectiveness of our enhancements in improving precision and efficiency for object-object interaction imitation in robotics.

</details>


### [7] [Trajectory Inversion for a Rudderless Dual Vectored-Thrust Flying Wing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10650)
*Weicheng Di,Qiqi Liu,Jinwu Xiang,Daochun Li,Zhan Tu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无舵面双矢量推力飞翼，提出联合优化路径几何与时间分配的轨迹生成方法，并通过轨迹反演得到满足耦合运动方程的状态与控制历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无舵面无尾飞翼仅靠矢量推力同时控制平移与俯仰，缺少气动控制面，导致轨迹动力学兼容性难以保证，自主特技机动规划仍是开放难题。

Method: 联合优化路径几何与时间参数，在优化中权衡曲率诱导加速度与气动支撑，降低矢量推力需求并保留机动特征；通过轨迹反演获得满足耦合运动方程的状态与控制序列，并用闭环仿真跟踪典型筋斗与滚转序列。

Result: 闭环仿真成功跟踪代表性筋斗与滚转机动序列，验证了所生成轨迹的动力学可行性与跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为无舵面矢量推力飞行器的自主特技机动规划提供了可行基础。

Abstract: A rudderless dual vectored-thrust flying wing combines vertical takeoff and landing with efficient for- ward flight in a compact tailless configuration. Without aerodynamic control surfaces, vectored thrust couples translation and pitch control. Therefore, generating dy- namically compatible trajectories remains an open chal- lenge. To address this challenge, this study proposes a trajectory generation method that jointly optimizes path geometry and timing. Trajectory inversion yields state and control histories that satisfy the coupled equations of motion. The optimization balances curvature-induced acceleration against aerodynamic support, lowering thrust- vectoring demand while retaining the defining maneuver features. Representative looping and rolling sequences are tracked in closed-loop simulations. These results provide a basis for planning autonomous aerobatic maneuvers with rudderless vectored-thrust aircraft.

</details>


### [8] [Which Taxels, Not How Many: Tactile Sensor Placement for Dexterous Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10647)
*Taeyun Kim,Jinsoo Shin,Taehwa Kim,Taeyoon Kim,Jinyoung Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在统一控制协议下，触觉传感器数量、位置和输入排列对灵巧操作策略的影响被系统测试，发现基于接触统计的少量触觉单元即可匹配大量均匀布置的传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作策略依赖训练前的设计选择（哪些传感器进入观测、放置位置、读数排列方式），但这些选择很少被检验；仿真研究中均匀布置触觉单元已成为未经测试的默认做法，因此需要检验这一默认设置。

Method: 采用单一控制协议，保持教师模型、训练过程和候选触觉单元固定，仅改变传感器配置和表示方式；在三种机器人手上评估掌内旋转和抓取任务。

Result: 从接触统计中选出的少量触觉单元可匹配远大于其数量的均匀传感器集；很少被接触位置的触觉单元等同于无触觉输入；使用全部候选触觉单元时，其在输入张量中的布局不影响性能，即使对卷积编码器也是如此。

Conclusion: 均匀布置会高估任务所需的触觉单元数量；研究提炼出触觉策略设计经验，并给出触觉策略研究应报告的最小设计选择集。

Abstract: Policies for dexterous manipulation depend on design choices made before training: which sensors enter the observation, where they are placed, and how their readings are arranged for the encoder. These choices are rarely examined. For tactile sensing, simulation studies place taxels uniformly over the hand, and this placement has become a default without being tested. We test this default under a single controlled protocol. The protocol holds the teacher, the training procedure, and the candidate taxels fixed and varies only the sensor configuration and the representation. We evaluate it on in-palm rotation and grasping with three robot hands. A small set of taxels selected from contact statistics matches a far larger uniform set. Taxels at rarely contacted sites are equivalent to no tactile input. With all candidate taxels, their layout in the input tensor does not affect performance, even for a convolutional encoder. Uniform placement therefore overestimates the number of taxels a task requires. We distill these results into lessons for designing tactile policies and into a minimal set of design choices that a tactile policy study should report.

</details>


### [9] [Model-Based Imitation Learning For Monocular Vision-Based Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10635)
*Basant Sharma,Prajyot Jadhav,Saqi Hussain Kalan,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的模仿学习方法，结合学习的碰撞模型与轨迹优化，在受限视野单目相机的杂乱环境中实现可靠导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目受限视野相机导航缺少深度信息且空间感知有限，使碰撞检测困难；现有模型规划器计算昂贵且需手动调参，端到端模仿学习对安全性隐式编码导致新场景碰撞率高。

Method: 模型部分学习碰撞模型，将历史图像序列与候选控制序列映射为最坏情况障碍间隙的分布，该预测分布用于轨迹优化中的碰撞风险代价；模仿部分利用人类演示学习场景相关的碰撞风险与其他任务代价（如目标进展）的平衡，并学习优化器的算法参数（如热启动）以加速求解。

Result: 在杂乱环境中于不同形态机器人上实现高度可靠的导航，相比近期端到端和基于模型的基线碰撞率大幅降低。

Conclusion: 基于模型的模仿学习兼顾泛化性与灵活性，可作为连接模型规划与端到端模仿学习的中间方案，提升受限感知下的导航安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable navigation with a single monocular limited field-of-view camera is challenging because the lack of depth measurements and limited spatial awareness complicate collision checking. Existing approaches to tackle these challenges fall into two categories. Model-based planners can incorporate explicit collision-avoidance guidance which leads to better generalization but are often computationally expensive and require manual tuning of the algorithmic parameters. In contrast, end-to-end approaches trained by imitation learning can be flexible but typically encode safety only implicitly and thus can suffer from high collision rates in novel scenarios. In this paper, we present a middle ground through model-based imitation learning. The model-based part is a learned collision model that can map a sequence of past images and a candidate control sequence to a distribution of worst-case obstacle clearance. This predictive distribution in turn is used to define a collision-risk cost in a trajectory optimization formulation. The imitation component uses human demonstrations to learn scene-dependent balancing of collision risk with other task costs such as goal progress. We also use it to learn the algorithmic parameters (e.g., a warm start) of our optimizer to accelerate the solution process. We show that our approach can demonstrate highly reliable navigation in cluttered environments on robots with different embodiments. We also demonstrate a substantial reduction in collision rate over recent end-to-end and model-based baselines.

</details>


### [10] [Online Geometric Consistency Assessment of LiDAR Measurements via Recursive Bayesian Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10566)
*Mingqiao Han,Qigeng Duan,Xuchen Liu,Jinqiang Cui,Xi Chen,Ben M. Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种递归贝叶斯对比方法NDE，在配准点云进入历史前评估几何一致性，实现LiDAR定位与建图的在线漂移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配准误差会在点云测量中产生重复表面和结构变形，缺乏独立参考时只能依赖历史几何进行在线评估，需容忍采样差异并保证观测先评估后积累。

Method: 用Normal-Inverse-Wishart模型表示历史体素几何及其不确定性，用弱各向同性参考模型适应当前采样，比较同一当前点在两模型下的边际预测支持度得到归一化漂移证据NDE；NDE为正触发警报并保留历史状态，否则更新历史。

Result: 在4类平台共约73分钟的14条真实序列中，警报识别出全部9条损坏序列且5条一致序列无误报；4个有事件参考场景首次警报均在参考时间0.9秒内且此前无警报；跨后端响应一致；消融与受控扰动验证了不确定性建模、递归历史、位移与几何支撑的作用；1Hz下平均每次更新4.2–6.1毫秒；10次无人机试验中警报触发里程计隔离、着陆、重定位与任务继续。

Conclusion: NDE能够在无独立参考条件下对配准点云几何进行低延迟在线评估，支持漂移检测与系统安全响应。

Abstract: LiDAR-based localization and mapping systems produce registered point clouds that serve as geometric measurements for downstream estimation and navigation. Registration errors introduce duplicated surfaces and structural deformation into these measurements. In the absence of an independent reference, online assessment relies on geometry accumulated from earlier measurements. Such assessment requires tolerance to sampling variation and an accumulation process in which observations enter the history only after evaluation. We therefore formulate a recursive Bayesian comparison that couples geometric assessment with history maintenance. A Normal-Inverse-Wishart model represents historical voxel geometry and its uncertainty, while a weak isotropic reference adapts to current sampling. Comparing the marginal predictive support of the same current points under both models yields Normalized Drift Evidence (NDE). Assessment precedes assimilation: positive NDE triggers an alarm and preserves the preceding historical state; otherwise, the current observations update the history. Across 14 real-world sequences totaling approximately 73 min from four platform types, alarms identified all nine corrupted sequences and were absent from all five consistent sequences. In four representative scenarios with event references, first alarms occurred within 0.9 s of the reference times, with no preceding alarms. Cross-backend evaluation demonstrates consistent responses to reconstructed geometry. Ablation and controlled perturbation results establish the roles of uncertainty modeling, recursive history, displacement, and geometric support. Mean processing time is 4.2--6.1 ms per update at 1 Hz. In ten UAV trials, alarms initiated odometry isolation, landing, relocalization, and mission continuation.

</details>


### [11] [HSGNav: Hypothesis Scene Graphs for Multi-Scale Lifelong Navigation under Object Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10547)
*Kaiyao Liao,Qingfeng Li,Chen Chen,Qing Sun,Ningbo Gu,Xiaoze Wu,Haochen Zhao,Ying Li,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出假设场景图（HSG）将持久3D家具实体与可修订的表面物体假设分离，并基于此实现多尺度终身导航HSGNav，同时发布FAROS-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实家庭环境中辅助机器人需反复定位从大型家具到桌面小物的多尺度目标，但统一将不同尺度检测实例化为3D对象对小物体脆弱，且缺乏对象状态验证会导致场景变化后残留过期条目。

Method: 形式化HSG，保留小物体视觉证据而不需独立几何；HSGNav利用该分离进行检索（按目标尺度选择实体或假设证据）、探索（获取额外表面观测）和验证（确认目标或使矛盾假设失效）；并构建FAROS-Bench用于表面物体中途移动与多尺度查找目标。

Result: HSGNav在GOAT-Bench上表现具有竞争力，在FAROS-Bench上显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 将持久实体与可修订假设分离能有效支持多尺度终身导航，且现有终身基准缺少中途记忆失效与多尺度搜索需求，需新基准评估。

Abstract: In real homes, assistive robots must repeatedly locate targets from large furniture to tiny tabletop items, as people move objects between household surfaces. Uniformly instantiating detections at different scales as 3D objects can be brittle for small on-surface items; without explicit object-state validation, stale entries may persist after scene changes. Our key insight is to separate persistent 3D furniture entities from revisable surface-object hypotheses, retaining their visual evidence without requiring independent object geometry. We formalize this design as the Hypothesis Scene Graph (HSG), which preserves small-object evidence and supports local revision without rebuilding the stable entity topology. Building on HSG, HSGNav uses this separation for multi-scale lifelong navigation: retrieval selects entity or hypothesis evidence according to target scale, exploration acquires additional surface observations, and verification confirms target presence or invalidates contradicted hypothesis records. Because existing lifelong benchmarks such as GOAT-Bench rarely require mid-episode memory invalidation or multi-scale search, we introduce FAROS-Bench (Find And Re-find Objects across Scales) for mid-episode relocation of surface objects and multi-scale find goals. Experiments show that HSGNav performs competitively on GOAT-Bench while substantially outperforming prior methods on FAROS-Bench.

</details>


### [12] [ReSkill: Selective Self-Improvement for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10510)
*Zheng Sun,Zhihao Li,Zhuo Li,Wenrui Liu,Xin Wang,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReSkill是一个选择性自我改进框架，通过定位能力缺口、局部修订并验证采纳，在组合操作任务中将成功率从30.0%提升至86.2%，且保留可复用技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合操作任务的自我改进需要决定复用哪些内容、在何处改进以及保留哪些改动，现有方法缺乏对能力缺口的精准定位与局部验证机制。

Method: 编码智能体根据指令和技能库构建以对象为中心的任务图，利用节点级执行记录定位失败；在任务层修订阶段组织、交接条件和恢复路径，在技能层强化反复失败的感知、运动、操作和验证能力；候选修订经全任务评估和成对比较后采纳。

Result: 在十个RoboDojo任务上平均成功率从30.0%升至86.2%，保留程序在鲁棒性和模型选择评估中达83.6%；八个真实任务中，80次重建仿真试验成功72次，80次真机试验成功62次（77.5%）；中位修订仅改动三个函数（约0.5%程序），后期任务中39%的任务特定函数直接复用已有实现。

Conclusion: 定位局部缺陷、验证候选改动并积累可复用技能，能够有效提升组合操作任务的执行表现，且部署时无需智能体参与。

Abstract: Self-improvement on compositional manipulation tasks requires deciding what to reuse, where to improve, and which changes to retain. We present ReSkill, a selective self-improvement framework that concentrates revision on capability gaps. A coding agent constructs an object-centric task graph from an instruction and a skill library, then uses node-level execution records to locate failures. At the task level, it revises stage organization, hand-off conditions, and recovery routes; at the skill level, it strengthens perception, motion, manipulation, and verification capabilities that repeatedly fail. Candidate revisions undergo full-task evaluation and paired comparisons before adoption, allowing local changes to accumulate into validated skills and an independently executable policy. The agent participates only during development; the deployed policy calls the retained skills without invoking the agent at run time. On the same development set, average success across ten RoboDojo tasks rises from 30.0% for the initial versions to 86.2% for the final adopted programs; retained programs achieve 83.6% in recorded robustness and model-selection evaluations. Across eight real-world tasks, ReSkill succeeds in 72 of 80 rebuilt-simulation trials and 62 of 80 real-robot trials (77.5%). The median revision changes only three functions, approximately 0.5% of a program, while 39% of task-specific functions in later-added tasks directly reuse existing implementations. These results show how locating local deficiencies, validating candidate changes, and accumulating reusable skills can improve execution of compositional manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [13] [EmbodiedIntentBench: Benchmarking Intent-Driven Runtime Adaptation for Mobile Robotic Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10498)
*Rajat Bhattacharjya,Changwoon Lee,Hyeonjong Ju,Sing-Yao Wu,Hamidreza Alikhani,Zhuoyi Yang,Ian Harris,Nalini Venkatasubramanian,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EmbodiedIntentBench基准，用于评估移动机器人任务中意图驱动的运行时自适应策略，关注任务完成而非孤立感知精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人感知系统需适应操作员需求、资源限制、连接变化和物理运动带来的观测机会，现有评估仅看感知精度或执行效率，无法判断运行时策略是否满足任务目标。

Method: 用结构化契约表示任务意图（覆盖、时间证据、报告、紧迫性、资源），将模拟无人机负载与因果、资源核算的执行耦合；计算决策影响任务时间、资源使用和可贡献证据的观测；在多阶段任务及变化的紧迫性、连接和电量下评估固定与自适应感知策略，并进行消融和Jetson AGX Orin CPU/GPU回放。

Result: 多阶段Depot研究中，可见目标观测全库召回率87.5%的配置仅完成16个任务中的1个，而较低召回配置完成14个；忽略执行导致的观测损失会提高总体任务成功率并反转两种运行时策略的排序；Jetson回放改变本地与远程策略的相对成功率；部分GPU回放中意图驱动自适应在任务成功不变下将建模任务能耗降低最多28%。

Conclusion: EmbodiedIntentBench提供了可扩展框架，在物理与资源约束下依据任务完成度评估运行时决策。

Abstract: Mobile robotic sensing systems must adapt to operator requirements, limited resources, changing connectivity, and observation opportunities shaped by physical motion. Evaluating runtime strategies therefore requires assessing whether their decisions fulfill mission objectives, beyond isolated perception accuracy or execution efficiency. We present EmbodiedIntentBench, a benchmark for intent-driven runtime adaptation in mobile robotic missions. It represents mission intent through structured contracts for coverage, temporal evidence, reporting, urgency, and resources, and couples simulated unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) workloads with causal, resource-accounted execution. Computation decisions affect mission time, resource use, and which observations can contribute evidence. We evaluate fixed and adaptive perception strategies in multi-phase missions and under changing urgency, connectivity, and battery conditions. In the multi-phase Depot study, a configuration with 87.5% full-bank target recall across visible target observations completes only 1 of 16 missions, whereas a lower-recall configuration completes 14. In an ablation, ignoring execution-induced observation loss increases aggregate mission success and reverses the ordering of two runtime strategies. Replays using Jetson AGX Orin CPU and GPU profiles change the relative success of local and remote strategies. In selected Jetson GPU-profile replays, intent-driven adaptation reduces modeled task energy by up to 28% with unchanged mission success. EmbodiedIntentBench provides an extensible framework for evaluating runtime decisions against mission fulfillment under physical and resource constraints.

</details>


### [14] [DreamLander: A 6-DOF World Model based Reinforcement Learning Planetary Lander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12824)
*George Nehma,Patrick Quinn,Madhur Tiwari,Camilo Riano-Rios*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamLander是一种基于DreamerV3的模型强化学习算法，用于6自由度进入下降着陆问题，生成最优制导与控制，实现2米内着陆精度和低于0.3米/秒的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软着陆在太空探索中至关重要，但燃料有限导致对最优制导轨迹和控制的高效性需求；基于模型的强化学习可简化复杂的最优制导轨迹生成过程。

Method: 提出DreamLander，基于DreamerV3的先进基于模型强化学习算法，利用状态传感器观测，针对真实的6自由度进入下降着陆问题生成最优制导与控制。

Result: 该基于模型的方法构建了样本高效且高度泛化的模型，可适用于多种场景并保持高精度，着陆点距指定着陆区2米以内，速度范数小于0.3米/秒。

Conclusion: DreamLander推动了进入下降着陆问题的端到端解决方案发展，展示了基于模型强化学习在航天软着陆制导控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Soft powered descent landings are becoming evermore important in the field of space exploration and scientific advancement, because of their ability to deliver mission critical payloads, both highly accurately and safely. The high efficiency restriction on planetary landings due to the severely limited fuel supply results in the need for optimal guidance trajectories and control. Model based reinforcement learning is proving to be an important tool in simplifying the highly complex process of generating optimal guidance trajectories and control. In this work, we present DreamLander, a state of the art model based reinforcement learning algorithm based on DreamerV3, to generate optimal guidance and control for a realistic 6 degree of freedom entry descent landing problem. The model based approach creates a sample efficient and highly generalizable model that can be applied to a variety of scenarios whilst maintaining a high degree of accuracy, landing within 2 meters of the designated landing zone, and with velocity norm less than 0.3 meters per second. DreamLander makes use of state sensor observations, making progress toward End-to-End solutions of the Entry Descent Landing problem.

</details>


### [15] [Simulating Vein Compressibility for Ultrasound-Guided Femoral Access: Speed, Accuracy, and Phantom Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12818)
*Amit Manicka,Laurent Coulon,Darwin Ang,Michael Loushin,Timothy M. Kowalewski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的物理仿真可快速复现超声引导股动脉穿刺中探头压迫导致的血管变形与静脉先于动脉塌陷的临床信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导股动脉穿刺时，探头压迫会改变血管解剖结构，机器人仿真器需快速复现这些力学过程；但实现有临床意义的信号（静脉先于动脉塌陷）所需计算量尚不明确。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim for Healthcare中，采用Stable Neo-Hookean组织模型，由Vertex Block Descent在单GPU上求解；对已知几何与力学属性的低保真圆柱台式体模进行机器人压迫仿真，并分析单元尺寸、时间步和求解迭代次数的影响。

Result: 动脉预测在各深度误差均在5个百分点内，静脉在25.4 mm压迫的前11 mm内误差在3个百分点内，并复现体模塌陷行为；帧耗时随求解工作量线性增长但精度不随工作量提升；所选设置将每帧求解时间从29,700 ms降至30.4 ms（976倍），与最精细参考设置相差1.03个百分点内；仅物理求解30.4 ms/帧，含超声与图形渲染的完整栈56.4 ms/帧；使用文献力学属性时，数字腿模型在各设置下静脉压缩均远大于动脉，复现静脉先塌陷的临床信号。

Conclusion: NVIDIA Isaac Sim平台或可用于增强强化学习训练或在线仿真，以支持超声引导下的自主股动脉穿刺。

Abstract: In ultrasound-guided femoral access, probe pressure deforms the tissue and changes the vascular anatomy a robot observes. A simulator must reproduce these mechanics fast enough for robotic interaction, yet the computational effort needed for a clinically meaningful signal like vein collapse before artery is unclear. We test the feasibility of a physics-based simulation in NVIDIA's Isaac Sim for Healthcare, with Stable Neo-Hookean tissue solved by Vertex Block Descent on one GPU. A simulation of a low-fidelity cylindrical benchtop phantom with known geometry and mechanical properties, pressed by a robot, predicted its artery within 5 percentage points at every depth. It matched the vein within 3 points through 11 mm of a 25.4 mm press and followed the collapse behavior of the low-fidelity physical phantom. Across element size, time step, and solver iterations, wall clock time per frame scaled linearly with solver work, but accuracy did not improve with more work. The chosen setting reduced solver time per frame by a factor of 976, from 29,700 ms to 30.4 ms, while remaining within 1.03 points of the finest reference setting. The physics solver alone took 30.4 ms per frame, while the full stack, including ultrasound and graphics rendering, took 56.4 ms per frame. Finally, when seeded with realistic literature-based mechanical properties, the digital leg model compressed the vein far more than the artery at every setting, reproducing the expected clinical signal of vein collapse before artery, which is used to distinguish them. In total, these results suggest that the NVIDIA Isaac Sim platform may be suitable for augmenting reinforcement learning training or online simulation for autonomous femoral access with ultrasound.

</details>


### [16] [Learning Heterogeneous Tactile Representation for General Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12806)
*Bing Han,Yichuan Peng,Haoyu Dong,Yuanmeng Hu,Yongyi Jia,Fangjing Wang,Kecheng Zheng,Yiming Zhang,Yanhong Zeng,Xiaoqing Zhang,Chen Song,Rundong Zou,Anqi Dong,Yuan Guan,Ka Leong Cheng,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Nan Xue,Wei Ning*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LingBot-Touch 是一个触觉基础模型，可在压阻、磁性和视触觉等异构传感器间生成统一表征，且触觉 token 可即插即用地集成到扩散策略和 VLA 模型（如 π0.5、LingBot-VLA）中以提升操作灵巧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对接触丰富的灵巧操作不可或缺，但商用触觉传感器发展尚早、各有优劣，核心挑战是让触觉理解摆脱传感器特定物理特性，使具身模型能有效利用异构触觉数据。

Method: 构建 LingBot-Touch 触觉基础模型，实现跨压阻、磁性和视触觉传感器的统一表征并易于扩展新传感器类型；生成的触觉 token 可即插即用地接入从扩散策略到 π0.5、LingBot-VLA 等 VLA 模型的策略框架。

Result: 在仿真和多硬件平台真机部署上的大量实验表明，该触觉表征显著有益于复杂操作任务，同时保持较强的计算效率。

Conclusion: 利用跨传感器数据可扩展触觉学习，并为未来硬件与具身模型设计提供有益启示。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is an indispensable modality for contact-rich dexterous manipulation, a capability that hints at the ultimate frontiers of embodied intelligence. However, commercially available tactile sensors are still at a relatively early stage of development, with each type of sensor offering irreplaceable strengths alongside its own drawbacks. A key challenge is thus to free tactile understanding from sensor-specific physics, so that embodied models can effectively leverage heterogeneous tactile data. To this end, we develop LingBot-Touch, a tactile foundation model characterized by the following properties. (i) Our model yields a unified representation across different tactile sensors (i.e., piezoresistive, magnetic, and visuotactile sensors), and is easily extensible to new sensor types. (ii) The resulting tactile tokens are plug-and-play: they can be seamlessly integrated into policy models across paradigms, from diffusion policy to vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $\pi_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA, consistently enhancing manipulation dexterity. (iii) Extensive experiments, spanning simulation and real-robot deployments across multiple hardware platforms, demonstrate that our tactile representation substantially benefits complex manipulation tasks while maintaining strong computational efficiency. We believe the findings in this work demonstrate the potential of leveraging cross-sensor data to scale tactile learning, and offer encouraging insights for the design of future hardware and embodied models.

</details>


### [17] [Benchmarking Real-World Robotic Manipulation through Stochastic Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12804)
*Elias Gläßer,Gülhan Şikaroğlu,Pavlo Kupyn,Marc Pollefeys,Zuria Bauer,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出随机挑战范式用于真实环境机器人操作基准测试，并实现移动操作基准，在两个实验室对比两款机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作研究面临真实环境可复现性和方法可比性难题，仿真基准无法覆盖真实世界复杂性。

Method: 基于可复现场景构建随机任务样本，设计移动操作基准，使不同实验室机器人按同一分布的成功率进行比较。

Result: 在两实验室对两款机器人进行测试，展示基于随机挑战的成功率对比可行。

Conclusion: 随机挑战可作为机器人基准测试的通用范式，兼顾可复现性与隐藏测试集的严格性和意外性。

Abstract: A challenging bottleneck in manipulation research is reproducibility and comparability of methods in real-world environments. Since manipulation is an interactive task, it cannot be evaluated on a static dataset of observations. Benchmarking in simulation is a common remedy, but cannot cover the whole complexity of real-world tests. This paper proposes a new paradigm of stochastic challenges. Like the well-known YCB objects, it is based on reproducible real-world scenes and therefore allows system-level innovation and comparability between different platforms. Due to their stochastic nature, our challenges however, mimic the rigor and surprise of hidden test sets. To this end, we show how different robots in different labs can be compared based on their success rate over different random task samples, if these samples come from the same distribution. We propose stochastic challenges as a general paradigm for robot benchmarking and implement this paradigm in a novel benchmark for mobile manipulation, on which we compare two robots tested in two different labs.

</details>


### [18] [Sliding into Balance: Benefits of a Sliding Payload for Load Distribution and Disturbance Rejection in Dual-UAV Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12754)
*Jorge Vicente-Martinez,Francisco José Mañas-Álvarez,Edgar Ramirez-Laboreo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文分析了两架无人机通过单根绳索悬挂可滑动负载的协同运输，并与固定负载配置进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双无人机悬挂负载采用两根独立绳索，可能存在绳索张力不均或失稳问题；让负载沿单根绳索滑动有望改善张力分布和可达性。

Method: 建立带粘性摩擦的平面无量纲滑动动力学模型；在无人机静止条件下，从平衡态和扰动响应两方面比较滑动负载与等绳长固定负载；并进行动态仿真，考虑真实阵风剖面。

Result: 滑动负载使两段绳索张力相等，平衡时无绳索松弛；可达区域扩大一倍；除低摩擦与大间距组合外，可容许扰动集增大；阵风下平均张力不平衡较固定负载方案降低。

Conclusion: 单绳滑动负载配置在张力均衡、可达性和抗扰性方面优于固定负载，是一种更优的双无人机协同运输方案。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the cooperative transportation of a payload suspended from two drones in which, instead of being attached to two independent ropes, the payload is allowed to slide along a single rope connecting both UAVs. First, a planar nondimensional dynamical model of the sliding motion with viscous friction is presented. Then, considering stationary drones, this configuration is compared with a fixed payload with the same total rope length, both at equilibrium and under disturbances. The analytical results show that the sliding payload equalizes the tension in both rope segments for any relative position of the drones, so that no rope loses tension at equilibrium; it doubles the region reachable by the payload and, except for low friction combined with a large drone separation, it enlarges the set of admissible disturbances. Finally, a dynamic simulation also shows that under realistic gusty wind profiles, the average tension imbalance between the drones is reduced with respect to the fixed-payload solution.

</details>


### [19] [Library of Hands: Fix the Hands, Not the World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12678)
*Hasaan Ahmad,Qizhen Ying,Yuxuan Sun,Tianjin Huang,Guangming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法Library of Hands（LoH），冻结世界模型，仅校正从指令到实际动作的映射（称为“手”），从而在相机或控制器变化时恢复规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型预测动作后的场景变化，规划器据此搜索动作；但相机移动或控制器改变会使同一指令产生不同动作，导致冻结规划器性能骤降（如LeWM在指令旋转90度后成功率从77.5%降至7.8%）。现有修复需重训模型、需训练数据，且设置恢复时会遗忘。

Method: LoH保持世界模型冻结，仅校正指令到应用动作的映射（hand）。它通过冻结模型从两幅相机图像间的变化中读取hand，无需额外传感器；存储识别出的每个hand，使回归的设置被识别而非重新学习。

Result: 闭环测试中，LoH在三个模拟任务上成功率从28.6%提升至62.3%（翻倍以上），无变化时仅损失0.6个百分点；无需改动即可用于另外两个世界模型，并在三个真实机器人数据预训练模型中的两个上有效，且收益随读取质量提升而增大。

Conclusion: LoH是一种免训练、无需额外传感器、可复用且不遗忘的校正方法，能显著提升冻结世界模型规划器对相机和控制器变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A world model predicts how a scene changes after an action, and a planner uses it to search for actions that reach a goal. If the camera moves or the controller changes, the same command moves the robot differently: when its commands are turned by 90 degrees, a frozen planner with the LeWM world model that succeeds in 77.5% of episodes succeeds in only 7.8%. Current fixes retrain the model, need training data, or forget a set-up when it returns. We propose Library of Hands (LoH), a training-free method that keeps the world model frozen and corrects only the map from commands to applied actions, which we call the hand. LoH reads the hand through the frozen model, with no extra sensor: it finds the action that best explains the change between two camera images (a reading). It stores every hand it identifies, so a returning set-up is recognised rather than re-learnt. In closed-loop tests, LoH more than doubles success on three simulated tasks, from 28.6% to 62.3%, and loses only 0.6 percentage points when nothing changes. It also works, unchanged, with two other world models and on two of three models pretrained on real robot data, and its gain grows with the quality of its readings.

</details>


### [20] [VLA Adaptation with Real and Simulated Data: A Case Study on Robotic Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12676)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人下游任务微调中的数据量、训练更新、检查点选择及仿真数据利用问题，以SO-101机械臂执行国际象棋移动任务为测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需针对每个下游任务和机器人进行微调，实践中缺乏关于所需数据量、训练更新量、检查点选择方法以及能否用仿真缓解真实数据采集成本的明确指导。

Method: 在需精确操作与空间理解的任务中，让SO-101机械臂按名称（如e4、a5）移动棋王；用不同数据集规模和更新预算微调π_{0.5}，数据来源包括遥操作演示和随机脚本专家生成的仿真演示；真实机械臂上最多用256条遥操作演示微调，并以每个策略25次试验评估10个策略。

Result: 仿真中成功率随数据和更新量增加而提升；短暂学习率冷却最多提升31个百分点，且对检查点比较必要；验证损失和动作误差不可靠。真实机械臂部分结果显示仿真演示预训练有显著帮助，真实数据稀缺时最明显；策略自身轨迹、过滤行为克隆、RECAP式优势条件、自动教练纠正或残差强化学习等方法均未带来可测量改进。

Conclusion: 学习率冷却对检查点选择至关重要，验证损失和动作误差不宜用于模型选择；仿真演示预训练可有效缓解真实数据不足，而多种先进训练策略在本设定下未优于普通行为克隆；作者发布了仿真器、专家和真实演示数据。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are trained as general-purpose foundation models for robot learning, and require fine-tuning for each downstream task and robot. This raises practical questions such as how much data and training updates are needed, how checkpoints should be selected, and whether simulation can be leveraged to alleviate the cost of real data collection. We study these questions on a task that requires both precise manipulation and spatial understanding of the scene. In this task, an SO-101 arm moves a chess king between two squares that are given only by their names, such as e4 and a5. We fine-tune $\pi_{0.5}$ with various dataset sizes and update budgets, using teleoperated demonstrations as well as simulated demonstrations from a randomized scripted expert that imitates the variability of human demonstrations. In simulation, the success rate scales with both data and updates. Importantly, we find that a short learning-rate cool-down adds up to 31 points and is necessary for comparing checkpoints, and that validation loss and action error are unreliable for model selection. On the real arm, we fine-tune on up to 256 teleoperated demonstrations and evaluate ten policies in 25 trials each. Partial results indicate that pretraining on the simulated demonstrations helps substantially, most clearly when real data is scarce. We also present a negative result, in which training on the policy's own rollouts with filtered behavior cloning, RECAP-style advantage conditioning, corrections from an automatic coach, or residual reinforcement learning did not yield a measurable improvement over plain behavior cloning in our setting. We release the simulator, the expert and the real demonstrations.

</details>


### [21] [Correctness Boundaries of Typed Plans for Natural-Language Robot Fleet Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12654)
*Artem Tuzov,Dmitrii Vizitei,Evgenii Shlomov,Senya Belykh,Anastasia Malakhova,Alexander Maloletov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文考察自然语言机器人集群控制中计划验证与执行报告能确立什么，并在ROS 2三级实现与44例基准上进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言机器人集群控制中，需明确计划验证与执行报告究竟能证明什么，以区分可执行动作变化与完成报告所覆盖需求的变化。

Method: 通过类型化计划、确定性修正、集群协调与结果检查追踪命令；采用二十个仿真机器人的ROS 2三级实现；离线基准44例检查计划结构与选定命令需求；在无响应模式的五种本地配置中测试，并进行18次仿真系列及编队轨迹分析。

Result: 五种本地配置通过率为70.0-87.3%；选定18次仿真系列均满足基于位姿的任务条件，含两次软件变更后替代不完整运行的成功重复；保存的计划与日志记录了原始任务区内的重复取货分配与目标调整；编队轨迹显示结构上被接受但队形错误的计划、被拒绝的路线，以及重规划后遗漏请求机器人的到达报告；携带完成记录同一机器人的取货与后续投放区存在。

Conclusion: 这些案例区分了可执行动作变化与完成报告所覆盖需求的变化，因此有必要将有效分配、目标与报告结果同原始命令进行核对。

Abstract: We examine what plan validation and execution reports establish in natural-language robot fleet control. Commands are traced through typed plans, deterministic corrections, fleet coordination and outcome checks in a three-level Robot Operating System 2 implementation with twenty simulated robots. An offline benchmark of 44 cases checks plan structure and selected command requirements. Across five local configurations without a response schema, 70.0-87.3% of responses pass. All runs in a selected 18-run simulation series meet their pose-based task conditions. This series includes two successful repeats that replaced incomplete runs after a software change. Saved plans and logs document duplicate pickup assignments and goal adjustments within the original task zones. Separate formation traces show a structurally accepted plan with the wrong formation shape and a rejected route, and an arrival report that omitted a requested robot after replanning. Carry completion records pickup and subsequent drop-off zone presence for the same robot. The cases distinguish changes to executable actions from changes to the requirements covered by a completion report. They motivate checking the effective assignments, goals and reported outcome against the original command.

</details>


### [22] [Modeling and Estimation of a Towed Marine System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12568)
*Josef Matouš,Simon Hoff,Damiano Varagnolo,Marius S. Rundhovde,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对双无人水面艇拖曳海洋系统，提出集中质量建模方法并设计降阶观测器和PMEKF两种状态估计器，仅利用艇载传感器测量，仿真与实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拖曳海洋系统（含双ASV、脐带缆、拖鱼和尾环缆）的状态估计对作业控制至关重要，但现有研究缺乏仅依赖ASV艇载传感器的完整建模与估计方案。

Method: 采用集中质量法对缆绳和拖鱼建模以兼顾动力学精度与计算可行性；通过Lyapunov分析验证模型稳定性与收敛性；基于该模型提出降阶观测器和完美测量扩展卡尔曼滤波（PMEKF）两种状态估计方法。

Result: 数值仿真与实验结果验证了所提建模方法及两种状态估计方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提集中质量模型与仅依赖ASV艇载传感器的状态估计方法能够有效实现双ASV拖曳海洋系统的状态估计。

Abstract: This paper addresses the modeling and state estimation of a towed marine system consisting of two autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs), a pair of umbilical cables, towfish, and a trailing loop cable. Inspired by the existing literature, we employ a lumped-mass modeling approach for the cables and towfish, capturing the essential dynamics while maintaining computational tractability. A Lyapunov-based analysis is conducted to verify the stability and convergence properties of the proposed model. Building on this model, we propose two state estimation methods, namely a reduced-order observer and a perfect-measurement extended Kalman filter (PMEKF), using only the measurements from the ASVs' onboard sensors. Numerical simulations and experimental results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [23] [Galilean Robots, Aristotelian Sensors: Joint Spatiotemporal Trajectory Estimation and Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12526)
*Abhishek Goudar,Jonathan Kelly,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出用伽利略群表示机器人轨迹、用亚里士多德子群表示传感器外参，将运动与时间偏移的耦合直接内嵌于群结构中，从而无需单独参数化的连续时间轨迹即可联合估计轨迹并完成时空标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空标定方法将外参表示为刚体变换加标量时间偏移，空间与时间参数通过不同机制进入估计问题：空间变换与机器人位姿直接复合，而时间偏移需对被平移时刻的轨迹求值，依赖运动补偿、插值或连续时间轨迹表示，增加了复杂度。

Method: 以特殊伽利略群表示机器人轨迹、以亚里士多德子群表示传感器外参，利用群结构直接刻画运动与时间偏移的耦合；推导基于旋量和IMU的运动先验，兼容理想与非理想时钟，并使用相机、位置和测距测量进行评测。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，在多种异构传感器配置下，该框架的标定与轨迹估计性能与已有成熟方法相当。

Conclusion: 该简化框架在不牺牲性能的前提下，较现有方法提供了使用便利性上的提升。

Abstract: Multi-sensor state estimation requires accurate spatial and temporal calibration between heterogeneous sensors. Existing spatiotemporal calibration methods typically represent sensor extrinsic parameters as a rigid-body transformation together with a scalar temporal offset, causing the spatial and temporal parameters to enter the estimation problem through separate mechanisms. While the spatial transformation composes directly with the robot pose, accounting for the temporal offset requires evaluating the trajectory at a shifted time, typically through motion compensation, interpolation, or a continuous-time trajectory representation. In this work, we formulate joint trajectory estimation and spatiotemporal calibration using the special Galilean group for the robot trajectory and the Aristotelian subgroup for sensor extrinsics. This representation incorporates the coupling between motion and temporal offset directly through the group structure, avoiding the need for a separately parameterized continuous-time trajectory. We develop twist- and IMU-driven motion priors, accommodate both ideal and non-ideal clocks, and evaluate the framework with camera, position, and range measurements. Simulated and real experiments demonstrate calibration and trajectory-estimation performance comparable to established methods across heterogeneous sensor configurations. Our simplified framework therefore offers a quality-of-life improvement over existing methods without compromising performance.

</details>


### [24] [V3DSAM: Semantic-Geometric Loop Verification and Geometry-Anchored Persistent Instance Mapping for Feed-Forward Dense SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12452)
*Maolin Ye,Dongqing Zhao,Linyang Li,Shuai Xue,Yuan Liu,Yiyuan Wang,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出V3DSAM，一种开放词汇语义稠密SLAM系统，通过场景级与对象级的物理对应关系解决长序列前馈稠密SLAM中不可靠的跨视角关联问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列前馈稠密SLAM中跨视角关联不可靠，现有方法依赖骨干网络内部特征和时序ID传播，鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将VGGT-SLAM 2.0的前馈几何估计与SAM3的开放词汇语义观测相结合；提出语义-几何双尺度回环验证，利用像素级双向重投影和对象级3D空间对应识别假回环，无需访问骨干内部特征；提出几何锚定的持久实例建图，在优化后的全局坐标系中通过类约束3D连通性定义物理实例并分配全局ID，无需帧间身份传播。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D评估中，验证器拒绝了全部四个假回环候选；长序列ScanNet实验显示实例身份一致性得到提升。

Conclusion: V3DSAM减少了对骨干内部特征和时序ID传播的依赖，实现了鲁棒的长期跨视角关联。

Abstract: To address unreliable cross-view association in long-sequence feed-forward dense SLAM, this letter presents V3DSAM, an open-vocabulary semantic dense SLAM system that establishes physical correspondences at both the scene and object levels. The system combines feed-forward geometric estimates from VGGT-SLAM 2.0 with open-vocabulary semantic observations from SAM3. We introduce semantic-geometric dual-scale loop verification, which identifies false loop closures using pixel-level bidirectional reprojection and object-level 3D spatial correspondences, without accessing backbone-internal features. We further propose geometry-anchored persistent instance mapping, which defines physical instances through class-constrained 3D connectivity in an optimized global coordinate system and assigns global IDs without inter-frame identity propagation. The proposed verifier rejects all four false loop candidates in our TUM RGB-D evaluation, while long-sequence ScanNet experiments show improved instance identity consistency. Thus, V3DSAM reduces dependence on internal backbone features and temporal ID propagation for robust long-term cross-view association.

</details>


### [25] [Bridging Task Planning and Execution for Language-Conditioned Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12409)
*Emanuele Cuzzocrea,Giuseppina Iannotti,Luca Rossini,Arturo Laurenzi,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出零样本视觉语言框架，用于执行感知的双臂操作，联合推理任务目标与物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在杂乱真实环境中执行自然语言操作任务时，双臂人形平台面临额外交互与运动学约束，需同时考虑任务目标与执行可行性。

Method: 基于结构化场景表示（语义物体关系与执行约束）进行规划，将兼容动作组织为带前后条件的并行双臂阶段，并用分层验证器在感知不确定性下评估条件、利用阶段级执行历史支持局部恢复或定向重规划。

Result: 在CENTAURO轮腿人形机器人九个语言条件操作场景中任务成功率高于非执行感知基线；在专门失败场景中分层验证以更少模型查询恢复执行失败。

Conclusion: 将执行推理直接融入任务规划具有明显优势。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly expected to execute natural-language manipulation tasks in real-world environments. This is particularly relevant in household assistance and logistics, where clutter requires reasoning about both task objectives and execution feasibility. This challenge becomes even more pronounced for bimanual humanoid platforms, where coordinating two manipulators introduces additional interaction and kinematic constraints. We present a zero-shot vision-language framework for execution-aware bimanual manipulation that jointly reasons about task objectives and physical feasibility. Planning is grounded in a structured scene representation that combines semantic object relations with execution constraints, enabling the selection of obstacle relocations and execution choices while preserving task semantics. Compatible actions are organised into parallel bimanual stages with explicit pre- and post-conditions. A hierarchical validator evaluates these conditions under perceptual uncertainty and uses stage-level execution history to support local recovery or targeted replanning. Experiments on the CENTAURO hybrid wheeled-legged humanoid robot across nine language-conditioned manipulation scenarios show higher task success than a non-execution-aware baseline. In dedicated failure scenarios, hierarchical validation recovers from execution failures with fewer model queries than always replanning. These results demonstrate the benefits of combining execution reasoning directly into task planning. Videos and additional material are available on the Project Webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [PCTac: Mechanochromic Photonic-Crystal Tactile Sensor for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12383)
*Ruilin Zhang,Xuanshuo Liu,Junzhe Dai,Kefei Wu,Xuhao Qin,Ziyuan Jiao,Wanlin Li,Yurui Qu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PCTac是一种基于力致变色光子晶体材料的开源视触觉指尖传感器，直接以密集色相场作为触觉观测，并在真实机器人操作任务中验证其优于RGB触觉和纯视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉传感器主要通过成像柔性界面变形来感知接触，力信息仍需借助标定、几何重建或学习映射间接推断；力致变色光子晶体材料可将局部应力直接编码为结构色场，但此前很少被用于闭环机器人学习的视触觉传感。

Method: 提出PCTac力致变色光子晶体指尖，输出密集色相场作为触觉表示；进行加载表征以验证法向载荷与色相响应的可重复关系；将该表示直接集成到Diffusion Policy中，在三个真实机器人操作任务上开展实验。

Result: 90次物理试验中，PCTac色相成功68次（75.6%），PCTac RGB为60次（66.7%），Wedge RGB为57次（63.3%），纯视觉仅29次（32.2%）；PCTac色相在各任务上均取得最高成功率，在扰动试管插入任务中为16/30，对比触觉RGB基线的13/30和纯视觉的1/30。

Conclusion: 力致变色结构色可作为一种紧凑、载荷敏感的触觉表示，用于机器人学习和闭环操作。

Abstract: Modern visuotactile sensors primarily sense contact by imaging the deformation of a compliant interface. Using techniques such as photometric stereo, they can recover detailed contact geometry, often represented as surface normals or depth. However, force-related information typically still needs to be inferred indirectly through calibration, geometric reconstruction, or learned mappings. Mechanochromic photonic-crystal (PC) materials offer an alternative physical encoding mechanism: contact loading changes their periodic microstructure and spectral response, directly transforming local stress into a spatially resolved structural-color field. Despite this potential, PC materials have rarely been explored as visuotactile sensors for closed-loop robot learning. We present PCTac, an open-source mechanochromic PC fingertip that uses a dense hue field as its tactile observation. Loading characterization demonstrates a repeatable relationship between the applied normal load and the observed hue response. We further integrate this representation directly into a Diffusion Policy and evaluate it on three real-robot manipulation tasks. Across 90 physical rollouts, PCTac Hue achieves 68 successes (75.6\%), compared with 60 (66.7\%) for PCTac RGB, 57 (63.3\%) for Wedge RGB, and 29 (32.2\%) for vision-only. PCTac Hue also achieves the highest observed success rate on each evaluated task, including Perturbed Test-Tube Insertion, where it achieves 16/30 successes versus 13/30 for tactile RGB baselines and 1/30 for vision-only. These results demonstrate the potential of mechanochromic structural color as a compact, load-sensitive tactile representation for robot learning and closed-loop manipulation. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [27] [AutoVLA-D: Learn a Trajectory-Geometric Condition from Reasoning for Autonomous Driving VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12382)
*Yuguang Yang,Zhewen Tan,Canyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Cheng Chi,Chunyang Liu,Kehua Sheng,Jinyu Yang,Baochang Zhan,Bo Zhang,Yan Wang,Xianbin Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrajCond 通过引入离散轨迹几何条件，改进自回归 VLA 规划的连续轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 VLA 规划通常直接预测连续轨迹，但驾驶指令描述的是动作而非自车状态，削弱了对最终连续轨迹的直接条件约束。

Method: 提出 TrajCond，将轨迹表示为 1D-Brohan 微分动作轨迹，先用两阶段训练框架学习，再预测最终连续轨迹；在自回归生成中同时产生离散和数值轨迹，方法命名为 AutoVLA-D（Double）。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest、nuScenes 和 NeuroNCAP 上的实验表明，离散轨迹作为直接输出较弱，但作为 TrajCond 更有效，并在闭环和开环驾驶基准上持续提升最终连续轨迹预测。

Conclusion: 离散轨迹更适合作为 VLA 规划的几何条件而非最终输出，TrajCond 能有效增强连续轨迹预测性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive VLA planning usually predicts continuous trajectories as the final output, but driving instructions often describe actions rather than ego states, which weakens direct conditioning on the final continuous trajectory. We address this gap with TrajCond, which introduces a discrete trajectory-geometric condition for VLA planning. Specifically, we represent TrajCond as a 1D-Brohan differential action trajectory and learn it with a two-stage training framework before final continuous trajectory prediction. With the simultaneous autoregressive generation of discrete and numeric trajectories, we name our method AutoVLA-D (Double). Experiments on NAVSIM navtest, nuScenes, and NeuroNCAP show that discrete trajectories are weaker as direct outputs but more effective as TrajCond, consistently improving final continuous trajectory prediction across closed-loop and open-loop driving benchmarks. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [28] [Safety-Constrained Intelligent Control of a 2-DOF Robotic Manipulator Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12378)
*Gabriel da Silva Lima,Made Widhi Surya Atman,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出一种RBF神经网络补偿器，其输出同时用于笛卡尔跟踪控制器和关节空间CBF滤波器，并引入由请求力矩与实际力矩之差驱动的辅助状态修正学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF滤波器依赖的动态模型在参数和外部力不确定时可能不准确，需要补偿以保持安全约束和跟踪性能。

Method: 采用RBF神经网络补偿器，输出共享给笛卡尔跟踪控制器与关节空间CBF滤波器；用请求力矩与应用力矩之差驱动辅助状态来修正学习信号，降低滤波器干预对自适应学习的直接影响；在两连杆机械臂上进行约束调节、消融、匹配不确定性与扰动试验以及工作范围和时序研究。

Result: 所提控制器在参数不确定性下改善了跟踪性能，同时保持关节、速度和力矩限制；共享补偿和辅助修正在该改进中均有贡献。

Conclusion: 共享RBF补偿与辅助学习信号修正相结合，可在模型不确定下兼顾跟踪性能与CBF安全约束。

Abstract: Control barrier function (CBF) filters depend on dynamic models that can be inaccurate when their parameters and external forces are uncertain. This paper investigates a radial basis function (RBF) neural network compensator whose output is shared by a Cartesian tracking controller and a joint-space CBF filter. An auxiliary state driven by the difference between requested and applied torques corrects the learning signal, reducing the direct influence of filter intervention on adaptation. A two-link manipulator is evaluated through constrained regulation, ablations, matched uncertainty and disturbance trials, and operating-range and timing studies. The results demonstrate that the proposed controller improves tracking while maintaining joint, velocity, and torque limits under parameter uncertainty, and that both the shared compensation and the auxiliary correction contribute to this improvement.

</details>


### [29] [UniOMA: Unified Optimal-Transport Multi-Modal Structural Alignment for Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12351)
*Xinrui Zu,Mark Hoogendoorn,Kevin Sebastian Luck,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Gromov-Wasserstein距离正则化的多模态对齐方法，在对比损失基础上增强跨模态结构一致性，并在五个机器人基准上验证了其有效性与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态对齐方法虽通过条件或高阶统计依赖建模来对齐模态，但未显式考虑跨模态的结构连贯性；同时机器人感知中力觉、触觉等模态数据样本密度差异大，亟需能保持结构一致性的对齐手段。

Method: 在现有对比损失上增加几何感知的Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）距离正则项：为每个模态编码模态内几何结构，并通过动态学习的GW重心减少跨模态结构差异，从而在实例级对比对齐之外引入结构一致性；该最优传输对齐策略可无缝集成到多数InfoNCE类目标中。

Result: 在五个机器人基准上的大量实验表明，相比最现进的多模态对比基线，该方法在结构对齐和下游任务（包括机器人及环境状态预测）性能上均有提升，且在样本密度差异大的力觉、触觉等模态上同样有效。

Conclusion: 显式引入基于GW距离的结构正则化可有效提升多模态表示的结构一致性与泛化性，且作为一种通用正则项易于嵌入现有对比学习框架。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable and well-aligned multimodal representations remains a core challenge in robot learning. While recent approaches have attempted to align modalities by modeling conditional or higher-order statistical dependencies, they do not explicitly consider the structural coherence across modalities. In this work, we propose a novel multimodal alignment method that augments existing contrastive losses with a geometry-aware Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distance-based regularization. To this end, we encode intra-modal geometry for each modality and reduce cross-modal structural discrepancies through a dynamically learned GW barycenter, thereby encouraging structural consistency in addition to instance-level contrastive alignment. We apply this optimal-transport-based alignment strategy to robot perception tasks involving underexplored modalities such as force and tactile signals, where modality data often exhibit varying sample densities. Extensive experiments on five robotic benchmarks show that our method improves structural alignment and downstream performance over state-of-the-art multimodal contrastive baselines across a wide range of downstream tasks, including robot and environment state prediction. Moreover, our GW-based regularization can be seamlessly integrated into most InfoNCE-like objectives.

</details>


### [30] [ExoStack: A Modular and Reconfigurable Software Framework for Wearable Robotics Using ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12306)
*Debadrata Sarkar,Upamanyu Ray,Soumen Sen,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExoStack 是一个模块化 ROS 2 框架，用于将体表传感、步态解读、辅助生成与执行连接起来，并在嵌入式枢纽与主机之间实现个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人依赖一条从体表传感到达步态解读、辅助生成和执行的软件链，个性化要求每次测量响应都与产生它的辅助参数保持关联；现有实现缺乏模块化、可复用的框架支持。

Method: 提出 ExoStack：ESA 库将 ESP32 配置为 microROS 传感与执行枢纽；ExoCore 提供多枢纽同步、步态事件检测、轮廓生成、运动规划、代价评估和人在回路优化等由配置实例化的节点；PASE 桌面应用负责编辑配置、启动处理阶段并可视化 ROS 2 话题；硬件驱动与算法插件可通过定义接口添加而无需修改通用处理节点。

Result: 在三种嵌入式计算机上表征了枢纽与规划器的时序性能，并在两套气动外骨骼服装上演示了复用。

Conclusion: ExoStack 为可穿戴机器人提供了一条模块化、可扩展且可复用的 ROS 2 软件链，支持个性化关联与插件式集成。

Abstract: Wearable robots depend on a software chain that links body-worn sensing to gait interpretation, assistance generation, and actuation. Personalization further requires each measured response to remain associated with the assistance parameters that generated it. This paper presents ExoStack, a modular ROS 2 framework for implementing that chain across embedded hubs and a host computer. Its Embedded Sensing and Actuation (ESA) library configures ESP32 boards as microROS sensing and actuation hubs. ExoCore supplies multi-hub synchronization, gait-event detection, profile generation, motion planning, cost evaluation, and human-in-the-loop (HIL) optimization as configuration-instantiated nodes. The PASE desktop application edits configurations, launches the processing stages, and visualizes ROS 2 topics. Hardware drivers and algorithm plugins can be added through defined interfaces without modifying the generic processing nodes. We characterize hub and planner timing on three embedded computers and demonstrate reuse on two pneumatic exosuits.

</details>


### [31] [Be My Eye: Learning to See Beyond a Robot's Own Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12295)
*Baifu Huang,Shilin Fang,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向任务的主动上下文共享框架，将机器人观测压缩为紧凑表示，在有限通信预算下支持多机器人协作决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的机器人存在部分可观测性，多机器人协作时单个机器人的观测往往不足以有效执行任务；关键挑战在于利用伙伴机器人的互补观测，而信息共享不应仅是被动传输原始观测或通用视觉特征，应按任务目标和执行进度选择性编码任务相关信息。

Method: 框架采用两阶段训练：第一阶段将机载观测与任务指令联合编码为紧凑上下文表示，通过面向任务的未来预测和软任务进度对应学习进行与协作无关的压缩；第二阶段将学习到的表示集成到预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中，实现双向上下文交换，各机器人结合伙伴互补信息与本地观测生成动作，动作监督联合优化上下文表示与策略。

Result: 受控实验表明在受限通信预算下取得显著性能提升，三项真实世界实验在复杂双机器人任务中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果体现了从被动视觉传输向主动、面向任务的上下文共享的转变，该共享方式直接支持协作决策。

Abstract: Robots operating in the real world are inherently subject to partial observability, particularly in multi-robot collaboration, where individual observations may be insufficient for effective task execution. A key challenge is leveraging complementary observations from partner robots to support collaborative decision-making. We argue that information sharing should go beyond passively transmitting raw observations or generic visual features. Instead, robots should selectively encode task-relevant information according to task objectives and execution progress. To this end, we propose a task-oriented active context sharing framework that transforms robot observations into compact representations, preserving critical information under constrained communication budgets. The framework adopts a two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, onboard observations and task instructions are jointly encoded into compact context representations. Task-oriented future prediction and soft task-progress correspondence learning guide this collaboration-agnostic compression, preserving information about task objectives and execution progress. In the second stage, the learned representations are integrated into a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, enabling bidirectional context exchange. Each robot combines complementary partner information with local observations to guide action generation. Action supervision jointly refines the context representations and policy, aligning information sharing with collaborative decision-making. Controlled experiments demonstrate substantial performance gains under constrained communication budgets, while three real-world experiments validate the framework in complex dual-robot tasks. These results highlight a shift from passive visual transmission toward active, task-oriented context sharing that directly supports collaborative decision-making.

</details>


### [32] [VLA-MOPD: On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Task VLA Policy Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12286)
*Yongxuan Lv,Zhen Fang,Lichen Man,Hao Li,Pu Li,Hengyi Wang,Langfeng Cao,Lin Chen,Jie Huang,Xiu Su,Shan You,Fei Wang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-MOPD提出一种多教师在线策略蒸馏协议，将多个流匹配VLA专家整合为单一共享学生策略，在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和Piper机器人上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理智能需要具身智能体将语言与观察对齐并执行动作，同时保留已有技能；VLA策略虽提供统一接口，但任务特定后训练常产生孤立专家，而混合任务强化学习信用稀疏、离线蒸馏会遗漏学生访问的观察、端点蒸馏无法监督中间动作生成状态。

Method: 先获得任务或套件专家，再让共享学生采集机器人rollout，并在每个访问到的观察和ODE中间状态查询按指令路由的冻结教师；用分离的每流步转移目标，通过掩码局部匹配损失监督有效动作块，并可选加入参考策略项以保持其他任务能力。

Result: 从LIBERO每任务一条轨迹起步，VLA-MOPD将mean success-once从75.4%提升至98.9%，端点动作蒸馏为98.15%；RoboTwin 2.0平均成功率从73.0%提升至89.3%；Piper机器人两轮整合将平均成功率从70.0%提升至90.0%；在近似匹配的Spatial-adaptation研究中，参考匹配将Object套件下降限制在3.0个百分点，而无参考项为13.2个百分点。

Conclusion: VLA-MOPD通过在线策略多教师蒸馏与中间ODE状态监督，能有效整合流匹配VLA专家，并在多种机器人和基准上取得优于端点蒸馏的成功率与更好的技能保持。

Abstract: Physical intelligence requires embodied agents to ground language in observations and execute actions while retaining useful skills. Vision-language-action (VLA) policies offer a shared interface, but task-specific post-training often produces separate specialists. Consolidation is difficult: mixed-task reinforcement learning provides sparse trajectory-level credit, while offline distillation may miss student-visited observations and endpoint distillation does not supervise intermediate action-generation states. We introduce VLA Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (VLA-MOPD), a protocol for consolidating flow-matching VLA specialists. After acquiring task or suite experts, a shared student collects robot rollouts and queries the instruction-routed frozen teacher at each visited observation and intermediate ordinary differential equation (ODE) state. Detached per-flow-step transition targets supervise valid action chunks through a masked local matching loss; an optional reference-policy term supports retention on other tasks. Starting from one trajectory per LIBERO task, VLA-MOPD raises mean success-once from 75.4% to 98.9%, compared with 98.15% for endpoint action distillation. It improves mean RoboTwin 2.0 success from 73.0% to 89.3%; on a Piper robot, two consolidation rounds improve average success from 70.0% to 90.0%. In a near-matched Spatial-adaptation study, reference matching limits the Object suite decrease to 3.0 points, versus 13.2 without the reference term.

</details>


### [33] [Hierarchical Multi-Channel Tip-Over Prediction via Isolated Temporal Models for Planetary Rover Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12261)
*Siddhant Shete,Raul Dominguez,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层多通道倾倒预测框架，通过隔离时序学习结合TCN多步预测与GRU前向动力学残差检测，实现行星车实时安全分级响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星车在非结构化环境通信延迟下需自主防倾倒，现有学习法仅优化单一预测目标，难以同时捕获渐进失稳与突发地形扰动。

Method: 多步TCN从IMU学习渐进失稳模式；GRU前向动力学模型预测名义运动并以残差检测异常偏差；决策级融合与优先级仲裁生成预警和紧急制动等分级动作，部署于Orocos RTT/ROCK框架，100Hz运行。

Result: 异构地形IMU实验中事件预测覆盖率97.58%，中位提前时间1.82s，优于单通道预测。

Conclusion: 隔离时序模型结合分层决策融合可为不确定地形下行星车实时安全提供稳健方案。

Abstract: Reliable tip-over prevention is critical for planetary rovers operating in unstructured environments where communication delays prevent real-time human intervention. Existing learning-based approaches typically optimize a single prediction objective, limiting their ability to capture both gradual instability evolution and abrupt terrain disturbances. This work presents a hierarchical multi-channel tip-over prediction framework based on isolated temporal learning. A multi-horizon Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) learns progressive instability patterns from inertial measurements, while a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-based forward dynamics model predicts nominal rover motion and detects unexpected dynamic deviations through prediction residuals. These complementary signals are integrated through decision-level fusion and priority-based arbitration to generate graded safety actions, including early warning and emergency braking. The proposed system is deployed as an Orocos Real-Time Toolkit (RTT)-based safety component within the Rock robotics framework, operating at 100 Hz on a planetary rover software stack. Experiments on Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) samples collected across heterogeneous terrains demonstrate 97.58% event prediction coverage with a 1.82s median lead time, outperforming individual prediction channels. The results validate that isolated temporal models combined through hierarchical decision fusion provide a robust solution for real-time rover safety in uncertain terrain.

</details>


### [34] [A Game-Theoretic MPC Planner for Cooperative and Adversarial Drone Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12226)
*Zdeněk Bouček,Miroslav Flídr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并评估一种博弈论模型预测控制规划器，用于无人机协作到达目标与对抗追逃，分析求解器、计算时间及仿真与实飞验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机协作目标到达与对抗追逃需要实时可行的规划方法，需评估不同博弈求解器及计算限制。

Method: 结合三维预测、个体目标、可选共享分离约束和轨迹跟踪接口的博弈论MPC；比较协作博弈求解器，研究无人机数、预测时域和移动阻塞对计算时间与数值接受率的影响；用PyBullet仿真和实验室飞行验证。

Result: 移动阻塞减少独立控制变量并降低计算时间；量化了不同配置下实时规划的实际限制；仿真演示协作到达与追逃，实飞验证轨迹跟踪接口。

Conclusion: 该规划器可作为进一步评估和控制器开发的基础。

Abstract: We present and evaluate a game-theoretic model predictive control planner for cooperative goal reaching and adversarial pursuit-evasion with drones. The planner combines 3D prediction, individual objectives, optional shared separation constraints and trajectory-tracking interfaces. A comparison of cooperative game solvers assesses their ability to find feasible trajectories that resolve conflicts. A computational study quantifies how number of drones, prediction horizon and move blocking affect computation time and numerical acceptance. Move blocking holds control inputs constant over groups of prediction steps, reducing the number of independent control variables and lowering computation time. Timing measurements identify the practical limits of real-time planning in the tested configurations. PyBullet simulations demonstrate cooperative goal reaching and pursuit-evasion. A laboratory flight complements the simulations by validating the trajectory-tracking interfaces on physical drones. The planner can serve as a basis for further evaluation and controller development.

</details>


### [35] [Graph-Based RL for Sequential Rendezvous Planning with Multiple Patrolling USVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12205)
*Seung Woo Han,Hyeon Bin Yeo,Seung Heon Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合网格路径规划、代理航行时间预测与图强化学习的框架，用于多无人水面艇巡逻任务中指挥艇的 rendezvous 路径规划与优先级感知排序，以最小化任务完成时间和总延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人水面艇巡逻任务中，指挥艇需依次与各巡逻艇会合，需解决运动可行路径规划与优先级感知的会合排序问题，以最小化任务完成时间及相对优先级相关截止时间的总延迟。

Method: 框架包含三部分：考虑洋流并通过多阶段机动近似转率约束的网格路径规划器（经轨迹跟踪仿真验证可行性）；用可行转移图（FTG）表示候选会合事件与时序可行转移，并利用图神经网络学习策略；用代理模型从紧凑的洋流与运动特征预测航行时间，避免FTG构建与策略训练中重复路径搜索。

Result: 与贪心、MLP-RL和两步前瞻贪心相比，平均目标值分别降低10.5%、3.5%和1.5%，且计算时间约为两步前瞻贪心的1/35；在未见优先级层级设置下仍保持对MLP-RL的优势，且舰队规模增大时增益更大。

Conclusion: 所提框架在保证路径运动可行性的同时，显著降低任务完成时间与总延迟，并以远低于基线方法的计算成本实现更优性能，且具备良好的泛化性与可扩展性。

Abstract: In patrol missions involving multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs), a designated command USV can support fleet management by sequentially rendezvousing with each patrolling USV. These operations require motion-feasible route planning and priority-aware rendezvous sequencing, with the objective of minimizing mission completion time and total tardiness relative to priority-dependent due dates. We propose a framework combining grid-based path planning, surrogate travel-time prediction, and graph-based reinforcement learning. The path planner accounts for ocean currents and approximates turn-rate constraints through multi-stage maneuvers, with path feasibility empirically validated through trajectory-tracking simulations. For rendezvous sequencing, a Feasible Transition Graph (FTG) represents candidate rendezvous events and temporally feasible transitions, enabling a graph neural network to exploit spatiotemporal rendezvous relationships for policy learning. A surrogate predicts travel times from compact current and motion features to avoid repeated path searches during FTG construction and policy training. Evaluation using grid-based path planning shows statistically significant average objective reductions of 10.5%, 3.5%, and 1.5% compared with greedy, MLP-RL, and 2-step lookahead greedy, respectively. The policy achieves these improvements while requiring approximately 1/35 of the latter's computation time. It maintains its advantage over MLP-RL under unseen priority-tier settings, with larger gains as fleet size increases.

</details>


### [36] [Embodied First Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12177)
*Kangrui Wang,Qineng Wang,Changhe Chen,Pingyue Zhang,Chi Gui,Feishi Wang,Canyu Chen,Wenli Xiao,Xiaoyu Huang,Tonghe Zhang,Linjie Li,Zihan Wang,Minyeong Kim,Yiyou Sun,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Qi Zhu,Han Liu,Eric P. Xing,Huaxiu Yao,Zhiwen Fan,Yuejiang Liu,Dawn Song,Pieter Abbeel,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Yuke Zhu,Guanya Shi,Ruohan Zhang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Embodied First Exam，一个面向编码智能体的统一机器人评测框架，含47台机器人、35套件、1156项任务，最强智能体在标准设置下核心任务成功率仅34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准围绕视觉运动策略学习构建，而编码智能体控制机器人的潜力尚不明确，缺乏检验其能否在物理世界约束下可靠控制多样机器人的评测平台。

Method: 构建统一、全面、智能体原生的评测框架，允许编码智能体自选控制方式（直接控制、代码控制、训练策略控制）；覆盖35套件1156项任务、47台机器人，含9个超越抓取放置的新套件（空间谜题、灵巧操作、多机器人交互、动态全身控制、sim-to-sim工程）；每项任务可运行于标准设置、实时变体和特权设置三种评测环境。

Result: 在456项核心任务上，八种智能体中最强的GPT-6 Astra（Codex中）在标准设置下成功率为34%；通过标注失败阶段并跨智能体、设置与阶段类别比较失败情况来定位失败原因。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在物理世界约束下控制多样机器人的能力仍远未成熟，该框架可作为编码智能体进入物理世界前的首场考试，并为改进指明方向。

Abstract: Early successes on agentic robotics point to a potential paradigm shift toward using coding agents to control robots. Existing robotics benchmarks are built around visuomotor policy learning, leaving it open whether coding agents can reliably control diverse robots under physical-world constraints. We therefore introduce the Embodied First Exam, a unified, comprehensive, and agent-native evaluation framework where coding agents can choose their own control approach: direct control, code to control, or train a policy to control. We hope it serves as a first exam for coding agents before they enter the physical world, with 1,156 tasks in 35 suites on 47 robots, including 9 new suites that go beyond pick-and-place to spatial puzzles, dexterous manipulation, multi-robot interaction, dynamic whole-body control, and sim-to-sim engineering. Each task can be run in up to three evaluation settings. The Standard Setting mimics the real world: the agent receives only sensor observations and, by default, cannot reset the environment. Its Realtime variant keeps the environment running while the agent reasons. The Privileged Setting gives the agent access to the full simulator state and unlimited resets, allowing it to search for an oracle solution for training purposes. Success rates in this setting serve as an approximate upper bound. On the 456 core tasks, the strongest of eight agents, GPT-6 Astra in Codex, achieves a success rate of 34% in the Standard Setting, leaving much room for improvement. To see where agents fail, we label the stage at which failed attempts go wrong and compare failures across agents, settings, and stage categories.

</details>


### [37] [GeoMind: A Benchmark for Spatial Understanding in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12175)
*Jinghe Wang,Xinrui Cao,Duo Wu,Chenghao Gu,Yong Zhong,Linjia Kang,Tianyi Xiong,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出GeoMind基准，用于系统评估具身智能体在桌面操作中的空间推理与空间记忆能力，发现现有智能体在空间变化后的身份追踪和将关系/几何约束转化为可执行动作方面存在持续弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基准存在两大不足：一是引入强视觉线索绕过空间推理，二是聚焦静态场景，无法评估智能体的空间记忆能力，因此需要新的基准来评估开放世界操作中可靠的空间理解。

Method: 构建GeoMind基准，包含54个围绕空间推理和空间记忆组织的评估任务变体，并提供可扩展的自动化pipeline来生成和标注任务实例，确保目标可验证且交互可执行；随后在多种具身智能体上进行广泛评估。

Result: 多样化具身智能体在空间理解上表现出一致性弱点：难以在空间变化后追踪目标身份，也难以将关系或几何约束转化为可执行动作。

Conclusion: GeoMind填补了具身基准在空间理解评估上的空白，揭示了当前智能体在空间推理与空间记忆方面的不足，并公开发布基准以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has advanced rapidly in mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, reliable manipulation in open-world environments requires robust spatial understanding: the ability to identify targets in complex layouts (spatial reasoning) and adapt to scenes that continuously evolve through interaction (spatial memory). Yet existing embodied benchmarks fall short in evaluating spatial understanding: they often introduce strong visual cues that bypass spatial reasoning, and focus on static scenes that offer little insight into an agent's capacity for spatial memory. To address this gap, we introduce GeoMind, a systematic benchmark for structured spatial understanding through tabletop manipulation. GeoMind comprises 54 evaluated task variants organized around spatial reasoning and spatial memory. It also incorporates a scalable pipeline that automates the generation and annotation of task instances with verified targets and executable interactions. Extensive evaluations across diverse embodied agents reveal their consistent weaknesses in spatial understanding. Our findings highlight that these agents struggle to track identities after spatial changes and convert relational or geometric constraints into executable actions. We release the GeoMind benchmark at this https URL .

</details>


### [38] [LHNav: Long-Horizon Humanoid Navigation via Multirate Coordination and Physics-Grounded Action Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12136)
*Xueyang Wang,Zehong Shen,Dingkun Wei,Huaijin Pi,Changpeng Wang,Xing Zhu,Hujun Bao,Yujun Shen,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LHNav异步系统，通过Brain、Cerebellum、Brainstem三角色协调实现长时程多目标人形导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务分解方法无法保证执行全程的进度推进，而基于路点的导航难以产生自然且忠实于路线的全身运动，因此需要一种能在不同时间尺度上同时保障任务进度与运动质量的方法。

Method: 将导航组织为三个互补角色：负责任务推理的Brain、负责局部运动生成的Cerebellum、负责全身执行的Brainstem；构建异步系统，利用进度与到达反馈协调导航目标、VLA运动token预测与全身控制；并设计基于物理的数据引擎，沿无碰撞路线提升运动参考的物理跟踪精度，将演示与渲染视觉观测配对以训练Cerebellum。

Result: 仿真中导航VLA在单目标成功率和运动平滑度上优于OmniNav和DualVLN，碰撞与跌倒更少；仅用单目标演示训练的模型在长时程任务上达到75.56%成功率；在Unitree G1上实现单目标导航及零样本长时程任务执行，包括物体位置变化的场景布局。

Conclusion: LHNav使任务规划与连续运动并行推进，将单目标导航扩展到连续多目的地，并保持自然、忠实于路线的全身运动，具备仿真与真实机器人上的有效性。
