Abstract: We study humanoid navigation across multiple destinations under long-horizon language instructions. This task is challenging because robots must maintain progress across successive goals while continuously producing natural, stable whole-body motion through confined spaces. However, task decomposition in existing methods does not by itself ensure progress throughout execution, while waypoint-based navigation does not guarantee natural, route-faithful whole-body motion. Our key idea is to organize navigation into three complementary roles operating at different time scales: a Brain for task reasoning, a Cerebellum for local motion generation, and a Brainstem for whole-body execution. We realize this idea in LHNav, an asynchronous system that uses progress and arrival feedback to coordinate navigation goals, VLA motion-token prediction, and whole-body control. To train the Cerebellum for natural, route-faithful motion, we develop a physics-grounded data engine that improves physical tracking of motion references along collision-aware routes and pairs the resulting demonstrations with rendered visual observations. Together, these designs allow task planning to proceed alongside continuous movement and extend single-goal navigation to successive destinations with natural, route-faithful whole-body motion. In simulation, our navigation VLA outperforms OmniNav and DualVLN in single-goal success and motion smoothness, with fewer collisions and falls. Using a VLA trained only on single-goal demonstrations, LHNav achieves a 75.56% success rate on long-horizon navigation tasks. Deployment on the Unitree G1 further demonstrates single-goal navigation and zero-shot long-horizon task execution in real-world environments, including layouts with repositioned objects. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [39] [Compact and Wide-Force-Range Magnetorheological Hand Exoskeleton for Kinesthetic Feedback in Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12130)
*Xianlong Mai,Rui Yu,Rui Chen,Daniele Leonardis,Wenbo Li,Domenico Chiaradia,Antonio Frisoli,Shuaishuai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种采用磁流变制动器的手部外骨骼系统，实现宽范围、紧凑可穿戴的力觉反馈，提升遥操作安全性与沉浸感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部力觉反馈对机器人手遥操作至关重要，渲染不同刚度物体需要宽范围力反馈，但现有方法难以兼顾宽范围与紧凑可穿戴设计。

Method: 设计基于磁流变制动器的手部外骨骼，提供0.01–0.918 Nm制动扭矩，总质量仅39.8 g；可渲染指尖力达21 N，反向驱动力1.01 N；通过实验评估刚度辨别与遥抓取任务。

Result: 用户仅凭力觉反馈即可辨别物体刚度；遥抓取任务中两种条件成功率均为100%，力觉反馈使物体接触力较仅视觉反馈降低28.2%；问卷显示力觉反馈提升可靠性、真实感和用户信心。

Conclusion: 磁流变制动器手部外骨骼能在紧凑可穿戴结构下实现宽范围力觉反馈，增强遥操作安全性与操作者沉浸感。

Abstract: Hand kinesthetic feedback is crucial for restoring and enhancing human perception of force and displacement in robotic hand teleoperation. Wide perception range of force feedback is vital in rendering objects of different stiffness, especially for high-stiffness rendering. However, existing approaches struggle to achieve wide range of force feedback while maintaining compact and wearable design. This paper presents a hand exoskeleton system employing magnetorheological (MR) brakes to provide a wide range kinesthetic feedback during teleoperation. The MR brake provides a braking torque ranging from 0.01 to 0.918 Nm, while maintaining a compact structure with a total mass of only 39.8 g. The proposed exoskeleton can render fingertip forces up to 21 N while maintaining back-driving force of 1.01 N. Experimental results show that the system allows users to discriminate object stiffness using kinesthetic feedback alone. In a tele-grasping task, when both conditions achieved a 100% success rate, kinesthetic feedback led to a 28.2% reduction in object contact force compared to visual feedback only, enhancing object safety and operator immersion. Questionnaire results further indicate that kinesthetic feedback improves reliability, realism, and user confidence during teleoperation.

</details>


### [40] [Motion Belief Action: From a Visual Scene Map to Physical Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12113)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Motion Belief Action（MBA）决策层，基于场景地图融合物体移动频率、所需力、负载与阻塞信息，在力限制下选择重看、轻推、打开、支撑或拒绝，风险由任务成本设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群中工作的机器人必须判断看不见的状态（物体是否仍在原处、拉门/抽屉是否值得冒险），而现有物体变化地图不含力信息、物理属性地图只回答固定风险问题、视觉-语言-动作模型无显式隐藏物理估计。

Method: MBA作为场景地图上的决策层，从人移动物体的频率与所需力、承载负载及是否阻塞中估计，在力限制下依据任务成本设定的风险选择动作（重看、轻推、打开、支撑、拒绝），并与FreMEn、Perpetua、持久性滤波器、部件类型平均及已发表风险规则对比；在9个厨房真实活动、17个位置真人用力传感夹爪拉门/抽屉、以及SmolVLA和Octo仿真中评估。

Result: 在9个厨房真实活动中，MBA预测物体是否在原处优于FreMEn和Perpetua，并以更低成本决定何时重看（约四分之一放置会在30秒内被取走）；在17个位置的真实拉拽中，地图力估计优于部件类型平均，按成本设风险也优于已发表固定风险规则；仿真中力限制取训练部件中位力时，MBA通过拒绝拉拽提升SmolVLA和Octo得分，多数被拒门拉会失败但抽屉更多本可打开，部件类型平均仅获约一半增益；已发表风险规则设为该成本隐含风险时等同MBA单次拉拽规则且主要设置下表现相同；开与持力关联、探测轻推、相机跟踪动作先验在主要设置无帮助，真实拉拽扫描中轻推略有代价。

Conclusion: MBA以任务成本设定风险、在力限制下统一决策重看与操作，在真实与仿真主要设置中优于基线；部分附加机制无益，轻推在真实扫描中略有代价。

Abstract: A robot that works among people must judge what it cannot see: whether an object is still where it was last seen, and whether a pull on a door or drawer is worth its risk. Maps that track object change hold nothing about force, maps of physical properties answer a risk question at a fixed risk level, and vision-language-action models keep no explicit estimate of hidden physics. We propose Motion Belief Action (MBA), a decision layer on the scene map. From how often people move an object and how much force it needs, how much load it holds and whether it is blocked, MBA chooses under a force limit to look again, nudge, open, brace or decline, with its risk set by the task's costs. On real activity in 9 kitchens, it predicts whether an object is still in place better than FreMEn and Perpetua, and decides when to look again at lower cost than these and the persistence filter, because about a quarter of placements end in a pick-up within 30 s. On real pulls of doors and drawers in 17 locations, made by a person with a force-sensing gripper, the map's force estimates decide better than a part-type average. Setting the risk from the costs also beats a published risk rule at its fixed level. In simulation, with the force limit at the training parts' median force, MBA raises SmolVLA's and Octo's scores by declining pulls; most declined door pulls would fail, but on drawers more would have opened, and a part-type average gets about half of this gain. In both, a published risk rule set to the risk these costs imply is MBA's rule for a single pull and decides as well in the main settings. A link between opening and holding force, a probing nudge and an action prior for camera tracking do not help in the main settings, and over the real-pull sweep the nudge costs a little.

</details>


### [41] [GAP: Generalized Adaptation Prior for Dexterous Piano Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12111)
*Jinyi Chen,Hongyi Li,Lei Jiang,Xianwei Liu,Yanhong Liang,Yulin Chen,Yucheng Tao,Yongbin Jin,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GAP框架，在仿真预训练中学习“如何适应”而非“如何行动”，将适应先验迁移到硬件，显著减少真实交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的灵巧手策略因非理想因素在硬件上退化，且高维动作空间使真实世界适应代价高昂；钢琴演奏需精确多指协调，是代表性测试平台。

Method: GAP利用重复执行间的反馈调整下一次动作：梯度引导网络从成对执行中学习动作调整对演奏性能的影响，并用其梯度指导调整；在仿真预训练后迁移该适应先验到真实手。

Result: 仿真中相同交互预算下F1提升约为现有快速适应基线的2.4倍，并泛化到预训练未见的曲目；真实灵巧手每首曲约7分钟适应后，三首不同难度曲目F1提升3.6%-23%。

Conclusion: 结果表明显著减少灵巧手部署所需真实世界交互的可行路径。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable versatile manipulation, but policies trained in simulation often degrade on hardware owing to non-idealities, and the high-dimensional action space makes real-world adaptation costly. Piano playing, which requires precise and coordinated multifinger motion, offers a representative testbed. We propose GAP (Generalized Adaptation Prior for Dexterous Piano Playing), a sample-efficient adaptation framework that learns how to adapt, rather than how to act, during simulation pretraining and transfers this adaptation prior to hardware. Between repeated executions, GAP uses feedback to adjust the actions of the next one: a gradient guidance network learns from paired executions how adjusting actions affects playing performance, and its gradients guide these adjustments. In simulation, GAP yields approximately 2.4 times the F1 scores improvement of the existing fast-adaptation baseline for dexterous piano playing under the same interaction budget. The learned prior also generalizes to musical pieces held out from pretraining. On a physical dexterous hand, the same simulation-pretrained prior improves F1 scores by 3.6%-23% across three pieces of varying difficulty after approximately 7 min of adaptation each. These results suggest a practical route to reduce the real-world interaction needed to deploy dexterous hands.

</details>


### [42] [SPARK: Simple Post-training for Adapting pRetrained Knowledge to Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12102)
*Taekyung Kim,Jeongeun Park,Byeongho Heo,Sungjoon Choi,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARK是一种后训练方法，通过时间抽象和知识保留两个原则将预训练基础模型适配到机器人控制，在多个基准和真实操作任务中提升成功率与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型的预训练目标并非为机器人表示学习设计，其嵌入偏向全局语义而非机器人控制所需的时间敏感表示；同时从零训练机器人模型受限于数据规模、采集成本和计算负担，因此需要一种高效适配预训练知识的方法。

Method: 提出SPARK后训练方法，基于两个原则：时间抽象（促使编码器学习紧凑特征，保留理解时间变化和动作相关场景结构的关键信息）和知识保留（将patch级表示与原始基础模型对齐以保留有用的预训练语义），从而获得既语义有意义又保留先验知识的紧凑视觉状态表示。

Result: 在多个机器人基准实验中，SPARK相比原始基础模型一致提升成功率和泛化能力，并证明这些增益可迁移到真实世界机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: SPARK能有效将通用视觉基础模型适配为机器人控制所需的紧凑、时间敏感表示，在保持预训练语义的同时显著提升机器人操作性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Large-scale representation learning has produced public foundation models that provide strong general-purpose visual features. However, their pretraining objectives are not designed for robot representation learning, so the resulting embeddings tend to emphasize global semantics rather than the temporally sensitive representations required for robot control. Training robot models from scratch is also undesirable due to the limited scale and high collection cost of robotics data, as well as the substantial computational burden of pre-training. In this paper, we propose SPARK (Simple Post-training for Adapting pRetrained Knowledge), a post-training method that adapts foundation models to robot control. SPARK is built on two principles: temporal abstraction, which encourages the encoder to derive compact features that preserve information essential for understanding temporal changes and action-relevant scene structure, and knowledge preservation, which aligns patch-level representations with those of the original foundation model to retain useful pretrained semantics. These objectives yield compact visual state representations that remain semantically meaningful while preserving useful prior knowledge. Experiments across multiple robotics benchmarks show that SPARK consistently improves success rates and generalization over vanilla foundation models, and further demonstrate that these gains transfer to real-world robot manipulation.

</details>


### [43] [SAM-VTP: Video Memory and Measured Geometry for Continuous Traversability Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12099)
*Lucas Hoefs,Hui Xie,Senjian An,Fangyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAM-VTP 通过微调 SAM2 的视频记忆通路实现时序一致的密集可通行性分割，融合深度几何特征并引入离线连续监督，在合成数据集上提升精度并减少闪烁，闭环实验显著降低规划失败率且实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的可通行性预测多为逐帧处理，产生时序不一致的预测；且直接从外观推断地形几何不可靠。

Method: 微调 SAM2 的视频记忆通路，使每帧以持久记忆为条件进行无提示密集分割；从深度计算坡度、粗糙度和高度并融合进图像特征；提出离线连续监督流程，从深度几何和语义先验生成 [0,1] 的逐像素可通行性目标，无需机器人部署和逐像素标注。

Result: 在 TartanGround 上，模糊 Jaccard 从 0.908 提升至 0.924，二值 IoU 从 0.854 提升至 0.886（种子间波动小于 0.001），帧间闪烁减少 31%；两个现场闭环试验中，基线规划器在铺装路面和崎岖地面分别以 3.3 倍和 6.9 倍更频繁地无可行路径，而 SAM-VTP 在 Orin Nano 上以 21.4 FPS 运行。

Conclusion: SAM-VTP 实现了时序一致、几何感知的密集可通行性预测，精度更高、闪烁更少，并在真实闭环导航中显著提升规划可靠性且满足实时性。

Abstract: Traversability prediction is a core component of autonomous navigation, yet most vision-based methods operate frame-wise and produce temporally inconsistent predictions. We present SAM-VTP (Video Traversability Prediction), which fine-tunes SAM2's video memory pathway for dense, prompt-free traversability segmentation, conditioning each frame on a persistent memory of prior observations. Rather than inferring terrain geometry from appearance, we measure it: slope, roughness and height are computed from depth and fused into the image features. We also introduce an offline continuous supervision pipeline that generates dense per-pixel traversability targets in [0,1] from depth-derived geometry and semantic priors, requiring no robot deployment and no per-pixel annotation, capturing conditional feasibility that binary labels cannot represent. On TartanGround, a large-scale synthetic ground-robot dataset, SAM-VTP improves fuzzy Jaccard from 0.908 to 0.924 and binary IoU from 0.854 to 0.886 over SAM-TP, a frame-wise SAM2 baseline, against a seed-to-seed spread below 0.001, and cuts frame-to-frame flicker by 31%. In closed-loop trials at two field sites, driving the same planner and controller, the baseline's dropouts leave the planner without a feasible path 3.3x more often on a paved course and 6.9x on rough ground, while SAM-VTP runs at 21.4 FPS on the Orin Nano the robot carries.

</details>


### [44] [Learning Contextual Motion Constraints for Object Rearrangement Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12072)
*Lexing Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Gang Zeng,Hangxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoCo-Net是一个双流神经网络，从部分视角点云联合预测 articulated 部件的连续运动范围和障碍物热力图，以同时捕获内部与外部运动约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要考虑物体自身的关节属性，忽略了杂乱环境中外部障碍物对铰接部件运动的限制。

Method: 提出双流神经网络MoCo-Net，分别预测铰接部件的连续运动范围与障碍物热力图，并利用两者构建场景配置空间供规划器探索重排动作。

Result: 实验表明双流设计相互增强，获得更准确的可行运动范围预测与障碍物识别，提升对新物体和新场景的泛化能力，并形成紧凑配置空间使定制规划器高效完成场景重排。

Conclusion: MoCo-Net通过联合建模内部关节约束与外部障碍约束，能更准确地预测可行运动范围并支持高效场景重排。

Abstract: The motion of an articulated part in cluttered surroundings is constrained not only by an object's intrinsic joint limits but also by extrinsic obstacles. However, existing approaches primarily consider object articulation properties alone. Here we present Motion Constraint Prediction Network (MoCo-Net), a two-stream neural network that jointly predicts the continuous motion ranges of articulated parts and obstacle heatmaps highlighting potential collision regions from partial-view point cloud observation. By capturing both internal and external motion constraints, MoCo-Net produces more accurate feasible motion range prediction and obstacles recognition, improves generalization for novel objects in new scenes, and enables robots to construct a scene configuration space for efficient exploration of rearrangement actions. Our experiments show that MoCo-Net's two-stream design enhances each other to deliver more accurate predictions, which subsequently form a compact configuration space for a customized planner to efficiently rearrange the scene.

</details>


### [45] [DPGS-SLAM: Diffusion-Prior and Provenance-Aware Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Dynamic Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12038)
*Yifan Luo,Takafumi Matsumaru,Vibekananda Dutta,Xin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DPGS-SLAM，一种单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM框架，通过异步扩散补全和来源感知高斯建图，在动态环境中实现稠密建图并保持跟踪稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体会污染视觉SLAM的相机跟踪与场景重建，现有3DGS-SLAM在动态场景下因违背静态假设而不稳定；直接剔除动态观测虽可减小影响，但会导致被遮挡的静态背景缺失或约束不足。

Method: 用光流运动发现和SAM2时序一致掩码传播识别动态区域；对选定关键帧，用微调扩散修复模型生成被遮挡区域的合理背景外观，并用Metric3D v2从补全图像预测度量深度以初始化临时高斯原语；通过来源标签区分生成原语与观测支持原语，生成内容不参与相机位姿估计和观测地图优化；当被遮挡背景可见时移除符合条件的生成原语，并从真实RGB观测和预测深度独立初始化观测支持原语。

Result: 相机跟踪具有竞争力，TUM RGB-D上平均ATE RMSE为1.2 cm，BONN RGB-D为2.7 cm；在StaDy4D上仅用单目RGB输入，平均PSNR较WildGS-SLAM提升4.60 dB。

Conclusion: DPGS-SLAM通过扩散先验补全与来源感知高斯映射，在动态环境中兼顾跟踪精度与稠密建图质量，并优于对比方法。

Abstract: This paper presents DPGS-SLAM (Diffusion-Prior and Provenance-Aware Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM) for dense mapping in environments containing dynamic objects. Dynamic observations can contaminate camera tracking and scene reconstruction in visual SLAM (simultaneous localization and mapping). Although 3DGS (3D Gaussian splatting) enables efficient dense mapping, existing 3DGS-SLAM systems may exhibit instability when dynamic objects violate their underlying static-scene assumption. Excluding dynamic observations can reduce their influence on tracking and mapping, but may leave occluded static backgrounds missing or weakly constrained. Our proposed DPGS-SLAM is a monocular RGB SLAM framework that supports provisional representations of insufficiently observed static backgrounds through asynchronous diffusion-based background completion and provenance-aware Gaussian mapping. Dynamic regions are identified through optical-flow-based motion discovery and temporally consistent mask propagation by SAM2 (segment anything model 2). For selected keyframes, a fine-tuned diffusion inpainting model generates plausible background appearance in occluded regions, while Metric3D v2 predicts metric depth from the completed images to initialize provisional Gaussian primitives. Provenance labels distinguish generated primitives from observation-supported primitives, excluding generated content from camera-pose estimation and observed-map optimization. When previously occluded backgrounds become visible, eligible generated primitives are removed, and observation-supported primitives are independently initialized from real RGB observations and predicted depth. Experimental results demonstrated competitive camera tracking, with average ATE RMSE values of 1.2 cm on TUM RGB-D and 2.7 cm on BONN RGB-D. On StaDy4D, DPGS-SLAM improved average PSNR by 4.60 dB over WildGS-SLAM using only monocular RGB input.

</details>


### [46] [Grasp-Taxonomy Conditioning as a Mode Switch for Dexterous Grasping of Climbing Holds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12017)
*Rumi Loghmani,Abraham George,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BetaGrasp通过将抓取标签条件化到点云扩散策略中，使机器人能按物体几何选择正确抓取类型；硬件实验表明平均滑移力从9.6 N提高到14.3 N，完全滑脱率从44%降至10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧抓取需要根据物体几何采用不同手部构型，但仅依赖点云扫描难以区分抓取类型，导致策略倾向于单一抓取方式。

Method: 提出BetaGrasp，一种以四类抓取标签（crimp、sloper、pinch、jug）为条件的DP3式点云扩散策略，运行于Franka机械臂与16自由度LEAP手；并构建攀岩把手基准测试台，通过棘轮机构读取牛顿级滑移力。

Result: 点云编码器未能区分四种把手，无标签基线从复位姿态的仿真 rollout 全部收敛到jug抓取（正确抓取类型25%，BetaGrasp为94%）；80次配对硬件试验中，BetaGrasp将平均滑移力从9.6 N提升至14.3 N，完全滑脱比例从44%降至10%。

Conclusion: 抓取标签作为策略执行何种抓取的开关，能显著提升抓取类型选择正确率与抗滑移性能，表明条件化标签对灵巧抓取至关重要。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in robotics relies on distinct hand configurations depending on object geometry. We introduce BetaGrasp, a 3D diffusion policy that conditions a DP3-style point-cloud policy on a four-class grasp label (crimp, sloper, pinch, and jug), running on a Franka arm with a 16-DoF LEAP hand. We also introduce a climbing-hold benchmark: a spring testbed whose ratchet gives a slip-force reading in newtons. In our analysis the policy's point-cloud encoder did not separate the four holds, so the scan alone gave it little to choose a grasp from. Without the label (a DP3-style baseline), simulated rollouts from the robot's reset pose all end in a jug grasp, whatever the hold (25% correct grasp type, versus 94% for BetaGrasp). The label works as a switch for which grasp the policy performs. On 80 paired hardware trials, BetaGrasp raises average slip force from 9.6 N to 14.3 N, and grasps slipping fully off the hold drop from 44% of trials to 10%.

</details>


### [47] [OASIS: Optimization-Aware Subgoal Inference with Safety Constraints for Autonomous Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11996)
*Arian Hajizadeha,Javad Pourrafia,Negar Shenavara,Sana Bashiria,Seyed Majid Esmailzadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OASIS分层导航框架，将控制器优化结果反馈给强化学习子目标生成策略，提升动态环境下的导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型子目标策略可引导模型预测控制超越局部预测时域，但几何上合适的子目标不一定能成功求解控制问题，需将优化可行性信息纳入子目标生成。

Method: 高层策略用Soft Actor-Critic生成局部子目标，低层非线性模型预测控制器在运动限制、几何避碰约束和嵌入的高阶控制屏障函数约束下跟踪子目标；每个子目标区间的控制器优化结果被汇总并在下次决策时提供给策略，无需额外优化调用。

Result: 在含静态与移动障碍的五个导航场景（含密集交互和感知不确定性）中评估，OASIS综合导航成功率最高；三个训练种子的匹配比较显示其相对无优化状态输入的相同分层结构具有一致成功率优势；所选策略比较显示主要优化失败更少且平均控制器时间相近。

Conclusion: 将优化结果反馈至子目标生成策略可有效提升分层导航框架的成功率与优化可靠性，且不增加显著计算开销。

Abstract: Navigation in dynamic environments requires mobile robots to make progress toward their goals while respecting motion limits and avoiding surrounding obstacles. Learned subgoal policies can guide model predictive control beyond its local prediction horizon, but a geometrically suitable subgoal does not necessarily lead to a successfully solved control problem. This paper proposes OASIS, a hierarchical navigation framework that incorporates recent optimization outcomes into reinforcement learning-based subgoal generation. A high-level policy trained using Soft Actor--Critic generates local subgoals, while a nonlinear model predictive controller tracks them subject to motion limits, geometric collision-avoidance constraints, and embedded high-order control barrier function constraints. The outcomes of controller optimizations over each subgoal interval are summarized and provided to the policy at its next decision, without requiring additional optimization calls. The framework is evaluated in five navigation scenarios with static and moving obstacles, including dense interactions and sensing uncertainty. Comparisons with hierarchical and direct-goal control variants show that OASIS achieves the highest aggregate navigation success. Matched comparisons across three training seeds further show a consistent success advantage over the otherwise identical hierarchy without optimization-status input. The selected-policy comparison also shows fewer primary optimization failures with similar mean controller time.

</details>


### [48] [Thermo-VLA: A Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model with Gated Residual Modulation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11981)
*Jingxuan Chen,Junshi Luo,Chencheng Dong,Xiaojian Nie,Tongge Huang,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Thermo-VLA 提出 ThermoRes 编码器，将热图分解为冷热属性表示，并通过语言条件残差调制注入 RGB token，在双臂 Unitree G1 上实现温度驱动的目标选择与连续操作，四个热依赖任务平均成功率 0.86，比直接输入 RGB-热图的 pi_0.5 基线高 18 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽引入热感，但把热图当作普通视觉输入，缺乏温度感知的关系推理能力，限制了依赖温度差异进行目标选择与操作的任务表现。

Method: 提出 Thermo-VLA 与 ThermoRes 编码器：将热图分解为冷、热属性表示，经语言条件残差调制注入 RGB token；并在双臂 Unitree G1 平台构建六个真实机器人任务，覆盖基础定位到关系推理，并将同一残差调制机制迁移到颜色属性以验证泛化性。

Result: 四个热依赖任务平均成功率 0.86，较直接融合 RGB-热图的 pi_0.5 基线提升 18 个百分点；在未见物体与场景变化下平均成功率 0.78，为对比方法中最佳；颜色属性迁移在仿真与真机实验中均带来一致提升。

Conclusion: 以属性分解加语言条件残差调制的方式注入热信息，能有效支持温度感知的关系推理与操作，且该机制可推广到颜色等其他属性，具备超越热感的通用潜力。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have begun incorporating thermal sensing, but existing approaches typically treat thermal images as ordinary visual inputs, providing limited support for temperature-aware relational reasoning in manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose Thermo-VLA with a ThermoRes Encoder, which decomposes thermal images into cold and hot attribute representations and injects them into the RGB token through language-conditioned residual modulation, enabling temperature-driven target selection and continuous manipulation. We further construct six real-world robotic tasks on a dual-arm Unitree G1 platform, spanning basic grounding to relational reasoning. Experiments show that Thermo-VLA achieves an average success rate of 0.86 across four thermal-dependent tasks, outperforming a pi_0.5 baseline that directly incorporates RGB-thermal observations by 18 percentage points. Under unseen-object and scene variations, Thermo-VLA maintains an average success rate of 0.78, achieving the best generalisation performance among the compared methods. Beyond thermal sensing, we transfer the same residual modulation mechanism to colour attributes and observe consistent improvements in both simulation and real-robot experiments, indicating its potential to generalise beyond thermal attributes. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [49] [CSG-Nav: Confidence-Aware Semantic-Geometric Fusion for Robust Exploration and Accurate Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11978)
*Shoubin Chen,Rui Xu,Zehao Wu,Kai Zhang,Tao Huang,Zhou Liu,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CSG-Nav是一个基于置信度感知多模态信息融合的零样本目标导航框架，通过语义源融合、空间置信度融合与语义-几何决策融合解决长程感知不可靠、幻觉方向偏差及停止决策几何错误问题，在HM3Dv1、HM3Dv2和MP3D上取得58.9%、71.6%和44.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标导航要求智能体在无任务特定训练下探索陌生环境并定位目标，现有基础模型方法虽提升泛化性，却受限于长程感知不可靠、幻觉导致的方向偏差以及语义合理但几何错误的停止判断，因此需要一种能融合多模态置信度信息以提升感知可靠性与目标终止物理合理性的方法。

Method: 提出CSG-Nav框架，包含三种互补融合机制：语义源融合整合开放词汇检测结果、检测置信度、场景观测与关系推理，生成更可靠的方向级语义分数并抑制幻觉方向偏差；空间置信度融合利用高斯衰减函数对多视角语义证据加权，抑制不可靠的远距离观测并累积空间一致的探索偏好；语义-几何决策融合结合语义目标检测与基于深度的三维定位，支持物理上合理的停止决策。

Result: 在HM3Dv1、HM3Dv2和MP3D数据集上分别取得58.9%、71.6%和44.3%的成功率，展现有竞争力的零样本性能；融合级消融实验及室内外真实环境部署进一步验证了CSG-Nav在不确定感知下鲁棒导航的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: CSG-Nav通过置信度感知的多模态信息融合有效缓解了零样本目标导航中的感知不确定性与决策几何错误问题，在仿真基准和真实场景中均表现出良好的鲁棒性与实用价值。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation requires autonomous agents to explore unseen environments and locate target objects without task-specific training, posing challenges in perceptual reliability under observation uncertainty and physically grounded goal termination. Although foundation-model-based approaches improve generalization, they remain vulnerable to unreliable long-range perception, hallucination-induced directional bias, and semantically plausible but geometrically incorrect stopping decisions. To address these limitations, we propose CSG-Nav, a zero-shot navigation framework centered on confidence-aware multimodal information fusion. CSG-Nav integrates three complementary fusion mechanisms. First, semantic-source fusion integrates open-vocabulary detection results, detection confidence, scene observations, and relational reasoning to produce more reliable direction-level semantic scores and suppress hallucination-induced directional bias. Second, spatial-confidence fusion weights multi-view semantic evidence using a Gaussian decay function, suppressing unreliable distant observations while accumulating spatially consistent exploration preferences. Third, semantic-geometric decision fusion combines semantic target detection with depth-based 3D localization to support physically grounded stopping decisions. Experiments on HM3Dv1, HM3Dv2, and MP3D demonstrate competitive zero-shot performance, achieving success rates of 58.9%, 71.6%, and 44.3%. Fusion-level ablations and real-world deployments in indoor and outdoor environments further verify the effectiveness and practicality of CSG-Nav for robust navigation under uncertain perception. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [50] [The Relative Value of Demonstration Data: Execution Time Limits and Operator Mixtures in Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11947)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Xingyu Ren,Youran Sun,Jingxin Yang,Kejia Zhang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用“数据倍数”衡量不同经验水平遥操作者所需演示数量的比例，并在robomimic的Square和Can任务上量化了Better与Worse组之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据集常混合不同经验遥操作者的数据，采集团队需要知道一组演示与另一组演示的等价数量关系，以便合理配比数据。

Method: 定义数据倍数（两组达到相同成功率所需演示数量之比）；在robomimic的Square和Can任务上训练行为克隆策略，改变数据集大小和Worse演示占比，并测试20至200秒不同时间限制。

Result: Square任务25秒限制下，全Worse训练集所需演示数为全Better的1.55倍，主要源于完成时间较晚，100秒限制下降至1.11；Can任务大部分差异在100秒仍存在。半Better半Worse集在Square上实际所需演示数比按1/1.55折算的预期多13%，且该超额在所有时间限制下持续存在，均匀采样时仍存在。

Conclusion: Worse遥操作者演示的边际价值低于Better演示，且混合数据存在额外效率损失；在Square任务25秒限制下，仅当Worse遥操作者时薪低于Better的三分之一时，切换才在等成功率下更经济。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets combine demonstrations from teleoperators of uneven experience, so collection teams need to know how many demonstrations from one group match those from another. We measure this with a data multiple: the ratio of the numbers of demonstrations two groups need for their policies to reach the same success rate. On robomimic's simulated Square and Can tasks, we train behavior-cloning policies on demonstrations from the two teleoperators robomimic labels Better and the two it labels Worse, varying dataset size and the share of Worse demonstrations. On Square, within robomimic's 25-second time limit, an all-Worse training set needs 1.55 times as many demonstrations as an all-Better set. Most of this difference is late completion: policies trained on Worse demonstrations finish later, as the Worse teleoperators do, and with a 100-second limit the multiple falls to 1.11. On Can, most of the difference remains at 100 seconds. If each Worse demonstration were worth 1/1.55 of a Better one, as in an all-Worse set, a half-Better, half-Worse set on Square would need 1.22 times as many demonstrations as an all-Better set. In fact, it needs 13% more than that, and this excess persists at every time limit from 20 to 200 seconds. The excess remains when training draws demonstrations uniformly instead of in proportion to their length. On Square, within the 25-second limit, switching to these Worse teleoperators is cheaper at equal success only if they earn less than a third of the Better teleoperators' hourly pay, because Worse demonstrations also take longer to record.

</details>


### [51] [Flip Anything with Agentic Robotics Self-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11941)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 编码智能体通过物理仿真中的迭代学习，自主开发并优化基于图结构的代码，实现双臂机器人抓取器翻转多种物体，任务成功率从24.7%提升至95.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻转是工业检测、装配和工具使用中的常见目标，但为带抓取器的双臂机器人编写翻转多样物体的代码极具挑战性。

Method: 让编码智能体在物理仿真中针对多样物体几何形状进行迭代学习，逐步获得从基础交接、倾倒与推动、鲁棒抓取与感知、到薄物体操作与恢复等技能，并生成融合高层策略选择与反应式控制的图结构代码，最终部署到物理机器人上。

Result: 任务成功率从24.7%提升到95.0%，生成的图结构代码在物理机器人上实现了多阶段翻转。

Conclusion: 编码智能体可通过仿真迭代自主学习并精炼物体翻转策略，所得图结构代码能有效迁移至真实双臂机器人完成多阶段翻转任务。

Abstract: Flipping is a fundamental robotic manipulation skill that requires reorienting a target object in a stable pose on a planar support surface by rotating it 180$^\circ$ about an axis parallel to the support surface -- think of flipping a coin from heads to tails. Although flipping is a common objective in industrial tasks such as parts inspection, mechanical assembly, and tool use, coding a bimanual robot with grippers to flip diverse objects is a significant challenge. In this paper, we show that a coding agent can autonomously develop and refine object-flipping strategies through iterative learning in physics simulations across diverse object geometries. Progressive skill acquisition, from basic handover to toppling and pushing, robust grasping and sensing, and thin-object manipulation with recovery, improves task success from 24.7% to 95.0%. The resulting graph-based code integrates high-level strategy selection with reactive control, enabling multi-stage flipping on physical robots.

</details>


### [52] [Reliability-Adaptive Multimodal Relative Pose Tracking for Underwater Leader-Follower Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11940)
*Mingyang Yang,Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Jun Wang,David Chen Wang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可靠性自适应多模态感知框架，用于水下双机器人六自由度相对位姿跟踪，融合视觉、声学测距和水动力测量，在嵌入式硬件上实现高效稳定的编队控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下leader-follower编队控制需要在感知条件退化和机载计算资源有限的条件下持续进行相对位姿估计，单一模态可靠性随时间变化，需自适应融合多源互补信息。

Method: 集成视觉、声学测距和水动力三类互补测量，考虑其物理特性与时变可靠性，自适应加权各模态贡献，并以线性计算复杂度建模时序依赖，在嵌入式硬件上实现位姿估计。

Result: 基于自建AUV平台开展648次水池试验（涵盖两种leader载体、三种光照条件、九种轨迹模式），平均平移误差3.8 cm、平均姿态误差2.4°；在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上推理延迟17.62 ms，较Transformer融合基线加速2.69倍；闭环实验验证移动leader下编队稳定维持。

Conclusion: 该框架能在机载计算约束下实现高精度、低延迟的水下相对位姿估计与稳定编队保持，并将公开包含全部648次试验测量的多模态数据集。

Abstract: Underwater leader-follower formation control requires continuous relative-pose estimation despite degraded sensing conditions and limited onboard computation. This paper presents a reliability-adaptive multimodal perception framework for six-degree-of-freedom relative-pose tracking between two moving underwater robots. The framework integrates complementary visual, acoustic-ranging, and hydrodynamic measurements while accounting for their distinct physical characteristics and time-varying reliability. It adaptively weights modality contributions and models temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity, enabling efficient pose estimation on embedded hardware. Extensive experiments were conducted using a custom-built AUV platform, with 648 pool trials spanning two leader vehicle types, three illumination conditions, and nine trajectory patterns. Evaluation yields an average translational error of 3.8 cm and an average orientation error of 2.4°. On an onboard NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, the fusion network achieves an inference latency of 17.62 ms, providing a 2.69-fold speedup over the evaluated Transformer-based fusion baseline. Closed-loop experiments further demonstrate stable formation maintenance with a moving leader under onboard computational constraints. A multimodal dataset containing measurements from all 648 trials will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [53] [Distilling Instructions into Weights: Closed-Form Edits Fix a Robot Policy's Misreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11894)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过闭式指令蒸馏直接编辑视觉-语言-动作策略的MLP权重，修复其将"in the bowl"错误绑定到物体上的指令误解，使成功率从27/128提升到106/128，且效果可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略常在原本可完成任务的某一步失败；作者探究能否不依赖梯度、训练数据或修改指令，仅通过直接编辑策略权重来修复这类失败，并以零样本π_0.5策略在RoboLab中提前松开方块为例。

Method: 先通过21次改写重放确认策略把"in the bowl"错误绑定到错误物体；再用闭式指令蒸馏把显式改写对语言模型各层的影响复制进MLP权重；在128个留出种子上评估，并与同尺寸随机方向更新、存储0.16%参数量的变体、以及放置在动作专家或动作头的版本对照；另测试针对感知、抓取、接触物理和夹爪阈值的编辑，以及七个盲测Claude Opus编辑器的效果。

Result: 成功率从27提升到106（81个修复、2个破坏，p=7×10^-22），随机同尺寸更新无变化；编辑还提升另外两个任务且对其余13个无可检测损害；紧凑变体保留大部分效果，置于动作专家或动作头效果更差或无效；发布工具在新留出种子上端到端复现（8/64到36/64）；针对感知等的编辑无效，七个盲测编辑器中仅获得误读指令信息者通过留出评分；未改权重的重跑约有三分之一配对结果翻转。

Conclusion: 权重编辑在已有已知良好输入可供蒸馏时最有效；由于结果波动大，评估应配对种子、重跑未编辑策略并在留出种子上确认增益。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies often fail at one step of a task they can otherwise perform. We ask whether such a failure can be repaired by editing the policy's weights directly, without gradients, training data, or a change to the instruction. Told to put the cube then the banana in the bowl, a zero-shot $\pi_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy in RoboLab releases the cube before it reaches the bowl. Replaying 21 of these decisions under rewordings shows that it binds "in the bowl" to the wrong object. Closed-form instruction distillation copies the per-layer effect of an explicit rewording into the language model's MLP weights. On 128 held-out seeds, success rises from 27 to 106 episodes, with 81 fixed and 2 broken ($p=7\times10^{-22}$), while an update of the same size in random directions leaves success unchanged. The edit also lifts two other tasks without detectable harm on the remaining 13, and a variant that stores 0.16% of the parameter count keeps most of its effect. Placed in the action expert or action head instead, the same distillation helps less or not at all. Our released tool reproduced the fix end to end on new held-out seeds, from 8/64 to 36/64. Edits aimed at perception, a missing grasp, contact physics, or the gripper threshold did not help, and of seven blind Claude Opus editors, only the one given the misread instruction produced an edit that passed held-out scoring. These results suggest that weight edits work best when a known-good input already exists to distil. Because reruns with unchanged weights flip about a third of paired outcomes, evaluations should pair seeds, rerun the unedited policy, and confirm gains on held-out seeds.

</details>


### [54] [Design of a hybrid tendon-linkage finger with coupled and adaptive motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11885)
*Hyojae Kang,Giup Seo,Jae-Young Lee,Yeong Jae Park,Jongwoo Park,Hyunmin Do,So-Hee Park,Dongil Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种三驱动器驱动四自由度混合肌腱-连杆机械手指，通过机械耦合实现DIP关节随动与接触自适应，指尖峰值力约4.56 N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手指需协调关节运动与接触适应性，但独立驱动多关节会增加传动机构复杂度，故希望在减少驱动器数量前提下保持多自由度与自适应能力。

Method: 设计混合肌腱-连杆手指：MCP外展/内收由锥齿轮驱动，MCP与PIP屈伸由独立双向肌腱传动驱动，DIP通过含扭簧与机械限位的四连杆与PIP耦合；肌腱走线引入MCP-PIP机械耦合；进行运动学分析并用ArUco标记实验评估关节运动特性。

Result: 耦合运动与理论预测高度吻合，自适应运动受连杆部件干涉部分受限；手指对圆柱、长方体及不规则物体呈现不同接触姿态，指尖平均峰值力为4.56 N。

Conclusion: 该机构无需为DIP关节增设驱动器即可同时实现耦合运动与接触相关自适应，为降低灵巧手指驱动复杂度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic fingers require coordinated joint motion and adaptability to object contact, while independently actuating multiple joints increases the complexity of the transmission mechanism. This paper presents a hybrid tendon--linkage robotic finger with four mechanical degrees of freedom driven by three actuators. The MCP abduction/adduction joint is driven through a bevel gear, while MCP and PIP flexion/extension are driven by separate bidirectional tendon transmissions. The DIP joint is not independently actuated but is coupled to the PIP joint through a four-bar linkage with a torsional spring and mechanical stopper. The tendon routing also introduces mechanical coupling between the MCP and PIP joints. Kinematic analysis was conducted to describe the coupled and adaptive motions, and experiments were performed to evaluate the joint motion characteristics. ArUco-based measurements showed that the coupled motion closely matched the theoretical prediction, whereas the adaptive motion was partially limited by interference between linkage components. The fabricated finger demonstrated different contact postures with cylindrical, cuboid, and irregularly shaped objects, and the mean peak fingertip force was 4.56 N. The proposed mechanism provides both coupled motion and contact-dependent adaptation without requiring an additional actuator for the DIP joint.

</details>


### [55] [Action Representation for Spatial Generalization in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11869)
*Yuhang Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过受控机器人剥离基准，评估世界、物体和表面坐标系下的匹配动作表示，发现空间泛化取决于保留决定物理后果的动作-状态关系，而非简单最小化坐标变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型的空间泛化取决于动作表示能否捕捉在空间配置变化下仍有效的物理关系，但不同坐标可描述同一交互、相似运动在不同局部物理条件下产生不同结果，这些区分对学习预测的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 在受控机器人剥离基准中，比较世界、物体和表面坐标系下匹配的动作表示，开展全局位姿与接触重定位实验，并移除剥离厚度信息以制造不可约歧义，同时联合中心化表面与工具。

Result: 等价交互获得不同空间描述时存在数据受限代价；移除剥离厚度信息导致相同预测输入对应不同材料去除结果的不可约歧义；相对表面的坐标在局部观测准确时改善后果预测与反事实动作选择，但预测器对坐标系不确定性仍脆弱；联合中心化消除共同原点的平移敏感性，而错误接触几何与实际执行偏差引入不同失效机制。

Conclusion: 有效的空间泛化依赖于保留动作与状态之间决定物理后果的关系，而非单纯最小化坐标变化；这为设计可跨空间配置复用物理知识并保留预测与规划必要区分的机器人世界模型提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Spatial generalization in robot world models depends on whether action representations capture the physical relationships that remain valid across changing spatial configurations. Yet different coordinates can describe the same interaction, whereas similar motions can produce different outcomes when local physical conditions change. How these distinctions affect learned prediction remains insufficiently understood. This study evaluates matched action representations defined in world, object, and surface coordinate frames using a controlled robotic peeling benchmark. Global pose and contact relocation experiments reveal a cost associated with limited training data when equivalent interactions receive different spatial descriptions. Removing information about peel thickness creates an irreducible ambiguity because identical predictive inputs can correspond to different material removal outcomes. Coordinates defined relative to the surface improve consequence prediction and counterfactual action selection under accurate local observations, but learned predictors remain vulnerable to uncertainty in the coordinate frame. Joint centering of the surface and tool removes sensitivity to translation of their common origin, whereas incorrect contact geometry and actual execution deviations introduce different failure mechanisms. These results show that effective spatial generalization depends on preserving the relationships between actions and states that determine physical consequences, rather than simply minimizing coordinate variation. This perspective provides a principled basis for designing robot world models that reuse physical knowledge across spatial configurations while retaining the distinctions essential to prediction and planning.

</details>


### [56] [Fade to... Red? Scheduling Coloured Noise Exploration Supports Efficient Robot Learning with Reduced Movement Jerk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11868)
*Francisco M. López,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在白噪声与红噪声之间测试49种线性噪声颜色调度，发现训练末期噪声颜色不影响学习效率或最终性能，但向红噪声渐变可将探索抖动减半；红噪声在效率与平滑度间取得最佳平衡，建议作为离策略机器人学习的默认调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续控制深度强化学习常用时间不相关的高斯白噪声探索，导致动作抖动大、局部探索受限；时间相关的有色噪声（尤其粉红噪声）已显示能提升探索效率，因此探究训练过程中噪声颜色调度是否能优于白噪声。

Method: 在Fetch机械臂和Shadow灵巧手的四个稀疏奖励操作任务上，系统研究49种白噪声到红噪声的线性调度，并与广义状态相关探索（gSDE）对比。

Result: 训练末期噪声颜色不影响学习效率或最终性能；向红噪声渐变使每个任务的探索抖动减半；红噪声学习效率与粉红噪声相当，但运动抖动减少近2倍。

Conclusion: 综合学习效率、最终性能和平滑度，红噪声是最优策略，建议将向红噪声渐变的探索调度器作为离策略机器人学习的默认方案。

Abstract: Exploration in deep reinforcement learning (RL) for continuous control is commonly driven by temporally uncorrelated Gaussian (white) action noise, which leads to jerky motions with locally constrained exploration. In contrast, temporally correlated coloured noise, particularly pink noise, has been shown to support more efficient exploration in RL. Motivated by these findings, we inquire whether scheduling changes to the noise colour over the course of training can outperform white noise for continuous control robotics. We perform a systematic study of 49 linear schedules between white and red noise, together with generalised state-dependent exploration (gSDE), on four sparse-reward manipulation tasks with a Fetch arm and a Shadow Dexterous Hand. We find that the noise colour at the end of training does not affect learning efficiency or end performance, whereas fading towards red noise halves the jerk of exploration in every task. Balancing learning efficiency, end performance and smoothness, red noise emerges as the preferred strategy: it learns as efficiently as pink noise with almost 2-fold less movement jerk. We therefore recommend exploration schedulers that fade towards red noise as the default for off-policy robot learning.

</details>


### [57] [Decoupled Asynchronous System for Humanoids (DASH): Latency-Agnostic Scaling of Vision-Language-Action Models for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11854)
*Bingzhang Wang,Nathan Zhao,Jialeng Ni,Jiajin Zheng,Yin Zhang,Kehua Chen,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DASH将VLA策略解耦为慢速视觉语言骨干（System 2）与快速流匹配动作头（System 1），通过LCM并发运行，使控制频率仅由动作头决定，从而在异步基准和真实人形机器人上显著提升反应性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环控制中VLA骨干位于控制关键路径，机器人持续运动导致执行动作基于越来越陈旧观测；增大骨干直接阻碍反应性，尤其在人形灵巧操作中问题突出。

Method: 将策略拆分为慢速VLM（System 2）和快速流匹配动作头（System 1），以LCM协议并发运行；动作头融合冻结骨干的陈旧语义与自身ResNet编码器的新鲜视觉；去同步训练暴露部署时陈旧性；自适应时间聚合器在可变推理延迟下对齐重叠动作块。

Result: 在按模拟时间计入推理延迟的异步RoboTwin基准上，DASH在0.8B至8B骨干下保持20 Hz控制频率，而同步方案从9.7 Hz降至4.1 Hz，且各规模成功率更高；真实灵巧手人形机器人上抓取放置成功率明显高于同步流水线。

Conclusion: 解耦异步架构可消除控制频率对VLM骨干规模的依赖，在保持大模型语义能力的同时实现高反应性闭环控制，适用于人形灵巧操作。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models pair a vision-language backbone with an action head, but in closed-loop control the backbone sits on the control-critical path: while it computes, the robot keeps moving, so each executed action is conditioned on an increasingly stale observation. A larger backbone therefore directly blocks reactivity, especially in humanoid dexterous manipulation. We present DASH, a Decoupled Asynchronous System for Humanoids that removes this coupling. DASH splits the policy into a slow vision-language model (System 2) and a fast flow-matching action head (System 1) that run as concurrent processes over the LCM communication protocol, so the control rate is set by the action head alone, independent of backbone size. The action head fuses stale semantics from a frozen backbone with fresh vision from its own ResNet encoder, desynchronized training exposes it to the staleness it meets at deployment, and an adaptive temporal aggregator aligns overlapping action chunks under variable inference latency. On an asynchronous RoboTwin benchmark that charges inference latency in simulated time, DASH holds 20 Hz control rate from 0.8B to 8B backbones while the synchronous rate falls from 9.7 to 4.1 Hz, with higher success at every size. On a real humanoid with dexterous hands, DASH achieves clearly higher pick-and-place success than the synchronous pipeline.

</details>


### [58] [A Benchmark and Layered Evaluation Protocol for Evidence-Grounded Shared Belief Updating in Connected Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11842)
*Ranulfo Bezerra,Kimitoshi Yamazaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CREG基准，将连接机器人共享推理评测为证据到信念的轨迹，而非仅看最终答案；实验发现关系内容重要但图序列化无一致优势，且信念准确率沿证据序列强烈非单调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只打分最终答案或动作，无法判断方法是否归因证据、更新信念、处理矛盾并论证决策，因此需要可测量的证据到信念轨迹评测框架。

Method: 构建CREG基准，含七个机制下350个确定性生成场景，每个证据前缀提供规范后验参考，经独立对数几率校验和盲审专家筛查；设九种表示条件分离关系内容与序列化、顺序和元数据；评测四个开放权重指令模型和六个透明融合基线。

Result: 实体接地解释了弱模型差距的相当部分；删除语义关系使关系依赖场景的严格信念状态准确率下降15.3分（Holm校正p<0.001），但信息匹配的结构化列表优于完整图，打乱图顺序无损害；信念准确率沿证据序列强烈非单调，存在仅端点评测无法检测的中途失败。

Conclusion: 应把连接机器人共享推理作为证据到信念轨迹来评测；关系内容在需要处重要，而图序列化无一致优势；CREG全资源开放以支持离线无硬件复现。

Abstract: Connected physical systems fuse observations from robots, IoT sensors, and edge-AI modules into a shared account of the world, yet most evaluations score only the final answer or action and cannot tell whether a method attributed the evidence, updated its belief, handled contradictions, and justified its decision. We argue that shared reasoning in Connected Robotics should be evaluated as an evidence-to-belief trajectory, and introduce the Connected Robotics Evidence Graph (CREG) Benchmark to make that trajectory measurable. CREG comprises 350 deterministically generated scenarios across seven regimes (justified revision, belief preservation, relation association, contradiction onset and resolution, stale/delayed evidence, and source reliability), each exposing a normative posterior reference at every evidence prefix, validated by an independent log-odds checker and screened by a blinded expert audit. Nine representation conditions separate relation content from serialization, ordering, and metadata. Evaluating four open-weight instruction models and six transparent fusion baselines yields three findings. First, entity grounding accounts for a substantial part of the weak-model deficit. Second, deleting semantic relations costs 15.3 points of strict belief-state accuracy on relation-dependent scenarios (Holm-corrected p<0.001), but an information-matched structured list outperforms the full graph and shuffling the graph does not hurt: relation content matters where required, while graph serialization confers no consistent advantage. Third, belief accuracy is strongly non-monotonic along the evidence sequence, with mid-trajectory failures that endpoint-only evaluation cannot detect. All scenarios, references, prompts, metrics, and analysis code are released for offline, hardware-free reproduction.

</details>


### [59] [Budget-aware Correspondence-driven View Selection for Real-time 3D Perception of Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11809)
*Vaishnav Ramesh,Jacob Carter,Shivali J. Patel,Sanjeev J. Koppal,Md Jahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种无需相机位姿的视角选择算法BCVS，在CPU上达到22+FPS，可在预算约束下在线筛除冗余图像，提升3D感知与重建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人部署产生长且冗余的图像序列，拖慢下游3D视觉与场景重建处理；现有方法依赖完整SfM流程或位姿估计，难以在移动平台上在线运行。

Method: BCVS采用纯对应关系驱动的评分策略，优先选择新颖视角同时保持足够重叠以保证几何稳定性；在用户给定预算下迭代选择最具区分度的帧并抑制近重复帧，生成保持连通性、最小化重建损失的紧凑子集。

Result: 基准实验中，BCVS在紧预算下优于CPU友好基线，预算增大时仍具竞争力；大型航空与海洋机器人现场试验中冗余降低达16%，同时保持稳健的SfM配准和高质量3D重建。

Conclusion: BCVS证明了无需位姿、低计算成本的在线视角选择在移动机器人和智能交通3D感知中的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: 3D visual perception and scene reconstruction are critical for autonomous robots and intelligent transportation, yet real-world deployments often generate long, redundant image sequences that hinder downstream processing. This report investigates BCVS (budget-aware correspondence-driven view sampling), a pose-free view selection algorithm that offers 22+ FPS on a CPU, enabling online filtering for efficient 3D perception by mobile robots. Unlike existing approaches that rely on full structure-from-motion (SfM) pipelines or camera pose estimation, BCVS employs a purely correspondence-based scoring strategy that prioritizes view novelty while maintaining sufficient overlap for geometric stability. Given a user-specified budget, BCVS iteratively selects the most distinct frames and suppresses near-duplicates, producing compact subsets that preserve connectivity and minimize reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments on benchmark show that BCVS outperforms CPU-friendly baselines at tight budgets and remains competitive as budgets scale. Field trials with large aerial and marine robotics data further validate its practicality and scalability: BCVS reduces redundancy up to 16% while sustaining robust SfM registration and high-quality 3D reconstructions.

</details>


### [60] [PAIR: Proprioception-Aligned Image-Ray Representations for Viewpoint-Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11798)
*Sung-Wook Lee,Xuhui Kang,Dylan Losey,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAIR提出一种基于机器人本体感知的对比学习框架，对齐图像与相机射线的联合表示，从而提升视点变化下的视觉运动策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在机器人操作泛化上进展显著，但视点鲁棒性仍是瓶颈：视觉输入在视点变化下存在外观与任务状态的纠缠，导致学习低效且难以泛化到未见视点；现有相机射线条件或几何对齐方法分别存在未将表示锚定到投影关系、以及忽略视点相关投影而引入虚假对应的问题。

Method: PAIR利用机器人本体感知对齐联合图像-射线表示，通过对比学习使相似机器人状态下的观测在表示空间中跨视点保持接近，为学习视点鲁棒特征提供结构化锚定信号；该框架可适配训练Diffusion Policy或后训练VLA模型。

Result: 在具有相机变化的仿真与真实操作基准上，PAIR分别比无射线条件的行为克隆基线高出12.7和26.7个百分点，表现出对未见视点更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PAIR通过本体感知对齐图像-射线联合表示，有效缓解视点依赖外观与任务状态的纠缠，是一种可灵活适配的策略学习框架，显著提升视点变化下的操作泛化性能。

Abstract: While imitation learning has made substantial progress toward generalizable robot manipulation through scaling, viewpoint robustness remains a major bottleneck. Visuomotor policies rely heavily on visual inputs, yet images provide an inherently ambiguous signal under viewpoint change: similar scenes may appear markedly different across viewpoints, while distinct states may share certain visual overlaps. A policy must therefore infer not only the task-relevant state, but also how it is visually projected under a particular camera configuration. This entanglement of viewpoint-dependent appearance and task-relevant state can make learning inefficient and limit generalization to unseen viewpoints. Recent work has begun to address this challenge through camera-ray conditioning and representation learning using geometric cues to induce invariance. However, providing camera information as additional context does not by itself ground visual representations in how the task scene is visually projected, while geometric alignment without camera context can introduce spurious correspondences by ignoring viewpoint-dependent projection. We propose PAIR, a contrastive learning framework that aligns joint image-ray representations using robot proprioception. PAIR encourages observations with similar robot states to remain close in representation space across viewpoints, providing a structured grounding signal for learning viewpoint-robust features. PAIR is a highly adaptable framework: it can be used to train Diffusion Policy (DP) or post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. In simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks with camera variation, PAIR consistently outperforms the behavior cloning baseline without ray conditioning by 12.7% and 26.7% points, respectively, demonstrating stronger generalization to unseen viewpoints. Project website: this http URL .

</details>


### [61] [HolidayFactory 1.0: Simulating, Generating, and Evaluating Industrial Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11789)
*Holiday Robotics,Jeongmin Lee,Taehyeon Kim,Dohyun Jang,Minji Lee,Seonghun Oh,Dongwoo Park,Geonhyup Lee,Jaehyung Kim,Jaemin Yoon,Jinseop Bok,Jungwoo Kim,Minseok Yang,Philsik Chang,Soomin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 框架，用于仿真、生成和评估工业机器人连续作业，包含 HOLIDAY-SIM 物理仿真、智能体化世界构建与 24/7-BENCH 七项无重置工业任务基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通常在重置后开始的回合中评估，而工业工作是结果影响后续操作的重复作业，需要大规模仿真关键物理交互、构建工业环境并在场景状态持续时测量性能。

Method: HOLIDAY-SIM 扩展基于 SDF 的液压弹性接触并加入本构定律和半隐式原始-对偶求解器，耦合有状态执行器动力学并导出触觉与关节观测；智能体化世界构建通过几何与交互检查生成仿真资产和场景变体并提出任务条件评估；24/7-BENCH 提供七项不重置场景的工业任务，测量完成/失败/未完成任务数、吞吐量、延迟和电机能耗。

Result: 受控实验表征了接触、执行器和触觉模型及验证引导世界构建的有效性；场景与评估生成实验揭示模型评审器的局限以及确保生成评估忠实反映任务要求的困难；使用脚本轨迹进行了作业级评估但尚未在持续运行中比较策略。

Conclusion: 该框架为持续状态下的工业机器人作业评估提供仿真与基准基础，但生成评估的忠实性和跨策略持续运行比较仍是待解决问题。

Abstract: Robots are typically evaluated in episodes that begin from a reset, while industrial work consists of repeated jobs whose outcomes affect subsequent operations. Evaluating such work at scale requires simulation of task-critical physical interactions, construction of industrial environments, and measurements of performance as scene state persists. We present HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0, a framework for simulating, generating, and evaluating industrial robot work. Given a human-specified workplace and task, the framework defines a world pairing a scenario of scene variants and process logic with an evaluation defining a job, metrics, and a rubric. HOLIDAYFACTORY 1.0 comprises three components. HOLIDAY-SIM extends Newton's SDF-based hydroelastic contact with additional constitutive laws and a semi-implicit primal-dual solver, couples stateful actuator dynamics to articulated motion, and derives tactile and joint observations from simulated states. Agentic world construction generates simulation-ready assets and scene variants using geometric and interaction checks, and proposes task-conditioned evaluations for human confirmation and executable checks. 24/7-BENCH introduces seven industrial tasks in which jobs are evaluated without resetting the scene, measuring completed, failed, and unfinished jobs, throughput, latency, and motor energy. Controlled studies characterize the proposed contact, actuator, and tactile models and examine the effectiveness of verification-guided world construction. Experiments on scene and evaluation generation further reveal the limitations of model critics and the difficulty of ensuring that generated evaluations faithfully capture task requirements. We examine job-level evaluations with scripted trajectories (e.g., code-as-policy) but have not yet compared policies over persistent runs.

</details>


### [62] [Planners Exploit Deterministic Error: Dithered Low-Bit World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11780)
*Yong Hwi Song,Keon Myung Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 低比特世界模型在基于采样的规划器中可能因舍入误差的持续性导致规划崩溃，而共享抖动（dither）可显著缓解该问题且几乎不增加延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低基于学习世界模型的模型预测控制（如MPPI、CEM）的计算成本，探索低比特量化世界模型在机器人规划中的可行性。

Method: 采用round-to-nearest激活量化且无校准，分析量化误差对规划器迭代的影响；提出在每个规划迭代中重新抽取并共享所有候选的dither方案，并在TD-MPC2、DINO-WM及317M参数多任务TD-MPC2上测试，同时用CUDA graphs在Ampere GPU上评估速度。

Result: round-to-nearest量化导致规划成功率下降最多100点，但单步隐预测误差与成功规划所用的随机舍入方案相当；共享dither在TD-MPC2的19个受影响Meta-World单元中至少恢复一半损失（中位数95%），DINO-WM 5个单元中4个有效，多任务TD-MPC2全部8个受影响任务有效；W8A4整数TD-MPC2规划rollout加速1.9-3.3倍，完整规划步加速1.3-1.5倍，共享dither增加最多2.3%延迟；block-scaled NVFP4和MXFP4部分缓解，失败与粗粒度共享尺度网格相关。

Conclusion: 低比特世界模型规划失败的关键不是舍入误差大小，而是误差是否跨规划迭代持续且在所有候选中共享；共享dither是一种无需训练的有效缓解手段，能在同比特宽度下恢复大部分性能并保持计算效率。

Abstract: Sampling-based planners such as MPPI and CEM query a learned world model thousands of times per control step, so low-bit world models are an obvious way to make model-based control cheaper on a robot. With round-to-nearest activation quantization and no calibration, we find that planning can collapse, by up to 100 points of success, while the world model's latent one-step prediction error stays no larger than that of a stochastic rounding scheme that plans well. At the same bit width, what decides the outcome is not the size of the rounding error but whether it persists across planner iterations and is shared across candidates. Round-to-nearest leaves an error that persists, and an iterating planner drifts toward the candidates that this error favors; in TD-MPC2, holding the same dither fixed within a step or an episode brings the drift back. Dither shared by all candidates and redrawn at every iteration removes most of the damage at the same bit width and without training: at least half of it in all 19 affected Meta-World cells (task $\times$ bit width) of TD-MPC2 (median 95%), in 4 of 5 DINO-WM cells, and in all 8 affected tasks of a 317M-parameter multi-task TD-MPC2. Captured as CUDA graphs on an Ampere GPU, a W8A4 integer TD-MPC2 world model runs its planning rollouts 1.9-3.3$\times$ and the whole planning step 1.3-1.5$\times$ faster than FP32, though not faster than BF16 with pre-converted weights; shared dither adds at most 2.3% latency. In TD-MPC2, block-scaled NVFP4 removes most of the damage and MXFP4 part of it, which ties the failure to coarse grids with a shared scale. Hypotheses, thresholds and analysis code were fixed before the data that test them, and the hypotheses that failed are reported.

</details>


### [63] [Graph-Sparse Distributed Predictive Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning vianEdge-Consensus ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11755)
*Josep Borras-Borras,Pablo Segovia,Carlos Ocampo-Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式模型预测控制架构，用于双积分器平面机器人的避碰运动规划，通过时变预测冲突图与ADMM分解降低协调需求，并由集中式HOCBF-QP选择性监督执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式完整图MPC协调需求大、计算可扩展性差，需要减少机器人间交互并实现并行求解，同时保证碰撞避免与任务性能。

Method: 基于时变预测冲突图选择相关成对交互；每次MPC更新用热启动轨迹对避碰约束凸化一次，得到固定凸二次规划；采用ADMM的边共识形式分解该QP，实现机器人本地并行优化与到分离半空间的独立闭式投影；当分布式候选不满足直接执行条件时，由集中式高阶控制屏障函数QP选择性监督。

Result: 在涉及8、16和32个机器人的C++仿真中实现无碰撞执行，显著提升计算可扩展性，并保持与集中式完整图基线相当的任务级性能。

Conclusion: 该分布式MPC架构在保证避碰与任务性能的同时有效降低协调开销并改善计算可扩展性，集中式HOCBF-QP监督可提供必要的执行安全保障。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed model predictive control (MPC) architecture for collision-avoiding motion planning of planar robots with double-integrator dynamics. To reduce coordination demands, a time-varying predicted conflict graph selects relevant pairwise interactions. The associated collision-avoidance constraints are convexified once per MPC update using warm-start trajectories, yielding a fixed convex quadratic program (QP). The resulting QP is then decomposed using an edge-consensus formulation of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), enabling parallel robot-local optimization and independent closed-form projections onto separating half-spaces. Execution is selectively supervised by a centralized high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) QP when the distributed candidate does not satisfy the direct-execution conditions. Evaluated in C++ simulations involving 8, 16, and 32 robots, the proposed architecture achieves collision-free execution and substantially improves computational scalability while maintaining task-level performance comparable to a centralized complete-graph baseline.

</details>


### [64] [Particle Filtering with Generative Trajectory Models for State Estimation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11732)
*Lukas Lao Beyer,Samvit Das,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在预训练生成模型推理阶段引入粒子滤波，用预测观测匹配度重加权粒子并引导动作选择，在不改模型的情况下提升状态估计与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可同时表示状态、动作与观测，但常用作直接采样动作的策略；作者希望利用其状态与环境演化预测能力，通过改变推理流程而非重新训练来改善状态估计与控制。

Method: 用粒子滤波维护多个状态历史假设；单个预训练扩散模型为每个粒子生成状态假设、预测观测和动作提议；新测量到来时按预测观测与实测的匹配度对粒子重加权，并将权重同时用于状态估计和动作选择，偏向解释观测更好的假设所对应的动作提议。

Result: 在部分可观测导航和基于视觉的机械臂操作仿真实验中，相比不做滤波的动作生成，状态估计与任务性能均有提升，且使用同一预训练模型、无需额外测试时信息。

Conclusion: 对预训练生成模型仅改变推理过程、结合粒子滤波即可同时改进状态估计与控制，无需模型修改或额外测试时信息。

Abstract: Generative models can support both state estimation and control in robotics. For control, a common approach is to use the model as a policy that directly samples actions conditioned on observation histories. However, models that jointly represent states, actions, and observations can also predict how the robot state and its environment evolve. We investigate whether these predictions can improve state estimation and control through a change in inference procedure, without modifying the pretrained model. To this end, we use particle filtering to maintain competing hypotheses about state histories. A single pretrained diffusion model generates these hypotheses along with their predicted observations and associated action proposals. As new measurements arrive, particles are reweighted according to how well their predicted observations match the measurements. While particle filtering conventionally uses these weights only for state estimation, we also use them to guide action selection, favoring proposals associated with hypotheses that better explain the observations. Simulation experiments in partially observed navigation and vision-based manipulation demonstrate improvements in both state estimation and task performance compared to action generation without filtering, using the same pretrained model and no additional test-time information.

</details>


### [65] [Accelerated Reinforcement Learning for Soft Robotics: Hours Instead of Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11718)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,Hehui Zheng,Robert K. Katzschmann,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用数据驱动的低维多项式常微分方程（aSSM约简模型）作为强化学习训练环境，在软体机械臂上3小时训练出轨迹跟踪策略，无需微调即可部署，误差比大参数RNN和Transformer策略低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习需要大量交互数据或可靠仿真器，而软体机器人硬件难以获得这两者；软体动力学存在时间尺度分离，可约简为低维不变流形（aSSM），因此希望用这种约简模型替代真实环境进行RL训练。

Method: 从预采集数据中学习一个仅含6,924个参数的多项式ODE（六维aSSM约简模型），将其作为RL训练环境，在气动软体机械臂上训练轨迹跟踪策略；与4.8M参数RNN和1.7M参数Transformer策略进行对比。

Result: aSSM模型识别仅需5分钟，RL训练3小时，部署无需微调；而同等硬件交互量外推需3年。策略跟踪动态目标误差比RNN和Transformer策略低40%，后两者识别分别需13和100小时。

Conclusion: 数学上合理的低维aSSM约简模型可作为高效、准确的RL训练环境，显著降低软体机器人策略训练的数据与时间成本，并优于大参数神经网络策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) requires large amounts of interaction data or a reliable simulator, both hard to obtain for nonlinear soft robotic hardware. Recent work shows that a time-scale separation inherent to soft robot dynamics justifies their reduction to low-dimensional invariant manifolds, known as adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs). We demonstrate that an aSSM-reduced model, a simple polynomial ordinary differential equation learned purely from pre-collected data, can serve as an RL training environment. On a pneumatically actuated soft arm, we train a trajectory-tracking policy in 3 hours on a six-dimensional aSSM-reduced model and deploy it without fine-tuning, whereas reproducing this policy training extrapolates to 3 years of hardware interaction. Our policy tracks dynamic targets with 40% lower error than otherwise identical policies trained on a 4.8-million-parameter recurrent neural network (RNN) or a 1.7-million-parameter transformer, which take 13 and 100 hours to identify, against 5 minutes for our mathematically justified 6,924-parameter model.

</details>


### [66] [Data-Driven Precision Control of Soft Helicoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11709)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,Mark Leone,Roberto Sabella,Hugo Buurmeijer,Luis Pabon,John Irvin Alora,Marco Pavone,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）理论的数据驱动建模方法从仿真扩展到硬件软体机器人，学习软螺旋躯干机器人的5维aSSM降阶非线性ODE模型，并用于模型预测控制，实现高精度实时轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前aSSM方法仅用于软体机器人仿真器的建模与控制，需要验证其在真实硬件平台上的有效性，以提升软体机器人闭环控制的精度。

Method: 从硬件软体机器人平台采集数据，学习5维aSSM降阶非线性ODE模型，并将该模型嵌入模型预测控制（MPC）方案，执行2D/3D、含静态与动态约束的闭环轨迹跟踪任务。

Result: 在所有控制任务中，该方法的精度和准确性均优于现有的数据驱动线性方法和无模型强化学习。

Conclusion: 该方法仅依赖硬件数据即可构建aSSM降阶模型，不仅适用于软螺旋躯干机器人，还可推广到更广泛的软体机器人系统。

Abstract: A purely data-driven method grounded in the theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) was recently used to model and control nonlinear soft robotic simulators. Here, we extend the methodology to learn aSSM-reduced models from data collected from hardware soft robotic platforms. Specifically, we learn a five dimensional (5D) aSSM-reduced nonlinear ordinary differential equation (ODE) that accurately models the dynamics of a soft-helicoid trunk robot. We then use this ODE in a model predictive control scheme to demonstrate real-time precision control across various challenging closed-loop trajectory-tracking tasks, ranging from 2D to 3D trajectories with static and dynamic constraints. We find that our methodology enhances accuracy and precision across all control tasks compared to available data-driven linear methods and model-free reinforcement learning. While we focus here on a soft-helicoid trunk robot, our methodology relies solely on hardware data and is therefore applicable to a broader class of soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [67] [Compute Regeneration through Re-Productization: Reorganizing Retired Edge AI Silicon into a Delivered Multilingual Service Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11698)
*Hongyu Luo,Sun Tie,John Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: This paper treats compute regeneration as re-productization: retaining a retired Jetson AGX Xavier compute module while redesigning the surrounding GEdgeBot service robot, and evaluates the resulting architecture through inventories, audits, and a replay campaign.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Useful edge hardware often loses product support while still offering substantial computation, motivating systems design that re-productizes the compute module rather than discarding it.

Method: Built GEdgeBot around a retired 32 GB Jetson AGX Xavier, splitting AI and orchestration on the Xavier from deterministic finite motor-pulse generation on an STM32F407; used source-grounded inventory, six-mode control state-machine audits, readiness barriers, sound orientation, historical single-prototype measurements, a November campaign re-measuring isolated AI workloads and control frames, a six-check static audit, and a 14-scenario replay executing the audited main() dispatcher with zero device opens.

Result: Historical measurements report 1,782 MB GPU memory with YOLO, Whisper-small, and NLLB-200 resident, direct-control p99 deviation rising from 0.24 to 49.93 ms under concurrent AI load, and six of six trigger-to-follow demos at 4.35 s mean latency with conversational voice unavailable; the finite-pulse boundary removes host jitter from the pulse train but not command start phase, stopping is host-enforced while the MCU 16-bit pulse field is unclamped, and the static audit exposes a host-MCU queue mismatch; the replay covered six modes with 54 contract-checked frames.

Conclusion: Results support an existence claim and a reproducible architectural hypothesis rather than fleet-level reliability; the 208 direct-control ratio remains unreplicated, and MCU waveform capture and on-site fault injection remain open.

Abstract: Useful edge hardware can lose product support while retaining substantial computation. We treat this as systems design: compute regeneration here means re-productization, retaining the compute module while redesigning the surrounding product organization. Our case is GEdgeBot, a multilingual service robot built around a retired 32 GB Jetson AGX Xavier. A source-grounded inventory shows the box alone supplies inference, while the combined system exposes five on-device AI services, a seven-language speech chain, three sensing domains, and one deterministic seven-byte control boundary. AI and orchestration stay on the Xavier; finite motor-pulse generation on an STM32F407. Audits cover the six-mode control state machine, readiness barriers and sound orientation. Historical single-prototype measurements report 1,782 MB GPU memory with YOLO, Whisper-small, and NLLB-200 resident, a direct-control p99 deviation rising from 0.24 to 49.93 ms under concurrent AI load, and six of six trigger-to-follow demonstrations at 4.35 s mean latency, with conversational voice unavailable. A November campaign restores an isolated inference stack, re-measures AI workloads, and repeats control-frame runs. Driving two execution organisations with the same 6,000 measured 50 ms frame intervals shows the finite-pulse boundary removes host jitter from the pulse train but not the command start phase; its stopping bound is host-enforced, while the MCU 16-bit pulse field is unclamped. A six-check static audit exposes a host--MCU queue mismatch, and a 14-scenario replay executes the audited main() dispatcher across six modes with zero physical device opens and 54 contract-checked frames. Results support an existence claim and a reproducible architectural hypothesis, not fleet-level reliability. The 208 direct-control ratio remains unreplicated; MCU waveform capture and on-site fault injection remain open.

</details>


### [68] [Streaming Failure Forecasting: VLA Failure Monitors That Keep Learning After Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11688)
*Amit Jadhav,Shaurya Beriwala,David Q. Liu,Yanfei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出流式故障预测（SFF）监控器，在部署后仅用每回合的成功标签在线重拟合监控器最后一层线性层，从而在任务或策略版本切换后仍能有效排序故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略的故障监控器离线训练后冻结，部署后遇到光照、物体、相机漂移或策略更新/任务切换时，冻结监控器排序故障的能力接近随机。

Method: SFF保持离线监控器网络固定，每回合后仅重新拟合最后的线性层；在Jetson CPU核上每次策略调用耗时2.18 ms；告警阈值仅在成功后移动。

Result: 在5个模拟测试套件中，SFF在4个上优于冻结监控器，并在所有套件上优于检测到漂移后重置；在真实SO-101机械臂上434次记录运行中，对四种VLA均优于冻结监控器，初步比较中在真实切换点优于完全重置。

Conclusion: 部署的故障监控器无需保持冻结：基于结果重拟合其读出层成本低，且在测试的变化后确有帮助。

Abstract: Failure monitors for vision-language-action (VLA) policies are trained offline and then frozen. After deployment, lighting, objects and cameras drift, and the policy itself is updated or moved to new tasks. On recorded real-robot runs, after a switch of task or policy version, a frozen monitor fit on the old task ranks failures little better than chance. We present Streaming Failure Forecasting (SFF), a monitor that keeps learning from the one label every episode provides: whether it succeeded. SFF keeps the offline monitor's network fixed and refits only its last linear layer after each episode, at 2.18 ms per policy call on a Jetson CPU core. Its alarm threshold moves only after successes, which in simulation kept false alarms near target except when two drifts came at once. With every setting locked before testing, SFF ranks failures better than the same monitor kept frozen on 4 of 5 simulated test suites under unannounced drift, and above resetting on detected drift on every suite. Carried with its locked learner settings to 434 recorded runs of four VLAs on real SO-101 arms, replayed across unannounced switches of task or policy version, it ranks failures better than the frozen monitor for every VLA and, in an exploratory comparison, above a full reset at the true switch. A deployed failure monitor need not stay frozen: refitting its readout from outcomes is cheap and helps after the changes we tested.

</details>


### [69] [Brain-Aligned Latent Actions: Imposing Premotor Cortex Geometry on a Robot's Action Vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11674)
*Amit Jadhav*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人策略使用的离散潜在动作码分配高度不稳定，利用这一自由度可将猕猴PMv皮层表征几何强加于分词器码分配，从而提升与PMv的表征相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究从无标签视频学习到的离散潜在动作码分配是否稳定，并利用其不稳定性将生物大脑皮层（猕猴PMv）的表征几何引入机器人分词器。

Method: 发现Moto潜在动作分词器码分配不稳定（两次训练ARI=0.001，bf16与fp32间74%令牌重分配）；在机器人视频上以直通相似性损失与重建目标联合训练，将猕猴画形状时PMv记录的表征几何施加于分词器码分配。

Result: 在五个未见过形状类别上，PMv训练的分词器与PMv表征相似度达0.52，对比发布版和无先验分词器为0.20-0.26，随机运动学编码器95百分位为0.42；运动学、语言和随机目标不迁移（-0.17至-0.03）；但手工运动学描述符（笔画初始方向）匹配更好（0.65），标签打乱的PMv对照得0.41。

Conclusion: 码分配的不稳定性为注入生物表征先验提供了机会，PMv训练可显著提升表征相似度，但对照结果限制了该主张的强度，且下游操作测试需在CALVIN流程上预先指定后运行。

Abstract: Robot policies increasingly act through a small vocabulary of discrete latent actions learned from unlabeled video, yet training leaves open which motion receives which code. We find this choice highly unstable: two independently trained runs of Moto's latent motion tokenizer assign the same robot clips to nearly unrelated codes (adjusted Rand index 0.001), and evaluating one tokenizer in bf16 instead of fp32 reassigns 74% of its tokens. We use this freedom to impose the representational geometry of macaque ventral premotor cortex (PMv), recorded while a monkey drew shapes, on the tokenizer's code assignment, with a straight-through similarity loss trained alongside its reconstruction objective on robot video. In one training run per target, on five shape categories the loss never saw, the PMv-trained tokenizer reaches a representational similarity of 0.52 with PMv, against 0.20-0.26 for released and no-prior tokenizers and 0.42 for the 95th percentile of random kinematic encoders. Kinematic, language, and random targets do not transfer (-0.17 to -0.03). Two results limit the claim: a hand-picked kinematic descriptor, the stroke's initial direction, matches PMv better (0.65), and a label-shuffled PMv control scores 0.41. We specify the downstream manipulation test, on a CALVIN pipeline that reproduces Moto's published results, before running it.

</details>


### [70] [HINT-VLM: Humanoid Interactive Navigation over Rough Terrain with Embodiment-Grounded Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11665)
*Ziwon Yoon,Seok Hwan Kim,Yiyu Chen,Quan Nguyen,Lu Gan,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合规划框架，结合VLM语义先验与高斯过程残差修正，实现人形机器人在非结构化户外崎岖地形中的可移动障碍物导航（NAMO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航假设环境不可交互，仅关注通行；现有NAMO研究多限于平坦环境与反应式交互，缺乏对物体可移动性的预测与前瞻规划。户外崎岖地形要求机器人同时推理地形可通行性与物体可移动性。

Method: 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成物体可移动性语义先验，通过高斯过程（GP）残差模型融合机器人实体交互数据对先验进行修正，得到基于具身性的概率化可移动性估计，并嵌入分层不确定性感知规划框架。

Result: 该方法使人形机器人能够联合利用移动与操作能力进行高效安全导航，成为首个通过预测性可移动性推理与概率化VLM集成解决崎岖地形NAMO的方案。

Conclusion: 提出的混合规划框架有效解决了户外崎岖地形中的人形机器人NAMO问题，验证了VLM与GP结合在具身可移动性推理中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved significant progress in locomotion and manipulation, enabling traversal over rough terrain and richer interaction with the environment, such as moving obstacles. However, most traditional navigation methods assume non-interactive environments and focus only on traversal. While Navigation Among Movable Obstacles (NAMO) approaches relax this assumption, prior efforts have largely focused on flat environments and reactive interaction, rather than predicting object movability and planning ahead. These limitations become critical in outdoor rough-terrain scenarios, where robots must jointly reason about terrain traversability and object movability. In this work, we address NAMO for humanoid robots in cluttered outdoor rough terrain by proposing a hybrid planning framework that combines semantic priors from a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with embodied robot interaction data through a Gaussian process (GP) residual model. The GP corrects the VLM-derived movability prior to produce embodiment-grounded probabilistic movability estimates, which are then incorporated into hierarchical uncertainty-aware planning. The proposed approach allows humanoids to jointly leverage locomotion and manipulation for efficient and safe navigation, representing the first solution to NAMO in rough terrain through predictive movability reasoning and probabilistic VLM integration.

</details>


### [71] [Enhancing Balance and Posture in Material Handling Tasks with a Wearable Robotic Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11662)
*Eisa Anwar,Sajeeva Abeywardena,Stuart C. Miller,Ildar Farkhatdinov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 可穿戴机器人尾巴能改善搬运任务中的平衡并减少肌肉负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频繁负重搬运导致肌肉骨骼劳损，下背痛影响高达40%的人工劳动者；受动物用尾巴保持平衡的启发，开发可穿戴机器人尾巴作为动态配重。

Method: 十名参与者完成模拟货架装卸任务，比较主动尾巴、被动尾巴和无尾巴三种条件；测量运动学、压力中心及腓肠肌、竖脊肌、胫骨前肌、肱二头肌的肌肉激活。

Result: 与无尾巴相比，主动尾巴使压力中心峰值位移减少>20 mm、躯干运动减少4.3度、竖脊肌和腓肠肌活动分别减少>9%和>20%；被动尾巴效果较小；主动较被动尾巴增加肱二头肌激活；踝关节运动学和胫骨前肌激活不变。

Conclusion: 可穿戴机器人尾巴有助于改善物料搬运中的平衡，主动运动比被动配重效果更佳。

Abstract: Frequent heavy lifting is linked to musculoskeletal strain, with lower back pain affecting up to 40% of manual labourers. Inspired by animals using tails for balance, we developed a wearable robotic tail as a dynamic counterbalance. Ten participants performed an imitated shelf loading/unloading task across three conditions: active tail, passive tail, and no tail. Kinematics, centre of pressure, and muscle activation (gastrocnemius, erector spinae, tibialis anterior, biceps brachii) were measured. Compared with no tail, the active tail achieved >20 mm reduction in centre of pressure peak displacement, 4.3 degree reduction in trunk motion, and >9% and >20% reductions in erector spinae and gastrocnemius activity, respectively. The passive tail produced smaller reductions, suggesting passive counterbalancing helps while active movement augments it further. Biceps brachii activation increased with the active versus passive tail. The ankle kinematics and tibialis anterior activation were unchanged. These findings support wearable robotic tails for improving balance during material handling.

</details>


### [72] [FlowEval: How Much Adaptation Does an Embodied Foundation Model Need at Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11650)
*Zhang Xi-Jia,Roman Yakunin,Shivang Chopra,Chengyue Huang,Che Chen,Siddharth Karamcheti,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlowEval——一组基于流匹配目标的免训练指标，利用少量目标域成功示范来评估预训练具身基础模型与目标部署的匹配程度，并预测所需适配数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练减少了具身基础模型部署所需的适配量，但预训练模型未必在每次部署中都已达到所需性能。实践者需要事先了解预训练模型与目标部署的匹配程度，以及还需收集多少适配数据。

Method: FlowEval使用流匹配目标对具身基础模型进行探测，量化模型在目标域成功示范上的预测分歧，从而刻画模型对目标部署的拟合程度。

Result: 在Pi-0.5、MolmoAct2、GR00T和DreamZero四个具身基础模型族上，FlowEval能区分真实与仿真操作中已见与未见部署；在17个RoboCasa365操作任务上微调Pi-0.5和GR00T后，FlowEval与随适配数据增加的成功率提升具有相关性。

Conclusion: FlowEval可作为轻量、免训练的模型-部署适配需求诊断工具，用于预判需多少适配数据。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining is reducing the amount of adaptation required to deploy embodied foundation models, but it is not guaranteed that a pretrained model already provide the level of performance required in every deployment. Practitioners therefore need to understand how well a pretrained model matches a target deployment and how much adaptation data is left to be collected. We study whether a small set of successful target-domain demonstrations can reveal whether and how much target-domain data will be required before any adaptation. We introduce FlowEval, a set of training-free metrics that use the flow-matching objective to probe an embodied foundation model with a set of demonstrations. FlowEval quantifies predictive disagreement on successful target demonstrations to assess how well a pretrained model fits a target deployment. Across four embodied foundation model families: Pi-0.5, MolmoAct2, GR00T, and DreamZero, FlowEval distinguishes seen from unseen deployments across real-world and simulated manipulation. Further, by finetuning Pi-0.5 and GR00T on 17 RoboCasa365 manipulation tasks, we showed that FlowEval correlates with how success rate improves as additional adaptation data is provided. Together, these results suggest that FlowEval can serve as a lightweight, training-free diagnostic of how much model--deployment adaptation may be required.

</details>


### [73] [PA-SaR: Perception-Aware Learning, Control, and Coordination for Multi-Robot Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11648)
*Jocubas Dimsa,Maria Grouev,Filip Keerberg,Nicolas Laplagne,Tigo Schutgens,Sven Schoonebeek,Anahita Jamshidnejad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PA-SaR框架，统一协调异构局部控制、学习探索、监督任务分配与自适应引导，仿真揭示自主搜救中各环节的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主搜救需探索未知环境、避障、团队协调并引导受害者，但提升单个机器人能力未必改善整体任务结果，需从任务层面统一建模与分析各机制交互。

Method: 构建数学与架构框架，区分环境真值、机器人感知与模拟受害者行为，集成模型预测与学习型探索、感知安全屏蔽、事件驱动任务分配、自适应接近-引导策略，并开展单/多机器人仿真实验。

Result: 发现避障与搜索完成度存在权衡；监督式分配优于仅信息共享；探索增加不必然带来更多救援；自适应引导相较持续引导提升救援数，但未检测到平均/最终信任提升或死亡率下降。

Conclusion: 应将局部控制、团队协调与疏散引导视为自主搜救中相互关联但彼此独立的部分，在仿真环境中分别评估。

Abstract: Autonomous SaR requires robots to explore unknown or partially known environments, avoid hazards, coordinate team activities, and guide discovered victims to safety. Improving individual capabilities of robots does not necessarily improve mission outcomes: safer motion can restrict the exploration behavior, faster discovery can increase the rescue demand, and effective guidance depends on victim responses.
