We introduce PA-SaR, a mathematical and architectural framework for coordinating heterogeneous local control, learning-based exploration, supervisory task allocation, and adaptive approach-lead victim guidance around mission-level objectives. PA- SaR distinguishes environmental ground-truth, robot-specific perceptions, and simulated victim behavior. This provides a common basis for integrating complementary mechanisms and analyzing their interactions across mission stages. We demonstrate the framework through single- and multi-robot simulation studies incorporating model-predictive and learning-based exploration, perception-based safety shielding, event-driven task allocation, and adaptive victim guidance. The results show trade-offs between collision avoidance and search completion, indicate that supervisory allocation improves search completion beyond information sharing alone, and demonstrate that increased exploration does not necessarily yield more rescues. Adaptive guidance increases rescues relative to continuous leading, without a detected improvement in mean or final victim trust or a reduction in mortality. These findings support evaluating local control, team coordination, and evacuation as related but distinct aspects of autonomous SaR within the considered simulation environments.

</details>


### [74] [ColNav: Uncertainty-Aware Spacetime Collision Checking with Bounding Volume Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11646)
*Sanjeev Ramkumar Sudha,Lars Ivar Hatledal,Erlend M. Coates*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ColNav将目标与自车的位置和速度不确定性编码为时空管，并用包围体层次结构实现一次性覆盖整个规划时域的稳健碰撞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态不确定环境中需避碰，而传统在采样点做前向传播碰撞检测的方法会漏检采样点之间的碰撞，且对状态估计不确定性不稳健。

Method: 提出ColNav：把目标和自车的位置与速度不确定性编码为时空管（分别支持有界集和高斯不确定性描述），将时空管组织为包围体层次结构，单次树遍历覆盖整个时域而非仅检测采样时刻，返回稳健几何证书及碰撞时间估计；静态和动态障碍共享同一表示。

Result: 在预测模型失配下成功率100%（最佳基线65%），密集交通中保持最高三倍于基线的间隙，并在实体机器人上验证了实时运行。

Conclusion: 基于时空管与包围体层次结构的碰撞检测公式能实现稳健、无漏检且实时的动态避碰规划，显著优于采样点检测的基线方法。

Abstract: Safe navigation for mobile robots in dynamic and uncertain environments requires planning paths that avoid collisions with other moving targets. Typically this involves forward propagating the robot motion and checking for collisions at sampled points in space and time. Such methods can miss collisions between sampled points and are not robust to uncertainty in the state estimates. We propose ColNav, a robust collision check formulation with geometric volumes. The key aspect of our approach is encoding the uncertainty in the targets' and the ego robot's positions and velocities into spacetime tubes, in two variants that consume bounded-set and Gaussian uncertainty descriptions, respectively. The tubes are organised in bounding volume hierarchies, so that a single tree traversal covers the entire horizon, rather than testing sampled time instants, and returns a robust geometric certificate together with an estimate of the time to collision. Static and dynamic obstacles share the same representation. Our approach reaches the goal with a 100% success rate under prediction model mismatch, where the best baseline reaches 65%, and maintains up to three times the clearance in dense traffic. We also validate real-time operation on a physical robot.

</details>


### [75] [ScanDreamer: A Map-Grounded World Model for Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11605)
*Yunyi Gao,Yixing Shi,Yize Liu,Hai-Duong Tran,Nhat-Hoang P. Nguyen,Shuai Wang,Yi Deng,Shengming Liu,Sim Kuan Goh,Duc-Thinh Pham,Sameer Alam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScanDreamer 是一种基于地图的世界模型智能体，通过在线二维扫描记忆地图和感知感知的运动约束，使四旋翼在有限视场深度相机下实现高效避障导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在未知杂乱的建筑和隧道中巡检时，前向深度相机视场有限，导致场景理解受限、避障困难；现有世界模型智能体（如 AirDreamer）数据需求大，且缺乏显式几何记忆和对相机可观测范围的感知。

Method: 构建由深度图像和载具位姿生成的在线二维扫描记忆地图，世界模型、actor 和 critic 均以该地图为条件，并用其定义测地进度奖励；引入前向锥惩罚抑制相机视场外的水平运动；在想象过程中，于冻结地图上传播四旋翼位姿，并从地图几何中推导进度和墙壁接触信号。

Result: 训练 80 万环境步（单 GPU 5.5 小时），在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 的 1560 个未见布局与参数范围回合中评估：ScanDreamer 成功率 96.3±0.3%、碰撞率 3.6±0.3%，相同在线交互预算下 AirDreamer 为 59.6±3.0% 和 28.8±2.8%；移除前向锥惩罚后碰撞率升至 9.9±3.1%。

Conclusion: 结合显式地图记忆、基于地图的想象和感知感知运动可显著提升导航性能与数据效率。

Abstract: Quadrotors inspecting buildings and tunnels must navigate unknown clutter with a forward-facing depth camera whose limited field of view constrains scene understanding and collision-avoiding navigation. Recent world-model agents learn navigation through imagined experience (e.g., AirDreamer), but remain data-intensive and often lack explicit geometric memory and awareness of what the camera can observe. We propose ScanDreamer, a map-grounded world model that constructs an online 2D scan-memory map from depth images and vehicle pose. Our world model, actor, and critic are conditioned on this map, which also defines a geodesic progress reward. Moreover, a forward-cone penalty discourages horizontal motion outside the camera's viewing direction. During imagination, ScanDreamer propagates the quadrotor pose over a frozen map and derives progress and wall-contact signals from the mapped geometry. We train ScanDreamer for 0.8 million environment steps (5.5 h on one GPU) and evaluate it in NVIDIA Isaac Sim over 1560 episodes spanning unseen layouts and parameter ranges. Across three seeds (mean $\pm$ SD), ScanDreamer achieves 96.3 $\pm$ 0.3% success with 3.6 $\pm$ 0.3% collision, compared with 59.6 $\pm$ 3.0% success and 28.8 $\pm$ 2.8% collision for AirDreamer at the same online-interaction budget. Removing the forward-cone penalty increases collisions to 9.9 $\pm$ 3.1%. These results show that combining explicit map memory, map-grounded imagination, and perception-aware motion substantially improves navigation performance and data efficiency. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [76] [Kinora: A Versioned Workflow for Text-to-Motion Generation, Robot Retargeting, and Interactive Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11601)
*Fangcheng Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kinora系统通过版本化产物连接文本生成动作、动作库、智能体工具、机器人重定向与轨迹精修，以支持可恢复执行、可追溯编辑、显式几何和模型特定验证；评估表明集成与保真契约在限定范围内成立，但未证明语义质量提升、群体可行性或硬件安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式机器人创作不仅需要生成候选轨迹，还需要可恢复的执行、可追溯的编辑、显式几何表示以及针对特定模型的验证；现有动作生成工具难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出Kinora系统，采用独立定义的52关节创作骨架与程序化骨架预览，将人体几何与外部生成模型及机器人资产分离；人体动作编辑保留区间外帧，机器人编辑生成不可变候选并由报告决定接受与导出；在服务器0.5.1和Gadget 0.15.2上执行156次核心回归测试、49次Gadget执行、4个CUDA生成/编辑案例、30帧G1重定向检查，以及3600帧合成G1夹具并配悬浮负对照。

Result: 4个CUDA案例均通过数值门限，但转向拼接仍保留14.3 cm支撑脚漂移代理警告；G1正例完成有界仿真回放，悬浮负对照在回放前被隔离；结果支持选定的集成与保真契约。

Conclusion: 结果支持选定的集成与保真契约，但未证明语义质量改进、群体级可行性或硬件安全性。

Abstract: Motion generation tools produce candidate trajectories, but interactive robot authoring also requires recoverable execution, traceable edits, explicit geometry, and model-specific validation. We present Kinora, a system that connects text-to-motion generation, a motion library, local agent tools, robot retargeting, and trajectory refinement through versioned artifacts. The current release uses an independently specified 52-joint authoring rig and procedural skeleton previews, separating the distributed human geometry from externally installed generation models and robot assets. Human-motion edits preserve frames outside a selected interval, while robot edits create immutable candidates whose reports govern acceptance and export. We evaluate server 0.5.1 and Gadget 0.15.2 with 156 core regression test executions, 49 Gadget executions, four CUDA generation/editing cases, a 30-frame G1 retargeting check, and a 3,600-frame synthetic G1 fixture paired with a suspended negative control. All four CUDA cases passed their numerical gates; the turning splice nevertheless retained a 14.3 cm support-foot drift proxy warning. The G1 positive fixture completed bounded simulation replay, whereas the suspended control was quarantined before replay. These results support selected integration and preservation contracts. They do not establish semantic-quality improvements, population-level feasibility, or hardware safety.

</details>


### [77] [Qubit-to-Motion: Traceable Action Provenance in Hybrid QRL for Robotic Bin-Packing Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11580)
*Hassen Nigatu,Howard Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Qubit-to-Motion证据中心框架，验证量子电路读出对受约束机器人决策的可追溯影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子强化学习评估多关注任务成功或收敛，未解决量子电路输出是否影响受约束动作及能否沿机器人执行链追溯的问题。

Method: 四个Pauli-Z读出作为actor和critic唯一学习输入，支撑掩码约束物体选择；每个决策通过步索引和SHA-256指纹链接到密封路线、确定性笛卡尔与逆运动学路点、规定关节运动及MuJoCo接触回放；在五个确定性30物体场景变体上评估冻结检查点。

Result: 150次放置全部满足支撑、容纳和稳定标准；固定状态零读出干预改变90.0±0.0%决策；平均宏行程34.537±0.206 m，每条路线推理8.60±1.27 ms，回放最终位置误差8.764±0.220 μm，最大互穿0.356 mm。

Conclusion: 结果确立了可复现的决策到接触可追溯性，表明量子电路表示对受约束动作具有实质影响。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum reinforcement learning has been explored for robotic decision-making, but evaluations often emphasize task success, return, or convergence, leaving unresolved whether quantum circuit outputs influence constrained actions and remain traceable through robot execution. We present \texttt{Qubit-to-Motion}, an evidence-centered framework for support-constrained sequencing and simulated robotic placement. Four Pauli-$Z$ readouts provide the sole learned input to the actor and critic, while a support mask enforces admissible object selection. Each decision is recorded and linked via step indices and SHA-256 fingerprints to a sealed route, deterministic Cartesian and inverse-kinematics waypoints, prescribed joint motion, and MuJoCo contact replay. A frozen checkpoint is evaluated across five deterministic 30-object scene variants. All 150 placements satisfy support, containment, and settling criteria. A fixed-state zero-readout intervention changes $90.0\pm0.0\%$ of decisions, demonstrating dependence on the joint circuit representation. Mean macro travel is $34.537\pm0.206$~m, inference requires $8.60\pm1.27$~ms per route, and replay achieves $8.764\pm0.220~\mu$m mean final position error with 0.356~mm maximum interpenetration. These results establish reproducible decision-to-contact traceability. The implementation is available at \href{ this https URL }{\textcolor{linkpink}{the anonymous repository}}.

</details>


### [78] [Kimodo-Policy: Learning Kinematic Action Models for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11554)
*Yunheng Wang,Jiaxi Zhang,Wei Lu,Yuetong Fang,Taowen Wang,Yixiao Feng,Shuning Zhang,Junzhe Xu,Yichi Wang,Zizhao Yuan,Kailun Huang,Lusong Li,Mingqian Zhou,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Kinematic Action Models (KAMs)新范式，以预训练运动生成器作为动作生成主干，实例化Kimodo-Policy在仿真与真实人形机器人全身移动操作任务上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和世界动作模型虽能迁移语义与视觉动力学先验，但未必捕捉机器人运动本身的结构；作者主张将全身运动作为直接预训练目标。

Method: KAMs以预训练运动生成器为主干，通过任务条件、视觉观测和执行反馈对齐时序与跨身体协调先验；Kimodo-Policy用冻结的Kimodo文本到运动主干加轻量ControlNet视觉条件，采用根-身-手分层协调移动与操作，并用运动历史条件与重复动作块预测衔接运动合成与闭环控制，先进行大规模移动操作预训练。

Result: 在HumanoidArena多任务上总体成功率72.14%为对比策略最高，SIMPLE上具竞争力，八个真实任务平均成功率60%；模型678M参数（92M可训练）；消融支持预训练运动主干的贡献。

Conclusion: 运动生成可作为人形机器人动作学习的互补性基础，为KAMs范式提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires continuous coordination of high-dimensional whole-body motion to realize task objectives. While vision-language-action models and world action models transfer semantic and visual-dynamics priors to robot control, these priors do not necessarily capture the structure of robot motion. We introduce Kinematic Action Models (KAMs), a paradigm that treats whole-body motion as a direct pretraining target and uses a pretrained motion generator as the primary action-generation backbone. KAMs ground learned temporal and cross-body coordination priors through task conditioning, visual observations, and execution feedback. We present Kimodo-Policy as an initial instantiation, combining a frozen Kimodo text-to-motion backbone with lightweight ControlNet-based visual conditioning. A root-body-hand hierarchy coordinates locomotion and manipulation, while motion-history conditioning and repeated action-chunk prediction bridge motion synthesis and closed-loop control. Following large-scale loco-manipulation pretraining, the policy achieves the highest overall success among compared policies on multi-task HumanoidArena (72.14%), competitive performance on SIMPLE, and 60% mean success across eight real-world tasks, with 678M parameters (92M trainable). Ablations support the contribution of the pretrained motion backbone. Together, these results provide initial evidence for motion generation as a complementary foundation for humanoid action learning.

</details>


### [79] [K-PWM: Control-Oriented Structured Bilinear World Models under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11521)
*Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Sriram Narayanan,Samuel E. Otto,Debdipta Goswami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: K-PWM 是一种面向部分可观测系统的概率世界模型，结合 Koopman 双线性潜在动力学与非线性观测解码器，用扩展卡尔曼滤波推断潜在状态，并以 GECM 训练，实现高效预测与 MPPI 控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限、含噪且部分观测的数据中学习世界模型仍具挑战；现有工作多聚焦像素观测，而许多物理系统仅提供揭示部分状态的向量测量，需要一种数据与计算高效的概率世界模型。

Method: 提出 K-PWM：将 Koopman 双线性潜在动力学与非线性观测解码器耦合，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波与平滑在学习模型上做概率潜在状态推断，无需单独推理网络；双线性结构支持高斯信念精确传播与高效 rollout，用于不确定性感知的 MPPI 控制；训练采用广义期望条件最大化（GECM），对生成元、噪声协方差和潜在先验闭式更新，并辅以基于梯度的解码器学习；使用 Koopman 一致的初始化从数据选择潜在维度并初始化动作相关动力学。

Result: 在部分观测的 Gymnasium MuJoCo 系统上，有限训练数据下 K-PWM 的长期预测误差低于对比基线，且训练时间较最强预测基线大幅缩短；在高维 MuJoCo Menagerie 机器人上取得有竞争力的跟踪精度与较低单步计算时间。

Conclusion: 结构化概率动力学是在部分观测下进行世界建模与控制的一种数据与计算高效途径，具备实时部署潜力。

Abstract: World models learn internal simulators of environment dynamics for prediction, planning, and control, yet learning them from limited, noisy, and partial observations remains challenging. While much progress has focused on pixel-based observations, many physical systems instead provide vector-valued measurements that reveal only part of the underlying state. We introduce K-PWM, a probabilistic world model for prediction and control under partial observation. K-PWM couples Koopman-bilinear latent dynamics with a nonlinear observation decoder and performs probabilistic latent-state inference through the learned model via extended Kalman filtering and smoothing, without a separately trained inference network. Its bilinear structure enables exact propagation of the maintained Gaussian belief through the latent transition and efficient rollouts for uncertainty-aware model predictive path integral (MPPI) control. For training K-PWM, we present a generalized expectation conditional maximization (GECM) procedure with closed-form updates for the generators, noise covariances, and latent prior, complemented by gradient-based decoder learning. A Koopman-consistent initialization selects the latent dimension from data and initializes the action-dependent dynamics. With limited training data, K-PWM achieves lower long-horizon prediction error than the evaluated baselines on partially observed Gymnasium MuJoCo systems, while substantially reducing training time relative to the strongest prediction baseline. On high-dimensional MuJoCo Menagerie robots, it achieves competitive tracking accuracy with low per-step computation times, demonstrating potential for real-time deployment. These results support structured probabilistic dynamics as a data- and compute-efficient approach to world modeling and control under partial observation.

</details>


### [80] [Breaking survivorship bias in driving world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11516)
*Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Yuhao Luo,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Soyoung Ahn,David A. Noyce,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 驾驶世界模型存在幸存者偏差，因训练数据中的未来几乎都以安全结束；作者提出中性世界模型NWM，混合常规驾驶与真实事故视频进行训练，使其能更真实地预测即将发生的碰撞，并用作决策的想象引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶数据集记录的未来几乎都是安全的，导致世界模型即使碰撞迫在眉睫也倾向想象良性结果，这种幸存者偏差削弱了模型在安全关键场景下的可靠性。

Method: 提出NWM，从常规驾驶数据与真实事故视频的混合数据中学习，采用边界框引导的目标函数；并将NWM作为想象引擎，对多条候选自车轨迹分别预测未来，再用奖励函数评估想象结果以选择动作。

Result: NWM能更准确地预见即将发生的碰撞，同时在常规驾驶中保持较低的误报碰撞率和有竞争力的生成质量；在NAVSIM挑战性场景中比基线世界模型更可靠地选出更优轨迹；使用自动驾驶研究车采集的真实行人假人横穿数据进行的零样本实验表明可迁移到未见传感器观测。

Conclusion: 纠正幸存者偏差可使世界模型更真实地推理未来，并支撑自动驾驶中的安全关键决策。

Abstract: World models allow autonomous vehicles to imagine the consequences of candidate actions before execution, but their reliability depends on the futures represented in their training data. We identify a survivorship bias in driving world models: because the futures recorded in prevailing driving datasets almost always end safely, existing models often imagine benign outcomes even when a collision is imminent. We introduce a neutral world model (NWM) that learns both routine and safety-critical futures from a mixture of routine driving and real accident videos with a bounding-box-guided objective. The NWM more faithfully anticipates imminent collisions, while maintaining a low false collision rate and competitive generation quality in routine driving. We further use the model as an imagination engine for decision-making. Given multiple ego trajectories, the NWM predicts the future associated with each candidate, and a reward function evaluates these imagined outcomes before selecting an action. On challenging NAVSIM scenarios, this procedure selects the better trajectory more reliably than the baseline world model. Zero-shot experiments with real-world pedestrian dummy crossing data collected using an autonomous research vehicle further demonstrate transfer to unseen sensor observations. These results show that correcting survivorship bias enables world models to more faithfully reason about futures and support safety-critical decision-making in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [81] [Review the Episodes, Refine the Lessons: Simple Memory Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11509)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Xing Gao,Guanbo Jiao,Yukai Wang,Jianming Ma,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimplEvo 让 MLLM 仅通过自写文本记忆在直接控制下自我进化，无需权重更新或后训练策略，即大幅提升具身任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作围绕 MLLM 构建复杂工程（后训练策略、递归自改进循环），但这些附加组件是否真正超越 MLLM 原生能力尚未被探索。

Method: 提出 SimplEvo：MLLM 从单次演示写一次无坐标任务记忆；在严格训练-测试协议下，仅在训练回合构建有界工作记忆，并在每回合后按流程、完成和恢复经验精炼；测试时两种记忆固定；无需权重更新、后训练策略或特权信息。

Result: 在 EBench 上，GPT-6-Astra 直接控制成功率从 46.7% 提升至 81.7%；在 RoboDojo 排行榜以 69.63% 成功率和 75.32 分登顶；仅用文本记忆即可达到存储视觉帧记忆的相近成功率且 token 更少；记忆固定时可泛化到未见布局和随机场景配置；工作记忆可跨模型迁移，由 GPT-6-Astra 写出的记忆可被较弱 MLLM 直接复用并提升其性能。

Conclusion: MLLM 在直接控制下借助自写文本记忆即可显著自我改进并泛化，其原生能力被此前复杂工程所低估；文本记忆高效且可迁移。

Abstract: Recent studies of embodied agents based on frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) develop harness engineering around the MLLM, such as harnesses with post-trained policies and recursive self-improvement loops. Yet whether these additions exceed the native capability of the MLLM remains unexplored. We introduce SimplEvo to explore how far an MLLM can improve under direct control when it retains its execution experience in self-written text memories. It requires no weight updates, post-trained policies, or privileged information. The MLLM writes a coordinate-free task memory once from a single demonstration. Under a strict train-test protocol, it then creates a bounded working memory on training episodes only and refines it after each episode with lessons on procedure, completion, and recovery. Both memories stay fixed at test time. On EBench, SimplEvo lifts the success rate (SR) of GPT-6-Astra under direct control from 46.7% to 81.7%, and tops the RoboDojo leaderboard with 69.63% SR and a score of 75.32. Compared with memories that also store past visual frames, SimplEvo reaches a similar SR with text memory alone while using fewer tokens. With its memories fixed, SimplEvo also generalizes to unseen layouts and randomized scene configurations. Moreover, the working memory transfers across models: written by GPT-6-Astra, it can be reused as is by weaker MLLMs and improve their performance without any adaptation of their own.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Memory Limits: A Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Map Representation for Large-Scale 3D Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11489)
*Yizhe Zhang,Jianping Li,Yilun Gao,Liangliang Yin,Pengfei Wan,Bisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLAVOR是一个分层时空框架，将密集几何地图限制在机器人局部，同时用轻量观测记录和待办任务保留全局上下文，从而在无人机自主探索中实现更短探索时间和极小地图内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机探索系统维护全局可查询几何地图，内存和空间查询成本随探索区域增长；而机器人中心局部建图虽能限制内存，却丢失长时程任务所需的全局上下文。

Method: 提出分层框架：观测层用轻量LiDAR关键帧可见性记录保留射线证据用于前沿推理；任务层将局部地图外的未解决前沿作为紧凑待办任务与里程计锚点关联；稀疏拓扑图连接锚点并保留路径；返回任务区域时从观测重建前沿和视点，拓扑路径在执行前用当前局部地图实例化验证。

Result: 密集地图容量固定，探索相关内存增长仅限紧凑观测和任务记录；相比完成三个仿真场景的最佳基线，探索时间缩短31.98–41.88%，地图内存占用减少94–99%；真实部署中森林减少92.67%、建筑工地减少62.42%（相对显式点云ikd-tree），并在受限机载资源下保持长期任务连续性。

Conclusion: FLAVOR通过分离局部密集几何与全局紧凑上下文，有效解决了长时程探索中内存与计算受限的难题，兼顾探索效率和任务连续性。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV exploration is increasingly used for large-scale surveying and inspection in inaccessible or disaster-affected environments, where onboard memory and computational resources are often severely constrained. Most existing UAV exploration systems maintain globally queryable geometric map representations for motion planning and exploration decision-making, causing memory consumption and spatial-query costs to increase with the explored area. Robot-centric local mapping bounds dense-map memory and computation but discards global task context needed for long-horizon this http URL present FLAVOR, a hierarchical spatio-temporal framework that confines dense metric geometry to a robot-centric local map while retaining compact global observation and task context. At the observation level, lightweight LiDAR-keyframe visibility records preserve ray-wise evidence for frontier reasoning. At the task level, unresolved frontiers beyond the local-map extent are retained as compact pending tasks associated with odometry anchors. A sparse topological graph connects these anchors through retained routes for future this http URL returning to a task region, FLAVOR reconstructs the frontier and exploration viewpoint from observations, while the topological route is instantiated and validated against the current local map before execution. Dense-map capacity remains fixed as exploration expands, with exploration-dependent memory growth confined to compact observation and task this http URL achieves 31.98--41.88% shorter exploration time and a 94--99% smaller map memory footprint than the best baseline that completes all three simulated scenes. In real-world deployments, it reduces this footprint by 92.67% in the forest and 62.42% at the construction site relative to an explicit point-cloud ikd-tree while preserving long-term task continuity under constrained onboard resources.

</details>


### [83] [Off-Policy RL with Online Inference Tuning: My Solution to the CoRL 2026 RoboEval Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11464)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍CoRL 2026 RoboEval挑战赛的解决方案：基于π0.5的策略自值函数，结合离策略RL（AWR+RECAP）与生成演示、恢复片段，在留出种子上达到0.990 RDS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoboEval挑战赛不仅评估任务是否成功，还按每个阶段的安全性、效率与双臂协调性对照人类演示打分，因此需要一种能直接优化官方评分、并覆盖本地探索难以触及区域的方法。

Method: 方案分两部分：通用部分沿用LeHome方案的离策略强化学习，在基于π0.5且自身作为价值函数的策略上，用AWR和RECAP基于官方精确分数的优势进行训练，并在每次rollout同时作为约20个推理参数的Thompson采样试验的循环中迭代；竞赛特定部分补充本地探索无法发现的数据，包括达到完整奖励的生成演示和从策略自身失败开始的恢复回合。

Result: 通用部分在两天内于留出种子上达到0.85 RDS（仍远未收敛）；加入竞赛特定数据后，提交策略在留出种子上达到0.990 RDS，在官方种子上达到0.987 RDS。

Conclusion: 将直接优化官方评分的离策略RL与生成演示及失败恢复数据相结合，可显著提升双臂操作策略在RoboEval挑战赛上的评分表现。

Abstract: The CoRL 2026 RoboEval challenge has eight bimanual tasks, and it ranks a policy not only on whether it succeeds but on how: every stage of the task, done safely, efficiently and with both arms in coordination, scored against human demonstrations. My solution has two parts. The generic part, carried over from my LeHome challenge solution, is off-policy RL on a $\pi_{0.5}$-based policy that is also its own value function. It is trained with AWR and RECAP on advantages of the exact official score, in a loop where every rollout is at the same time a Thompson-sampling trial of about 20 inference parameters. Out of the box, it took the policy to 0.85 RDS on held-out seeds in two days (and it was still very far from convergence). The competition-specific part adds data that local exploration cannot find: generated demonstrations that reach the full reward, and recovery episodes that start from the policy's own failures. The submitted policy reaches 0.990 RDS on held-out seeds and 0.987 on the official seeds.

</details>


### [84] [When Coding Agents Get Arms: Safety Risks Across the Digital-Physical Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11438)
*Yiming Sun,Mi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向零样本机器人控制的编码智能体系统安全基准ArmedAgent，覆盖鲁棒性、恶意使用与间接提示注入三类风险，在仿真与实体机械臂上验证现有前沿模型存在显著安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型具备空间理解能力后，可零样本、端到端地驱动机器人手臂，无需任务演示或重训练；这一新范式带来两类风险：一是模型广博知识使智能体能执行过去VLA/WAM范式无法完成的任务，产生新的能力滥用；二是编码智能体可访问网页、邮件等数字资源，其中的间接提示注入等威胁会随机器人控制传导至物理世界。

Method: 构建ArmedAgent基准，涵盖5个场景下36项任务，覆盖鲁棒性、恶意使用和间接注入三个维度，在仿真环境中评估并在实体机械臂上验证；其中部分任务专门针对早期VLA范式无法执行的有害行为；在主流智能体框架下测试包括GPT-6-Astra在内的多个前沿模型。

Result: 在多种主流智能体框架下测试多个前沿模型发现，当前系统普遍存在显著安全风险。

Conclusion: 零样本机器人控制编码智能体的安全风险已被系统性证实，亟需在能力滥用与数字到物理的注入传导两方面加强防护。

Abstract: Advances in spatial understanding have enabled frontier models to power coding agents that control a real robot arm zero-shot and end-to-end, without task-specific demonstrations or retraining. This brings two new safety challenges. First, the extensive knowledge of frontier models lets these agents perform tasks that earlier VLA and WAM paradigms could not, opening the door to new forms of capability misuse. Second, coding agents can access digital resources such as web pages and emails. Threats in these resources, such as indirect prompt injection, can carry over into the physical world when the agent controls a robot. We therefore introduce ArmedAgent, the first systematic benchmark to evaluate both risks for coding agents performing zero-shot robot control. It covers robustness, malicious use, and indirect injection across 36 tasks in 5 scenarios, evaluated in simulation and validated on a physical robot arm. A distinctive portion of the tasks target harmful behaviors that earlier VLA paradigms could not perform. Testing several frontier models, including GPT-6-Astra, under widely used agent frameworks, we find substantial safety risks in current systems.

</details>


### [85] [Single-Radar Bilateral 3D Near-Field Imaging for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11296)
*Yingqi Wang,Tingwei Chen,Zhongqin Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种运动辅助框架，利用单个前向毫米波雷达在机器人运动过程中重建轨迹两侧的3D结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭移动机器人需要可靠感知附近家具与结构，但单雷达前向安装时受近场波前曲率、视角相关可见性及仰角多样性不足限制，难以重建路径两侧信息。

Method: 建立近场模型，用球面路径关联场景反射率与跨频率和机器人位置的步进频率MIMO观测；采用双侧发射合成长宽带方向加权，滑动孔径反投影按近场传播距离相干聚焦，并用Top-K功率融合合并不同可见性的局部图像；随后进行多层高度提取与基于原始MIMO通道的受保护自适应高度细化。

Result: 在受控、办公室/书房和走廊环境中，平均横向-前向定位误差分别为4.65、5.63和5.98厘米，峰值背景比超过13 dB。

Conclusion: 该框架仅凭单部前向雷达和一次前向通过即可恢复双侧3D散射结构，且不依赖学习到的场景表示。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating in homes need reliable 3D perception of nearby furniture and surrounding structures, yet recovering this information on both sides of the robot's path with a single forward-mounted millimeter-wave radar remains challenging because of near-field wavefront curvature, viewpoint-dependent target visibility, and limited elevation diversity. This paper presents a motion-assisted framework for reconstructing structures on both sides of a robot's trajectory. Our near-field model relates scene reflectivity to stepped-frequency multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) observations across frequencies and robot positions using spherical paths from transmitter (Tx) to scatterer to receiver (Rx). Bilateral Tx combining provides wideband directional weighting, while sliding-aperture back-projection coherently focuses the measurements using near-field propagation distances. Top-K power fusion combines local images with different visibility. The reconstruction provides lateral-forward support for multi-layer height extraction, followed by guarded adaptive height refinement using the original MIMO channels. Experiments in controlled, office/study, and corridor environments produce mean lateral-forward localization errors of 4.65, 5.63, and 5.98 cm, respectively, with peak-to-background ratios (PBRs) exceeding 13 dB. The framework recovers bilateral 3D scattering structure from one forward pass using a single radar, without relying on learned representations of the scene.

</details>


### [86] [DeepSoMo: Deformable Object Motion Estimation under Proprioceptive Partial Observability via Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11242)
*Hanwen Zhao,Weicheng Dai,Luoyao Chen,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepSoMo，一个基于学习的框架，通过自监督潜在预测约束在潜在动力学中强制预测结构，从本体感知部分可观测性角度估计软体机器人的可变形运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从本体感知传感估计软体机器人可变形身体的运动具有挑战性，因为内部观测只能间接提供全局运动、接触和形状演化的信息，存在部分可观测性问题。瞬时观测不足，准确估计需要时间一致的潜在表示。

Method: 从潜在动力学视角研究本体感知部分可观测性下的可变形运动估计，提出DeepSoMo框架，通过自监督潜在预测约束在潜在动力学中强制预测结构，并使用高保真有限元模拟作为受控的真值来源进行评估。

Result: 在轨迹估计、方向恢复、形状重建和接触推断方面，相较于架构匹配的循环基线方法均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 长期估计的关键要求是潜在时间一致性，即潜在状态保持对未来观测的预测能力，DeepSoMo通过强制潜在动力学中的预测结构有效实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Estimating the motion of deformable bodies in soft robotics from proprioceptive sensing is challenging due to partial observability, as internal observations provide only indirect access to global motion, contact, and shape evolution. In such settings, instantaneous observations are insufficient, and accurate estimation requires temporally consistent latent representations. This paper studies deformable motion estimation under proprioceptive partial observability from a latent dynamics perspective. We argue that a key requirement for long-horizon estimation is latent temporal consistency, where latent states remain predictive of future observations. To this end, we present DeepSoMo, a learning-based framework that enforces predictive structure in latent dynamics through a self-supervised latent prediction constraint. We evaluate the approach in a canonical deformable dynamics setting using high-fidelity finite-element simulation as a controlled source of ground truth. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in trajectory estimation, orientation recovery, shape reconstruction, and contact inference over architecture-matched recurrent baselines.

</details>


### [87] [DexForesight: Grounding Dexterous Policies with Implicit Spatial Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11166)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yi Yang,Xinyi Chen,Mingzhe Ye,Yaping Li,Zhihong Liu,Congsheng Xu,Haonan Wang,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexForesight通过将动态感知的世界表示注入灵巧操作策略的潜在空间，隐式学习空间预见能力，在仿真和真实机器人任务上均提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要对周围场景和机器人手部有丰富的空间预见能力，现有世界-动作模型通常通过显式未来预测来获取这种预测知识，效率较低且部署时需要额外输入。

Method: 训练阶段，从离线场景深度、运动学手部几何和演示轨迹中学习动作条件空间世界模型，预测3D手部点运动和视觉特征变化；冻结世界模型，将策略查询与世界模型的预测查询对齐，把学到的空间动态迁移到策略潜变量中并用于条件化动作生成；部署时仅保留策略，基于RGB观测、语言和机器人状态运行，无需深度输入或显式未来预测。

Result: 在11个DexJoCo仿真任务上，整体成功率从基础策略的49.4%提升至60.5%；在三个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，成功率达到58.3%，而最强基线为50.0%。

Conclusion: 通过隐式注入动态感知世界表示，DexForesight有效增强了灵巧操作策略的空间预见能力，并在仿真与真实环境中均取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires rich spatial foresight over both the surrounding scene and the robot hand. Existing world-action models often acquire such predictive knowledge through explicit future prediction. We introduce DexForesight, a framework that learns spatial foresight implicitly by injecting dynamics-aware world representations into the latent space of a dexterous policy. During training, DexForesight learns an action-conditioned spatial world model from offline scene depth, kinematic hand geometry, and demonstration trajectories. The world model predicts 3D hand-point motion and visual feature changes, capturing both articulated hand dynamics and scene changes under demonstrated actions, including the motion of fingers occluded from the cameras. We then freeze the world model and align policy queries with its predictive world queries, transferring the learned spatial dynamics into policy latents and using them to condition action generation. At deployment, only the policy is retained, operating on RGB observations, language, and robot state without depth input or explicit future prediction. On 11 DexJoCo simulation tasks, DexForesight improves the overall success rate of the base policy from 49.4% to 60.5%. On three real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks, DexForesight achieves 58.3% success, compared with 50.0% for the strongest this http URL page this https URL

</details>


### [88] [Tutorial: Vision-Based Formation Control with Differential-Drive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11164)
*Christian Marowski,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本教程介绍使用差速驱动机器人、俯视相机和Python实现多机器人编队控制的逐步方法，利用ArUco标记定位并通过期望位移向量定义圆形编队，开源代码支持实践学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合机器人教育中理论理解与实际实现之间的差距，强调将数学概念转化为物理机器人上的协调行为。

Method: 使用ArUco标记估计机器人位姿，定义圆形编队的期望成对位移向量，比较实测与期望相对位置计算运动向量，转换为差速驱动轮命令，并加入基于接近传感器的避障；教程涵盖硬件软件设置、位姿估计、编队几何、反馈控制、系统集成和编队误差评估。

Result: 提供了完整的Python实现、设置说明和演示，集成感知、控制与避障，并可通过编队误差评估验证性能；虽基于特定平台，但原理可适配其他差速驱动机器人和仿真环境。

Conclusion: 该教程通过开源GitHub仓库支持动手学习和多机器人系统的进一步探索，有效促进机器人教育中的理论与实践结合。

Abstract: This tutorial presents a step-by-step introduction to multi-robot formation control using differential- drive robots, an overhead camera, and Python. ArUco markers are used to estimate robot positions and orientations, while desired pairwise displacement vectors define a circular formation. By comparing measured and desired relative positions, the controller computes movement vectors that are converted into differential-drive wheel commands, with basic proximity-sensor-based obstacle avoidance. The tutorial guides readers through hardware and software setup, robot pose estimation, formation geometry, feedback control, system integration, and formation-error evaluation. Motivated by the need to bridge the gap between theoretical understanding and practical implementation in robotics education, we emphasize how mathematical concepts are translated into coordinated behaviors on physical robots. Although the implementation uses a specific robotic platform, the underlying perception and formation-control principles can be adapted to other differential-drive robots and simulation environments. The accompanying open-source GitHub repository provides the Python implementation, setup instructions, and demonstrations to support hands-on learning and further exploration of multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [89] [MOSU2: Long-range Navigation System with Multi-modal Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11148)
*Jing Liang,Zhenyu Hou,Senthurbavan Kirubaharan,Md Safwan Mondal,Ashok Gudivada,Xuesu Xiao,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSU2 通过两阶段轨迹选择，在长距离户外导航中按需调用几何与动态信息，显著提升导航成功率与条件检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离户外机器人导航不仅需避障，还需判断地形适宜性并决定前进、让行或绕行；现有VLM缺乏度量几何与运动信息，逐步提供证据过慢，手工触发器难以覆盖开放式场景。

Method: 提出MOSU2两阶段轨迹选择系统：第一阶段进行上下文推理，判断何时需要额外证据及检索内容；仅当交互未解决时触发第二阶段，检索几何与动态信息并与场景上下文对齐；基于GPS路线规划，经多模态可通行性分析筛选候选轨迹后选择运动轨迹。

Result: 在停止手势、施工现场、人行横道及与车辆、骑行者、行人共享道路的真实实验中，平均导航成功率由61.8%提升至94.2%，条件检测准确率由72.2%提升至91.4%，平均决策延迟约为前代的2.2倍。

Conclusion: 按需触发第二阶段证据检索的两阶段轨迹选择可有效提升长距离户外导航的鲁棒性，但以约2.2倍决策延迟为代价。

Abstract: Robots that travel long distances through public outdoor spaces must do more than avoid obstacles: they must remain on terrain suited to their mobility and decide when to proceed, yield, or detour around pedestrians, cyclists, vehicles, traffic signals, and temporary restrictions. These decisions are difficult because their correctness often depends on how a scene evolves, such as whether an approaching car is slowing or a cyclist will cross the robot's path. Vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret such cues, but they reason over images that lack metric geometry and motion. Supplying this evidence at every step is too slow for real-time navigation, and hand-crafted triggers cannot anticipate the open-ended variety of outdoor situations. We present MOSU2, a long-range navigation system with two-stage trajectory selection, in which first-stage contextual reasoning determines when additional evidence is needed and what to retrieve. Only when an interaction remains unresolved does the first stage trigger the second stage to retrieve geometric and dynamic information and align it with scene context to make navigation decisions. Building on MOSU, MOSU2 follows routes from GPS-based route planning and filters candidate trajectories through multi-modal traversability analysis before selecting a trajectory to move the robot. In real-world experiments spanning stop gestures, construction sites, crosswalks, and roads shared with vehicles, cyclists, and pedestrians, MOSU2 raises mean navigation success from 61.8% to 94.2% and condition-detection accuracy from 72.2% to 91.4% over its single-image predecessor, at about 2.2 times its mean decision latency. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [90] [From Festival Video to the Stage: A Sim-to-Real Ladder for Long-Horizon Expressive Whole-Body Dance on a Commercial Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11127)
*Hesham Shehata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将日本传统阿波舞从单目视频迁移至Unitree G1 EDU人形机器人的完整流水线，并在硬件上完成多轮部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将传统舞蹈动作从单目视频迁移到商用双足人形机器人，解决仿真训练到真机部署中的动作跟踪、状态估计漂移与部署工件损坏等实际问题。

Method: 流水线包含人体网格恢复、运动学重定向、强化学习仿真运动跟踪，以及通过ONNX的板载部署；训练使用单块RTX 5070 GPU，并在硬件上以11个版本的部署阶梯评估三类干预措施。

Result: 最终策略实现连续三次无障碍完成43秒动作、46秒自动交接表演，以及由采集动作组合的9.7分钟连续无障碍表演；现场交接中单次远程请求即可让机器人从厂商行走控制器启动并返回，测得无扭矩窗口45-140毫秒。

Conclusion: 验证了从单目视频到商用双足人形机器人舞蹈部署的可行性，并揭示了柔性地面与单目遮挡等失败边界。

Abstract: We present a pipeline for transferring Awa Odori, a traditional Japanese festival dance, from monocular video to a commercial Unitree G1 EDU humanoid. The pipeline combines human mesh recovery, kinematic retargeting, reinforcement learning for motion tracking in simulation, and onboard deployment via ONNX. Training ran on a single RTX 5070 GPU. An eleven-version deployment ladder evaluated on hardware examines three interventions. We detect and repair silent corruption of deployment artifacts, model state-estimator drift during training, and edit the reference motion to support stable execution and a controlled ending. The final policies achieved three consecutive rope-free completions of a 43-second routine, a 46-second performance with automatic handoff, and a continuous 9.7-minute rope-free performance composed from the captured motion. In a live handover, a single remote request let the robot start from and return to the manufacturer's walking controller on stage, with a measured torque-free window of 45-140 ms. We report the training dynamics and hardware outcomes and discuss failure boundaries involving compliant flooring and occlusion in monocular capture.

</details>


### [91] [HARP-Bench: Benchmarking Robot Manipulation Around Active Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11123)
*Qinhong Zhou,Riley Zong,Chunru Lin,Diyi Hu,Shivraj Singh Bhatti,Hongxin Zhang,Lin Qiu,Hanqing Zeng,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HARP-Bench 是一个将人类活动视为操作问题内在部分的机器人操作基准，覆盖提示、贡献、中断与共现四类人机共享工作空间条件，并揭示现有策略在人存在场景下的性能与安全性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准普遍假设机器人是唯一活跃主体、任务语义主要由指令和物体状态决定，忽视了真实世界中人类主动共享工作空间这一常见变化来源，人类可能隐含目标、完成部分任务、临时改变物体可用性或约束安全运动。

Method: 提出 HARP-Bench 基准，将人机共享操作组织为 Cueing、Contribution、Interruption、Co-presence 四种条件，并通过日常操作任务及与标准操作配对的人存在变体进行实例化；同时提供面向动态人存在场景的演示生成流水线，并评估 VLA 模型、模仿学习方法和启发式策略。

Result: 人存在变体对当前策略构成挑战，任务成功率更高并不稳定带来安全性提升；定量失败分析表明操作与恢复是主要剩余难点；使用 HARP-Bench 演示训练可提升仿真中的人存在性能；在单一任务族真实世界研究中，人类贡献条件下性能提升，但中断条件下未提升，显示数据效用与迁移局限。

Conclusion: HARP-Bench 将人类活动纳入操作问题建模，弥补现有基准假设的不足，并为评估和改进人机共享工作空间中的机器人策略提供了基准、数据生成方法与初步迁移证据。

Abstract: Recent robot manipulation benchmarks have enabled systematic evaluation of general-purpose robot policies across diverse objects, scenes, instructions, and embodiments. However, most existing benchmarks still assume that the robot is the only active agent and that task semantics are determined primarily by the instruction and object state. This assumption overlooks a common source of real-world variation: humans actively sharing the workspace. In such settings, humans may reveal an implicit goal, complete part of the task, temporarily change object availability, or constrain safe robot motion while working nearby. We introduce HARP-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation policies in workspaces shared with active humans. HARP-Bench treats human activity as part of the manipulation problem, rather than as background scene variation. It organizes human-present manipulation into four conditions: Cueing, Contribution, Interruption, and Co-presence. These conditions are instantiated through everyday manipulation tasks, including paired human-present variants for controlled comparison with standard manipulation. HARP-Bench also provides a demonstration-generation pipeline for dynamic human-present settings. We evaluate VLA models, imitation-learning methods, and heuristic policies on HARP-Bench. Human-present variations challenge current policies, and higher task success does not consistently improve safety. Quantitative failure analysis identifies manipulation and recovery as the dominant remaining challenges. Training with HARP-Bench demonstrations improves human-present performance in simulation. A real-world study on one task family shows improved performance under human contribution, but not interruption, providing initial evidence of the data's utility and identifying limits of transfer. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [92] [Large-Scale Hierarchical Decision-Making in Robot Control via ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11090)
*Kai Pfeiffer,Jia Wang,Gordon Owusu Boateng,Yuquan Wang,Vincent Bonnet,Abderrahmane Kheddar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于ℓ0范数的稀疏分层非线性规划（SH-NLP）在机器人运动控制中的应用，提出用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）高效求解SHQP子问题，并引入辅助变量替代ℓ0范数代理项，在低至中等精度下相比内点法计算时间减少最多23.5%，并将分层决策扩展到人形机器人行走的全身动态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于内点法的顺序稀疏分层二次规划（S-SHQP）虽能利用分层稀疏结构，但难以在连续控制步之间有效热启动，限制了计算效率。

Method: 构建ADMM求解SHQP子问题，为表示ℓ0范数代理项的辅助变量引入高效替代，并按合适顺序评估线性代数表达式以处理可分离决策函数。

Result: 在大规模运动学与动态控制中，相比利用结构的内点法，在低至中等精度区计算时间最多减少23.5%；同时将分层决策扩展到人形机器人行走的全身动态控制，并强调高精度解的需求。

Conclusion: ADMM方法可有效求解SHQP子问题并提升低至中等精度下的计算效率，但全身动态控制等场景仍需高精度求解，需进一步权衡精度与效率。

Abstract: Sparse hierarchical nonlinear programming (SH-NLP) based on the $\ell_0$-norm provides a powerful framework for integrated, efficient hierarchical decision-making in robot kinematic control, for example in the presence of a large set of possible end-effector candidate locations. While Sequential Sparse Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (S-SHQP) with interior-point method based SHQP sub-problem solvers exploit the hierarchical, sparse structure of such problems, they are less amenable to effective warm-starting between subsequent, coherent control steps. In this work, we formulate an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to efficiently solve related SHQP sub-problems. We introduce efficient substitutions for auxiliary variables representing $\ell_0$-norm surrogates. The solver efficiently handles separable decision functions by evaluating linear algebra expressions in the appropriate order. Results in large-scale kinematic and dynamic control show reduced computation times of up to 23.5% compared with a structure-exploiting interior-point method in low- to moderate-accuracy regimes. Furthermore, hierarchical decision-making is extended to whole-body dynamic control for humanoid walking, where the need for high-accuracy solutions is highlighted.

</details>


### [93] [Control-Aware Command Generation for Asynchronous Control and Action Inference with Vision-Based Policies towards Fast Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11089)
*Takayuki Osa,Kazuki Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异步推理与控制框架，将不连续模型预测生成连续目标位置建模为跟踪控制问题，并结合自适应动作跳过，在不修改策略学习的前提下加速视觉控制策略执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉控制策略（含视觉-语言-动作模型）因动作推理计算开销大而执行速度受限；本文希望仅从控制层面、不改变策略学习过程来提升执行速度。

Method: 构建异步推理与控制框架，将不连续模型预测生成连续目标位置表述为跟踪控制问题，使异步推理适用于真实机器人；并基于预测位置方差实现自适应动作跳过以进一步加速。

Result: 在双臂机器人上，使用Action Chunking Transformer与OpenVLA-OFT进行实验，实现显著加速，同时保持高任务成功率与平滑稳定的机器人运动。

Conclusion: 仅通过控制层面的异步推理、跟踪控制与自适应动作跳过即可在保持任务成功率的同时大幅提升视觉控制策略的执行速度。

Abstract: Vision-based control policies, including vision-language-action models, have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex tasks. However, their execution speed is often limited by the high computational cost of action inference. In this work, we investigate what can be achieved at the control level without modifying the underlying policy learning process. We introduce an asynchronous inference and control framework that enables fast task execution while maintaining smooth and stable robot motions. By formulating the generation of continuous target positions from discontinuous model predictions as a tracking control problem, our method makes asynchronous inference practical for real robots. We further accelerate execution through adaptive action skipping based on the variance of predicted positions. Experiments with Action Chunking Transformer and OpenVLA-OFT on a bimanual robot demonstrate substantial speedups while preserving high task success rates.

</details>


### [94] [ObjectWorld: Scaling Object-Centric World Models with A Large-Scale 3D Full-Object Flow Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11085)
*Haonan Chen,Sicheng Isabella Lu,Haitian Li,Jiajin Wu,Hexiang Wei,Jiawei Zhang,Xin Fei,Yiwen Hou,Chenrui Tie,Chongkai Gao,Sheng Wang,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出3D全物体流表示及1210小时数据集，训练世界模型预测完整物体三维运动，用于可扩展机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉数据的世界建模有望实现可扩展机器人操作，但需要一种可大规模监督且对下游控制可操作的中间表示。

Method: 定义3D全物体流，为目标物体完整表面（含遮挡区域）分配稠密3D轨迹，统一适用于刚体和柔体；构建1210小时全物体流数据集，整合真实视频与物理仿真，并提供可扩展标注流程和质量度量；训练以图像和任务描述为输入、在重建物体几何上预测稠密全物体流的世界模型。

Result: 实验表明标注质量可靠、流预测可随数据规模扩展，并在新场景和新机器人本体上实现有效的下游操作。

Conclusion: 3D全物体流是一种可大规模监督且面向控制的物体运动表示，配合大规模数据集可支撑可扩展的机器人操作世界建模。

Abstract: Generative world modeling from large-scale visual data offers a promising path toward scalable robot manipulation. This calls for an intermediate representation that can be supervised at scale and remains actionable for downstream control. We propose 3D full-object flow, an object-centric motion representation that assigns dense 3D trajectories to points across the complete surface of a target object, including its occluded regions. By extending visible-surface object flow to the full object geometry, this representation makes complete object motion explicit and applies uniformly to both rigid and deformable objects. To realize the potential of this representation at scale, we introduce a 1210-hour full-object flow dataset that addresses the scarcity of large-scale supervision for complete 3D object motion. The dataset integrates diverse real-world videos and physical simulations, with a scalable annotation pipeline for recovering full-object motion from real-world observations and quality metrics for systematically assessing annotation quality. We then train a world model that takes an image and a task description as input and predicts dense full-object flow over the reconstructed object geometry. Experiments show reliable annotation quality, data-scalable flow prediction, and effective downstream manipulation across novel scenes and robot embodiments. Project website with additional results and videos: this https URL .

</details>


### [95] [Floquet-Parameterized Koopman-MPC for Tracking Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11050)
*Kartik A. Pant,Vishnu Vijay,Yufei Zhu,Xintong Deng,Taewoo Nam,Inseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种物理信息Koopman学习方法，将周期轨道稳定性直接融入学习的线性预测器，用于非线性系统的周期轨道跟踪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为复杂耦合非线性系统（如系留风筝）设计实时周期轨道跟踪控制面临挑战，因为真实模型参数难以获取。

Method: 结合动力学的周期结构与周期状态-输入轨迹数据，利用Floquet理论通过单值矩阵获取周期稳定性信息，在最小二乘EDMD框架中施加开环单值约束，学习Floquet参数化的线性预测器，并基于此设计MPC律跟踪周期参考。

Result: 在周期强迫的cartpole系统上评估所设计框架，并与纯数据驱动基线进行比较。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过约束搜索空间有助于Koopman学习，能够为周期轨道跟踪提供有效的模型预测控制方案。

Abstract: Designing real-time control for nonlinear systems to track periodic orbits can be challenging, especially for complex and coupled dynamical systems (e.g., tethered kites), where the true model parameters are hard to obtain. To address this, we propose a physics-informed Koopman learning approach that incorporates periodic-orbit stability directly into the learned linear predictor. The approach combines the underlying periodic structure of the dynamics along with periodic state-input trajectory data to learn physically consistent linear predictors (in lifted/observable space). We leverage Floquet theory, which studies the stability of periodic orbits in nonlinear systems, to obtain the periodic stability information of a given orbit via a monodromy matrix (i.e., the state transition matrix over an entire period). Subsequently, we impose open-loop monodromy constraints to learn Floquet-parameterized linear predictors using a least-squares Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) formulation, thereby constraining the search space and aiding in Koopman learning. We then utilize these linear predictors to design a model predictive control (MPC) law for tracking periodic references. Finally, we evaluate the designed framework on a cartpole system under periodic forcing and compare it against a purely data-driven baseline.

</details>


### [96] [RoboMoCap: A Large-Scale Whole-Body Motion Capture Dataset with Egocentric Vision for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10994)
*Mayur Deshmukh,Aaron Tung,Junyi Li,Nikash Bhardwaj,Ademi Adeniji,Vincent Liu,Michael Cho,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboMoCap是一个大规模多模态数据集，结合第一人称视觉与光学动捕，专为以运动为核心的人形机器人学习设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需协调运动与操作，从人类演示学习需准确全身运动与第一人称视觉，尤其下半身与足部动作常不可见，但现有第一人称数据集缺乏高保真光学动捕及多样化运动-操作行为覆盖。

Method: 构建RoboMoCap数据集，含14名参与者超200小时记录，配备6个头部相机、腕部视觉与惯性传感、120Hz全身光学动捕，系统覆盖多样运动基元、动态全身运动及运动-操作任务，提供时间对齐多模态观测与度量全身运动表示。

Result: 在三个基准任务上系统评估现有方法：第一人称全身运动重建、足部接触与落点估计、人到人形机器人的运动重定向。

Conclusion: 通过将第一人称视觉与高保真人类运动配对，RoboMoCap为第一人称运动理解及从人类演示进行人形机器人学习提供了基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots must coordinate locomotion and manipulation to navigate complex environments and perform everyday tasks. Learning such behaviours from human demonstrations requires accurate whole-body motion paired with egocentric vision, particularly for lower-body movements and foot placements that are frequently invisible from a first-person perspective. However, existing egocentric datasets often lack high-fidelity optical motion capture or systematic coverage of diverse loco-manipulation behaviours. To address this gap, we introduce RoboMoCap, a large-scale multimodal dataset combining egocentric vision with marker-based optical motion capture, specifically designed for locomotion-centric humanoid learning. RoboMoCap comprises over 200 hours of recordings from 14 participants, featuring six head-mounted cameras, wrist-mounted visual and inertial sensing, and optical full-body motion capture at 120 Hz. The dataset systematically covers diverse locomotion primitives, dynamic whole-body movements, and loco-manipulation tasks, including variable-speed walking, turning, constrained navigation, terrain traversal, object transportation, and manipulation during locomotion. It provides temporally aligned multimodal observations and metric whole-body motion representations, enabling the study of human movement beyond directly observable body regions. We systematically evaluate existing methods on three benchmark tasks: egocentric full-body motion reconstruction, foot contact and placement estimation, and human-to-humanoid motion retargeting. By pairing egocentric vision with high-fidelity human motion across diverse locomotion and interaction scenarios, RoboMoCap provides a foundation for egocentric motion understanding and humanoid learning from human demonstrations.

</details>


### [97] [BAT-WAM: Bridging Action and Touch in World-Action Models for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10967)
*Dong Fang,Feixuan Huang,Yuanxin Zhong,Yuxiang Gao,Qi Song,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BAT-WAM框架，通过视觉、关节状态与触觉表征的联合建模，在缺失模态下重建和预测触觉信息，并提升灵巧操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）依赖手部配置与接触信息，但关节状态和全手触觉观测可能部分或完全缺失；RGB图像虽提供场景与手物几何信息，却不足以可靠恢复缺失模态。作者观察到手部状态与触觉信息之间存在相关性，因此希望桥接视觉、关节状态与触觉表征。

Method: BAT-WAM为触觉流和动作流分别学习独立的码本与解码器；利用RGB条件化的连续潜在桥连接两个流以进行双向学习，并以视觉信息作为连续上下文。

Result: 在HTBench协议下，完全掩码条件下触觉重建RMSE相对图像条件化的HandTouch检查点降低34.73%，部分掩码条件下降低38.84%；以目标时刻RGB和历史动作为条件的未来触觉预测中，平均RMSE相对OpenWAM with HandTouch降低85.56%。真实机器人实验显示BAT-WAM持续优于相关WAM基线，且在未见物体与场景上的泛化实验也显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 联合建模视觉上下文、手部状态与触觉可作为多模态WAMs的一种有效表征方法。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with world action models (WAMs) relies on hand configuration and contact information, yet joint-state and whole-hand tactile observations may be partially or entirely missing. RGB images provide scene context and hand-object geometry but are insufficient to reliably recover these missing modalities. We further observe a correlation between hand state and tactile information. Motivated by this observation, we introduce BAT-WAM, a framework that bridges visual, joint-state, and tactile representations for WAMs. BAT-WAM learns separate codebooks and decoders for the tactile and action streams. An RGB-conditioned continuous latent bridge connects the two streams for bidirectional learning, while visual information serves as continuous context. Under the HTBench evaluation protocol, BAT-WAM reduces the root mean squared error (RMSE) of tactile reconstruction by 34.73% under fully masked conditions and 38.84% under partially masked conditions, relative to an image-conditioned HandTouch checkpoint. For future tactile prediction conditioned on target-time RGB and historical actions, it reduces mean RMSE by 85.56% relative to OpenWAM with HandTouch. Real-robot experiments further show that BAT-WAM consistently outperforms related WAM baselines in dexterous manipulation. Generalization experiments on unseen objects and scenes also demonstrate substantial improvements over baseline models. These findings support joint modeling of visual context, hand state, and touch as a representation approach for multimodal WAMs.

</details>


### [98] [Explicitly capturing demonstrator multi-modality for distillation into diffusion policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10949)
*Shao Yuan Chew Chia,Rebecca H. Jiang,Abhinav Agarwal,Andreas Hofmann,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现蒸馏扩散策略的多样性对测试时搜索和策略改进等下游应用至关重要，提出通过vine采样在相同观测下多次查询示范者，使策略更好复现示范者的条件动作分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆数据收集使用滑动窗口，将独特观测历史与动作配对，导致数据集既可由多模态策略拟合，也可由高Lipschitz常数的单模态策略拟合；先前工作发现稀疏数据训练的扩散策略会记忆每个观测的一个动作，无法保留示范者的多模态和多样性。

Method: 将vine采样适配到行为克隆数据收集：在示范者访问的某些状态上，从完全相同的观测查询示范者k次，得到每个观测的多个动作；在合成任务上研究k对学习策略的影响，并在2D卡车卸货任务上验证。

Result: 在合成任务上，较大的k使策略更好地匹配示范者的条件分布，包括其多模态性和多样性；在2D卡车卸货任务上，vine采样使策略更接近示范者，并显著提升best-of-N和专家迭代性能。

Conclusion: 通过vine采样增加每个观测的动作样本数，可消除行为克隆数据中的歧义，使蒸馏扩散策略复现示范者的多模态条件动作分布，从而支持测试时搜索和策略改进等需要探索的下游应用。

Abstract: The diversity of a distilled diffusion policy's outputs matters beyond how it impacts the policy's success rate. In particular, downstream applications like test-time search and policy improvement require exploration. Thus, we want to distill multi-modal demonstrators into diffusion policies which reproduce their conditional action distributions. Standard data collection for behavior cloning applies a sliding window to demonstration episodes, which pairs unique observation histories with actions. These datasets can be fit by a policy that is either multi-modal, or unimodal with high Lipschitz constant to capture a diversity of actions seen at nearby observation samples. Prior work finds that diffusion policies trained on sparse data learn the latter, memorizing one action per observation. We adapt vine sampling to behavior cloning data collection to resolve this ambiguity: at some states the demonstrator visits, query it $k$ times from exactly the same observation. On a synthetic task, we study how the number of actions per observation, $k$, affects the learned policy, and find that larger $k$ yields a policy that better matches the demonstrator's conditional distribution, including its multi-modality and diversity. On a 2D truck unloading task, vine sampling likewise leads to a policy closer to the demonstrator and substantially improves both best-of-$N$ and expert iteration performance.

</details>


### [99] [BRACE: Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control for Safe Navigation under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10938)
*Jaeyoun Choi,Niklas Schmid,Oswin So,Augustinos Saravanos,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BRACE是一种分层强化学习框架，通过从预测误差推断扰动信念并据此调整参考轨迹，实现未知有界空间变化扰动下的到达-规避导航，在仿真和Crazyflie硬件上优于MPC和RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航中的扰动常随空间变化且可能不连续，而现有跟踪控制器通常假设有足够控制权限来抑制扰动并保持安全；该假设失效的情形研究不足。

Method: 提出BRACE：从模型预测误差推断扰动信念；用该信念条件化高层策略以自适应调整参考轨迹；策略由名义项和信念引导的残差项组成，并用似然比优势重加权启发式联合训练。

Result: 在三个具有突变风场的四旋翼仿真环境中评估，并在Crazyflie硬件上用风扇生成扰动验证；BRACE在到达-规避性能上优于模型预测控制和RL基线。

Conclusion: 利用扰动信念引导残差自适应控制可有效应对未知有界空间变化扰动，提升到达-规避导航的安全性与性能。

Abstract: Disturbances encountered during autonomous navigation generally vary across space, often discontinuously, as obstacles redirect wind and changing terrain alters traction. Tracking controllers typically assume sufficient control authority to mitigate disturbances and maintain safety; the regime where this assumption fails remains underexplored. We present Belief-guided Residual Adaptive Control (BRACE), a hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework for reach-avoid navigation under unknown, bounded, spatially varying disturbances. BRACE infers a disturbance belief from model prediction errors and uses that to condition a high-level policy that adapts the reference trajectory. The policy combines a nominal and a belief-guided residual term, trained jointly with a likelihood-ratio advantage reweighting heuristic. We evaluate BRACE in three simulated quadcopter environments with abruptly switching wind fields, and validate it on Crazyflie hardware with fan-generated disturbances. Across these settings, BRACE improves reach-avoid performance over model predictive control and RL baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Zero-Shot VLA Adaptation with Retrieved Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10894)
*Subin Kim,Anh-Quan Pham,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGu 将检索增强生成引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过从域不匹配的野外演示数据中检索并引导下游动作生成，显著提升机器人在域迁移下的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模预训练 VLA 模型在相机视角、背景、物体身份或机器人形态变化等小域偏移下仍表现脆弱；受语言模型 RAG 成功启发，作者希望利用 DROID 等野外演示数据来指导目标域中的 VLA 行为。

Method: 提出 RAGu，解决机器人 RAG 的特有挑战：（1）用简单几何标定把源演示变换到目标设置以缩小跨域差异；（2）采用由粗到细的检索策略，从大量预变换候选轨迹中选出与基础 VLA 最兼容的一条；（3）在 flow-matching 推理过程中施加引导，而非重训模型或扩展有限上下文窗口。

Result: 在真实世界、仿真、sim-to-real 迁移和跨形态人形操作中评估，RAGu 将真实世界 VLA 成功率从 20% 提升到 80%，并在仿真中优于最接近的基线方法。

Conclusion: 检索增强是提升 VLA 在域偏移下鲁棒性的有效途径，RAGu 通过几何标定、粗到细检索和推理期引导实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Pre-training large parametric "Vision-Language-Action" (VLA) models on large demonstration datasets has recently yielded promising results for general-purpose manipulation. However, these models still struggle under even small domain shifts relative to the training data, such as changes in camera viewpoint, background, object identity, or robot morphology. Motivated by the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in language models, we pursue retrieval to boost VLA performance. Specifically, we seek to leverage retrieval from domain-mismatched, "in-the-wild" demonstration datasets like DROID to guide a VLA's behavior in a new target domain. To achieve this, we introduce RAGu, which overcomes robotics-specific challenges for RAG: (1) It incorporates simple geometric calibration to transform source demonstrations into the target setup and reduce cross-domain differences. (2) It uses a coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy that selects, from a large pool of pre-warped candidates, the trajectory most compatible with the base VLA. (3) It guides the VLA's flow-matching inference procedure rather than seeking to retrain the base model or expand its limited context window. We evaluate RAGu across real-world, simulation, sim-to-real transfer, and cross-embodiment humanoid manipulation. We find it improves real-world VLA success rates from 20% to 80% and outperforms the closest-related baselines in sim. Project website: this http URL

</details>


### [101] [Sampling-based Model Predictive Control as Sequential Monte Carlo Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10877)
*Austin Garrett,Muyang Yan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于采样的模型预测控制（包括预测采样、MPPI、交叉熵方法及其变体）可统一为一种序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）推理算法，由提议分布、更新规则和重采样阈值三个独立维度指定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的MPC算法（预测采样、MPPI、交叉熵方法及其偏置、有色噪声、退火变体）被视为彼此不同的算法，缺乏统一框架；本文旨在证明它们可由单一SMC推理算法实现。

Method: 提出用单一序贯蒙特卡洛推理算法统一实现上述算法，通过三个独立轴参数化：提议分布、将代价加权粒子缩减为名义计划的更新规则、以及用于重采样的有效样本量阈值；在MuJoCo非线性控制、障碍场开环导航、7自由度运动规划和非抓取推T任务上（仿真及Franka FR3硬件）进行评估。

Result: 重采样在长时域上缓解粒子退化，在导航域28节时域下碰撞率较非重采样基线降低30%；可编程提议分布在推T任务上优于扰动基线，仿真成功率从1%提升至100%，硬件上从10%提升至90%。

Conclusion: 基于SMC的规划为多种采样MPC算法提供了统一框架，重采样和可编程提议分布分别显著改善长时域粒子退化和困难任务成功率。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) is treated as a family of distinct algorithms: predictive sampling, MPPI, the cross-entropy method, and their biased, colored-noise, and annealed variants all sample control sequences and reweight them by cost. We show a single Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) inference algorithm implements them all, specified by three independent axes: the proposal, the update rule that reduces cost-weighted particles to a nominal plan, and the effective-sample-size threshold for resampling. We evaluate SMC-based planning on MuJoCo non-linear control, open-loop navigation through obstacle fields, 7-DoF motion planning, and non-prehensile push-T, in simulation and on a Franka FR3. Resampling combats particle degeneracy at long horizons. At a 28-knot horizon in our navigation domain it cut the collision rate by 30% over non-resampling baselines. Programmable proposals improve push-T success over perturbative baselines, improving from 1% to 100% in simulation and 10% to 90% on hardware.

</details>


### [102] [DreamVLA: From Generated Human Motions to Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10872)
*Kevin Park,Izat Rasyad,Dvij Kalaria,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamVLA用生成的运动轨迹引导仿真强化学习，通过参考条件DAgger蒸馏将纠错轨迹聚合，从而在无需新遥操作数据的情况下训练视觉-语言-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练VLA策略依赖大量机器人示教数据，人工遥操作成本高且繁琐，全身人形交互尤为困难；仿真强化学习可扩展数据，但设计任务特定奖励仍是挑战。

Method: 提出DreamVLA：以生成的运动轨迹作为参考引导仿真RL，将其转化为可靠的任务执行轨迹；核心是参考条件DAgger蒸馏——失败的学习者 rollout 后，从采样的学习者状态以相同参考后缀重启RL专家，聚合成功的纠错轨迹，以缓解离线模仿造成的分布偏移。

Result: 在从平台抓取、打开抽屉、按按钮、行走并从平台抓取等任务上评估，并对DAgger迭代次数、残差RL和生成数据集规模进行了消融实验。

Conclusion: 该方法无需新的任务特定遥操作数据即可在仿真中训练VLA模型，生成的参考轨迹结合DAgger蒸馏能有效纠正分布偏移并支持多类全身交互任务。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) policies typically depends on substantial robot demonstration data, making human teleoperation a costly and cumbersome bottleneck, particularly for whole-body humanoid interaction. Reinforcement learning in simulation offers an alternative for scaling this data, but designing task-specific rewards remains challenging. To address this, we propose DreamVLA, which uses generated motion trajectories to guide reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation, converting them into reliable task execution trajectories. These trajectories train VLA models in simulation without new task-specific teleoperation data. Our central contribution is a reference-conditioned DAgger distillation procedure: after a failed learner rollout, we restart the RL expert from a sampled learner state with the same reference suffix and aggregate successful corrective trajectories, addressing the distribution shift left by offline imitation. We evaluate picking from a platform, opening a drawer, pressing a button, and walking and picking from a platform, with ablations of DAgger iterations, residual RL, and generated dataset scale. Project website: this http URL .

</details>


### [103] [MoE-DAgger: Transition-Aware Router-Entropy Gating for Selective Human Correction in Phase-Structured Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10825)
*Sungjun Heo,Sungjun Yang,Heedon Jeong,Junhyoung Ha,Jeong hwan Jeon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoE-DAgger利用因果路由历史区分正常相邻阶段切换与异常高熵，从而在DAgger中选择性请求人工纠正，显著提升操作任务成功率与失败样本筛选精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有有序阶段的机器人操作中，仅靠阶段监督软混合专家（MoE）的路由熵无法判断高熵是正常相邻阶段交接还是非相邻/早期阶段支持，导致DAgger数据采集的干预决策不准确。

Method: 提出MoE-DAgger：不向策略提供阶段标签、进度变量或硬阶段命令，仅用因果路由历史判断；采集规则允许相邻切换自行稳定，但在非切换期高熵持续、切换超时或路由持续偏向早期阶段时请求纠正。

Result: 在匹配主动人工控制预算下，Green任务成功率84.0%、Red任务78.0%，分别比相位无关过滤熵高22和26个百分点，每任务50次 rollout 无碰撞；每任务50条共享无干预轨迹中，选15和25条时首提议落在失败窗口内的比例为83.3%和80.0%，高于时序动作块分歧（70.0%、72.0%）和集成分歧（63.3%、60.0%）。

Conclusion: 在正常相邻阶段交接处抑制查询是改进数据分配的关键因素，MoE-DAgger能更有效地选择需要纠正的失败 rollout。

Abstract: Selective data acquisition in Dataset Aggregation (DAgger) on robots requires deciding which learner rollouts to correct and when to intervene. In manipulation with ordered phases, router entropy from a phase-supervised soft mixture-of-experts (MoE) is insufficient: the same value can reflect either a normal adjacent-phase handoff or support for nonadjacent or earlier phases. We propose MoE-DAgger, which uses causal router history to distinguish these cases without providing the policy with a phase label, progress variable, or hard phase command during execution. The acquisition rule allows an adjacent transition to settle, but requests correction when high entropy persists outside such a transition, the transition times out, or routing persistently favors an earlier phase. Under matched active human-control budgets, MoE-DAgger achieves 84.0% task success on Green and 78.0% on Red, exceeding phase-unaware filtered entropy by 22 percentage points on Green and 26 points on Red, with no observed collisions in 50 rollouts per task. On 50 shared intervention-free traces per task, 83.3% and 80.0% of its first proposals fall on failed rollouts within predefined failure windows when 15 and 25 traces are selected, compared with 70.0% and 72.0% for temporal action-chunk disagreement and 63.3% and 60.0% for ensemble disagreement. Shadow replay and an active no-veto ablation indicate that suppressing queries at normal adjacent handoffs is a key contributor to the improved allocation.

</details>


### [104] [Tactile Sensors Are an Effective Substitute for Wrist Cameras in Contact-Rich or Occluded Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10490)
*Aijia Yang,Arsh Tangri,Sergey Zakharov,Krishna Murthy Jatavallabhula*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在视触觉ACT策略中用低成本指尖触觉阵列替代腕部相机，在接触密集型任务中取得更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部相机可提供近距离视觉反馈，但接触密集型操作中的关键接触常被遮挡，即使腕部相机也无法观察；指尖触觉传感器可直接、可靠地测量接触，无需视觉通路。

Method: 将两个低成本触觉阵列集成到视触觉ACT策略中，训练与部署均不使用腕部图像；在六类真实任务（放置、插入、遮挡取物、持续接触工具使用）上评估，并在三个接触密集型任务中固定演示、策略架构与优化设置，分别改变腕部相机与触觉的可用性进行对比。

Result: 加入腕部相机相比无腕部视觉提升成功率18.3–28.3个百分点；加入触觉传感提升31.7–60.0个百分点，且在三个任务上均优于带腕部相机的纯视觉策略，其中两项在Holm校正检验下优势显著。

Conclusion: 在所评估的接触密集型场景中，指尖触觉传感可作为腕部相机的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies often rely on wrist-mounted cameras for close-range visual feedback that complements external views of the workspace. However, contact-rich manipulation requires establishing and maintaining contacts that can remain hidden even from wrist cameras. Tactile sensors mounted on fingers can reliably and directly measure contact, without needing visual access. In this work, we show that this feedback enables policies to recover the performance advantage of wrist cameras using scene and egocentric vision together with touch. Our approach incorporates two low-cost tactile arrays into a visuotactile ACT policy, without wrist images during training or deployment. We evaluate six real-world task families spanning placement, insertion, occluded retrieval, and sustained-contact tool use. On three contact-rich tasks, we independently vary wrist-camera and tactile availability while keeping the demonstrations, policy architecture, and optimization settings fixed. Adding wrist cameras improves success by 18.3-28.3 percentage points over vision without wrist images. Adding tactile sensing instead improves success by 31.7-60.0 percentage points and yields higher observed success than vision-only policies with wrist cameras on all three tasks, with significant advantages on two under Holm-corrected tests. These results support finger-mounted tactile sensing as an effective alternative to wrist cameras in the evaluated contact-rich settings.

</details>


### [105] [Grip, Trace, Write: Force-Aware Imitation and Reinforcement Learning for High-DoF Robotic Handwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10451)
*Rongqian Chen,Yiding Chen,Jijie Zhou,Tom Z. Jiahao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Grip, Trace, Write力感知仿真环境与学习框架，实现高自由度自由持笔书写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人书写刚性固定笔，将书写简化为轨迹跟踪；自由持笔需同时耦合抓握稳定性、笔尖运动与接触力。

Method: 构建双分支actor将持笔与臂腕书写分离并保持闭环耦合；先训练抓握控制器，用逆运动学演示行为克隆初始化书写，再通过奖励墨迹、力、时间和保持度的强化学习改进书写。

Result: 在多种任务上，完成度、精度、力调节和效率均优于行为克隆与逆运动学，且无需微调即可泛化到未见英文和中文内容。

Conclusion: 力感知书写可作为灵巧工具使用的测试平台。

Abstract: Most robotic handwriting rigidly mounts the pen and reduces writing to trajectory tracking. A freely held pen instead couples grasp stability, tip motion, and contact force. We present Grip, Trace, Write, a force-aware simulation environment and learning framework for high-DoF handwriting. A two-branch actor separates pen retention from arm-wrist writing while preserving their closed-loop coupling. Training learns a grasp controller, initializes writing from inverse-kinematics demonstrations by behavior cloning, and improves writing through reinforcement learning with rewards on ink, force, time, and retention. Across diverse tasks, the policy improves completion, precision, force regulation, and efficiency over behavior cloning and inverse kinematics, and generalizes without fine-tuning to unseen English and Chinese content. These results establish force-aware handwriting as a testbed for dexterous tool use.

</details>


### [106] [Spatial Representation Distillation and Knowledge Routing for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10443)
*Jungin Park,Chaoran Zhu,Changjae Oh,Kwanghoon Sohn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARK-VLA 提出 preserve-specialize-route 框架，通过空间表征蒸馏与知识路由，使 VLA 无需推理时 3D 输入即可获得空间感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 获取三维结构信息要么依赖推理时的显式几何输入，要么只用单一视觉通路，可能损害预训练模型继承的视觉-语义知识。

Method: 包含两部分：(1) 空间表征蒸馏，将冻结 3D 专家的三维理解通过注意力响应对齐迁移到 LoRA 适配的视觉分支，同时保留原始视觉通路；(2) 轻量知识路由器，基于动作损失梯度得到的路由监督，有选择地把动作相关视觉 token 送入语言模型。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN 及真实机器人操作任务上表现强劲，仅用 0.5B 语言主干和约 53M 可训练参数。

Conclusion: SPARK-VLA 提供了一种有效且参数高效的三维感知 VLA 学习方案，推理时无需外部 3D 输入或专家模型。

Abstract: Understanding three-dimensional physical structure has become essential for vision-language-action models (VLAs). However, existing approaches either rely on explicit geometric inputs at inference time or adopt a single visual pathway, potentially compromising the visual-semantic knowledge inherited from pre-trained models. We present SPARK-VLA, Spatial Representation distillation and Knowledge routing for VLAs, a preserve-specialize-route framework for spatially adapting VLAs without requiring external 3D inputs or experts at inference. SPARK-VLA consists of two key components: (1) spatial representation distillation, which transfers 3D understanding from a frozen 3D expert to a LoRA-adapted visual branch by aligning attention responses while preserving the original visual pathway, and (2) a lightweight knowledge router, which selectively forwards action-relevant visual tokens to the language model using routing supervision derived from action-loss gradients. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN, and real-world manipulation, SPARK-VLA achieves strong performance with only a 0.5B language backbone and approximately 53M trainable parameters, providing an effective and parameter-efficient approach to 3D-aware VLA learning. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Objective Adaptation After Deployment: A Position on World Models for Remote Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10435)
*Jamie Santos,Kagan Tumer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对远程非结构化环境下自主机器人团队部署后环境、硬件和团队组成变化导致原策略失效的问题，提出使用世界模型进行部署后多目标自适应，通过预测策略结果来重新评估已有策略而无需物理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人团队在远程非结构化环境中部署时，环境、硬件和团队组成的变化会降低原有策略的有效性；任务目标变化可从现有Pareto集选择不同解，但部署条件变化会重排Pareto集，使原本被支配的策略变得有竞争力或需要新策略，而在新情境下评估这些策略并最小化硬件风险仍是未解决问题。

Method: 论证世界模型在该领域虽基本未经测试但值得研究用于部署后多目标自适应；利用世界模型在当前条件更新估计下预测策略结果，使团队无需物理执行每个候选策略即可重新评估现有策略，前提是预测的性能变化能反映部署系统上实际实现的变化；并提出基于模型的策略重评估、预测性能与实际性能对比验证、以及将共享信息融入世界模型以支持团队协同自适应等研究重点。

Result: 论文为立场性/研究议程类工作，未报告实验或定量结果，主要识别了模型化策略重评估、预测与实际性能验证、共享信息融合三项研究优先方向。

Conclusion: 尽管世界模型在部署后多目标自适应方面尚缺乏充分验证，但其通过预测策略结果实现无需物理执行的风险可控重评估，有望支持机器人团队在部署条件变化时进行协同自适应，相关研究优先级应聚焦于预测准确性验证与共享信息融合。

Abstract: Autonomous robot teams deployed in remote, unstructured environments may face changes in the environment, hardware, and team composition that degrade previously effective policies. Shifting mission objectives can be handled by selecting a different solution from an existing Pareto set, but shifting deployment conditions can reorder the set itself, making previously dominated policies competitive or new policies necessary. However, evaluating these policies in new contexts while minimizing risk to the hardware remains an open problem. We argue that, although largely untested in this domain, world models merit investigation for post-deployment multi-objective adaptation. By predicting policy outcomes under updated estimates of current conditions, world models could enable teams to re-evaluate existing policies without physically executing each candidate, provided predicted performance changes reflect those realized on deployed systems. We identify research priorities for model-based policy re-evaluation, validation of predicted against realized performance, and incorporating shared information into world models to support coordinated team adaptation.

</details>


### [108] [Deploying Physically and Epistemically Compliant Agents by Distilling Belief-Space Task and Motion Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10430)
*Phillip J.K. Christoffersen,Rachel Ma,Patrick Barragán,Dylan Hadfield-Menell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将信念空间规划器离线生成的数据蒸馏为基于原始观测的策略，并引入两个改进：Dual Averaging损失和ρ-Optimal Reinforcement反馈机制，在Scavenger任务中成功率超90%（RL仅5%），且对未见指令和更大场景有良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习理论上可通过设计奖励来训练智能体遵循规则，但实际中RL无法完美优化奖励，导致行为无效或不符规范；而信念空间规划器虽能保证行为符合开发者规范，却需要手工编程在线规划器和信念状态估计器，工程复杂度高。

Method: 结合二者：离线规划生成样本轨迹数据集，然后将规划蒸馏为作用于原始观测的策略。针对基于历史的策略提出两个改进：Dual Averaging（结合每时间步预测损失和任务变化平均损失的损失函数）和ρ-Optimal Reinforcement（当策略自身成功轨迹接近最优时进行强化的反馈机制）。此外，给出静态搜索问题在无噪声观测模型下完备性的充分条件，并证明在连续信念的静态搜索中最大似然观测规划蕴含该条件。创建新领域Scavenger（智能体需获取信息才能成功），评估视觉和自然语言命令两种信息提供方式下的蒸馏策略。

Result: 在Scavenger两种版本中，学习策略成功率均超过90%，而RL最多5%；蒸馏规划器对未见且故意模糊的命令迁移成功率超过60%，对大于训练中见过的平面图成功率超过75%。

Conclusion: 规划器蒸馏方法能有效训练智能体在不确定性下遵循信念相关规则，显著优于RL，并展现出对未见指令和更大场景的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work, we train agents to act under uncertainty according to specified, belief-dependent rules. Deep reinforcement learning (RL) can in theory train agents to follow rules if encoded into well-designed rewards, but in practice, RL fails to perfectly optimize these rewards, leading to ineffective or noncompliant agent behavior. Belief-space planners by construction produce behavior in line with developer specification, but require hand-programming online planners and belief-state estimators, adding significant engineering complexity at scale. We combine the two: we plan offline to generate a dataset of sample trajectories and then distill the plans into a policy that acts on raw observations. We propose two changes to planner distillation that address problems specific to history-based policies: Dual Averaging, a loss that combines per-timestep predictive loss and average loss on task changes, and $\rho$-Optimal Reinforcement, a feedback mechanism that reinforces the policy's own successful rollouts when these are near-optimal. We provide a sufficient condition under which static search problems with noiseless observation models is complete, and show that maximum-likelihood-observation planning implies this condition in static search with continuous beliefs. We create a challenging new domain, Scavenger, where an agent needs to gain information in order to succeed. We evaluate our distilled policy in two versions of Scavenger, one where information is provided visually and one where it is provided as a command in natural language. In either case, the learned policy attains a success rate above 90% in Scavenger, compared with at most 5% for RL. We also find that distilled planners transfer with greater than 60% success to following unseen and intentionally vague commands and greater than 75% success to following floorplans larger than those seen in training.

</details>


### [109] [SimDuoReal: Object-Centric Bimanual Policy for Emergent Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10410)
*Qiyang Yan,Yulin Liu,Akash Sharma,Haozhi Qi,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 以物体为中心的简单奖励即可让双臂灵巧操作行为涌现，并通过蒸馏得到单一通用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧操作需要协调手臂与手，但手工设定这种协调困难且往往任务特定，作者希望验证复杂双臂灵巧性能否从简单的以物体为中心的学习目标中涌现。

Method: 在共享的以物体为中心的奖励下训练强化学习策略，仅改变训练环境而非为每种行为单独调参奖励；随后在过渡状态上精炼专家，并用DAgger和强化学习将多个专家蒸馏为单一策略以组合各类行为。

Result: 真实硬件上同一策略在四项任务、未见物体且无任务特定微调下80次试验成功61次（76.3%）；模拟条码扫描任务成功率85.0%，而无交接的两个单臂策略为60.4%。

Conclusion: 该目标为学习通用双臂灵巧性提供了简单且可扩展的接口，将负担从手工设计机器人协调转移到指定物体应发生什么。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation requires coordinated arm and hand control, yet specifying such coordination manually is challenging and often task-specific. In this work, we explore whether complex bimanual dexterity can emerge from a simple object-centric learning objective. We train reinforcement learning policies under a shared object-centric reward, varying their training environments rather than tuning separate rewards for each behavior. We find that the policies discover pre-grasp manipulation, grasping and regrasping, in-hand adjustment and handover to reach object-pose goals without hand-designed motion sequences. We then refine the experts on transition states and use DAgger and reinforcement learning to distill them into a single policy that composes these behaviors for diverse bimanual tasks. On real hardware, the same policy succeeds in 61 of 80 trials (76.3\%) across four tasks with unseen objects and no task-specific fine-tuning. In simulated barcode scanning, our policy achieves 85.0\% success, compared with 60.4\% for two single-arm policies without handover. These results suggest that this objective provides a simple and scalable interface for learning general bimanual dexterity, shifting the burden from manually designing robot coordination to specifying what should happen to the object.

</details>


### [110] [Detecting and Explaining Robot Policy Failures from Sparse Internal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10400)
*Riad Ahmed,Sujosh Nag,Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIND利用稀疏自编码器分解视觉运动策略的内部激活，并将特征与机器人位置、运动及环境关联，从而在执行中检测并诊断失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略虽能处理多种机器人操作任务，但其失败原因难以理解；仅观察机器人行为无法揭示策略内部出了什么问题。

Method: MIND使用稀疏自编码器将策略内部激活分解为可单独研究的特征，通过关联特征活动与机器人位置、运动和周围环境赋予其含义，并通过引导激活考察其对动作的影响；从成功与失败执行中学习特征在失败前的行为模式，执行时标记偏离并利用贡献最大的有意义特征描述问题。

Result: 在仿真和实体机器人实验中，这些内部特征的变化有助于检测和解释失败。
