Conclusion: 通过将同一组特征同时用于检测和诊断，MIND把失败信号与策略内部可理解的变化联系起来。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies can handle many robot manipulation tasks, but understanding their failures remains difficult. Unfamiliar conditions and small action errors can lead to failure, yet watching the robot alone tells us little about what went wrong inside the policy. We introduce MIND (Mechanistic Interpretability for Neural Diagnosis), a method that looks inside a trained policy to detect failures during execution and identify the internal concepts involved. MIND uses a sparse autoencoder to break down the policy's internal activations into features that can be studied individually. We give meaning to these features by linking their activity to the robot's position, movement, and surroundings, and by steering their activations to examine how they affect the robot's actions. From successful and failed executions, we learn how the features behave on the way to a failure. During execution, MIND flags deviations from these patterns and uses the meaningful features contributing most to the change to describe the problem. Experiments in simulation and on a physical robot show that changes in these internal features help detect and explain failures. By using the same features for detection and diagnosis, MIND connects failure signals to understandable changes inside the policy.

</details>


### [111] [CPNav: Calibrated Vision-Language Navigation for Object-Goal Search in Unseen Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10366)
*Qizhao Chen,Yuanhong Zeng,Shoh Nishino,Anushri Dixit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CPNav将碰撞感知规划与经保形预测校准的VLM动作评分结合，实现无地图目标物体导航，在仿真和真实机器人上均提升成功率与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中让机器人定位目标物体仍是具身AI的核心难题；基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的方法虽有丰富语义推理能力，却常给出未校准、过度自信的动作决策。

Method: 从RGB-D输入构建可导航地图并生成无碰撞运动基元，查询两个VLM分别完成目标检测与动作评分；利用带回溯的树状探索过程获得的oracle动作，通过保形预测（CP）离线校准评分，在用户指定风险水平下提供有限样本保证，且该CP层与模型无关、可包裹任意VLM策略；在线时用校准后的VLM评分引导动作选择，并以树状探索历史记录备选动作、支持回溯。

Result: 在AI Habitat仿真中，相较未校准基线，CPNav在HM3D v0.2上成功率（SR）和SPL相对提升6.2%和11.4%，在MP3D上提升12.4%和8.8%；在改装Hiwonder MentorPi真实机器人部署中，SR和SPL分别提升24.9%和15.2%。

Conclusion: 将保形预测校准引入VLM动作评分并与碰撞感知规划、树状回溯探索结合，可有效提升无地图目标物体导航的成功率与路径效率，并在真实机器人上取得更大收益。

Abstract: Directing a robot to locate a target object in unseen environments remains a central challenge in embodied AI. Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based methods provide rich semantic reasoning but often produce uncalibrated and overconfident action decisions. We introduce CPNav, a navigation framework that combines collision-aware planning with statistically calibrated VLM action scoring for map-free object-goal navigation. From RGB-D input, CPNav constructs a navigability map, generates collision-free motion primitives, and queries two VLMs for goal detection and action scoring. The resulting scores are calibrated offline via conformal prediction (CP) using oracle actions obtained through a tree-structured exploration procedure with backtracking, providing finite-sample guarantees at a user-specified risk level. The CP layer is model-agnostic and can wrap any VLM policy. Online, calibrated VLM scores guide action selection, while a tree-structured exploration history records alternative actions and allows backtracking. We evaluate CPNav in both simulation and real-world hardware settings. In AI Habitat simulation, compared with the uncalibrated baseline method, CPNav achieves relative SR and SPL improvements of 6.2% and 11.4% on HM3D v0.2, and 12.4% and 8.8% on MP3D, respectively. In real-world deployment on a modified Hiwonder MentorPi robot, the corresponding improvements in SR and SPL are 24.9% and 15.2%, respectively. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [112] [LSRP*: Scalable and Anytime Planning for Multi-Agent Path Finding with Asynchronous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10363)
*Dachuan Liu,Shuai Zhou,Shizhe Zhao,Zhongqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LSRP*是一种新的任意时间多智能体路径规划算法，能在初始解基础上持续优化，最多支持1000个智能体，相比LSRP多处理25%智能体并降低40%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF算法大多假设同步动作（所有智能体同时启动且动作耗时相同），限制了实际应用；LSRP虽能处理松散同步但次优性无界导致解质量差。

Method: 提出LSRP*算法，在获得初始解后持续改进解质量直至运行时间耗尽，并分析其性质，在多种地图上与基线对比测试（最多1000个智能体）。

Result: LSRP*比LSRP多处理25%的智能体，并将LSRP找到的解成本最多降低40%。

Conclusion: LSRP*作为一种任意时间规划器，有效提升了松散同步MAPF的解质量与可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) seeks collision-free paths for multiple agents from their respective starting locations to their respective goal locations while minimizing path costs. Although many MAPF algorithms were developed, most of them rely on a common assumption on synchronized actions, where the actions of all agents start at the same time and always take a time unit. This assumption may limit the use of MAPF planners in practice. To get rid of this assumption, recently, an algorithm called Loosely Synchronized Rule-based Planning (LSRP) was proposed, which can find sub-optimal solutions for many agents. However, LSRP often finds poor quality solutions due to its unbounded sub-optimality. This paper develops a new anytime planner called LSRP* that can keep improving solution quality after the initial solution is obtained until the runtime budget depletes. We analyze the properties of LSRP* and test it against several baselines with up to 1000 agents on various maps. LSRP* can handle up to 25% more agents than LSRP and can reduce up to 40% of the solution cost found by LSRP.

</details>


### [113] [GUARD: Anomaly-Informed Q-Steering for Generative Robot Policies in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10343)
*Aryaman Gupta,Xiaoyue Wu,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Mark Van der Merwe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GUARD提出一种基于异常信息的Q引导框架，利用策略训练分布构建稠密失败预警信号，在不修改预训练策略的情况下提升生成式机器人策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流匹配生成式机器人策略能建模复杂多模态行为，但在闭环执行中易受协变量偏移影响；现有缓解方法依赖额外示范、策略微调或测试时引导，通常需要人工干预、工程化奖励或任务特定信号。

Method: GUARD将稀疏任务奖励与从策略训练数据学到的稠密异常分数结合，并通过学习到的Q函数传播二者；其梯度用于引导去噪过程，使动作既提升任务回报又避免轨迹进入支持不足的区域，且不直接修改预训练策略。

Result: 在四个Adroit灵巧操作任务上，GUARD在名义和扰动动力学下平均成功率达96.0%和80.0%，比最强基线分别高21.0和20.0个百分点，同时保持最低异常分数；在手内螺丝刀旋转与重定向任务上实现直接仿真到现实迁移，成功率分别提升40和25个百分点。

Conclusion: 异常信号不仅可用于失败监测，还能作为稀疏任务奖励的补充，成为鲁棒策略引导的有效监督信号。

Abstract: Generative robot policies based on diffusion and flow matching can model complex, multimodal behaviors, but remain susceptible to covariate shift during closed-loop execution. Existing approaches mitigate such failures through additional demonstrations, policy fine-tuning, or test-time guidance, but often require human intervention, engineered rewards, or other task-specific signals. We introduce GUARD, an anomaly-informed Q-steering framework that leverages the policy's training distribution to construct a dense, failure-anticipatory steering signal. GUARD pairs sparse task rewards with dense anomaly scores learned from policy training data and propagates both through a learned Q-function. Its gradients then steer the denoising process toward actions that improve task return while preventing trajectories from entering poorly supported regions, without directly modifying the pretrained policy. Across four Adroit dexterous manipulation tasks, GUARD achieves average success rates of $96.0\%$ and $80.0\%$ under nominal and perturbed dynamics, outperforming the strongest baseline by $21.0$ and $20.0$ percentage points while maintaining the lowest anomaly score. GUARD further demonstrates direct sim-to-real transfer on in-hand screwdriver rotation and reorientation tasks, achieving $40$ and $25$ percentage-point gains in success rate. These results demonstrate that anomaly signals can serve not only for failure monitoring but also to complement sparse task rewards as effective supervision for robust policy steering.

</details>


### [114] [Mobility Information Capacity of Multi-Robot Systems: Safety-Aware Maneuver Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10298)
*Weijie Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出移动信息容量（MIC）框架，以机动序列与其含噪轨迹观测之间的互信息为指标，量化机群可观测的机动自由度；在线性高斯模型下给出有限时域基准与长时域谱极限，并把安全约束转化为协方差限制，用有限码本实现并仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数量、工作空间占用率和任务吞吐量无法刻画机群能变化多少运动、以及这些变化能被多可靠地观测，因此需要新的度量来量化机群可观测的机动自由度。

Method: 定义机动信息为联合机动序列与含噪轨迹观测之间的互信息，并在机动资源约束下最大化得到MIC；对带反馈动力学的线性高斯机动-观测模型，推导有限时域信息基准及稳态长时域谱极限，处理异构机动增益、有色不确定性和时间耦合机动代价；将工作空间边界、机器人间距和执行器机会约束映射为耦合机动协方差限制，并给出协方差优化退化为机动模式资源分配的条件；通过个体风险筛选和噪声白化轨迹分离构造有限联合机动码本，并给出序列恢复误差与实现信息相对容量基准的界。

Result: 针对空中机器人仿真，评估了谱收敛性、安全感知分配、有限码本识别和机群规模扩展；所提框架给出可观测机动自由度的上限，并补充了占用率和任务吞吐量度量。

Conclusion: MIC框架可从信息论角度量化移动系统支持的最大可观测机动自由度，为共享工作空间中多机器人协调与安全约束下的机动能力评估提供补充性度量。

Abstract: Multiple robots operating in shared workspaces must adjust their speed, direction, and trajectories to coordinate with one another and respond to changing tasks and disturbances. The available maneuver freedom depends on the dynamics, uncertainty, and safety margins, even for a fixed fleet size and workspace. However, robot count, workspace occupancy, and task throughput do not quantify how much the fleet can vary its motion or how reliably these variations can be observed. We develop a mobility information capacity (MIC) framework that jointly characterizes these two aspects. Mobility information is defined as the mutual information between a joint maneuver sequence and its noisy trajectory observation, and MIC maximizes this quantity under maneuver-resource constraints. For a linear Gaussian maneuver-to-observation model with feedback dynamics, we characterize the finite-horizon information benchmark and derive its stationary long-horizon spectral limit, accounting for heterogeneous mobility gains, colored uncertainty, and temporally coupled maneuver costs. We further map workspace-boundary, inter-robot separation, and actuator chance constraints into coupled maneuver-covariance restrictions and derive conditions under which covariance optimization reduces to resource allocation across mobility modes. Finite joint maneuver codebooks are then constructed through individual risk screening and noise-whitened trajectory separation, with bounds linking sequence recovery error and achieved information to the capacity benchmarks. An aerial-robot simulation study evaluates spectral convergence, safety-aware allocation, finite-codebook identification, and fleet-size scaling. The resulting framework complements occupancy and task-throughput measures by quantifying the maximum observable maneuver freedom supported by a mobility system.

</details>


### [115] [Who Taught the Robot That? Removing One User's Corrections from a Robot Policy That Learns Online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10280)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人部署后通过多用户依次纠正进行持续学习，当其中一人要求被移除时，能否遗忘该用户取决于纠正方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究机器人持续学习场景下的"用户数据移除"问题，即如何让机器人遗忘某位用户，使其恢复到该用户从未出现过的状态。

Method: 在两项带脚本用户的模拟任务中精确计算"移除目标机器人"；对比两种纠正方式（接管控制式纠正 vs. 微调动作式纠正）和多种移除方法（按原顺序重训剩余会话、单次删除重训、继续训练不包含该用户等）。

Result: 接管控制式纠正下，只需按原顺序重训剩余会话即可接近目标机器人（受训练噪声限制）；单次删除并重训会因丢失训练顺序而失败，即使其他用户纠正不依赖被移除者。微调动作式纠正下，其他用户纠正携带被移除者影响，所测方法均无法恢复目标机器人；继续训练可去除大部分习惯但无法消除训练缓冲区中其数据的影响。日志能识别明显有害用户，但无法比计数纠正次数更好地按影响力排序用户。

Conclusion: 机器人能否遗忘某用户取决于纠正方式；有序训练日志对接管控制式纠正至关重要，而微调式纠正下的影响传播使精确移除难以实现。

Abstract: A robot that keeps learning after deployment is corrected by several people in turn, and one of them may later ask to be removed. We show that whether the robot can forget that person depends on how people correct it. The goal of removal is the robot that would exist if the person had never been there, which we compute exactly in two simulated tasks with scripted users. When users correct the robot by taking over control, an ordered log of training updates is enough: retraining on the remaining sessions in their original order gives a robot as close to the goal as training noise allows. Deleting the person's data and retraining in one pass does not, even when the other users' corrections do not depend on the removed person, because it loses the order of training. When users only nudge the robot's action, the other users' corrections carry the removed person's influence, and no method we tested recovers the goal robot. Continuing to train without the person removes most of their habits, but not what their data in the training buffer keep alive. The log can identify one clearly harmful user, but not rank users by influence better than counting their corrections.

</details>


### [116] [Look Only Where You Plan for Real-Time End-to-End Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10264)
*Haohua Que,Tianle Zhu,Qianyi Wu,Zhipeng Bao,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOWP是一种仅在最需要规划的区域进行感知的自动驾驶规划器，利用两组各16个可学习查询通过标定直接读取相机特征，无需构建稠密BEV或监督目标与地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密鸟瞰图（BEV）构建在近期强规划器中占参数量的60%，造成计算与参数冗余，因此需要一种更紧凑、仅关注规划相关区域的表示方法。

Method: 提出LOWP规划器：两组各16个可学习查询经已知相机标定读取图像特征，一组在投影3D锚点处采样图像特征，另一组对带视射线标签的特征进行注意力交互；不构建稠密BEV，也不监督物体或地图。

Result: 在nuScenes开环上平均L2从0.302m降至0.296m，参数量为稠密对应模型的53%，同GPU速度为其1.7倍；基于锚点的读取器在转弯场景优于全局读取器。NAVSIM伪仿真上，将规划器自身轨迹与词表联合评分无需重训练即达85.9 PDMS。闭环Bench2Drive在218条路线上得65.5分，存在刹车导致的停车失败，其释放规则的总体收益尚不确定；并区分了实测延迟与硬件迁移估计，后者不足以建立同硬件排名。

Conclusion: LOWP以紧凑表示实现了有竞争力的规划性能，验证了“只在规划处观察”的可行性，同时指出闭环失败模式与延迟评估的局限性。

Abstract: Dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) construction accounts for 60% of the parameters in a strong recent driving planner. This paper presents LOWP, a planner that looks only where it plans: two sets of sixteen learnable queries read the cameras through the known calibration, one by sampling image features at projected 3D anchors and one by attending to features labeled with their viewing rays, so no dense BEV is built and no object or map is supervised. Evaluations on open-loop nuScenes, pseudo-simulated NAVSIM and closed-loop Bench2Drive show competitive planning with a compact representation. On nuScenes, average L2 changes from 0.302 to 0.296 m with 53% of the dense counterpart's parameters and 1.7 times its speed on the same GPU; the anchor-based reader performs better on turns than the global reader. On NAVSIM, scoring the planner's own trajectories alongside a vocabulary gives 85.9 PDMS without retraining. The closed-loop control configuration scores 65.5 on 218 routes, with a brake-induced standstill failure whose release rule has an inconclusive aggregate benefit. We distinguish measured latency from hardware-transferred estimates, which do not establish a common-hardware ranking.

</details>


### [117] [OfficeTwin: Realizing 3D Reconstruction of Redundant Scenes through Partial Viewpoint Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10241)
*Quanyun Wu,Kyle Gao,Daisy Li,Kshitij Goyal,Dayou Mao,David A. Clausi,Jonathan Li,Yuhao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OfficeTwin 是一个面向机器人采集的框架，可将不完整的单目观测转化为仿真就绪的数字孪生，通过度量与结构约束以及重复工位配准，生成每个工位独立水密、度量的实体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈三维重建速度快且对弱纹理重复表面可靠，但原始点云缺乏物体身份、物理属性和水密体积；机器人受自由度限制导致观测不完整，办公室重复弱纹理结构使不同工位难以区分，现有图像到三维生成方法在遮挡下产生幻觉并混淆重复实例。

Method: OfficeTwin 首先施加度量与结构约束，然后利用办公室结构冗余性，将重复隔间配准到共享规范帧，在规范帧中融合互补观测以恢复单视图被遮挡的几何，避免幻觉。

Result: 在真实办公室扫描中，OfficeTwin 将每个隔间恢复为独立水密、度量的实体，优于无法生成仿真就绪资产的前馈重建和生成式基线。

Conclusion: OfficeTwin 能够从机器人原生不完整观测中生成仿真就绪的数字孪生，解决现有方法在部分观测和重复实例下的几何幻觉与混淆问题。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction turns an uncalibrated monocular stream into a dense point cloud in a single inference pass, orders of magnitude faster than classical structure-from-motion (e.g., COLMAP), and reliable on the weakly textured, repetitive surfaces where keypoint matching fails outright. However, raw point clouds lack the object identity, physical properties, and watertight volume required for robot learning. This gap widens under robot-native capture. A camera mounted on a robot constrained to a few degrees of freedom sees each object only partially, and the repetitive, weakly textured structure of an office makes distinct workstations nearly indistinguishable, leaving redundant and partial observations that downstream reconstruction cannot resolve. Existing image-to-3D generation methods struggle with these conditions, hallucinating geometry under incomplete views and confusing repeated instances. We present OfficeTwin, a robot-native framework that converts these incomplete observations into simulation-ready digital twins. OfficeTwin first imposes metric and structural constraints, then exploits the office's structural redundancy by registering repeated cubicles into a shared canonical frame, where complementary observations recover geometry occluded in any single view without hallucination. On a real office scan, OfficeTwin recovers every cubicle as an individually watertight, metric solid, outperforming feed-forward reconstruction and generative baselines that fail to produce simulation-ready assets.

</details>


### [118] [DASH-SLAM: Calibration-Free Metric Monocular SLAM via Depth Scale Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10239)
*Otavio Ribas,Jacopo Panerati,Pierre-Yves Lajoie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DASH-SLAM 将预训练 Depth Anything 3 的度量深度预测集成到共享关键帧位姿图中，无需任务特定微调，通过深度比导出尺度校正并累积，以阻尼因子控制影响，重缩放里程计平移、回环约束和地图几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何基础模型能从单目图像估计稠密几何和相机参数，但可靠 SLAM 还需跨连续重建的一致尺度和准确数据关联。

Method: 提出 DASH-SLAM：在共享关键帧位姿图中融合 Depth Anything 3 的度量深度预测；从共享锚点图像的深度比计算尺度校正并跨重建累积，引入阻尼因子调节其影响，并据此重缩放里程计平移、回环约束和地图几何。

Result: 在 TUM、Replica、7-Scenes 上优于现有先进方法；用刚性和相似变换对齐分别评估度量尺度保真度与尺度内精度；在 Unified Autonomy Stack 现场数据集的隧道、雾和冰湖四个感知退化序列上优于基础模型 SLAM 先进方法，并在经典 SLAM 失效处保持跟踪。

Conclusion: DASH-SLAM 无需微调即可利用预训练度量深度基础模型实现一致尺度与准确数据关联，在常规和感知退化环境下均表现优越。

Abstract: Geometric foundation models estimate dense geometry and camera parameters from monocular images, but reliable SLAM also requires consistent scale and accurate data association across successive reconstructions. We present DASH-SLAM, a monocular SLAM system that integrates metric depth predictions from pretrained Depth Anything 3 into a shared-keyframe pose graph without task-specific fine-tuning. DASH-SLAM derives scale corrections from depth ratios at shared anchor images and accumulates them across reconstructions, with a damping factor controlling their influence. These corrections rescale odometry translations, loop constraints, and map geometry. Experiments on TUM, Replica, and 7-Scenes show that DASH-SLAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We evaluate performance using both rigid and similarity alignment to distinguish metric-scale fidelity from accuracy up to scale. We further evaluate DASH-SLAM on four challenging sequences in perceptually-degraded environments from the Unified Autonomy Stack field dataset, covering tunnels, fog, and a frozen lake. On these sequences, DASH-SLAM outperforms the foundation-model SLAM state-of-the-art and maintains tracking where classical SLAM methods fail.

</details>


### [119] [Agibot: An Open-Source Modular Swerve-Drive Robot Platform for Agricultural Robotics Research and Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10235)
*Moeen Ul Islam,Bishal Adhikari,Kori Edwards,Lorien Harvey,J. Alex Thomasson,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出开源、模块化、低成本农业机器人平台Agibot，集成四轮独立转向驱动、可换计算、分布式嵌入式控制、多模态感知与可配置操作，在感知-规划-执行-验证框架下完成行作物导航与操作任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业生产面临持续劳动力短缺与自动化需求增长，但农业机器人研究受限于缺乏低成本、灵活且易获取的研究平台。

Method: 设计开源模块化低成本平台，集成四轮独立转向驱动、可更换计算单元、分布式嵌入式控制、多模态传感和可配置操作机构，构建统一的感知-规划-执行-验证框架，并在不同光照与土壤条件下开展行作物导航与操作任务实验。

Result: 实验实现了可靠的车道跟随、自主行间导航、目标识别、播种、植物操作及任务成功闭环验证；控制器调参与集成试验验证了平台研究能力。

Conclusion: Agibot以低成本、模块化、开源、可配置操作和端到端自主能力，为农业机器人研究、技术开发与人才培养提供实用平台。

Abstract: Agricultural production faces persistent labor shortages and increasing demand for automation, yet progress in agricultural robotics remains constrained by the limited availability of affordable, flexible, and accessible research platforms. This paper presents Agibot, an open-source, modular, low-cost agricultural robotics platform designed for autonomy research and experimentation, with potential applications in hands-on robotics education. The platform integrates four-wheel independent swerve drive, swappable computing, distributed embedded control, multimodal sensing, and configurable manipulation within a unified sense-plan-act-verify framework supporting perception, control, navigation, manipulation, and autonomous task verification. The platform was evaluated through representative row-crop navigation and manipulation tasks under varying lighting and soil conditions. Experiments demonstrated reliable lane following, autonomous row-to-row navigation, target identification, seed dispensing, plant manipulation, and closed-loop verification of task success. Its modular architecture supports independent testing, modification, and validation of autonomy components for rapid prototyping and experimentation. These proof-of-concept tasks, controller-tuning studies, and integrated trials demonstrate the platform's research capabilities, while its accessible and reconfigurable design supports its use for hands-on robotics education. By combining affordability, modularity, open-source accessibility, configurable manipulation, and end-to-end sense-plan-act-verify autonomy, Agibot provides a practical platform for agricultural robotics research, technology development, and workforce training.

</details>


### [120] [Capturability-Aware Footstep Planning in Cluttered Environments: A Recursively Feasible Sequential MPC on Axis-Aligned Safe Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10169)
*Athanasios Kyriakopoulos,Shubham S. Kumbhar,Kunal S. Narkhede,Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Ioannis Poulakakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑可捕获性的序列MPC框架，用于基于LIP的人形机器人足迹规划，同时保证避障与运动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人足迹规划需同时满足避障与运动稳定性，现有方法难以兼顾，且需应对执行中意外受阻（如门意外关闭）等受限场景。

Method: 通过由相交矩形序列构造的安全走廊，将避障与可捕获性约束结合，形成具有递归可行性保证的序列MPC框架；规划器自动适应受限场景，在前进受阻时切换为原地行走。

Result: 性能基本与预测时域无关，在保持低计算成本的同时实现高成功率；在高保真仿真中以人形机器人Digit验证有效。

Conclusion: 该方法为杂乱环境中可捕获的人形机器人足迹规划提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental aspect of humanoid footstep planning is to jointly guarantee obstacle avoidance and locomotion stability. This paper presents a capturability-aware sequential Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework for Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based humanoid footstep planning in the presence of obstacles. The proposed method combines obstacle avoidance with capturability constraints through safe corridors constructed as sequences of intersecting rectangles, yielding a sequential MPC framework with recursive feasibility guarantees for the LIP-based planning layer. The planner autonomously adapts to constrained scenarios, switching to in-place walking when obstructions---such as doors assumed to be open during planning but unexpectedly found to be closed during execution---block forward motion. Performance is largely independent of the prediction horizon, allowing high success rates while keeping the computational cost low. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulations with the humanoid robot Digit, demonstrating a practical solution for capturable humanoid footstep planning in cluttered environments.

</details>


### [121] [BATCH: Behavior Alignment for Cross-Embodiment Learning from Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10148)
*Zeyi Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BATCH 是一个跨具身学习框架，通过自监督时序表征和循环一致性目标对齐人类与机器人行为，从而更有效地将人类演示迁移到机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据规模大且多样，但因具身差异、视角、动作空间和时序结构不同，直接用于机器人学习存在困难。

Method: 提出 BATCH 框架：用循环一致性目标学习自监督时序表征以建立跨具身对应关系，并在自回归视频-动作扩散模型中引入行为对齐协同训练与人类到机器人风格迁移两个训练目标。

Result: 在真实操作任务上，BATCH 相比未对齐数据和已有跨具身基线，对未见任务配置的泛化更好，风格迁移目标带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 显式行为对齐能有效促进人类演示向机器人策略的迁移。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide a scalable source of diverse manipulation data, but leveraging them for robot learning remains challenging due to differences in embodiment, viewpoint, action spaces, and temporal structure. We introduce BATCH, a cross-embodiment learning framework that explicitly aligns human and robot behaviors. BATCH learns self-supervised temporal representations with a cycle-consistency objective to establish cross-embodiment correspondences without manual annotation. We further introduce two complementary training objectives that leverage these correspondences within an autoregressive videoaction diffusion model: behavior-aligned co-training and human-to-robot style transfer. On real-world manipulation tasks, BATCH improves generalization to unseen task configurations compared with training on unaligned data and prior cross-embodiment baselines, with additional gains from the style-transfer objective. Our results demonstrate that explicit behavior alignment enables more effective transfer from human demonstrations to robot policies.

</details>


### [122] [A Unified Inertial Encoder for Human and Humanoid Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10137)
*Yutian Chen,Shibo Zhao,Rocky Duan,Karen Liu,Carmelo Sferrazza,Guanya Shi,Yuheng Qiu,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一惯性编码器，借助运动学IMU合成对齐人体与人形机器人的惯性输入空间，联合训练速度估计与动作-文本对齐，实现零样本迁移至Unitree G1真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的状态估计与语言动作理解受限于训练数据覆盖范围，而人体运动数据集覆盖更广，但受身体几何、传感、物理扰动差异制约难以迁移。

Method: 设计共享惯性接口的统一惯性编码器，用运动学IMU合成在对应身体部位表示人体与人形运动；在该公共输入空间联合训练人体与运动学重定向人形动作，结合速度估计、动作-文本对齐、连续时间编码及传感器与运动随机化。

Result: 无需物理仿真、真机训练轨迹或微调即可零样本迁移到物理Unitree G1，在五个数据集上取得最低速度误差，真机G1上为0.099m/s；动作描述检索可扩展到仅在人体训练中出现的重定向G1动作类别。

Conclusion: 人体运动知识可支撑人形惯性里程计与语言动作理解的泛化。

Abstract: Learning humanoid state estimation and language-grounded motion understanding from robot recordings ties training coverage to the robot's motion repertoire. Human motion datasets offer broader coverage, yet transferring this knowledge requires overcoming differences in body geometry, sensing, and physical disturbances. To enable this transfer, we propose a unified inertial encoder whose shared inertial interface uses kinematic IMU synthesis to represent human and humanoid motion at corresponding body sites. Within this common input space, joint training on human and kinematically retargeted humanoid motion combines velocity estimation and motion--text alignment with continuous-time encoding and sensor and motion randomization. The resulting model transfers zero-shot to a physical Unitree G1 without physics simulation, real-robot training trajectories, or fine-tuning, achieving the lowest velocity error among evaluated methods across five datasets, including 0.099m/s on real G1. Its motion-description retrieval also extends to retargeted G1 motion categories observed only in human training, demonstrating how human motion knowledge can support the generalization of humanoid inertial odometry and language-grounded motion understanding.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Semantic Classes: Online Physical Parameter Estimation via Embedding-Conditioned Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10107)
*Alexander Borca-Tasciuc,Prayag Sharma,Franck Djeumou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时估计器，用交互驱动的混合高斯后验替代语义类别索引，在接触前从视觉嵌入推断物理属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉外观对摩擦、刚度等物理属性存在歧义，现有方法常压缩为点估计或固定语义类别，无法保留不同物理状态。

Method: 以逆建模得到的交互高斯参数为混合分量，用连续视觉嵌入相似度检索并加权历史交互；稀疏时用条件生成先验融合，并学习嵌入精化以提升检索相关性。

Result: 实验表明估计器能恢复多模态物理信念、拒绝无关历史、避免空间地图离散化，并以控制器频率运行；在未知高低摩擦路面的自主赛车仿真中，后验使控制器随交互证据累积而自适应，提升多圈性能并保持摩擦感知。

Conclusion: 交互驱动的混合高斯后验能有效保留物理歧义并在实时控制中支持自适应，优于点估计或语义类别方法。

Abstract: Enabling robots to infer interaction-relevant physical properties from vision before contact would improve planning and control in unstructured environments. Vision provides useful pre-contact information, but appearance alone is often ambiguous: visually similar regions can induce different friction, stiffness, or sinkage responses upon contact. Existing approaches often collapse this ambiguity to a point estimate or aggregate experience through fixed semantic classes, limiting their ability to preserve distinct physical regimes. Instead, we propose a real-time estimator that replaces semantic class indexing with an interaction-grounded Gaussian-mixture posterior over latent physical parameters, conditioned directly on continuous visual embeddings. Our approach maintains interaction-derived Gaussian parameter estimates, typically from inverse modeling, as mixture components while using embedding similarity to retrieve and weight relevant past interactions for each new query region. A support-aware fusion mechanism incorporates a conditional generative prior model when interaction history is sparse, while a learned embedding refinement improves retrieval relevance without discretizing the representation. Our experiments show that the estimator recovers multimodal physical beliefs, rejects unrelated interaction history, avoids spatial map discretization, and evaluates at controller rates. In a high-fidelity autonomous racing simulation with unknown low- and high-friction surface regions, the posterior enables the controller to adapt over repeated laps as interaction evidence accumulates, improving performance while maintaining friction-aware behavior.

</details>


### [124] [UniLatent: Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Latent Observation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10061)
*Yufei Ding,Yi Du,Weiduo Yuan,Hao Jiang,Dylan Goetting,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Daniel Seita,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLatent 是一个跨具身迁移框架，通过对齐共享潜在空间减少具身差异带来的观测差距，实现零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据集采集成本高且通常绑定特定具身，难以在不同几何、外观和运动学的机器人之间复用演示，因此需要跨具身迁移方法。

Method: 在仿真中渲染成对的源-目标机器人观测，将源视觉编码器对齐到冻结的目标编码器，训练得到的策略可直接在目标观测上运行，无需像素级图像翻译。

Result: 在三个仿真任务和两个目标机械臂上，UniLatent 实现最高 93% 的零样本成功率，平均超过最强像素翻译基线 30 多个百分点；在真实 xArm 到 Franka 的迁移中，每个任务仅用 10 条目标演示微调即达到 27/30 次成功部署。

Conclusion: UniLatent 能有效缩小跨具身观测差距，实现高效零样本与少样本跨机器人策略迁移，优于像素级翻译方法。

Abstract: Robot datasets are costly to collect and are usually tied to a specific embodiment, making it difficult to reuse demonstrations across robots with different geometry, appearance, and kinematics. We introduce UniLatent, a cross-embodiment transfer framework that reduces the embodiment-induced observation gap in a shared latent space. UniLatent renders paired source-target robot observations in simulation and aligns a source visual encoder to a frozen target encoder. A policy trained on encoded source demonstrations can then run directly on target observations, with no pixel-level image translation. Across three simulation tasks and two target robot arms, UniLatent reaches up to 93% zero-shot success, exceeding the strongest pixel-translation baseline by over 30 points on average. In real-world xArm-to-Franka transfer, fine-tuning with only 10 target demonstrations per task yields 27/30 successful rollouts. Code, videos and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [125] [Efficient Learning of Differentiable MPC Policies on the GPU via Smoothed ADMM and First-Order Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09937)
*Jianghan Zhang,Roland Schwan,Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,John Zhang,Thomas Lew,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SmoothedTurboMPC：结合SQP与对数障碍平滑ADMM的GPU并行可微MPC求解器，并配合SHAC强化学习实现高效MPC策略训练，在赛车任务中显著加速并实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微MPC虽能端到端优化约束感知策略，但存在两大瓶颈：活跃集切换处灵敏度不光滑，以及每次策略评估都需解优化问题导致训练昂贵。

Method: 1) 设计SmoothedTurboMPC：将序列二次规划(SQP)与对数障碍平滑的ADMM内层求解器结合，在GPU上并行执行，实现平滑梯度、收敛处精确灵敏度与高效计算；2) 训练阶段采用一阶强化学习FORL中的Short-Horizon Actor-Critic(SHAC)，复用 rollout 中的MPC解而非反复重评估。

Result: 端到端训练吞吐量最高提升4.4倍；在车辆竞速任务中，相比MPC PPO获得更高回报且加速46倍，7分钟训练330万条转移；结合域随机化的RL训练出更可靠的MPC竞速策略并在全尺寸赛车上验证；成果集成到开源TurboMPC求解器。

Conclusion: 该框架通过平滑可微GPU求解器与高效一阶RL训练相结合，有效缓解MPC策略学习的灵敏度与计算瓶颈，并在仿真与实车竞速任务中验证了其高效性与可靠性。

Abstract: Differentiable model predictive control (MPC) enables structured, constraint-aware policies to be optimized end-to-end. However, learning MPC policies faces two bottlenecks: (1) nonsmooth sensitivities at active-set transitions and (2) expensive policy evaluation, since each requires an optimization solve. We first introduce SmoothedTurboMPC, a GPU-parallel differentiable MPC solver combining sequential quadratic programming (SQP) with a log-barrier-smoothed alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) inner solver. Its design enables smooth gradients, exact sensitivities at convergence, and efficient execution on GPUs. Second, to efficiently train MPC policies, we pair our solver with first-order reinforcement learning (FORL) using Short-Horizon Actor-Critic (SHAC), which reuses rollout MPC solutions instead of repeatedly re-evaluating them. Comparisons with state of the art solvers show that the approach achieves up to $4.4\times$ higher end-to-end training throughput. On a vehicle racing task, our method achieves higher returns than MPC PPO with a $46\times$ speedup, training on 3.3 million transitions in only 7 minutes. Finally, we use the framework to train a more reliable MPC racing policy via RL with domain randomization, and validate it on a full-scale race car. We integrate our advances into the open-source TurboMPC solver.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [DriveFoundry: Co-Evolving Data and Model for Efficient Closed-Loop Iteration in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11532)
*Ziyang Yao,Yuncheng Jiang,Fang Li,Haochen Liu,Shaoqing Xu,Wen Zhang,Xiangyang Chu,Zibin Guo,Jiale Hou,Chuang Wan,Jia Hua,Gengzhao Xiang,Xiaoran Luo,Chong He,Yuechen Luo,Junzhe Wang,Zhiyu Sun,Jun Zhang,Wei Li,Lei Li,Hongwei Xie,Bing Wang,Yuannan Shen,Jianwei Cui,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Jingwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DriveFoundry闭环框架通过结构化因果数据精炼与自适应推理，在数据与算力预算下提升迭代效率；9B模型在PhysicalAI取得最低ADE@1，AlpaSim多指标优于Alpamayo 1.5，自适应推理仅用28% token达到完整推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶基础模型需从稀有关键事件中学习可复用决策知识，并在严格运行时约束下应用，因此需在有限数据与算力预算下提升迭代效率。

Method: 数据引擎构建可追溯的场景-候选记录，关联场景证据、因素-决策关系与候选轨迹及结果评估，通过候选比较与反事实标注明确场景因素对行为可行性和偏好的影响，并以验证的模型失败指导VLM辅助标注与专家修正；模型引擎Xiaomi-Drive-o1在共享视觉-语言骨干中统一感知、因果、行动、世界与价值，采用分块潜在重填在任务块间传递候选证据与预测视觉后果，并通过学习到的计算价值头按预期收益与成本选择任务、分配潜在token。

Result: 在PhysicalAI上9B模型在所评估划分与 horizon 中取得最低ADE@1；在AlpaSim上所有指标优于Alpamayo 1.5，近距离遭遇与越野率低于34B Alpamayo 2 Super，但进度与AlpaSim分数落后；0.8B变体联合训练使5 s minADE@6降低约54%且不改变推理路径或token预算；自适应推理仅用28% token匹配完整推理性能；实车测试确认运行时可行性。

Conclusion: 结果支持从关键经验学习并动态分配算力的统一范式，兼顾性能与运行时约束。

Abstract: A driving foundation model must learn reusable decision knowledge from rare critical events and apply it under strict runtime constraints. We introduce DriveFoundry, a closed-loop framework improving iteration efficiency under data and computation budgets by coupling structured causal data refinement with adaptive inference. The Data Engine builds traceable scene-candidate records linking scene evidence and factor-decision relations to candidate trajectories and outcome assessments. Candidate comparisons and counterfactual annotations clarify how scene factors affect behavioral feasibility and preference. Verified model failures guide VLM-assisted annotation and expert correction, refining training data and annotation systems. The model engine, Xiaomi-Drive-o1, unifies Perception, Causation, Action, World, and Value within a shared vision-language backbone to enable cross-task knowledge transfer. Block-wise latent refill passes candidate-specific evidence and predicted visual consequences between task blocks, connecting scene understanding, planning, and evaluation. A learned value-of-computation head selects tasks and allocates latent tokens based on expected benefit and cost, adapting inference to each decision. On PhysicalAI, the 9B model achieves the lowest ADE@1 across evaluated splits and horizons among tested models. In AlpaSim, it outperforms Alpamayo 1.5 across all metrics and achieves lower close-encounter and off-road rates than the 34B Alpamayo 2 Super, while trailing in progress and AlpaSim scores. For the 0.8B variant, joint training reduces 5 s minADE@6 by about 54% without altering the inference path or token budget. Adaptive inference matches full reasoning performance using only 28% of tokens. Real-vehicle tests confirm runtime feasibility, supporting a unified paradigm for learning from critical experience and dynamically allocating computation.

</details>


### [127] [WARP-JEPA: World-Model-in-the-Loop Amortized Rollout Planning for Frozen Hierarchical Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10806)
*Junjie Li,Zhaojiacheng Zhou,Kaixiang Wang,Zihan Wang,QingSong Zhao,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WARP-JEPA 用学习的多候选轨迹提议器替代测试时梯度优化，在冻结世界模型下实现更高效且更成功的长时程视觉控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H-JEPA 依赖昂贵的测试时梯度下降做规划，迭代优化占回合运行时间高达 97.7%，且预测 rollout 成本低不一定转化为成功控制；此外存在长时程顶层组合与短两步下层规划的规划时域不对称问题。

Method: 提出分层摊销规划框架 WARP-JEPA：顶层使用轻量级世界模型在环的因果 Transformer，下层使用并行残差 MLP 作为轨迹提议器；保持世界模型冻结，用基于模型的 rollout 成本和 best-of-K 行为克隆训练提议器，以候选选择替代测试时梯度优化。

Result: 在四个基准的 30 个冻结 H-JEPA 模型上，WARP-JEPA 在长时程任务上实现 9.6-18.8 倍更快的回合推理（Cube 上 2.9-3.0 倍），任务成功率最高提升 33.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 摊销规划可在不重新训练世界模型的情况下显著提升推理效率与控制性能，表明学习式候选提议是替代在线梯度规划的有效方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (H-JEPA) enable long-horizon visual control but rely on costly test-time gradient descent for planning. Our analysis reveals that iterative optimization consumes up to 97.7% of episode runtime and that low predicted rollout costs do not necessarily translate into successful control. We further identify a planning-horizon asymmetry between long-horizon top-level composition and short two-step subordinate planning. To address these challenges, we propose WARP-JEPA, a hierarchical amortized planning framework that replaces online gradient optimization with learned multi-candidate trajectory proposers while keeping the world models frozen. WARP-JEPA combines a lightweight world-model-in-the-loop causal Transformer at the top level with parallel residual MLPs at subordinate levels. The proposers are trained using model-based rollout costs and best-of-K behavior cloning, enabling efficient candidate selection without test-time gradient optimization. Across 30 frozen H-JEPA models on four benchmarks, WARP-JEPA achieves 9.6-18.8x faster episode inference on long-horizon tasks (2.9-3.0x on Cube) and improves task success by up to 33.3 percentage points. These results demonstrate that amortized planning can substantially improve both inference efficiency and control performance without retraining the world models. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [128] [Look Ahead: Search-Driven Reasoning in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10769)
*Ahmed Zeer,Osama Shbib,Muzaffer Kaan Yuce,Himmet Toprak Kesgin,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在PushT任务中，对冻结的ACT策略采用选择性束搜索，将成功率从0%提升至18%，并提出One Step Ahead门控机制以约3倍加速保持或提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型测试时规划的启发，探索在动作块层面进行选择性搜索，以在提交动作前评估候选未来。

Method: 使用冻结的Action Chunking with Transformers（ACT）策略，通过束搜索在策略预测周围生成扰动候选，并利用克隆模拟器状态的推演进行评分；提出One Step Ahead（OSA）门控，仅当名义策略块的预测覆盖下降超过阈值时才触发完整搜索。

Result: 50个评估回合中，束搜索将任务成功率从0%提升至18%，平均累积奖励从39.44提升至103.2；OSA在消融中保持或提升成功率，同时将运行时间较完整束搜索减少约3倍；树深度、分支因子、束宽和重规划频率的消融表明更大搜索预算并不一致地提升性能。

Conclusion: 结果支持对动作分块策略采用选择性测试时搜索；语言条件VLA和物理机器人上的评估仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Search allows a robotic policy to evaluate candidate futures before committing to an action. Motivated by test-time planning for vision-language action (VLA) models, we study selective search over action chunks in PushT using a frozen Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) policy. Beam search generates perturbed alternatives around the policy prediction and scores them through rollouts from cloned simulator states. Across 50 evaluation episodes, beam search raises task success from 0% to 18% and increases average cumulative reward from 39.44 to 103.2. We introduce One Step Ahead (OSA), a gate that simulates the nominal policy chunk and invokes full search only when its predicted coverage drop exceeds a threshold. In the reported ablations, OSA preserves or improves success while reducing runtime by approximately 3x relative to full beam search. Ablations over tree depth, branching factor, beam width, and replanning frequency show that larger search budgets do not consistently improve performance. The results support selective test-time search for action-chunking policies; evaluation on language-conditioned VLAs and physical robots remains future work.

</details>


### [129] [Decentralised Drone Swarm Formation under Silent Failures with ACO Pheromone Claims and PSO Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10454)
*Md. Abu Horaira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究无中心规划器的去中心化无人机编队，在未知存活数量、静默故障、短距有损通信和噪声执行条件下，结合蚁群优化（ACO）的蒸发信息素声明机制与粒子群优化（PSO）飞行控制，实现图案覆盖与容错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实无人机集群面临数量未知、故障静默、通信短距有损、执行噪声等困难，集中式规划不可行，需要一种去中心化且能隐式检测故障的编队控制方法。

Method: 采用ACO通过类似强化蒸发信息素的声明在邻居间 gossip 分配无人机到图案位置，蒸发同时充当故障检测；用PSO律控制飞行，噪声缓冲Voronoi滤波器防止碰撞，并离线用PSO调参。理论上证明失效声明有界时间消失、交换表的冲突一致解决、幸存者覆盖图案在二倍因子内、安全单元可行时不碰撞。

Result: 在留出种子和两种调参配置下，最难场景95-99%运行成功成图；正常难度下静默损失半数集群后96-100%恢复；未改动迁移到8个未见形状和300架无人机；21,088次运行中仅5次重叠。CBBA风格基线（共享蒸发声明但用argmax无交换）同预算调参后可靠性不低于本方法且修复更快，但本方法对未见形状泛化更好。

Conclusion: 性能收益来自信息素动力学与调参，而非ACO的随机构造或PSO的社会项；集中式oracle更快。

Abstract: We study decentralised drone formation, without a central planner, under the conditions that make it hard: nobody knows how many drones are alive, failures are silent, radio is short-ranged and lossy, and actuation is noisy. In our controller, ant colony optimisation (ACO) allocates drones to picture positions through claims that behave as reinforced, evaporating pheromone and are gossiped between neighbours, so evaporation doubles as failure detection. A particle swarm optimisation (PSO) law flies the drones, a noise-buffered Voronoi filter keeps them apart, and PSO also tunes the controller offline. We prove that claims of destroyed drones vanish within bounded time, that conflicts between drones that exchange tables are resolved consistently, that survivors cover the picture to within a factor of two, and that drones cannot collide while their safety cells are feasible. On held-out seeds and with two tuned configurations, the controller formed the picture in 95-99% of runs in the hardest regime, restored it after a silent loss of half the swarm in 96-100% of runs at normal difficulty, transferred unchanged to eight unseen shapes and to 300 drones, and overlapped in 5 of 21,088 runs. A CBBA-style baseline that shares the evaporating claims but uses argmax without swaps was at least as reliable as ours once tuned with the same budget, and repaired faster, whereas ours generalised better to unseen shapes. The benefit thus comes from the pheromone dynamics and from tuning, not from ACO's stochastic construction or PSO's social terms. A centralised oracle is faster.

</details>


### [130] [SituRecBench: Evaluating Frontier Models as Situated Recommendation Assistants in 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10349)
*Jingtong Yang,Dongding Lin,Jian Wang,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SituRecBench基准，在高保真3D环境中评估视觉语言模型作为具身推荐助手，涵盖空间导航、视觉锚定与多轮社交交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统研究忽视空间导航、视觉锚定与多轮社交交互的综合复杂性，且缺乏从静态检索向物理环境交互助手转型的评估手段。

Method: 构建交互式基准SituRecBench，模拟超市、家居店等真实购物环境，将推荐重构为助手与用户共享第一人称视角的具身辅助过程，要求助手通过对话引导偏好、在环境中锚定推荐并导航用户至相关位置，并以统一交互协议评估对话质量、推荐效用与导航有效性。

Result: 大量实验显示模型在空间锚定与偏好引导方面持续存在困难，难以将全局上下文信息与局部观察有效整合。

Conclusion: SituRecBench为推进现实世界具身助手发展提供了严谨基础。

Abstract: Recommender systems are transitioning from static retrieval engines into interactive assistants grounded in physical environments. However, current studies largely neglect the combined complexities of spatial navigation, visual grounding, and multi-turn social interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce SituRecBench, an interactive benchmark for evaluating frontier vision-language models (VLMs) as situated recommendation assistants within high-fidelity 3D environments. We reformulate recommendation as a situated assistance process in which an assistant interacts with a user while sharing the user's egocentric view. Our benchmark simulates real-world shopping environments (e.g., supermarkets and home furnishings stores), requiring assistants to elicit preferences, ground recommendations in the environment, and guide the user to relevant locations through dialogue. The benchmark evaluates dialogue quality, recommendation utility, and navigation effectiveness through a unified interactive protocol. Extensive experiments reveal persistent challenges in spatial grounding and preference elicitation, highlighting limitations in their ability to integrate global contextual information with local observations. SituRecBench provides a rigorous foundation for advancing the development of situated assistants in the real world.

</details>


### [131] [Rethinking Radar 3D Perception: Representation Learning from Raw Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12334)
*Miao Zhang,Mohamed Abdelsamad,Bin Yang,Sherif Abdulatif,Benedikt Loesch,Marco Altmann,Abhinav Valada,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文挑战原始雷达频谱因噪声过多而无法直接进行表示学习的观点，提出结构是局部破坏下仍可重建的成分，并据此设计泄漏感知的频谱掩码自编码器，在3D感知任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达感知方法依赖手工降维、稀疏点云或紧凑多普勒描述子，认为原始雷达频谱噪声过多不适合直接学习；作者认为真正限制在于缺乏区分结构化信号与噪声的学习目标，而非原始表示本身。

Method: 提出Leakage-aware Spectral MAE，一种针对原始雷达立方体的掩码自编码器，采用受频谱泄漏跨bin能量扩散启发的破坏策略；并将多普勒作为x-y-D表示中的主要空间维度、高度编码为特征，避免简单通道堆叠。

Result: 无需手工降维，该方法在雷达检测器和基于点云的自监督学习对比方法上均持续取得更优性能，并能从稠密4D频谱中有效学习。

Conclusion: 原始雷达频谱结合合适的预训练目标可成为3D感知的有效表示，结构可通过局部破坏下的可重建性来定义，而无需预定义信号-噪声分离。

Abstract: Raw radar spectra are widely viewed as too cluttered and noisy for direct representation learning, motivating handcrafted reductions, sparse point clouds, or compact Doppler descriptors in radar perception. We challenge this assumption and argue that the main limitation lies not in the raw representation itself, but in the absence of a learning objective that separates structured signal from noise. Handcrafted reductions partially address this problem but impose rigid assumptions about what constitutes signal, discarding weak yet informative structure while retaining artifacts that fit the reduction model. Once removed, such information cannot be recovered by supervised learning. We propose an alternative: structure is the component of a radar spectrum that remains reconstructable under localized corruption, while noise is the component that does not. This perspective motivates self-supervised masked modeling for dense radar spectra without predefined signal-noise separation. We instantiate it with Leakage-aware Spectral MAE, a masked autoencoder for raw radar cubes using a corruption strategy inspired by cross-bin energy spreading caused by spectral leakage. We also demonstrate the importance of Doppler organization: treating Doppler as a primary spatial dimension in an x-y-D representation, with height encoded as features, substantially outperforms naive channel stacking and enables effective learning from dense 4D spectra. Without handcrafted reductions, our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art radar-based detectors and point-cloud-based self-supervised learning (SSL) counterparts. These results establish raw radar spectra as an effective representation for 3D perception when paired with an appropriate pretraining objective.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [TACTUS: A Robotic Palpation System for Spatially Heterogeneous Hardness Mapping of Woody Breast Myopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12695)
*Chaitanya Pallerla,Yihong Feng,Avisekh Tripathy,Yang Tian,Pouya Sohrabipour,Amirreza Davar,Casey M. Owens,Cengiz Koparan,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACTUS 是一种自动化触觉阵列，可在约 30 秒内于 20 个解剖位置生成厚度归一化的三维硬度图，用于表征木质胸肌病的空间异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械参考方法依赖单点或顺序压缩测量，难以刻画木质胸肉的空间硬度变异性。

Method: 集成 20 个独立电机驱动触觉单元（含力敏电阻与编码器线性执行器）及 LiDAR 深度相机，实现表面轮廓与自适应压缩；对 100 个鸡胸肉样本（正常、轻度、中度、重度四类）进行评估，采用 ANOVA、相关分析、半变异函数、局部变化熵和异质性分析。

Result: TACTUS 在严重程度类别间检测到显著力差异（ANOVA，p < 0.001），颅侧区域区分最强，并发现纹理分析仪未检测到的显著尾侧效应；样本级力与纹理分析仪测量中度显著相关（Pearson r = 0.441，p < 0.001）；随严重程度增加，硬度变异性呈解剖结构化的上升。

Conclusion: TACTUS 可作为自动化、不依赖操作者的空间分辨力学表征平台，并为高光谱和近红外光谱的木质胸分类系统提供地面真值。

Abstract: Woody breast (WB) myopathy is a spatially heterogeneous fibro-degenerative condition of the broiler Pectoralis major muscle. Existing mechanical reference methods rely on single-point or sequential compression measurements, limiting characterization of spatial hardness variability. This study presents TACTUS (Tactile Adaptive Conformal 3D Uniform Sensing), an automated tactile array that generates thickness-normalized, three-dimensional hardness maps at 20 anatomically distributed locations within approximately 30 s. TACTUS integrates 20 independently motorized tactuators with force-sensing resistors and encoder-driven linear actuators, alongside a LiDAR depth camera for surface profiling and adaptive compression. The system was evaluated using 100 broiler breast fillets across four WB severity classes (Normal, Mild, Moderate, Severe). TACTUS identified significant force differences among severity classes (ANOVA, p < 0.001), with the strongest differentiation in the cranial region and a significant caudal effect not detected by texture-analyzer measurements. Fillet-level TACTUS force showed moderate but significant correlation with texture-analyzer measurements (Pearson r = 0.441, p < 0.001). Semivariogram, local change entropy, and heterogeneity analyses revealed increasing and anatomically structured hardness variability with WB severity. These findings demonstrate TACTUS as an automated, operator-independent platform for spatially resolved mechanical characterization and ground-truth generation to support hyperspectral and near-infrared spectroscopy-based WB classification systems.

</details>


### [133] [Actions Should EARN Their Credit: Test-Time Adaptation and Route Consolidation for Frozen LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11565)
*Bian Sun,Kevin Zhai,Mubarak Shah,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EARN 提出一种无需更新模型参数的 LLM 智能体经验利用方法，通过条件性重放有效动作序列并重新分配动作信用，在有限交互预算下提升并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可通过重复尝试的反馈改进决策，但正向动作信用会持续偏向已反复取得相同分数的动作，阻碍更高分；且在固定交互预算下，末期尝试新动作可能降低表现。

Method: EARN 利用历史经验引导进展并保留已有性能：降低达到此前重复得分的动作信用，提高达到更高得分的动作片段信用，用这些信用调整 LLM 提议动作的概率；在最后若干回合，当当前上下文与记录匹配且动作仍可用时直接重放存储动作，跳过 LLM 候选生成。

Result: 以交互预算末期最后四个回合的平均分为指标，在 Jericho、ScienceWorld 和 Crafter 上，EARN 在被评估方法中于 33 个任务-预算设置中的 31 个取得最佳（含并列）；在 Jericho 各预算下，最后四个回合平均绕过 78.9–86.3% 的候选生成调用。

Conclusion: EARN 通过重分配动作信用与条件性动作重放，在有限交互预算内有效提升并保持 LLM 智能体性能，同时显著减少末期候选生成开销。

Abstract: LLM agents can use feedback from repeated attempts to improve their decisions without updating model parameters. However, positive action credits can continue to favor actions that repeatedly achieve the same score, even when higher scores are achievable. Under a fixed interaction budget, trying new actions in the final attempts can also lower performance. We introduce EARN, a method that uses past experience to guide further progress and retain earlier performance through conditional reuse of effective action sequences. EARN reduces action credits for reaching scores repeatedly attained in earlier episodes and assigns larger credits to action segments that reach higher scores. These credits adjust the probabilities of LLM-proposed actions. During the final episodes, EARN directly replays stored actions when the current context matches the recorded context and the actions remain available, bypassing LLM candidate generation. We evaluate performance at the end of the interaction budget using the average score over the final four episodes. Across Jericho, ScienceWorld, and Crafter, EARN achieves the best score on this metric in 31 of 33 task--budget settings among the evaluated methods, including ties. Across the evaluated Jericho budgets, replay bypasses 78.9--86.3\% of candidate-generation calls in the final four episodes, averaged across games.

</details>


### [134] [Learning Actionable Information Landscapes for Multimodal Active Sensing in Hawkmoths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11011)
*Abdelrahman Sharafeldin,Yaqing Wang,Simon Sponberg,Hannah Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态预测编码框架，从视觉和机械感觉观察中学习减少不确定性的动作地图，并发现这些地图与天蛾行为一致，且基于不确定性门控的词典序算法能提升学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究局限于单模态感知框架，不清楚动作条件信息地图如何从多模态感觉经验中学习。

Method: 引入多模态预测编码框架，在模拟环境中训练变分生成模型，从视觉和机械感觉观察中学习不确定性减少地图；并开发基于感知不确定性的不确定性门控词典序算法。

Result: 学习到的地图捕捉了视觉模式、表面几何和机械感觉线索如何塑造探索动作的价值，与天蛾行为特征一致；自然轨迹倾向于占据高不确定性减少区域；不确定性门控在花蜜搜索中比仅利用、ε-贪婪、固定时间和不确定性校准的非词典序控制学习更好，泛化性和鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 通过生成建模学习的信息景观与生物感知和具身探索相联系。

Abstract: Animals integrate information across sensory modalities to guide exploration, suggesting that they maintain internal estimates of where actions are expected to reduce uncertainty. However, how such action-conditioned information maps are learned from multimodal sensory experience remains unclear, as previous studies have been largely confined to unimodal sensing frameworks. We introduce a multimodal predictive-coding framework for learning uncertainty-reduction maps from visual and mechanosensory observations in hawkmoth flower interaction tasks. A variational generative model trained on simulated environments recovers modality-specific information landscapes over the action space, capturing how visual patterns, surface geometry, and mechanosensory cues shape the value of exploratory actions. These learned maps are consistent with several features of hawkmoth behavior, including nonzero tracking offsets, probing along visual patterns, and geometry-dependent exploration. Applying the model to behavioral data from flower tracking and nectary search, we find that natural trajectories tend to occupy regions of high uncertainty reduction in these learned maps. Building on these observations, we develop an uncertainty-gated lexicographic algorithm that switches between information gathering and reward exploitation based on perceptual uncertainty. In nectary search, uncertainty gating improves learning relative to exploit-only, $\epsilon$-greedy, fixed-time, and uncertainty-calibrated non-lexicographic controls, with better generalization and robustness to sensory and curvature shifts. These results link information landscapes learned through generative modeling to biological sensing and embodied exploration.

</details>


### [135] [DPDP: Diffusion Policy-Steered Diffusion Planner for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10993)
*Ruyi An,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPDP将远视扩散规划器与价值感知扩散策略结合，在规划器的执行步骤用价值优化策略替换行为先验，得到既保持计划转移似然又偏好高价值动作的干净动作分布；提供免训练采样和偏好目标两种实现，在D4RL上优于所用规划器，在长程导航上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中扩散模型承担两类互补角色：扩散策略价值感知但短视，从不规划未来状态；扩散规划器远视但执行时仅行为驱动。二者优势恰好互补，因此需要将二者结合以兼顾远视规划与高价值动作选择。

Method: 在规划器的执行步骤（计划转移变为动作处）融合二者：将规划器的逆动力学执行器视为动作后验，其环境转移似然关联动作与计划下一状态，行为先验按离线数据加权；用价值优化扩散策略替换该先验，保持计划转移似然而偏好可服务计划的高价值动作。针对所得干净动作分布采用两种方式：(1)免训练采样器在匹配动作噪声水平上组合策略与逆动力学得分并施加Langevin校正，使用共享动作噪声调度和近似共同终端先验；(2)偏好目标将迁移摊销进单一扩散执行器。

Result: 在D4RL上，DPDP改进了其所基于的规划器，在locomotion任务上与反应式扩散策略保持竞争力，并在长程导航任务上领先。

Conclusion: 将价值优化扩散策略嵌入扩散规划器的执行步骤，可在保留规划器远视计划转移似然的同时引入价值偏好，免训练采样与偏好摊销两种实现均可行，验证了规划与策略互补结合的有效性。

Abstract: In offline reinforcement learning, diffusion models fill two roles with complementary strengths. Typically guided by a critic, a diffusion policy is value-aware and favors high-return, behavior-supported actions, but it is short-sighted: it never lays out the states it intends to visit. A diffusion planner is far-sighted, synthesizing such a long-horizon trajectory, but it is only behavior-driven at execution, realizing each planned transition using only offline this http URL is strong exactly where the other is weak. We introduce DPDP to bring them together at the planner's execution step, where a planned transition becomes an action. Reading the planner's inverse-dynamics executor as a posterior over actions: the environment's transition likelihood relates an action to the planned next state, while the behavioral prior determines its weight under the offline data. Replacing this prior with a value-optimized diffusion policy preserves the planned transition likelihood and favors valuable actions that can serve the plan. We target the resulting clean-action distribution in two ways. A training-free sampler composes policy and inverse-dynamics scores at matched action noise levels and applies Langevin correction; this route uses a shared action noise schedule and an approximately common terminal prior. A preference objective instead amortizes the transfer into a single diffusion executor. Across D4RL, DPDP improves the planner it builds on, staying competitive with reactive diffusion policies on locomotion and leading on long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [136] [Stochastic Port-Hamiltonian Gaussian Processes for Learning from Noisy Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10864)
*Thribhuvan Rapolu,Luke McLennan,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LieSPHGP是一种基于李群和Gaussian过程的随机框架，用于学习具有port-Hamiltonian结构和稳定性感知鲁棒性的刚体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测数据稀疏且含噪，而动力学在非线性配置流形上演化并受环境耗散和随机扰动影响；现有神经动力学模型易违反物理结构、长期累积误差或引入万向节锁等奇异性。

Method: 在Lie群和Lie代数上表示运动以避免奇异参数化；用Matérn核Gaussian过程分别建模质量、势能、耗散和控制分量，并引入扩散项表示随机强迫；采用李群指数映射更新保持旋转约束，正定阻尼确保非负能量耗散，隐显式积分器为冻结线性阻尼子系统提供无条件稳定性。

Result: 在多个含噪刚体基准上，相比神经动力学基线，预测精度更高、长期稳定性更强、流形约束满足更好。

Conclusion: LieSPHGP能学习对噪声鲁棒、尊重刚体几何并同时捕捉内在运动与环境耗散的预测动力学模型。

Abstract: Learning predictive models for dynamics is difficult because observations are often sparse and noisy, while the underlying dynamics evolve on nonlinear configuration manifolds and are affected by environmental dissipation and stochastic disturbances. Standard neural dynamics models can fit short-term trajectories but often violate physical structure, accumulate error over long horizons, or rely on parameterizations that introduce singularities such as gimbal lock. We introduce LieSPHGP, a stochastic Gaussian-process framework for learning rigid body dynamics with port-Hamiltonian structure and stability-aware robustness. LieSPHGP represents motion on Lie groups and Lie algebras, enabling coordinate-consistent optimization without singular parameterizations. Learned mass, potential energy, dissipation, and control components retain physical roles and a separate diffusion term represents stochastic forcing. Each component is modeled by Matérn kernel Gaussian processes which capture uncertainty induced by noisy observations. To improve physical validity, Lie group exponential map updates preserve rotational constraints, while positive-definite damping ensures nonnegative deterministic energy dissipation. The implicit-explicit integrator additionally provides unconditional stability for the frozen linear damping subsystem. The resulting model learns predictive dynamics that are robust to noise, respect rigid body geometry, and capture both intrinsic motion and environmental dissipation. Experiments on various noisy rigid body benchmarks show improved prediction accuracy, stronger long-horizon stability, and better satisfaction of manifold constraints compared with neural dynamics baselines. Code, data and interactive results are available on the project website: this https URL

</details>


### [137] [Intra-Option Fitted Q-Evaluation: Evaluating Hierarchical Policies from Non-Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10543)
*Yunfu Deng,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出IO-FQE方法，用于分层策略的离线策略评估，无需选项标注，在长时域任务中消除偏差并显著降低均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层策略的离线评估方法（如选项级重要性采样）需要选项标注且方差随horizon增长，而直接扩展拟合Q评估（FQE）到分层策略会产生偏差。

Method: 提出Intra-Option Fitted Q-Evaluation（IO-FQE），在增广的状态-选项空间中估计价值函数，并开发了两种连续动作实例化方法。

Result: 在长时域分层连续控制任务（AntMaze导航和OGBench拼图操作）上，IO-FQE消除了朴素FQE的偏差，并相比选项级重要性采样大幅降低了均方误差。

Conclusion: IO-FQE能够有效评估分层策略，即使缺乏选项标注，且在长时域任务中表现出更低的偏差和方差。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the expected return of a target policy from previously collected data without additional environment interaction. While OPE methods for flat Markovian policies are well studied, little work has addressed evaluating hierarchical policies in which a high-level policy selects temporally extended options that generate primitive actions until a termination condition is met. The primary existing approach applies per-decision importance sampling at the option level, but requires option-annotated trajectories and exhibits variance that still grows with the horizon. For non-hierarchical policies, fitted Q-evaluation (FQE) typically achieves lower error than importance sampling by leveraging the classic Bellman equation for policy evaluation; however, a naive extension of FQE to hierarchical policies results in a biased policy value estimate. In this paper, we introduce Intra-Option Fitted Q-Evaluation (IO-FQE), which estimates a value function in the augmented state-option space; this approach enables OPE of hierarchical policies even when we lack annotations of what option was run in the data. We develop two continuous-action instantiations of IO-FQE and show on long-horizon hierarchical continuous control tasks (AntMaze navigation and OGBench Puzzle manipulation) that IO-FQE eliminates the bias incurred by a naive application of FQE to hierarchical policies and substantially lowers mean squared-error compared to option-level importance sampling.

</details>


### [138] [An Evaluation of Tabular Foundation Models as Training-Free World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11921)
*Xian Yeow Lee,Ryoichi Takase,Ahmed Farahat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预训练表格基础模型（TabFM）可作为无训练动力学模型，在数据稀缺且上下文充足时表现良好，但尚不能替代训练好的世界模型用于规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常需要针对特定任务进行大量交互数据训练。作者探究预训练表格基础模型（TabFM）能否通过将下一状态预测视为转移对上的上下文回归，作为一种无需训练的世界模型，并明确其适用与失效场景。

Method: 在合成动力系统和机器人运动基准上，将TabFM与从头训练的LSTM、MLP以及高斯过程等回归器进行对比，评估单步预测精度、自回归多步展开精度（不同上下文长度）及推理速度。

Result: 在几乎全部有限转移数据设置下，TabFM比从头训练的LSTM和MLP更准确；在运动任务上，它是拟合相同数据的最准确回归器，但在平滑低维系统上高斯过程更优；全量数据训练的模型在多数系统上仍显著更好。自回归展开精度强烈依赖上下文长度：短上下文下迅速退化至平凡基线，长上下文下显著提升，并在一个运动任务上超过训练好的LSTM；推理速度比LSTM慢约三个数量级。

Conclusion: 当前阶段，无训练先验在数据稀缺且上下文充足时可作为有效预测器，但尚不能替代训练好的世界模型用于规划。

Abstract: World models are widely considered to be a potential key component of model-based reinforcement learning and robot planning, but typically require task-specific training on substantial interaction data. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model (TabFM) can serve as a training-free dynamics model for world modeling by framing next-state prediction as in-context regression over transition pairs, and where such a model helps and where it breaks. Across synthetic dynamical systems and robot locomotion benchmarks, TabFM is more accurate than LSTM and MLP models trained from scratch on the same limited transitions in almost all settings, and among regressors fitted to the same data it is the most accurate on locomotion, while a Gaussian process is more accurate on smooth low-dimensional systems. However, models trained on the full dataset remain substantially better on most systems. When rolled out autoregressively over multiple steps, TabFM's accuracy depends strongly on how much context it is given. With a short context, its rollouts quickly degrade to the level of a trivial baseline, whereas with a long context they improve substantially and, on one locomotion task, exceed a trained LSTM. Inference is also about three orders of magnitude slower than an LSTM. Overall, in their current state, we find that training-free priors are useful as predictors when data are scarce and enough context is available, but they are not yet a substitute for trained world models in planning.

</details>


### [139] [Where Should Models Run? A Collaborative Inference Framework for Embodied Intelligence in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11023)
*Wanlu Sun,Zhenhui Yuan,Xiang Ji,Yi Han,Qing Li,Gan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文综述了具身智能的协同推理，提出以边缘为中心的框架并实验验证最优模型划分点随网络条件和模型结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能系统需要实时感知、推理和控制，但大规模AI模型（LLM、VLA）在机器人平台上受限于计算资源和能耗，因此需要确定推理应在何处执行。

Method: 综述协同推理现状，提出由边缘协调模型划分与执行的边缘中心框架；通过WiFi和5G网络下的仿真与真实实验，在Jetson Nano/AGX Xavier设备与AGX Thor边缘服务器之间测试ResNet-101、Qwen2.5-VL-7B和OpenVLA-7B的协同推理。

Result: 实验表明最优划分策略随网络条件和模型结构变化，并识别出不同无线条件下各模型的最小化延迟划分点。

Conclusion: 协同推理是具身智能部署大模型的有效方案，边缘中心框架可行，未来可面向6G赋能的具身智能系统发展。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence systems rely on fast and reliable inference to enable real-time perception, reasoning, and control. Recent advances in large-scale artificial intelligence (AI) models, including Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have significantly enhanced the capabilities of embodied agents in complex environments. However, deploying these models remains challenging due to the limited computational resources and energy budgets of robotic platforms. A fundamental question is where inference should be executed. Collaborative inference, which partitions models across end devices, edge servers, and the cloud, has emerged as a promising solution. Yet determining the optimal partitioning point remains an open problem, as it depends on wireless network conditions, model structure, and privacy requirements. This article reviews the state of the art in collaborative inference for embodied intelligence and proposes an edge-centric framework in which the edge coordinates model partitioning and execution. Through both simulation and real-world experiments over WiFi and 5G networks, we demonstrate that the optimal partitioning strategy varies with network conditions and model structure. We further identify latency-minimizing partition points for ResNet-101, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and OpenVLA-7B in collaborative inference between NVIDIA Jetson Nano or AGX Xavier devices and an AGX Thor edge server under varying wireless conditions. Finally, we discuss the future directions for collaborative inference in 6G-empowered embodied intelligence systems.

</details>


### [140] [Horizon-Scalable Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10987)
*Haoran Xu,Pragya Srivastava,Xinjie Liu,Siddhant Agarwal,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HQL通过自动在单步与多步回报之间平衡，实现长视界离线Q学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线Q学习随任务视界增长而变难：单步TD误差逐步累积，早期状态估值偏差大；多步TD虽缩短误差链，但行为策略次优导致每步动作引入偏差，固定步数不适用于整条轨迹。

Method: 提出Horizon-Scalable Q-Learning（HQL）：仅当critic对数据集动作的估值不低于从BC策略采样的best-of-N动作时，才沿数据集动作扩展目标，否则回退到单步目标；该规则是一种无需动作密度的原则性离策略修正，兼容flow与diffusion策略。

Result: 在跨视界的组合锁任务上，HQL避免了单步与多步TD的失败；在长视界OGBench和OCBench任务上优于现有视界缩减方法。

Conclusion: HQL通过自动平衡单步与多步回报，实现了准确的长视界离策略价值学习。

Abstract: Learning accurate off-policy state-action value functions (Q-functions) from offline datasets becomes harder as the task horizon grows. One-step temporal-difference (TD) learning bootstraps after every transition, so small per-step errors accumulate along the horizon, and early states can be badly misvalued even when the TD loss is small. Multi-step TD shortens this chain by following the dataset trajectory for several steps; however, since the behavior policy is often suboptimal, every followed action adds a bias, and no fixed number of steps suits a whole trajectory. In this paper, we propose Horizon-Scalable Q-Learning (HQL), a simple method that enables accurate long-horizon value learning by automatically balancing between one-step and multi-step returns. HQL extends a target through a dataset action only when the critic values it at least as highly as the best-of-N actions sampled from the BC policy, and otherwise falls back to a one-step target. We show that this rule is a principled off-policy correction that needs no action densities, which makes HQL compatible with expressive flow and diffusion policies. Experiments show that HQL avoids the failures of both one-step and multi-step TD on a combination-lock task across horizons, and outperforms prior horizon-reduction methods on long-horizon OGBench and OCBench tasks.

</details>


### [141] [$Σ$-LeWM: Learning World Models via S-Information for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12732)
*Xinrui Zu,Michael Przystupa,Zhao Yang,Shujian Yu,Kevin Sebastian Luck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在LeWorldModel基础上增加反向动力学预测器和逆向动力学动作预测器两个潜在损失，可进一步约束潜在嵌入结构，提升性能与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型近期倾向于不使用解码器，仅依赖潜在前向动力学预测器和熵稳定项来学习嵌入，但此类方法在实时机器人控制中的性能与规划效率仍需提升。

Method: 在LeWorldModel中引入两个额外的潜在损失：反向动力学预测器和逆向动力学动作预测器，以进一步结构化潜在嵌入。

Result: 在标准世界模型基准和两个模拟机器人操作任务上，所提方法在性能和规划效率两方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: 额外潜在损失能有效增强潜在嵌入结构，使无解码器世界模型更适用于真实机器人的实时基于模型的控制。

Abstract: World models have increasingly been applied as a model-based control method for robotics tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. While early world model architectures relied on decoders to prevent mode collapse and learn a predictable latent space, recent works on world models, such as Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture or LeWorldModel, increasingly aim to learn embeddings without a grounding decoder, utilising only a latent forward dynamics predictor and a stabilising entropy term. In this paper, we set out to demonstrate that the further introduction of two additional latent losses to LeWorldModel, a backward dynamics predictor and an inverse dynamics action predictor, enforces further structure on the latent embedding, leading to further gains in both performance and planning efficiency. These are critical properties for improving the applicability of such methods to real-time model-based control of real-world robots. We demonstrate these gains on standard world model benchmarks and two simulated robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [142] [Simulation-aided Reinforcement Learning with Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12643)
*Omer Hemo,Ido Sefi,Mirco Mutti,Aviv Tamar,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于控制变量的保守模拟辅助强化学习方法，模拟数据仅用于降低学习方差而非直接塑造学习目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依赖目标环境数据，成本高且噪声大；模拟器可生成大量数据，但标准模拟混合方法直接混合模拟与真实经验，会因模拟器不准确（尤其当偏差使解偏离最优策略时）而降低性能。

Method: 基于控制变量的保守模拟辅助学习方法：模拟数据仅用于减少学习过程中的方差；先在策略评估中开发该方法，再结合actor-critic框架进行策略改进，并扩展到离线强化学习场景。

Result: 在多个MuJoCo基准任务上实验，相比标准模拟混合方法取得改进，表明控制变量是模拟辅助强化学习的有效工具。

Conclusion: 控制变量方法能有效利用模拟数据降低方差，避免模拟器不准确带来的负面偏差，提升模拟辅助强化学习性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms often rely on data collected from a target environment, which can be expensive and noisy. Although simulators can generate large amounts of additional data, standard simulation mixing approaches, which directly combine simulated and real experience, can degrade performance due to simulator inaccuracies, especially when they bias the solution away from the optimal policy. We propose a conservative simulation-aided learning method based on control variates, where simulated data is used solely to reduce variance in the learning process rather than directly shaping the learning target. We develop our approach for Policy-Evaluation, which is a fundamental building block of RL algorithms. Then we combine it within the actor-critic framework towards policy improvement, as well as extend it towards offline RL scenarios. Experiments on multiple MuJoCo benchmark tasks demonstrate improvements over standard simulation mixing approaches, highlighting control variates as an effective tool for simulation-aided reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [143] [LawKAN: Group-Law Layers for Composition -- When Imposing a Known Law Pays Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12567)
*Anna Mishchenko,Nikolai Gavrilin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将已知非交换群律嵌入自包含层节点，仅学习局部增量与零初始化校正项，从而在旋转组合任务上以极少参数逼近贝叶斯下界，并在真实机械臂上优于高斯-牛顿法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多任务按已知规则组合变换（如链式旋转），学习模型需重新学习该规则并累积误差，经典估计器硬编码规则却忽略硬件偏差；因此希望强制已知结构、仅学习剩余部分。

Method: 将精确非交换群律置于自包含层节点，边学习局部增量，零初始化校正ψ学习律的未知偏差；提出预训练准则预测精确可表示性。

Result: 固定角度SU(2)组合中，54参数稠密边为最准稠密模型，参数比最强固定矩阵基线少27倍，距贝叶斯下界仅0.05度；真实机械臂上306参数ψ在N≥60时达1.12-1.14 mm，优于高斯-牛顿的1.29-1.33 mm，且跨三厂商两协议迁移。

Conclusion: 该方案在数据服从所施加群律时匹配经典拟合，在群律不适用于任务时性能下降。

Abstract: Many tasks compose transformations by a known rule: chained rotations in robotics and navigation. Learned models relearn it, compounding error; classical estimators hardcode it and miss hardware deviations. We impose the known structure and learn only the remainder: an exact non-commutative group law sits in the node of a self-contained layer, its edges learn local increments, and a zero-initialized correction psi learns unknown deviations of the law. A pre-training criterion predicts exact representability. On fixed-angle SU(2) composition, a 54-parameter dense edge is the most accurate dense model, at 27x fewer parameters than the strongest fixed-matrix baseline, and lies 0.05 deg above the Bayes floor. On real robot arms, an isolated 306-parameter psi reaches 1.12-1.14 mm versus 1.29-1.33 mm for Gauss-Newton at N >= 60, and the recipe transfers across three vendors and two protocols. The recipe matches classical fits where the data obey the law and loses where the imposed law is not the task's.

</details>


### [144] [RAIL: Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning for Student-Compatible Teacher Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12251)
*Meraj Mammadov,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Johannes Andreas Stork*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAIL通过在教师训练阶段学习教师与学生观测共享的对比学习潜表示，并将教师策略约束在该空间内训练，从而显著缩小模仿差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始传感器输入进行强化学习面临高维观测和稀疏奖励的挑战；特权信息教师策略蒸馏到学生策略时，教师依赖学生不可得的信息会导致不可消除的模仿差距，现有方法多靠奖励塑形或事后微调来缓解。

Method: 提出表示对齐模仿学习（RAIL）：用对比学习学习教师与学生观测共享的潜在表示，并直接在该共享空间中训练教师策略，使教师行为可从学生观测复现。

Result: 在多个环境中，RAIL无需奖励修改或事后学生微调即优于强基线，并超过直接从原始观测的强化学习；学习到的表示还支持仅训练教师即可零样本迁移到新任务。

Conclusion: 在教师训练阶段对齐表示即可有效减少模仿差距并保持任务性能，是一种优于奖励塑形与事后微调的蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) from raw sensory inputs such as images or onboard sensors can be challenging due to high-dimensional observations and sparse rewards. A common strategy is to train a teacher policy with access to privileged state information and then distill it into a student policy that acts from raw inputs alone. However, when the teacher relies on information unavailable to the student, exact imitation may be impossible, creating an irreducible imitation gap. Existing approaches typically mitigate this mismatch through careful reward shaping or additional reinforcement learning on the student policy after the teacher has been trained. Instead, we introduce Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning (RAIL), which addresses the mismatch during teacher training itself. RAIL learns a latent representation shared across teacher and student observations using contrastive learning, and trains the teacher policy directly in this space. Because the teacher policy is constrained to operate on this shared space, it learns behaviors that are reproducible from student observations. This substantially reduces the imitation gap while preserving task performance. Across multiple environments, RAIL outperforms strong baselines without reward modification or post-hoc student fine-tuning, and surpasses direct reinforcement learning from raw observations. The learned representations further enable zero-shot transfer to new tasks through teacher-only training. Project site: this https URL .

</details>


### [145] [How Many Candidates Does a One-Step World Model Need? A Random-Codebook Account of Nearest-of-m Training on Frozen DINO Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11887)
*Yong Hwi Song,Keon Myung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在一阶随机世界模型中，nearest-of-m（IMLE）目标在模式不确定性下优于流匹配和能量分数生成器，m由码本失真I(m)决定，并存在记忆化、模式概率平坦化和连续高维结果欠离散三个可预测特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶随机世界模型可避免确定性特征预测器的模式平均问题，且无需扩散/流匹配每步数十次网络评估；近期工作猜测小m足以用于特征空间世界模型，并认为严格proper的能量分数应取代硬最小值，本文检验这两个主张。

Method: 在冻结的DINOv2/v3 patch token上训练一阶世界模型，使用可枚举未来的基准，比较nearest-of-m（m=16）、50步流匹配、一阶流生成器和能量分数生成器（参数量匹配）；将nearest-of-m目标解释为从生成器抽取m个样本的随机码本的期望失真，并据此预注册三项预测进行验证；同时采用自rollout的逐步匹配并在随机Push-T变体上评估。

Result: m=16的nearest-of-m在模式类不确定性下比50步流匹配（约1/30延迟）、一阶流生成器和能量分数生成器更好地覆盖可枚举未来，rollout保持在token流形上而流rollout漂移；码本失真I(m)而非漏失概率支配记忆化强度，能量分数生成器提高m也无法摆脱；模式概率随m平坦化，与已知最优匹配在0.01以内；连续高维结果上出现欠离散，限制真实机器人视频应用；自rollout逐步匹配将长时程误差降低三分之一，消融分离了流形与覆盖收益；规划成功率未提升，尽管风险估计准确，并给出诊断。

Conclusion: 小m的nearest-of-m是特征空间一阶世界模型的有效目标，其行为可由码本失真I(m)统一解释；能量分数替代硬最小值的猜想不成立；方法在模式覆盖和流形保持上优于流匹配，但受连续高维欠离散限制，且规划成功未受益于准确风险估计。

Abstract: One-step stochastic world models avoid the mode averaging of deterministic feature predictors without the tens of network evaluations per step that diffusion and flow matching require. The simplest such objective is nearest-of-m matching (conditional implicit maximum likelihood estimation, IMLE): draw m candidate futures and pull only the one closest to the observed outcome. Recent work conjectures that a small m suffices for feature-space world models and that a strictly proper energy score should replace the hard minimum. We test both claims with one-step world models on frozen DINOv2/v3 patch tokens, using benchmarks whose futures can be enumerated. Under mode-like uncertainty, nearest-of-m with m=16 covers the enumerated futures better than 50-step flow matching (at about 1/30 of its latency), one-step flow generators and an energy-score generator at matched parameter counts, and its rollouts stay on the token manifold where flow rollouts drift. One quantity governs the choice of m: the nearest-of-m objective is the expected distortion of a random codebook of m draws from the generator. This view yields three predictions, recorded before the corresponding runs: (i) memorization of the realized outcome in contexts that never repeat, whose strength is governed by the codebook's miss distortion I(m) rather than its miss probability, and which an energy-score generator does not escape by raising m; (ii) a flattening of mode probabilities with m, matching a known optimum within 0.01; and (iii) under-dispersion on continuous high-dimensional outcomes, which bounds the method on real robot video. Per-step matching on self-rollouts cuts long-horizon error by a third on a stochastic Push-T variant; an ablation separates its manifold and coverage benefits. Planning success does not improve despite accurate risk estimates, a negative result we report with its diagnosis.

</details>


### [146] [DeltaForge: A Post-hoc Mixture-of-Experts VLA for Multi-Domain Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11856)
*Bingzhang Wang,Mo Poorsartep,Yasaman Esfandiari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaForge是一种事后混合专家VLA模型，通过SVD分解将多个独立微调的领域模型权重拆分为共享部分与微小领域特定部分，并用训练于代理数据集的双盲路由器实现高效多域整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶VLA模型通常按领域逐个微调共享基座，导致专家模型成本高昂且难以整合；权重合并随异构领域增多而精度下降，稠密MoE路由成本过高。

Method: 利用不同操作者对同一基座微调时权重更新共享大量结构的特性，联合SVD将所有更新分解为共享部分和微小领域特定部分；路由器仅处理这些小规模领域特定片段；训练两个协作的片段级盲路由器（分别针对VLM推理专家和流匹配动力学专家），使用代理数据集上的软标签。

Result: 在涵盖城市、越野和高速驾驶的六个多样领域（汽车、卡车、全地形车）上，DeltaForge接近各领域专家精度，在接近单模型参数与计算成本下超越所有合并、路由、集中式和联邦基线；新增领域仅需将其权重更新投影到共享基，无需端到端重训；模型还能泛化到未显式训练的领域组合。

Conclusion: DeltaForge首次实现了驾驶领域的事后MoE VLA，以低参数与计算成本高效整合多个领域专家，支持可扩展的领域增量并具备组合泛化能力。

Abstract: Fleets of driving vision-language-action (VLA) models are developed one domain at a time: each operator finetunes a shared base model on private data, producing specialized experts that are costly to consolidate into a single model. Weight merging loses accuracy as heterogeneous domains accumulate, and dense mixture-of-experts routing is prohibitively expensive to combine them. We present DeltaForge, to our knowledge the first post-hoc mixture-of-experts VLA for driving, which consolidates N independently finetuned per-domain models into a single multi-domain model from their weights and a small proxy set alone. When different operators finetune the same base model, their weight updates end up sharing a lot of structure. We exploit this by decomposing all the updates together using singular value decomposition (SVD), splitting each one into a shared part (the same across all domains) and a tiny domain-specific part.
