The router then only needs to work with those tiny domain-specific pieces, not the full expert weights, which is what keeps the approach efficient. Then two cooperating clip-level blind routers, one over the VLM reasoning experts and one over the flow-matching dynamics experts, are trained from soft labels on the proxy dataset. Across six diverse domains spanning urban, off-road, and highway driving on cars, trucks, and all-terrain vehicles (ATVs), DeltaForge approaches the accuracy of the per-domain experts and surpasses every merging, routing, centralized, and federated baseline at close to single-model parameter and compute cost. Adding a new domain requires only projecting its weight update onto the shared basis with no end-to-end retraining needed. The resulting model also generalizes to domain combinations it was never explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [147] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Internal Computation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10822)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应内部计算（AIC）策略，学习何时停止精炼潜在表示并行动，以降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代神经策略通常对每个决策花费相同计算，但额外处理带来的收益因状态而异，需要状态依赖的计算分配。

Method: 联合训练动作选择与停止，使用期望深度惩罚和中间值预测作为辅助监督，实现自适应停止。

Result: 在12个基准任务上平均执行3.3步（最多8步），四个控制与机器人任务延迟降低31-44%；平均奖励在7个任务上超过最佳固定深度策略，4个任务上略低；消融显示停止机制在PickCube和AntMaze上贡献主要奖励增益，监督在RareEvent上贡献主要增益。

Conclusion: AIC能根据状态自适应分配计算，在保持或提升奖励的同时显著降低延迟，验证了状态依赖计算分配的有效性。

Abstract: Iterative neural policies usually spend the same computation on every decision, although the benefit of additional processing varies across states. We introduce Adaptive Internal Computation (AIC), an iterative policy that learns when to stop refining its latent representation and act. In AIC, we train action selection and stopping jointly, an expected-depth penalty values computation and intermediate value predictions provide auxiliary supervision. We show that when only some states benefit from further refinement, an ideal state-dependent allocator outperforms every state-independent stopping schedule at the same expected depth. With at most eight refinement steps per decision, AIC executes 3.3 steps on average across twelve benchmark tasks and lowers per-decision latency by 31--44% relative to a fixed eight-step policy on four control and robotics tasks. Its mean reward exceeds the best fixed-depth policy on seven tasks and trails it on four. Ablating adaptive stopping and value supervision separately shows that stopping accounts for most of the reward gain on PickCube and AntMaze, where it also executes more refinement steps and that supervision accounts for most of it on RareEvent.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [World In-Context Learning for Agentic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12885)
*Heng Fang,Zehui Guo,Yuehan Zhang,Mingyang Du,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World In-Context Learning (WICL) 范式与 RoboScript 框架，让冻结模型通过仿真排练把任务经验写入上下文而非权重，实现无需梯度更新的机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练式动作策略把操作技能耦合到训练分布上，难以泛化；在上下文学习（ICL）虽避免耦合，但因跨场景演示缺乏当前世界反馈而性能不及训练策略。作者观察到智能体在上下文中学习的是语义（任务步骤与物体角色），而非视觉外观，因此经验可跨世界迁移。

Method: 基于该洞察提出 WICL：冻结模型从单次演示出发，在仿真等世界中排练任务，并将反馈写入上下文而非权重。构建模块化基础设施 RoboScript，从单次演示经仿真排练学习每个任务，将知识冻结为 Task Memory，并在新场景部署，采用数据隔离确保每次部署输入在真实机器人上均可得。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个任务上，每任务仅一次演示且无梯度更新，RoboScript 在干净与随机化设置下平均成功率达 83.4%，超过最强训练式动作策略 20 分以上；方法可跨视觉分布泛化、通过反思改进、随基础模型规模提升，并经跨具身记忆适配迁移到真实双臂机器人。

Conclusion: 将世界置于循环中，机器人可以在上下文中学习操作任务，效果可与在权重中学习相媲美。

Abstract: Trained action policies write manipulation skill into network weights, tying what is learned to the distribution it is learned from. In-context learning (ICL) avoids this coupling but has not matched trained policies, because a demonstration recorded in another scene carries no feedback from the world the robot currently faces. We observe that what an agent learns in context is semantic, recording task steps and object roles rather than visual appearance, so experience gathered in one world transfers to another. Based on this insight, we propose World In-Context Learning (WICL), a paradigm in which a frozen model starts from a demonstration, rehearses the task in a world such as a simulator, and writes the feedback into its context rather than its weights. We build RoboScript, a modular infrastructure that implements WICL for agentic manipulation. From a single demonstration, RoboScript learns each task through rehearsal in simulation, freezes the learned knowledge as a Task Memory, and deploys the frozen memory on new scenes with data isolation that ensures every deployment input is available on a real robot. On all 50 tasks of RoboTwin 2.0, RoboScript reaches 83.4% mean success across clean and randomized settings with one demonstration per task and no gradient updates, exceeding the strongest trained action policy by over 20 points. The method generalizes across visual distributions, improves through reflection, scales with the foundation model, and transfers to a physical dual-arm robot through cross-embodiment memory adaptation. With a world in the loop, a robot can learn a manipulation task in its context as well as it learns one in its weights. All code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [149] [Grasping the Future: Joint 3D Hand-Object Forecasting From Internet Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12879)
*Rustin Soraki,Matthew Wallingford,Ryan Aby,Brandon Chee,Jason Ren,Ali Farhadi,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Roozbeh Mottaghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Grasping-the-Future，一个统一生成模型，联合建模3D手-物运动（腕部、手指关节、物体位姿），通过掩码时空补全与flow matching训练，利用互联网视频重建3D监督，在多项预测任务上优于现有方法并可迁移至机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与物体交互需要同时预测物体如何运动以及手应如何随之运动；人类视频蕴含丰富的日常交互关系，是学习未来交互预测的自然数据源，但需要从已完成的交互中恢复运动并用于预测未来。

Method: 构建统一生成模型联合建模腕部运动、手指关节与物体位置朝向；采用掩码时空补全和flow matching训练单一模型以支持未来交互预测、由物体预测手/由手预测物体、生成趋近期望末态位姿的轨迹、补全部分观测交互等任务；显式建模手-物关系并引入接触约束以保持抓取关系；设计可扩展流程，结合预训练感知模型与联合几何优化从互联网视频重建完整交互的3D轨迹，并与标注数据集共同作为训练数据。

Result: 在所有任务上表现强劲，在具3D标注的数据集上优于已发布的预测器；将预测轨迹重定向到机器人实体后验证了几何与物理合理性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可从人类视频中学习统一的3D手-物联合运动生成，实现多任务交互预测，并具备向机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: To interact with an object, we need to anticipate both how it will move and how our hands should move with it. Human videos capture these relationships across everyday activities, offering a natural source for learning to predict future interactions. Learning from them requires recovering motion from completed interactions and using it to anticipate what could happen next. We present $\textit{Grasping-the-Future}$, a unified generative model of joint 3D hand-object motion, including wrist motion, finger articulation, and object position and orientation. Through masked spatiotemporal completion and flow matching, we train a single model for several prediction tasks: forecasting future interactions, predicting hand motion given object motion or vice versa, generating both trajectories toward a desired final object pose, and completing partially observed interactions. We explicitly model hand-object relations and incorporate contact constraints to help preserve grasp relationships during motion. To obtain 3D supervision from internet videos, we develop a scalable pipeline that combines pretrained perception models with joint geometric optimization to reconstruct completed interactions. We combine these recovered trajectories with annotated interaction datasets as training data for all tasks. We demonstrate strong performance across all tasks, outperforming released forecasters on datasets with 3D annotations. Moreover, we show geometric and physical plausibility of the predicted trajectories by retargeting them to a robotic embodiment. Videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [150] [WanderBench: Can World Models Explore Dynamic Worlds from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12557)
*Yuhang Chen,Jiheng Li,Jiale Wang,Zai Wang,Guangyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出WanderBench基准测试视频世界模型在动态场景探索中的保真度，并发现现有模型在长视频中丢失相机控制和源动态、重访一致性差；提出免训练框架WanderLand，通过帧级源条件与重访自条件提升五个模型各维度表现，源动态保真度最高提升8.3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频条件世界模型可用于重拍、自由视角回放和具身智能体真实到仿真环境，但现有基准仅从单图或文本出发，视频条件生成主要评估感知质量与相机跟随，无法直接衡量探索中主体身份、事件进展及重访场景一致性是否保持。

Method: 构建WanderBench：745段真实与AI生成单镜头源视频，分三档难度（3-25秒），配键盘动作相机路线，含24个经人工验证的子指标、覆盖六个维度；评测八个模型。提出WanderLand：免训练框架，将冻结模型每帧条件于同时刻源帧，重访时条件于自身早期静态内容。

Result: 长片段下模型保持静态场景与视觉质量，但丢失相机控制和源动态，且无模型保持重访地点一致性；WanderLand在五个模型上提升所有维度，源动态保真度最高提升8.3分。

Conclusion: WanderBench揭示了视频世界模型在动态探索保真度上的关键缺陷，WanderLand作为免训练方案有效改善多模型多维度表现，为重访一致性与源动态保持提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Video world models conditioned on an input video make it possible to explore real, recorded dynamic scenes, enabling re-shooting of footage, free-viewpoint replay, and real-to-sim environments for embodied agents. Such uses require the generated rollout to stay faithful to the recorded world, yet existing benchmarks start from a single image or a text prompt, and video-conditioned generation is evaluated mainly on perceptual quality and camera following. These evaluations do not directly assess whether exploration preserves subject identity, event progression, and scene consistency upon revisiting a location. We introduce WanderBench, a benchmark for exploring dynamic worlds from video. It comprises 745 single-shot source videos of real and AI-generated content in three difficulty tiers (3-25 s), paired with camera routes given as keyboard actions, and evaluates six dimensions with 24 human-validated sub-metrics. Benchmarking eight models, we find that as clips grow longer, models keep the static scene and visual quality but lose camera control and the source dynamics, and that none keeps revisited places consistent. We further propose WanderLand, a training-free framework that conditions each frame of a frozen model on the same-instant source and, on revisits, on its own earlier static content. WanderLand improves five models on every dimension, by up to 8.3 points in source-dynamics fidelity.

</details>


### [151] [QCC-WM: Linking Macro-State Dynamics and Visual Generation via Quotient Causal Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12353)
*Zhizhong Fu,Wei Zhou,Zhaoyang Jiang,Yudong Lin,Songlun Li,Zhi Li,Feifan Chen,Gaoming Li,Xiaorong Ding,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QCC-WM提出一种基于商空间因果构造的视觉世界模型，通过动作响应构建宏变量并递归预测，实现与视觉生成一致的动作条件世界建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界建模的关键挑战是从高维观测中构建支持递归预测的宏状态，并保持其预测演化与生成观测的视觉变化一致。

Method: QCC-WM利用动作响应引导宏变量构建，采用共享参考商测量约束预测状态与观测变化的一致性；通过因果抽象将局部视觉特征聚合为具有空间对应关系的宏节点，递归预测节点内容与运动，宏级预测指导未来观测生成，预测运动传输历史外观信息，并将节点级误差映射回空间区域进行校正。

Result: 在US-VLA数据集上，四步预测的ROI-MSE为0.01861；与各预测步最佳基线相比，四步预测及八步、十六步递归外推的LPIPS分别降低48.59%、41.68%、30.23%。消融与机制分析表明，运动引导的参考传输主要提升像素重建，节点级误差反馈进一步改善感知质量及生成观测与宏级预测的一致性。

Conclusion: QCC-WM通过商空间因果构造与共享参考测量，有效实现了宏级预测与视觉生成的一致性，显著提升了动作条件世界建模的预测性能与感知质量。

Abstract: A key challenge in action-conditioned world modeling is to construct macro-states from high-dimensional observations that support recursive prediction under actions, while keeping their predicted evolution consistent with visual changes in generated observations. We propose QCC-WM, a visual world model based on Quotient-based Causal Construction. QCC-WM uses action responses to guide macro-variable construction for recursive action-conditioned prediction. A shared reference quotient measurement constrains the consistency between predicted states and observed changes, allowing macro-level predictions to provide structured constraints for future visual generation. Using translational and rotational actions and their predicted responses, the model aggregates local visual features into macro-nodes with spatial correspondence through causal abstraction, and recursively predicts node content and motion under a shared reference measurement. Macro-level predictions guide future observation generation, while predicted motion transports historical appearance information. Node-level errors between generated observations and macro-level targets are then mapped back to the corresponding spatial regions for correction, improving consistency between visual generation and macro-level predictions. On the US-VLA dataset, QCC-WM achieves an ROI-MSE of 0.01861 for four-step prediction. Compared with the best baseline at each prediction horizon, it reduces LPIPS by 48.59%, 41.68%, and 30.23% for four-step prediction and eight- and sixteen-step recursive extrapolation, respectively. Ablation studies and mechanism analyses show that motion-guided reference transport mainly improves pixel reconstruction, while node-level error feedback further improves perceptual quality and consistency between generated observations and macro-level predictions.

</details>


### [152] [ReVMap: Vectorized Global Mapping via Connectivity-Aware Local Map Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12346)
*Hongjae Shin,Jungho Kim,Donghyuk Kwak,Myeongjun Kim,Seunghoon Yu,Jiyong Oh,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReVMap提出一种可靠性驱动的局部到全局高精地图自动标注框架，利用不确定性引导的先验融合与连通性引导的全局地图修正，迭代提升局部预测与全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模矢量化高精地图标注成本高，现有局部到全局自动标注方法忽略预测不确定性且依赖启发式地图集成，容易传播误差并引入拓扑不一致。

Method: ReVMap通过逐点定位不确定性将全局地图先验融入局部地图预测以增强对不准确先验的鲁棒性，并预测局部到全局连通性，基于局部预测可靠性进行不确定性感知融合以选择性更新全局地图，迭代耦合局部预测与全局修正。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2基准上的大量实验表明，ReVMap在局部地图预测和全局地图构建两方面均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性引导的先验利用与连通性引导的全局修正迭代耦合，ReVMap有效缓解误差传播与拓扑不一致，实现了更可靠的局部到全局高精地图自动标注。

Abstract: Large-scale annotation of vectorized high-definition (HD) maps is labor-intensive and costly, motivating local-to-global auto-labeling approaches that incrementally construct global maps from local vectorized predictions. However, existing approaches often overlook prediction uncertainty and rely on heuristic map integration, potentially propagating errors and introducing topological inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose ReVMap, a reliable local-to-global framework that iteratively refines local map predictions and global map construction through uncertainty-guided prior utilization and connectivity-guided global map revision. Specifically, ReVMap leverages point-wise localization uncertainty to incorporate global map priors into local map prediction, improving robustness against inaccurate prior information. It then predicts local-to-global connectivity and selectively updates the global map through uncertainty-aware fusion based on the reliability of local predictions. By iteratively coupling local prediction and global revision, ReVMap progressively improves both local map accuracy and global map consistency. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 benchmarks demonstrate that ReVMap achieves state-of-the-art performance in both local map prediction and global map construction.

</details>


### [153] [RAPA: Ray-Aware Physics Augmentation for Robust Camera-LiDAR Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12129)
*Kyung-Rin Oh,Ji-Hoon Kim,TaeJoon Ha,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出射线感知物理增强（RAPA）方法，将棋盘格定位转化为圆柱LiDAR反射率图像的2D检测问题，仅需单次标注即可合成多样样本，显著提升检测鲁棒性和标定效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于标定板的相机-LiDAR标定依赖从LiDAR点云中提取棋盘格特征，但点云稀疏导致特征提取困难；现有方法需人工标注3D角点或依赖几何特征提取，在观测不足时不可靠。

Method: 将棋盘格定位建模为圆柱LiDAR反射率图像上的2D检测问题；为克服标注数据不足，提出射线感知物理增强（RAPA），从单次棋盘格观测生成多样合成样本以提升检测器鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 在四个独立采集的室内外场景中，RAPA在mAP@0.5上比最强图像空间增强基线高16.1-43.1个百分点；在242次观测中，RAPA返回的有效目标定位数量最多，96.4%的检测给出有效目标位姿，并支持全部四个场景的下游相机-LiDAR标定。

Conclusion: RAPA仅需单次标注的目标观测即可实现自动化的LiDAR侧目标定位，解决了基于标定板标定的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR calibration is essential for reliable perception in robotic and autonomous systems. Most target-based calibration methods rely on checkerboard observations, but accurate checkerboard feature extraction from LiDAR point clouds remains challenging due to measurement sparsity. Existing approaches either require manual annotation of 3D corners or depend on geometric feature extraction methods, both of which can become unreliable when checkerboard features are insufficiently observed. In this paper, we formulate checkerboard localization as a 2D detection problem using cylindrical LiDAR reflectivity images. To overcome the limited availability of labeled training data, we propose ray-aware physics augmentation (RAPA), which generates diverse synthetic samples from a single checkerboard observation. The generated data are used to improve detector robustness and generalization under varying sensing conditions. Across four independently captured indoor and outdoor locations, the proposed synthetic augmentation exceeds the strongest evaluated image-space augmentation baseline by 16.1-43.1 percentage points in mAP@0.5. Compared with training-free target-based methods on 242 observations, RAPA yields the largest number of valid target localizations, with 96.4% of its returned detections yielding valid target poses. These reliable localizations support downstream camera-LiDAR calibration across all four evaluated locations. Overall, RAPA addresses a key bottleneck in target-based calibration by enabling automated LiDAR-side target localization with only a single annotated target observation.

</details>


### [154] [LO-World: Unified BEV Latent Modeling for Joint 4D Occupancy and LiDAR Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11912)
*Zhimin Liao,Ping Wei,Kai Mao,Shuaijia Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LO-World统一4D世界模型，在共享BEV潜在空间中同时预测LiDAR点云与3D语义占用，实现跨模态互正则并达实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云与3D语义占用分属度量几何与稠密体素语义两种模态，天然互补，但现有世界模型独立预测或启发式级联，错失跨模态互正则机会并带来多管线冗余。

Method: 构建单流式4D世界模型，将两种模态统一到共享BEV潜在空间；耦合高效混合range-voxel自编码器与流匹配生成器，后者缓存历史上下文以降低迭代采样延迟。

Result: 实验优于现有最优基线，占用预测IoU提升10.9%，LiDAR Chamfer距离降低30.1%，单张RTX 4090上吞吐超过25 FPS。

Conclusion: 统一单流BEV潜在空间可让稠密语义与度量几何互相增强，同时消除多管线冗余并保持实时性。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds and 3D semantic occupancy represent two distinct modalities capturing fine-grained metric geometry and dense volumetric semantics, respectively, holding natural potential for mutual complementarity. However, existing world models typically forecast these modalities independently or cascade them heuristically, missing the opportunity for reciprocal cross-modal regularization. To address this, we propose \textbf{LO-World}, a unified 4D world model that forecasts both modalities within a shared BEV latent space. This single-stream formulation enables dense semantics and metric geometry to mutually reinforce each other, while eliminating multi-pipeline redundancy. Specifically, LO-World couples an efficient hybrid range-voxel autoencoder with a flow-matching generator that caches historical context to reduce iterative sampling latency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LO-World outperforms prior state-of-the-art baselines, improving occupancy forecasting IoU by 10.9% and reducing LiDAR Chamfer Distance by 30.1%. Meanwhile, it sustains a real-time throughput exceeding $25$~FPS on a single RTX 4090. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [NightSteps: A Night-Time Dataset and Benchmark for Pedestrian Crossing Intention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11583)
*Sjoerd Takken,Shaodi You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建并评估了夜间行人过街意图预测基准数据集NightSteps，比较了人类与两种大模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人过街意图预测模型多在白天光照充足条件下训练，缺乏夜间低光照场景数据集，模型鲁棒性未知。

Method: 基于全球六个城市夜间行车记录仪视频构建NightSteps数据集，包含行人位置、轨迹ID及过街行为真实标签；用Gemini、ChatGPT和人工评估进行过街预测并与标签对比。

Result: 人工评估准确率88.17%、F1值80.21%；Gemini准确率60%、F1值42.50%；ChatGPT准确率79.13%、F1值63.64%。

Conclusion: NightSteps可作为夜间行人过街意图预测的基准数据集，用于后续模型评估，以提升夜间道路安全。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles use pedestrian crossing intention prediction (PCIP) to participate safely in traffic. Existing models are largely trained under favourable daytime conditions with sufficient daylight. Little is known about the robustness of these models under night-time and low-light conditions, due to the lack of datasets containing night-time footage. This paper investigates how a dataset, named NightSteps, can be constructed and evaluated as a baseline benchmark. The dataset is based on nighttime dashcam footage collected from six different cities around the world, capturing a wide variety of road conditions. The dataset consists of dashcam-recorded point-of-view footage, raw data of pedestrians' locations with corresponding 'track ids' and ground-truth labels containing accurate information about pedestrian crossing behaviour, resulting in a diverse dataset. Gemini, ChatGPT, and human assessment are used to demonstrate the usability of the dataset by predicting crossings in clips from the dataset and comparing the results with the initial ground-truth labels. These results show that human input achieves an accuracy of 88.17% and an F1-score of 80.21%. Predictions from Gemini and ChatGPT perform lower, with accuracies of 60% and 79.13% respectively, and F1-scores of 42.50% and 63.64%. The evaluation demonstrates that the NightSteps dataset can be used for future model evaluation to test the performance of PCIP, with the aim of improving road safety at night.

</details>


### [156] [Reaching Is Not Finding: Map-Anchored Visual Prompting for the Last Mile of Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11510)
*Ningbo Li,Kebin Liu,Jingcan Chen,Weichen Zhang,Rong Ding,Yunhao Liu,Mo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有空中视觉语言导航器常到达目标附近却停在错误位置，原因是它们学会了到达地标而非识别目标物体；提出即插即用模块MAP-VP，在导航器停止后将指令中的地标轮廓绘制到当前视野，再用微调的4B视觉语言模型定位目标，在CityNav上显著提升六种导航器的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前导航器（从模仿学习到微调的7B视觉语言模型）普遍存在“到达但未找到”的问题：它们倾向于接近指令中命名的地标而非识别被描述的目标物体。替换指令中的目标物体后，导航器仍停在原位置，说明其学的是到达地标而非目标识别。

Method: 提出MAP-VP，一个即插即用的最后一步模块。在任何导航器停止后，利用基准提供的地图地标轮廓，将指令中命名的地标绘制到无人机当前视图上；随后用微调的4B视觉语言模型基于这些地标定位目标。训练数据由基准标注自动生成。

Result: 单独绘制地标或单独微调效果有限，二者结合高度有效。无需重新训练任何导航器，MAP-VP在CityNav上显著提升全部六种导航器：两个最强训练导航器成功率约翻倍，HETT从25.9%升至54.6%，FlightGPT从24.2%升至48.9%；而CityNav官方基于检测器的最后一步对HETT提升不到两个百分点。

Conclusion: 导航器失败的主因是只学会到达地标而非识别目标；通过将地标显式绘制到视野并结合微调视觉语言模型进行目标定位，MAP-VP作为即插即用模块可大幅提升多种导航器的成功率，优于官方检测器方案。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) asks a drone to find an object described in natural language. The description usually locates the object relative to named landmarks, such as streets and buildings. We observe that current navigators often bring the drone close to the target and then stop at the wrong place: they reach the target without finding it. This gap is shared by navigators of very different designs, from imitation learners to a fine-tuned 7B vision-language model. A simple test reveals the cause. When we replace the target object in the instruction with another one next to the same landmarks, no navigator goes to it, and most stop where they did before. Navigators learn to reach the landmarks, not to identify the described object. We therefore propose MAP-VP, a plug-and-play module for the last step. After any navigator stops, MAP-VP draws the landmarks named in the instruction onto the drone's current view, using the landmark outlines that the benchmark provides on a map. A fine-tuned 4B vision-language model then locates the target relative to these landmarks. The training data are generated automatically from the benchmark annotations. Neither drawing the landmarks nor fine-tuning helps much alone, but together they are highly effective. Without retraining any navigator, MAP-VP significantly improves all six navigators we test on CityNav. It roughly doubles the success rate of the two strongest trained navigators, raising HETT from 25.9% to 54.6% and FlightGPT from 24.2% to 48.9%, whereas CityNav's official detector-based last step adds less than two points to HETT.

</details>


### [157] [MoT3DVG: A Benchmark for Outdoor 3D Visual Grounding with Motion-Aware Descriptions and Temporal Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11334)
*Shijia Zhao,Xusheng Guo,Qiming Xia,Xun Huang,Kezheng Xiong,Zhihong Liu,Chenglu Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向动态室外场景的3D视觉定位数据集MoT3DVG及融合运动感知编码的DynaVG框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉定位研究集中于静态室内物体，难以适用于动态室外自动驾驶场景，而运动描述可捕捉移动物体的时序动态以应对该挑战。

Method: 构建大规模数据集MoT3DVG（含nuScenes中850个场景、31,128帧、144,568条运动感知语言提示），并提出DynaVG框架，在标准模态编码器之后、语言-点云对齐之前引入短长期集成动态编码模块SLIDE，通过短时窗口编码与长时窗口移位分别建模局部运动线索与全局时序上下文。

Result: 大量实验表明DynaVG在MoT3DVG上取得最先进性能，验证了运动感知编码的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了室外3D视觉定位的开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding (3DVG) localizes language-referred objects in 3D scenes, with broad applicability in both indoor and outdoor environments. However, existing 3DVG research focuses on static indoor objects, limiting its use in dynamic outdoor autonomous driving. Motion descriptions, which capture the temporal dynamics of moving objects, offer a promising way to address this challenge. We therefore introduce MoT3DVG, a large-scale dataset for dynamic-aware outdoor 3DVG with temporally evolving motion descriptions. It contains 850 scenarios with 31,128 frames from nuScenes dataset, and provides 144,568 language prompts with motion-aware descriptions for dynamic objects across time. We further propose DynaVG, a novel framework that effectively leverages temporal cues for outdoor 3DVG. In addition to the standard modality-specific encoders, DynaVG introduces a Short-Long Integrated Dynamic Encoding (SLIDE) module before the language-point cloud alignment. SLIDE models both local motion cues and global temporal context via short-term window encoding and long-term window shifting. Extensive experiments demonstrate its state-of-the-art performance on MoT3DVG, validating the effectiveness of motion-aware encoding. Our work reveals open challenges and promising directions for future research in outdoor 3DVG.

</details>


### [158] [TransArt3D: From Two States to Articulated 3D Reconstruction with Video Transition Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11304)
*Chenhao Guan,Liying Yang,Xinxing Yu,Hao Mo,Tianshun Han,Renjun Gao,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TransArt3D 从两张端点图像重建可交互的铰接三维资产，将视频扩散模型的运动先验转化为显式的部件结构与关节运动学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的方法在静态三维先验下独立评分每个状态，导致逐状态重建误差泄漏到运动部件和关节估计中形成虚假运动；视频扩散模型虽能捕捉物体状态间的运动，但仅隐式地存在于二维中。

Method: 提出优化框架 TransArt3D：首先用过渡耦合的三维一致性联合跨帧应用静态三维先验，提升端点间生成帧并初始化部件与关节；再用整流流运动学细化让视频先验监督整个过渡的三维运动场，并通过部件分配与共享关节约束该场；最后用运动感知的规范外观融合，借助恢复的铰接关系将两张端点图像映射到同一规范表面，为仅单状态可见的区域贴图。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility 上，TransArt3D 在几何与关节精度上优于使用相同两张端点图像的方法，并在铰接状态几何、关节定位与外观上超过使用六个观测状态的方法。

Conclusion: 将视频过渡先验转化为显式部件结构与关节运动学，可有效抑制逐状态误差对铰接估计的干扰，实现从少量图像高质量重建铰接资产。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactable articulated assets from a few images is key to robotics and simulation, yet highly underconstrained. Existing optimization-based methods fit an articulated asset under a static 3D prior that scores each state independently, so per-state reconstruction errors leak into the estimated moving part and joint as false motion. Video diffusion models capture how objects move between states, but only implicitly in 2D. To this end, we present TransArt3D, an optimization-based framework that reconstructs an articulated 3D asset from two endpoint images by translating this video transition prior into explicit part structure and joint kinematics. It first lifts frames generated between the endpoints with a transition-coupled 3D consensus that applies the static 3D prior jointly across frames, stabilizing unchanging regions and initializing the parts and joint. A rectified-flow kinematic refinement then lets the video prior supervise a 3D motion field over the whole transition, while part assignment and a shared joint constrain the field throughout optimization, keeping per-state errors out of the articulation. Finally, a motion-aware canonical appearance fusion uses the recovered articulation to map both endpoint images onto one canonical surface, texturing regions that only one state reveals. On PartNet-Mobility, TransArt3D outperforms methods given the same two endpoints in geometry and joint accuracy, and surpasses methods given six observed states in articulated-state geometry, joint localization and appearance.

</details>


### [159] [Map2SLAM: From Streaming Map Prediction to Real-Time SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11169)
*Jian Gao,Lin-Zhuo Chen,Yihang Chen,Jiagang Chen,Jiacheng Dong,Xun Cao,Nan Xue,Yinghao Xu,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Map2SLAM通过冻结流式3D基础模型、仅训练轻量检索头，将模型预测转化为外部持久SLAM状态的观测，实现可回环修正的在线SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D基础模型（3DFMs）能在线估计相机位姿和稠密几何，但预测一旦输出便固定，无法通过回环闭合修正累积漂移，因此需要将其推理结果作为可修正状态纳入SLAM框架。

Method: 保持LingBot-Map冻结，仅训练轻量检索头；前端用模型因果流式推理跟踪每帧并决定关键帧写入持久状态；后端异步决定何时及对哪些历史帧重新观测，带新上下文重跑模型联合推理，验证后的回环观测在线修正轨迹，分段观测在序列结束后精化稠密几何；所有调用共享每图单次帧编码器前向，缓存特征同时用于回环检索。

Result: 在KITTI、VBR和Oxford Spires上达到SOTA，平均ATE较最佳未标定基线降低超40%，在NVIDIA RTX 5090D上以518×154分辨率保持超28 FPS实时跟踪。

Conclusion: 将冻结流式3DFM的推理接口作为SLAM观测来源，并把其预测作为外部持久状态的可修正观测，可有效结合强几何能力与回环修正，实现高精度实时SLAM。

Abstract: Streaming 3D foundation models (3DFMs) estimate camera poses and dense geometry online, but they fall short of SLAM: their predictions are fixed once emitted, so loop closures cannot correct accumulated drift. With such models already providing strong geometric capability, a central question for SLAM becomes how to schedule their inference and how to keep its results as revisable state. We present Map2SLAM, which keeps LingBot-Map frozen, trains only a lightweight retrieval head, and builds SLAM on the model's inference interface, turning its predictions into observations of an external persistent SLAM state. The front-end tracks every incoming frame with the model's causal streaming inference and decides which frames are written to the persistent state as keyframes. The back-end asynchronously decides when and which past frames to re-observe, re-running the model's joint inference over them with fresh context; verified loop observations correct the trajectory online, and segment observations refine dense geometry after the sequence. All invocations share a single frame encoder pass per image, whose cached features also drive loop retrieval, so frequent re-invocation incurs no additional encoding cost. Map2SLAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on KITTI, VBR, and Oxford Spires, reducing mean ATE by over 40% compared with the best uncalibrated baselines, while maintaining real-time tracking at over 28 FPS at a resolution of $518\times154$ on an NVIDIA RTX 5090D GPU. Please visit our project page at this https URL .

</details>


### [160] [Remembering to Reason: Structured Memory for Scene and Workflow Understanding in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10917)
*Zhiran Zhang,Hu Wang,Yichen Wu,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemSurg是一个通过结构化记忆图存储手术视频历史观察并进行任务自适应检索的框架，用于提升手术视频理解中的场景与流程推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频中相似的视图可能对应不同动作或手术阶段，现有以帧为中心的提示方法无法获取早期观察历史，导致推理缺乏上下文。

Method: 构建结构化记忆图，存储带时间戳的实体类别、位置、运动线索和交互摘要；用时间顺序链接保留近期上下文，用实体关联链接连接跨手术过程的重复观察；再通过任务自适应检索提供基于证据的提示，支持冻结推理和记忆条件下的监督微调。

Result: 在机器人辅助前列腺切除术及两个互补手术基准上，相比SurgRAW搭配GPT-4o提升10.43个百分点；在匹配的Qwen3-VL-8B微调下记忆条件化带来6.52个百分点的提升；推理时插件为EndoChat增加7.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆可作为手术视频理解中可复用的程序性证据来源，有效提升场景与流程推理性能。

Abstract: Surgical video understanding is an important foundation for intelligent assistance in robot-assisted surgery. Similar surgical views can reflect different actions or procedural stages. Interpreting these views requires access to earlier observations, yet frame-centric prompting leaves this history unavailable. We introduce MemSurg, a framework that remembers surgical observations before reasoning over them. Its structured memory graph stores timestamped entity categories, locations, motion cues, and interaction summaries. Chronological links preserve recent context, while entity-associated links connect recurring observations across the procedure. Task-adaptive retrieval then supplies evidence-grounded prompts for scene and workflow reasoning, supporting both frozen inference and memory-conditioned supervised fine-tuning. Evaluation on robot-assisted prostatectomy and two complementary surgical benchmarks shows a 10.43-percentage-point gain over SurgRAW with GPT-4o. Memory conditioning adds 6.52 points under matched Qwen3-VL-8B fine-tuning, and an inference-time plug-in adds 7.04 points to EndoChat. These results support structured memory as a reusable source of procedural evidence for surgical video understanding. Our code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [161] [AmodalBox: Automated 3D Bounding Box Annotation under Partial Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10821)
*In-Jae Lee,Namtae Kim,Robert Exley,Kwonyoung Ryu,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自动化开放词汇3D边界框标注框架，利用图像生成的3D资产作为非模态几何先验，解决稀疏驾驶场景中部分观测导致的标注难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型的开放词汇3D标注方法在稀疏驾驶场景中面临部分观测挑战：可见LiDAR点仅覆盖物体表面小部分，导致边界框定位错误或物体漏标。

Method: 提出\Ours{}框架，通过图像生成3D资产作为非模态几何先验，建立输入图像与3D资产渲染视图的对应关系，将规范非模态几何与部分观测的世界空间LiDAR点云对齐；每个实例生成单个资产，在单帧估计度量尺度并跨帧传播（动态实例），聚合多帧观测（静态实例），以提高时间一致性并降低计算成本。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，使用\Ours{}标注训练的3D目标检测器比强基线提升14.1 mAP；在Waymo Open Dataset上，车辆类别在IoU 0.5下提升12.45 AP。

Conclusion: \Ours{}有效解决了部分观测下的自动化3D标注问题，生成的标注能显著提升3D目标检测器性能，验证了利用图像生成3D资产作为非模态几何先验的可行性。

Abstract: Recent progress in vision foundation models (VFMs) has made automated open-vocabulary 3D bounding box annotation increasingly feasible by jointly leveraging LiDAR point clouds and images. However, partial observations remain a major challenge in sparse driving scenes, where visible LiDAR points often cover only a small fraction of the object's surface and provide insufficient cues for estimating its full spatial extent. As a result, existing methods often produce mislocalized boxes or leave objects unlabeled. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{\Ours{}}, an automated annotation framework that leverages image-generated 3D assets as amodal geometric priors for partially observed objects. By establishing correspondences between input images and rendered views of 3D assets, \Ours{} robustly aligns canonical amodal geometry with partially observed world-space LiDAR point clouds. To improve temporal consistency and reduce computational cost, \Ours{} generates a single asset per instance, estimates its metric scale once at a single frame and propagates it across frames for dynamic instances, and aggregates multi-frame observations for static instances. 3D object detectors trained on annotations from \Ours{} improve performance over strong baselines by 14.1 mAP on the nuScenes dataset and by 12.45 AP at IoU 0.5 for the vehicle class on the Waymo Open Dataset (WOD). Our project page is available at: \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }

</details>


### [162] [WorldFoundry: Towards Unified Infrastructure for World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10771)
*Juanxi Tian,Jiadong Hou,Baiang Li,Xin Lin,Zhengbo Jiao,Weijia Zhang,Yeying Jin,Truong Nguyen,Haitian Zheng,Zhifei Zhang,Jianfei Yang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Felix Heide,Wei Xiong,Enze Xie,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出生成式世界模型的形式化定义与面向智能的评估框架，并发布开源基础设施WorldFoundry，以支持跨视频、3D/4D、具身动作与交互系统等异构系统的可复现推理与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型日益模糊“合成观测”与“模拟环境”的界限，但视觉逼真并不等于成功的世界建模：输出可能缺乏持久状态、物理动力学或对交互的因果一致响应，因此需要明确世界模型定义并建立可测量、可复现的评测体系。

Method: 将生成式世界模型定义为维护持久且历史条件化环境表示、能响应动作/干预更新该表示、并生成反映观测与反事实干预结果的时间连贯轨迹的预测动力系统；据此构建涵盖感知落地、持久状态建模、可控变换、动力学感知预测与闭环交互的智能导向框架；并开发WorldFoundry开源基础设施，统一模型与基准发现、运行时与资产准备、执行编排、产物管理和交互可视化，通过模态适配器保留原生输入、执行语义、输出表示与评测协议，并以显式清单、持久产物契约和证据感知评分卡区分执行就绪度与评测结论。

Result: WorldFoundry目录包含270个模型条目、74个基准和62个注册指标条目，就绪度与执行证据分别记录，使每项结论可追溯至支撑产物。

Conclusion: WorldFoundry实现了跨异构系统对持久、可控动力学的可复现比较，推动该领域从感知合成迈向可测量的世界智能。

Abstract: Visual generative models increasingly blur the line between synthesizing observations and simulating environments. Yet visual plausibility alone does not constitute successful world modeling: compelling outputs may lack persistent state, physical dynamics, or causally consistent responses to interactions. We define a generative world model as a predictive dynamical system that maintains a persistent, history-conditioned representation of an environment, updates that representation in response to actions or interventions, and generates temporally coherent trajectories that reflect outcomes under observed and counterfactual interventions. From this definition, we derive an intelligence-oriented framework spanning perceptual grounding, persistent state modeling, controllable transformation, dynamics-aware prediction, and closed-loop interaction. We present WorldFoundry, open-source infrastructure for reproducible inference and evaluation across video, 3D and 4D, embodied action, and interactive world systems. WorldFoundry unifies model and benchmark discovery, runtime and asset preparation, execution orchestration, artifact management, and interactive visualization, while modality-specific adapters preserve native inputs, execution semantics, output representations, and evaluation protocols. Explicit manifests, durable artifact contracts, and evidence-aware scorecards separate execution readiness from evaluative claims, making each claim traceable to its supporting artifacts. The catalog contains 270 model entries, 74 benchmarks, and 62 registered metric entries, with readiness and execution evidence recorded separately. WorldFoundry enables reproducible comparison of persistent, controllable dynamics across heterogeneous systems, advancing the field from perceptual synthesis toward measurable world intelligence.

</details>


### [163] [Latent-WAM++: Shaping World and Action Representations for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10632)
*Yichen Zhang,Yupeng Zheng,Linbo Wang,Pengxuan Yang,Zebin Xing,Junli Wang,Shiwei Li,Kangwei Liu,Fan Ren,Qichao Zhang,Yifeng Pan,Ji Tao,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Latent-WAM++ 是一种以表征为中心的世界动作模型，通过匹配各表征的监督信号与其在规划中的角色，实现端到端自动驾驶，在 NAVSIM v2 和 HUGSIM 上达到先进性能，并大幅降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型主要训练预测未来状态或最终动作，导致连接预测与规划的中间世界表征和动作表征仅受到间接约束，未能充分发挥其规划作用。

Method: 提出 Latent-WAM++，包含三部分：世界表征构建（结合场景-自车未来预测、SIGReg 分布稳定化和冻结几何先验，无需 EMA 目标编码器）；轨迹 MeanFlow 规划器（直接预测多模态轨迹提案，采用改进的 MeanFlow 速度空间目标实现一步采样）；视觉-动作表征对齐（将输出前动作特征与基于当前视觉语义的停止梯度目标对齐）。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v2 上达到 90.1 EPDMS，在 HUGSIM 零样本评估中达到平均 RC 47.0 和平均 HD-Score 30.1；端到端训练规划器仅需 190 GPU 小时，相比 Latent-WAM 的 1600 GPU 小时减少 88.1%。

Conclusion: Latent-WAM++ 通过按规划角色监督中间表征，在保持先进性能的同时显著提升训练效率，验证了表征中心设计在端到端自动驾驶中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Latent-WAM++, a representation-centered World Action Model (WAM) for end-to-end autonomous driving. Existing WAMs are primarily trained to predict future states or final actions, leaving the intermediate world and action representations that connect prediction to planning only indirectly constrained. Latent-WAM++ matches the supervision of each representation to its role in planning. World Representation Construction combines scene-ego future prediction, SIGReg-based distribution stabilization, and frozen geometric priors to learn a temporally predictive, non-degenerate, and geometry-aware world representation without an EMA target encoder. Trajectory MeanFlow Planner directly predicts multimodal trajectory proposals while being optimized with an Improved MeanFlow velocity-space objective, enabling one-step sampling. Visual-Action Representation Alignment further aligns the resulting pre-output action features with a stop-gradient target derived from current visual semantics. Latent-WAM++ achieves state-of-the-art performance on NAVSIM v2 and under zero-shot evaluation on HUGSIM, reaching 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 and 47.0 average RC and 30.1 average HD-Score on HUGSIM. Training the planner end to end costs 190 GPU-hours for Latent-WAM++ versus 1,600 for Latent-WAM, a reduction of 88.1%.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Row Alignment: Virtual-Camera-Aware Online Stereo Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10518)
*Yizhao Peng,Rui Gong,Zaiwang Gu,Xudong Jiang,Jun Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VirtualRect在线立体校正方法，通过估计虚拟立体相机并联合图像变形监督，解决现有方法仅优化行对齐导致图像畸变和深度估计退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中相机校准后的物理偏移（运动、机械漂移、振动等）会破坏标定假设，导致深度估计性能下降，需要在线立体校正；现有方法主要优化行对齐，但行对齐不足以保证可部署的立体深度估计，可能出现图像区域裁剪、空白、拉伸或尺度不均等问题。

Method: VirtualRect不将校正简单视为左右图像行对齐，而是估计一个校正后的虚拟立体相机，并结合对图像整体变形行为的监督，使校正后的图像与相机参数来自同一几何，保持匹配图像与深度计算模型的一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，相比先前最先进的在线校正器，VirtualRect在Carla-Flowguided上垂直光流误差降低17%，在Semi-Truck Highway上降低7%，并在Carla-Flowguided上使三种下游立体匹配器的深度MAE降低30-41%。

Conclusion: VirtualRect通过虚拟相机估计与图像级变形监督实现更可靠的在线立体校正，显著提升垂直对齐精度和下游深度估计性能，适合真实部署场景。

Abstract: Stereo matchers estimate depth by comparing the left and right images from a stereo camera pair, but they assume that the two cameras are accurately calibrated and rectified. In real deployments, small physical shifts after calibration, caused by camera motion, mechanical drift, vibration, or installation changes, can break this assumption and degrade depth estimation. Online stereo rectification is therefore needed to correct the image pair during operation and recover a stereo geometry where standard stereo matching can work reliably. Recent online rectification methods mainly optimize row alignment, making the left and right views of the same scene point fall on the same image row. We show that this target, while necessary, is insufficient for deployable stereo depth estimation. A rectifier may make a small set of matched points look well row-aligned, but still distort the full images in ways that hurt stereo matching: useful image regions may be cut off, large blank areas may appear, objects may be stretched or squeezed, or image scale may change unevenly across the view. We propose VirtualRect, an online stereo rectification method designed for reliable real-world deployment. Rather than treating rectification as simply making the left and right image rows line up, this method estimates a rectified virtual stereo camera and combines image-wide supervision of image warp behavior. This makes the rectified images and the camera parameters come from the same geometry, so the images used for matching and the model used for depth computation remain consistent. Experiments on multiple datasets show that, compared with the prior state-of-the-art online rectifier, VirtualRect reduces vertical flow error by 17% on Carla-Flowguided and 7% on Semi-Truck Highway, and lowers depth MAE by 30-41% on Carla-Flowguided across three downstream stereo matchers.

</details>


### [165] [SRA: Spatial Reasoning Adapter via Evolving Social Interaction Graphs for Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10506)
*Jaewoo Jeong,Seonkyu Song,Hyeonwoo Park,Jegyeong Cho,Yujin Bae,Giwon Lee,Daehee Park,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRA是一种可插拔的图去噪适配器，在每个反向扩散步根据宿主当前未来预测重建稀疏交互图并给出残差修正，在四个多智能体基准上对三种宿主模型均提升ADE和FDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机预测器仅从观测历史读取一次交互信息并在整个生成过程中保持不变，无法在联合未来逐步锐化时重新检验相互的几何约束。

Method: 提出空间推理适配器SRA，嵌入扩散与流匹配预测器而不改动其生成核心；每个反向步读取宿主未来估计，在预测轨迹上构建稀疏欧氏交互图并回传残差修正；含三个组件：带学习query-key分数与几何偏置的稀疏邻域检索、在演化预测上重算的未来条件关系特征、以及让高置信智能体作为不确定智能体锚点的不确定性加权消息传递。

Result: 在SDD、NBA、Soccer、Football四个基准上实例化于LED、MID、MoFlow三种结构不同的宿主，每个宿主在每个数据集上ADE均提升，平均ADE提升5.8%、FDE提升7.0%；累积消融显示三组件需协同才能在全部体育数据集上提升所有宿主。

Conclusion: 基于模型自身演化未来并按可信度加权的空间推理对多种去噪架构普遍有效，且可作为模块化组件即插即用。

Abstract: Predicting where multiple agents will move next is fundamental to autonomous driving, robotics, and any system that must share space with people safely. Modern stochastic predictors read off inter-agent interactions once from the observed past and keep that view fixed throughout the generative rollout, so the reciprocal geometric constraints that emerge as the joint future sharpens are not re-examined. We address this gap with SRA (Spatial Reasoning Adapter), a modular graph denoiser that is integrated into existing diffusion and flow-matching predictors without redesigning their generative cores. At every reverse step, SRA reads the host's current future estimate, builds a sparse Euclidean interaction graph on the predicted trajectories, and returns a residual correction to the host's decoding path. Our adapter consists of three core components: a sparse neighborhood retrieved with a learned query-key score and a geometric bias, a future-conditioned relational feature recomputed on the evolving prediction, and uncertainty-weighted message passing where confident agents act as anchors for uncertain ones. We instantiate SRA on three structurally different hosts (LED, MID, and MoFlow) across four multi-agent benchmarks: SDD, NBA, Soccer, and Football. SRA improves ADE for every host on every dataset, with average gains of 5.8% in ADE and 7.0% in FDE. A cumulative ablation shows that the three components act as one mechanism: only together do they improve every host on every sports dataset, indicating that spatial reasoning over the model's own evolving future, weighted by how far that future can be trusted, is broadly useful across denoising architectures. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [166] [Tiresias: Towards Language-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10392)
*Fengyi Wu,Yifei Dong,Yetong Sha,Lingdong Kong,Qiyu Hu,Bohan Xiong,Qi Dai,Alexander G. Hauptmann,Zhi-Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出语言条件世界建模的统一任务LCWM及其基准LCWM-Bench（整合15个数据集、142,987条轨迹与428,961条指令），并提出通过选择性想象进行规划的智能体Tiresias。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言条件世界建模研究在动作空间、数据集和评估协议上彼此割裂，缺乏统一的评测环境。

Method: 构建统一动作接口与评估协议，整合导航、机器人、3D游戏和驾驶四类共15个数据集；提出Tiresias智能体：由语言条件扩散策略生成候选轨迹，轨迹排序器筛选，视频预训练世界模型想象视觉后果，视觉排序器评估并融合轨迹分数选择最终方案。

Result: Tiresias在LCWM-Bench上取得有竞争力的规划与想象性能；消融实验表明轨迹排序与视觉验证具有互补收益；替换候选生成方法后排序器仍有效。

Conclusion: LCWM-Bench与Tiresias为研究基于世界模型的语言引导规划提供了统一评估环境和有效起点。

Abstract: World models enable planning through internal simulation, while natural language provides intuitive task specification. Yet, language-conditioned world modeling remains fragmented across domains with disparate action spaces, datasets, and evaluation protocols. We introduce LCWM, a shared task that maps an initial agent-view observation and a language instruction to an action sequence that fulfills the instruction. Its accompanying benchmark, LCWM-Bench, integrates 15 datasets across navigation, robotics, 3D gaming, and driving under a shared action interface and evaluation protocol, pairing 142,987 episodes with 428,961 instructions in three styles. We further propose Tiresias, an agent that plans via selective imagination: a language-conditioned diffusion policy generates candidate trajectories, a trajectory ranker shortlists them, and a video-pretrained world model imagines their visual consequences. A visual ranker evaluates these rollouts, complementing trajectory scores to select the final plan. Tiresias achieves competitive planning and imagination performance on LCWM-Bench. Ablations demonstrate the complementary benefits of trajectory ranking and visual verification, while experiments with alternative proposal methods show that the trained rankers remain effective beyond our diffusion policy. Together, LCWM-Bench and Tiresias provide a unified evaluation setting and an effective starting point for studying language-guided planning through world models.

</details>


### [167] [DAC-MACE: Context-Conditioned Expert Routing for Compact Scene Coordinate Regression in Camera Relocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10263)
*Dingying Fan,Mingkai Liu,Haohua Que,Haojia Gao,Xianliang Huang,Qian Zhang,Xueqian Wang,Fei Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAC-MACE通过重新校准和上下文化特征来改进软专家路由，从而提升场景坐标回归的相机绝对位姿估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混合专家（MoE）的场景坐标回归中，路由器使用与专家回归相同的局部描述子为每个像素分配专家，导致不同位置的重复立面和窗户获得相似的路由输入，限制了大规模户外场景的定位精度。

Method: 提出DAC-MACE：用残差上下文适配器（RCA）对冻结编码器特征进行通道和位置重加权；用全局上下文编码器（GCE）通过多尺度自注意力混合特征；将图像级池化描述子加入路由器输入以实现软专家路由；并用双阈值RANSAC–Levenberg–Marquardt估计器恢复位姿。

Result: 在Cambridge Landmarks上，DAC-MACE三次种子平均的每场景中位平移误差为13.0 cm，优于GLACE（13.6 cm）和MACE（14.4 cm），最大场景误差从24 cm降至19 cm；消融显示MoE头、GCE、RCA和双阈值估计器将平均误差从17.0 cm依次降至14.4、14.0、13.6和13.0 cm。在7-Scenes上，为单一ACE头加入GCE将Recall@(5 cm, 5°)从86.56%提升至87.60%。

Conclusion: 对路由输入进行特征重校准与全局上下文增强，并配合鲁棒位姿估计，可显著提升紧凑SCR地图的相机绝对位姿估计精度。

Abstract: A camera becomes an absolute pose sensor when its images can be relocalized against a prebuilt map of the scene. On mobile robots and augmented-reality devices, that map must be small and quick to build. Scene coordinate regression (SCR) stores the scene in a compact network that predicts a 3D coordinate for every pixel, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) variants extend its capacity to larger outdoor scenes. In existing MoE-based SCR, however, the router assigns each pixel to an expert from the same local descriptor that the experts regress from, so repeated facades and windows at different locations receive similar routing inputs. We present DAC-MACE, which conditions soft expert routing on recalibrated and contextualized features. A residual context adapter (RCA) reweights the channels and locations of frozen encoder features, a global context encoder (GCE) mixes them with multi-scale self-attention, and an image-level pooled descriptor is added to the router input. A dual-threshold RANSAC--Levenberg--Marquardt estimator recovers the pose. On Cambridge Landmarks, DAC-MACE attains a mean per-scene median translation error of 13.0 cm over three seeds, the lowest among the RGB-only SCR methods compared (GLACE 13.6 cm, MACE 14.4 cm), and reduces the error on the largest scene from 24 to 19 cm relative to MACE. In a cumulative study, the MoE head, GCE, RCA, and the dual-threshold estimator lower the mean error from 17.0 cm to 14.4, 14.0, 13.6, and 13.0 cm. On 7-Scenes, adding GCE to a single ACE head raises Recall@(5 cm, 5$^\circ$) from 86.56% to 87.60%.

</details>


### [168] [ATLAS: A Multi-Procedure Benchmark and Context-Aware Model for Surgical Anatomy Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10182)
*Ronald L. P. D. de Jong,Tim J. M. Jaspers,Raf A. H. Vervoort,Aron F. H. A. Bakker,Anna L. Dijk,Yiping Li,Jip L. Tolenaar,Jelle P. Ruurda,Willem M. Brinkman,Daan de Geus,Fons van der Sommen,Josien P. W. Pluim,Marcel Breeuwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ATLAS-120k手术视频语义分割数据集和ATLAS视频语义分割模型，在分割精度和时序一致性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中解剖结构识别有助于术中导航和患者安全，但受限于小型单术式数据集以及面向自然场景的分割方法。

Method: 构建了包含121,018帧、100个手术视频、覆盖14种术式和41个类别的ATLAS-120k片段级语义分割数据集，标注结合人工标注、跟踪器传播和外科医生审核；提出ATLAS模型，基于域内基础模型嵌入，加入术式和手术阶段的上下文查询及帧间查询传播实现短期记忆。

Result: ATLAS在分割精度和时序一致性上优于自然图像及域内基础模型，ViT-L下Dice达0.70，实时运行76.2 FPS；消融表明上下文查询提升精度、查询传播提升时序一致性；在CholecSeg8k上零样本匹配专用模型，在AutoLaparo上较SurgeNet初始化提升阶段识别。

Conclusion: ATLAS-120k数据集与ATLAS模型有效解决了手术解剖识别中的数据与方法局限，且模型权重、数据集标注和标注工具已公开。

Abstract: Recognizing anatomical structures during minimally invasive surgery (MIS) could support intraoperative navigation and improve patient safety, but progress is limited by small, single-procedure datasets and by segmentation methods developed for natural scenes. This work addresses both limitations through a new dataset and model. First, we introduce ATLAS-120k, a clip-level semantic segmentation dataset of 121,018 annotated frames from 100 surgical videos, covering 14 laparoscopic and robot-assisted procedures and 41 classes. Annotations were created with a pipeline that combines manual labeling, tracker-based propagation, and review by surgeons. Second, we propose ATLAS (Anatomy Recognition with Context Learning using Foundation Representations), a video semantic segmentation model for surgical anatomy recognition. ATLAS builds on in-domain foundation-model embeddings and adds context queries for the procedure and surgical phase, together with query propagation between frames for short-term memory. ATLAS outperforms state-of-the-art natural-image and in-domain foundation models in both segmentation accuracy and temporal consistency, achieving a Dice score of 0.70 with ViT-L while running in real time at 76.2 FPS. Ablations show that the context queries improve accuracy and that query propagation improves temporal consistency. ATLAS also generalizes well, matching a dedicated model zero-shot on CholecSeg8k and improving phase recognition on AutoLaparo over SurgeNet initialization. The model weights, dataset annotations, and annotation tool are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [169] [From Video to Digital Twin: An Exploratory Study of World Models and Gaussian Splatting on In-the-Wild Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10013)
*Somesh Mohapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用公开食品银行仓库新闻视频，测试生成式世界模型与3D高斯泼溅在无标注真实场景中的表现，并构建数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型和3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）有望将普通视频转为可探索的3D场景，但其在未经整理的野外视频上的表现鲜有报道。

Method: 以一段包含讲解员走动的公开新闻视频为唯一输入，商业世界模型从20帧恢复相机位姿和度量深度（3个区域），融合约74.7万个高斯泼溅（花费200积分/0.2美元）；本地CPU优化泼溅模型（免费）；在保留帧上以未被人覆盖像素计算PSNR，并测试移除背景填充与人像凸包裁剪的影响；尝试让世界模型外推未拍摄区域；将输出修正为有效的度量OpenUSD资产（原生splat模式），作为数据层、浏览器查看器、Blender和NVIDIA Omniverse的唯一接口。

Result: 商业模型花费0.2美元恢复位姿与度量深度；本地CPU泼溅模型在拍摄路径附近照片级真实，但远离路径迅速退化。保留帧PSNR：64帧训练达21.2 dB，43帧达23.1 dB；仅用世界模型接受的8帧时分别下降3.8 dB和5.4 dB。移除训练器的背景填充与人像凸包裁剪可提高保留帧PSNR，表明清理移动人后空间会牺牲已观测像素的保真度。世界模型外推未拍摄区域两次在提供商侧失败，无法评估外推，未观测空间由脚本模型填充。

Conclusion: 当前模型能以低成本、度量尺度恢复真实场景的已观测部分，但少视角保真度和未观测空间外推仍是开放问题；该工作为方法探索而非部署产品。

Abstract: Generative world models and 3D Gaussian Splatting promise to turn ordinary video into explorable 3D scenes, but little is reported on how they behave on uncurated, in-the-wild footage. We study what these models recover when the only input is a publicly available news walkthrough of a food bank warehouse, with presenters walking through every shot and no drawings, scans or site visit, and we use the result as the geometry of a digital twin. A commercial world model given 20 frames recovered camera poses and metric depth for three areas, which we fused into about 747,000 Gaussian splats for 200 credits (20 US cents). Splat models optimized locally on a CPU, at no credit cost, were photorealistic near the filmed path but degraded quickly away from it. On held-out frames, scored in pixels not covered by people, they reached 21.2 and 23.1 dB PSNR from 64 and 43 training frames, and lost 3.8 and 5.4 dB when given only the 8 frames a world model accepts. Removing the trainer's background fill and person-hull carving raised held-out PSNR, showing that cleaning the space behind moving people costs fidelity in observed pixels while its gain lies where no ground truth exists. Asking the world model to generate views of areas the video never showed failed on the provider's side twice, so extrapolation could not be evaluated and unseen space was filled by a scripted model instead. We also describe the corrections needed to turn world-model output into a valid, metric Universal Scene Description (OpenUSD) asset with its native splat schema, which serves as the only interface to a data layer, a browser viewer, Blender and NVIDIA Omniverse. The findings suggest that today's models recover the observed parts of a real scene cheaply and at metric scale, while few-view fidelity and extrapolating unobserved space remain open. The work is an exploration of these methods, not a deployed product.

</details>


### [170] [A four-view smartphone video dataset of a robot-held flexible strip, with camera poses and a 3D LaGSplat baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09959)
*Louen Pottier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个由机械臂驱动柔性条带的多视角视频数据集，包含相机位姿与夹爪逐帧输入，并首次用LaGSplat在该真实数据上取得3D重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaGSplat原论文的多视角同步素材仅来自合成渲染，需要真实世界多视角数据来验证其学习低维潜状态与高斯泼溅解码器的能力。

Method: 用机械臂沿一维路径往复运动驱动柔性条带，四部iPhone（三固定一手持）拍摄十分钟；视频同步、由场景估计相机位姿、逐帧给出夹爪位置作为系统输入；以夹爪加速度作为强迫项拟合LaGSplat。

Result: 在留出视角上渲染达到26至33 dB PSNR，并可在网页浏览器中实时交互演示；数据集、检查点和训练代码均已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了真实世界多视角可变形物体数据集，并证明LaGSplat在真实拍摄数据上同样有效且可实时交互。

Abstract: This note describes a multi-view video dataset of a deformable object driven by a measured input, with the measured pose of every camera, and a first 3D result obtained on it with LaGSplat. LaGSplat learns from video a low-dimensional latent state that is both the generalized coordinate of a learned dissipative Lagrangian and the conditioning variable of a Gaussian Splatting decoder; fitting the decoder in 3D calls for synchronized multi-view footage, which the original paper only had from synthetic renderings. Here a robot arm moves a flexible strip by oscillating along a one-dimensional path, and four iPhones, three fixed and one handheld, film it for ten minutes. The videos are synchronized, the cameras are posed from the scene, and the position of the gripper is given per frame as the input of the system. LaGSplat fitted on this data with the gripper acceleration as a forcing term renders held-out views at 26 to 33 dB PSNR and runs as a real-time interactive demo in a web browser. Dataset, checkpoints and training code are public.

</details>


### [171] [SWAM: Scaling World Action Models with Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12870)
*Yu Zhou,Xinyuan Xia,Xuankun Yang,Yinghao Yang,Wendi Chen,Bowen Liu,Jianhan Ma,Weicheng Duan,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Yanwei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了在世界动作模型（WAM）中引入动作学习时如何保持语言-视频对齐，并给出了数据与监督设计的最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM研究主要关注架构与训练机制，默认语言-视频对齐可由视频预训练获得，但引入动作学习可能破坏该对齐，且缺乏关于数据与监督设计的原则性指导。

Method: 构建约47,000小时视频池（通用视频、第一视角视频、UMI演示、机器人演示），系统比较语言-视频监督、语言-视频-动作监督及二者联合的影响，分析语言标注质量与动作监督的作用，并研究联合训练配方与模型架构。

Result: 固定训练预算下，混合第一视角视频与UMI演示提升控制泛化；在RoboTwin Clean演示上后训练后，Easy与Hard条件成功率分别为88.10%和61.56%；真实世界三任务平均成功率40.00%，最强基线为23.33%。

Conclusion: 合适的数据混合、监督形式与架构设计能在动作学习中保持并扩展语言-视频能力，显著提升未见条件下的泛化性能，并开源数据、代码与权重。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict video and actions conditioned on language. Existing studies focus primarily on architectures and training mechanisms, typically treating language-video alignment acquired through video pretraining as an established foundation. However, introducing action learning may impair this alignment. Principled guidance on data and supervision design for maintaining language-video alignment during action learning remains limited. To investigate this, we curate a pool of approximately 47,000 hours of general videos, egocentric videos, UMI demonstrations, and robot demonstrations. Building on this, we systematically study how language-video supervision, language-video-action supervision, and the joint use of these two forms affect WAM performance. In particular, we examine how language annotation quality affects pretrained language-video capabilities, and how action supervision from UMI and robot demonstrations extends these capabilities to language-video-action modeling. To better scale the model, we further conduct detailed studies on joint training recipe and model architecture. Under a fixed training budget, mixing egocentric videos and UMI demonstrations improves control generalization. Model architecture affects how effectively these experiences are used, while scaling pretraining under the selected configuration brings further gains. The resulting recipe, post-trained on RoboTwin Clean demonstrations only, achieves success rates of 88.10% and 61.56% under Easy (Clean-to-Clean) and Hard (Clean-to-Random) conditions, respectively. Our model achieves 40.00% SR across three real-world tasks, compared with 23.33% for the strongest baseline, indicating better generalization under unseen conditions. We release the curated data, training code, and model weights.

</details>


### [172] [EgoLabel: Zero-Shot Egocentric Auto-Labeling with Cross-Modal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12149)
*Xiaozhi Zhang,Changjia Xu,Yaoyuan Yan,Haoxiang Jie,Bingjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoLabel是一个零样本、无需目标域标注的即插即用流水线，整合多个现成模型实现第一人称自动标注，并提供跨模态配准与一致性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称自动标注要求深度、相机位姿、物体和人体姿态等独立开发的模型估计相互一致，但单位、坐标约定和尺度差异会导致各自看似合理的预测在几何上不一致。

Method: 提出EgoLabel零样本流水线，整合MonST3R（几何）、LocateAnything结合GMC-BoT-SORT与SAM 2（物体）、V-HPOT（手部）以及FisheyeViT（全身兼容性演示），并加入显式跨模态配准与一致性诊断；在H2O传感器深度上对20个序列进行逐手深度锚定评估。

Result: 逐手深度锚定相较单一全局尺度将每序列手部—深度残差中位数均值降低23%，汇总中位数为84.6 mm（真值参考20.5 mm）；手部PA-MPJPE为14.53±1.29 mm，而绝对MPJPE为119.1±31.3 mm；三个纯RGB序列在Sim(3)对齐后相机ATE为1.40±0.29 cm；同时发现滑动窗口实现问题会使传感器初始化的深度在优化中发生改变。

Conclusion: 手部锚定与其残差共享同一深度图，因此残差衡量的是内部一致性而非独立精度；结果既展示了配准流水线的实用性，也揭示了其当前局限。

Abstract: Egocentric auto-labeling requires depth, camera pose, object, and human-pose estimates to agree across independently developed models. Differences in units, coordinate conventions, and scale can make individually plausible predictions geometrically inconsistent. We present \textbf{EgoLabel}, a zero-shot pipeline that combines off-the-shelf models without target-domain labeled training and provides explicit cross-modal registration and consistency diagnostics. The pipeline integrates MonST3R for geometry, LocateAnything with GMC-BoT-SORT and SAM~2 for objects, and V-HPOT for hands; FisheyeViT provides a separate whole-body compatibility demonstration. Our main hand evaluation uses H2O sensor depth. Across 20 sequences, per-hand depth anchoring reduces the mean of per-sequence median hand--depth residuals by $23\%$ relative to a single global scale, yielding a pooled median of $84.6\,\mathrm{mm}$ against a $20.5\,\mathrm{mm}$ ground-truth reference. Hand PA-MPJPE is $14.53\pm1.29\,\mathrm{mm}$, whereas absolute MPJPE is $119.1\pm31.3\,\mathrm{mm}$, revealing a substantial gap between aligned and metric accuracy. On three RGB-only sequences, camera ATE is $1.40\pm0.29\,\mathrm{cm}$ after $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ alignment. We also identify a sliding-window implementation issue that allows sensor-initialized depth to change during optimization. Because hand anchoring and its residual share the same depth map, the residual measures internal consistency rather than independent accuracy. These results characterize both the utility and the current limits of the registration pipeline.

</details>


### [173] [Compass 1: Towards Open-World and Persistent Embodied Navigation from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12087)
*Hai Zhang,Siqi Liang,Yanjun Guo,Shoufeng Zhang,Yunhao Wang,Hangyu Du,Haoguang Mai,Hongchen Li,Jiazhi Yang,Jialong Zeng,Yu Hu,Chen Chen,Gangcheng Jiang,Zishun Zhuo,Lirui Zhao,Zhigang Li,Yu Qiao,Zhenjie Yang,Fu Zhang,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Compass 1 是首个统一语言理解与视频生成、并支持近两分钟视觉历史记忆回溯的具身导航基础模型，通过历史表示与历史利用机制实现长时程稳定导航，开放世界成功率79.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航架构难以在开放世界中同时维持持续空间感知与长时程执行稳定性，仅能有效利用最近少数帧，缺乏长期历史记忆与回溯能力。

Method: 在不改变基础架构的前提下，引入历史表示（稀疏视觉关键帧与显式历史表示地图以保留长期时空上下文）和历史利用（含任务阶段定位、探索一致性、时空目标检索三类的结构化VQA监督协议），并建立单目RGB视频标注流水线，开源500+小时真实交互与2000+小时标注网络视频数据集。

Result: 在开放世界评估中总体成功率达79.4%，最强基线仅17.8%；基础任务零样本执行超90%，探索能力提升250%，挑战性历史回溯从0%跃升至58%；在0.35m轮式机器人、0.5m四足狗、0.85m人形、1.5m无人机等多种具身形态与场景中无缝泛化。

Conclusion: Compass 1 通过历史表示与历史利用有效实现长期时空记忆与稳定长时程导航，具备跨具身、跨场景的强泛化能力，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to achieve persistent spatial awareness and long-horizon execution stability in open-world environments. However, existing navigation architectures struggle to sustain both capabilities, remaining effective only over a handful of recent frames. We present Compass 1, the first embodied navigation foundation model to unify language understanding and video generation while enabling memory backtracking through nearly 2mins of visual history. Without altering the underlying architecture, we introduce History Representation, which integrates sparse visual keyframes with an explicit History Representation Map to preserve long-term spatiotemporal context. To effectively leverage this history, we further introduce History Utilization, a structured VQA supervision protocol comprising three categories: task-stage grounding, exploration consistency, and spatiotemporal target retrieval, enabling map-grounded reasoning and stable persistent long-horizon navigation. To fuel this system, we establish a complete annotation pipeline for monocular RGB videos and open-source a massive dataset featuring over 500 hours of high-quality real-world interactions and over 2,000 hours of annotated web videos. Trained mainly on the distribution of 1m height camera, Compass 1 generalizes seamlessly across diverse embodiments and scenarios, ranging from indoor environments to pedestrian-dense streets, spanning wheeled robot at 0.35m, quadruped dog at 0.5m, humanoid at 0.85m, and aerial drone at 1.5m. Compass 1achieves a dominant overall success rate of 79.4% across open-world evaluations, compared to 17.8% for the strongest baseline. It demonstrates exceptional zero-shot execution exceeding 90% on foundational tasks, a 250% boost in exploration, and an unparalleled leap from 0% to 58% in challenging persistent history backtracking.

</details>


### [174] [Scale-Consistent Monocular Fisheye Visual Odometry via Spherical Bundle Adjustment-Guided Unsupervised Depth Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11934)
*Wanhui Luo,Hongbo Zhao,Yi Zhou,Qijun Chen,Yiming Nie,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S²D-FVO单目鱼眼视觉里程计框架，通过球面光束法平差引导无监督深度学习，提升尺度一致性与深度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼眼相机视野广且单目配置简单，但长轨迹下尺度漂移严重；无监督深度学习可缓解该问题，然而鱼眼图像强畸变影响深度估计精度。

Method: 采用球面对数映射构建切空间角度重投影残差，使其对两个切向扰动均敏感且范数等于标量角度残差；在SBA中引入基于中值深度的尺度正则项约束重建尺度；用SBA替换无监督深度学习中的位姿估计网络以提供可靠相对位姿，训练好的深度网络反过来为SBA提供尺度参考并细化三维路标。

Result: 在多个公开数据集及两个新采集的大规模数据集上，深度与轨迹估计精度均优于代表性现有方法，且适用于不同相机模型。

Conclusion: SBA引导的无监督深度学习与深度引导的SBA形成互补，有效提升单目鱼眼VO的尺度一致性和深度估计精度。

Abstract: The wide field of view of fisheye cameras and the simplicity of a monocular setup make monocular fisheye visual odometry (VO) attractive for robotic localization and mapping. Nevertheless, scale drift remains a major challenge over long trajectories. Unsupervised depth learning can help to mitigate this issue, but the pronounced distortion of fisheye images poses challenges for accurate depth estimation. This article presents S$^2$D-FVO, a monocular fisheye VO framework integrating spherical bundle adjustment (SBA)-guided unsupervised depth learning to improve scale consistency and depth estimation accuracy. Given that pixel reprojection residuals do not uniformly reflect angular deviations between viewing rays and that the first-order linearization of a scalar angular reprojection residual is insensitive to perturbations along one tangent direction, SBA employs tangent-space angular reprojection residuals derived from the spherical logarithmic map. These residuals retain sensitivity along both tangent directions, with their norms equal to the corresponding scalar angular residuals. A median depth-based scale regularization term is further integrated into SBA to constrain the reconstruction scale. Building on these formulations, SBA replaces the pose estimation network during unsupervised depth learning, providing more reliable relative poses for view synthesis. In turn, depth estimates from the well-trained depth estimation network provide scale references for SBA, improving scale consistency while allowing individual 3-D landmark positions to be refined. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple public datasets, as well as two newly collected large-scale datasets, demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms representative existing approaches in both depth and trajectory estimation accuracy and is applicable to different camera models.

</details>


### [175] [SurgTAP-3D: A Large-Scale Cross-Surgery Video Dataset with Contact-Aware 3D Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11697)
*Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Taoyu Wu,Haomin Kuang,Mengya Xu,Kejian Shi,Ziyi Wang,Tongfan Guan,Mingkai Jia,Yue Wang,Qi Dou,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了大规模体内伪3D手术运动跟踪数据集与基准SurgTAP-3D，并提出可扩展的统一标注框架，将未标定单目手术视频转换为结构化4D标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集在真实临床场景中可扩展性和多样性有限，且标注通常只关注组织或器械之一，忽视工具-组织交互，难以支撑增强现实导航、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型所需的规模化高质量3D监督。

Method: 设计统一的手术3D点跟踪框架，通过接触区域提示、活动工具分割、动态光束法平差和全局4D细化，将未标定单目手术视频转换为结构化4D标注，产出时间一致的轨迹、伪尺度几何、相机位姿和器械掩码；数据集包含520K帧、4,549个动作片段、1.4M条2D/3D点轨迹，覆盖机器人与手工操作并统一动作类别。

Result: 在SurgPose、SurgT和STIR上建立覆盖器械与组织跟踪的综合基准，验证了器械位移估计、可变形表面重建和基础模型微调任务；数据集涵盖遮挡、烟雾、出血和器械快速运动等真实挑战。

Conclusion: 实验与分析表明，该数据集和标注框架对3D手术点跟踪、重建及手术动作理解均有效。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scene dynamics in minimally invasive surgery (MIS) is a fundamental step toward physically intelligent and autonomous surgical systems. However, existing public datasets offer limited scalability and diversity in real-world clinical scenarios. Moreover, annotations typically focus on either tissue or instruments alone, overlooking tool-tissue interactions. This gap limits applications such as augmented reality guidance and autonomous surgery driven by vision-language-action (VLA) models or world models, which demand large-scale, high-quality 3D supervision. In this work, we construct SurgTAP-3D, a large-scale in-vivo pseudo-3D surgical motion tracking dataset and benchmark, comprising 520K frames, 4,549 action clips, and 1.4M 2D/3D point trajectories from diverse robotic and manual procedures under unified action categories. To make annotation scalable, we design a unified framework for surgical 3D point tracking that converts uncalibrated monocular surgical videos into structured 4D annotations through contact-region prompting, active-tool segmentation, dynamic bundle adjustment, and global 4D refinement. This pipeline adapts foundation priors to surgical videos and produces temporally consistent trajectories, pseudo-scale geometry, camera poses, and instrument masks. The dataset captures realistic challenges inherent to surgical environments, including occlusions, smoke, bleeding, and rapid instrument motion. We establish a comprehensive benchmark on SurgPose, SurgT, and STIR, covering both instrument and tissue tracking. We demonstrate instrument displacement estimation, deformable surface reconstruction, and foundation-model fine-tuning. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate the effectiveness of our dataset and annotation framework for 3D surgical point tracking, reconstruction, and surgical action understanding. Homepage: this https URL

</details>


### [176] [ForeAct: Geometry-Guided Action Learning from World Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11500)
*Wei Lu,Jiaxi Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Taowen Wang,Yuetong Fang,Zhenhui Zhang,Shuning Zhang,Zizhao Yuan,Yixiao Feng,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeAct将预训练视频世界模型复用为单次前向传播的表示主干，通过由下一帧相对深度监督的可学习token注入几何信息，实现低延迟、几何感知的机器人操作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将场景几何与交互动态分开处理：世界动作模型依赖迭代扩散/整流流推理导致计算成本高且缺乏显式空间结构；显式几何建模需增加参数与算力的辅助模块；几何中心策略仅恢复观测结构，缺乏对未来几何变化的监督。

Method: 复用预训练视频世界模型作为单次前向传播的表示主干，直接处理当前观测的干净潜变量，省去未来帧生成与迭代去噪；不引入独立几何主干，而是用受下一帧相对深度监督的可学习token、经双向自注意力塑造共享视频表示；轻量几何读出仅在训练时使用，部署时移除，无需深度输入或外部几何主干。

Result: 在无需额外大规模预训练的情况下，ForeAct在LIBERO上平均成功率达96.6%，在SimplerEnv-Bridge上达63.5%，在90次真实世界rollout中成功率为74.4%。

Conclusion: ForeAct以极小参数开销将主干中已有的空间知识转化为面向动作生成的未来感知表示，实现了低延迟、几何感知的机器人操作控制。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires representations that capture both scene geometry and its evolution during interaction. Existing approaches have largely treated these two aspects separately. World action models capture such dynamics by coupling action prediction with future video generation, but their reliance on iterative diffusion or rectified-flow inference leads to high computational cost, while their representations lack explicit spatial structure. Explicitly modeling scene geometry typically requires auxiliary modules that increase both parameter count and computational cost. Conversely, geometry-centric policies primarily recover observed spatial structure and often lack explicit supervision for anticipating future geometric changes during interaction. To address these limitations, we introduce ForeAct, which repurposes a pretrained video world model as a single-pass representation backbone for low-latency control. ForeAct directly processes the clean latent of the current observation in one forward pass, eliminating future-frame synthesis and iterative denoising while retaining the backbone's pretrained spatiotemporal priors. Rather than introducing a separate geometry backbone, ForeAct uses learnable tokens supervised by next-frame relative depth to shape the shared video representation through bidirectional self-attention. This compact interface turns the spatial knowledge already encoded in the backbone into a future-aware representation for action generation, with little parameter overhead. The lightweight geometry readout is used only during training and removed at deployment, enabling low-latency, geometry-aware control without depth input or an external geometry backbone. Without additional large-scale pretraining, ForeAct achieves 96.6% average success on LIBERO, 63.5% on SimplerEnv-Bridge, and a 74.4% success rate across 90 real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [177] [CrossLiDAR: Controllable Multi-Sensor LiDAR Sequence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11131)
*Khanh Nguyen,Thomas Walker,Hichem Telli,Carlos de la Torre-Ortiz,Stefano Fiorini,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Wassim Swaileh,Tinghuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossLiDAR是一个基于Transformer的统一框架，能够生成可控且时空一致的多传感器LiDAR序列，在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR生成器通常局限于单一传感器配置、对结构和几何控制有限，且长时4D生成存在漂移，难以满足自动驾驶系统可扩展开发与验证的需求。

Method: 采用基于MM-DiT骨干的rectified flow目标，在紧凑的range image潜在空间中生成；引入Inter-sensor Module实现多传感器交互，ControlNet风格分支提供结构条件（如BEV先验或3D边界框）；通过生成短N帧序列并自回归扩展来提升长时一致性。

Result: 在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上，JSD平均提升29.56%，MMD平均提升15.36%，显著优于最先进基线。

Conclusion: CrossLiDAR通过实现校准一致的多传感器LiDAR长时 rollout，为可控的基于场景的自动驾驶仿真测试弥合了差距。

Abstract: High-fidelity LiDAR simulation remains a bottleneck for scalable development and validation of autonomous systems. Existing generators can synthesize realistic scans, but they are typically constrained to a single sensor configuration, offer limited control over structure and geometry, and drift in long-horizon 4D generation. We present CrossLiDAR, a unified transformer-based framework capable of generating controllable and spatio-temporally consistent multi-sensor LiDAR sequences. Our approach leverages a rectified flow objective with a Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer (MM-DiT) backbone, within a compact latent space of range images. To support multiple LiDAR sensors, an Inter-sensor Module enables interaction across sensors, while a ControlNet-style branch provides structural conditioning (e.g., BEV priors or 3D bounding boxes). For modeling the temporal consistency, CrossLiDAR generates short $N$-frame sequences and extends them autoregressively to improve long-horizon coherence. Our model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average improvement of 29.56\% in JSD and 15.36\% in MMD evaluated on nuScenes and the Waymo Dataset. CrossLiDAR bridges the gap to modern autonomous-driving simulation by enabling calibration-consistent multi-sensor LiDAR rollouts over long horizons for controllable, scenario-based testing.

</details>


### [178] [SoftPAT3D: Physics-Augmented Text-to-3D Scene Generation with Deformables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11087)
*Lei Shu,Alberta Longhini,Leonidas Guibas,Gordon Wetzstein,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftPAT3D 是一个物理增强的文本到3D场景生成框架，可将布料、毛绒玩具和绳索等可变形物体生成为物理有效、可直接仿真的刚体，并与支撑家具、彼此及自身保持无穿透接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理增强流程仅模拟刚体，无法处理文本到3D生成返回的几何无效、可能自交、无静止形状、无材料模型且无可行初始状态的可变形网格，而这些正是可变形仿真所需输入。

Method: 从制造方式而非生成的物理不一致变形构型中获取静止形状；材料库为每个可变形物体分配可优化的初始弹性参数；重建被遮挡支撑并转为水密碰撞网格；每个物体从提示指定支撑部件上方可优化放置位置释放；在统一接触模型下驱动完整场景至静态平衡，并用几何检查和视觉语言模型验证完成度；场景不符合提示时用网格搜索修正放置，并用同一接触能量可微仿真的伴随方法联合放置多物体、恢复并优化材料参数。

Result: 多体场景在单次仿真中达到平衡，流程也可仅从文本提示运行；在全部65个提示上，SoftPAT3D 未产生任何非期望互穿，而其他系统均出现互穿，其场景直接支持场景编辑和机器人操作。

Conclusion: SoftPAT3D 通过物理增强与可微仿真有效解决了文本到3D场景中可变形物体的物理有效生成与无穿透接触放置问题，并具备编辑和机器人操作应用潜力。

Abstract: We present SoftPAT3D, a physics-augmented framework for text-to-3D scene generation in which deformable objects, such as cloth, stuffed toys and ropes, are generated as physically valid, simulation-ready bodies and placed in intersection-free contact with the furniture that supports them, with one another and with themselves. Existing physics-augmented pipelines simulate rigid bodies only and cannot accommodate a generated deformable object, which text-to-3D generation returns as a geometrically invalid mesh, sometimes self-intersecting, that has no rest shape, no material model and no admissible initial state. These are the inputs a deformable simulation requires, and we construct each from information already in the pipeline. The rest shape comes from how the object is manufactured rather than from the generated physically inconsistent deformed configuration, and a material library assigns each deformable object its initial elastic parameters, which can then be optimized. The occluded support is regenerated and converted into a watertight collision mesh, each object is released from an optimizable placement above the supporting part that the prompt specifies, and the complete scene is driven to static equilibrium under one unified contact model, with completion verified by geometric checks and a vision-language model. When a scene fails the prompt, a grid search over placements corrects it, and the adjoint of a differentiable simulation of the same contact energy reaches the targets the grid does not, places several bodies jointly and recovers and optimizes material parameters. Multi-body scenes equilibrate in a single simulation, and the pipeline also runs from the text prompt alone. On all sixty-five prompts, SoftPAT3D produces no undesired interpenetration of any kind, whereas every other system does, and its scenes directly support scene editing and robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [179] [Locating and Repairing Domain Shift in VLM Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10720)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Michael Alexander Riegler,Guandong Xu,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MoLaRx，将VLM驾驶规划器的跨城市域偏移建模为模态×层二维张量，并据此定位后修复，在六个设置中取得最低轨迹误差差距且参数和延迟更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法把跨城市域偏移当作单一量做统一对齐或单点编辑，作者认为该偏移在模态和层两个维度上是结构化分布的，需按结构定位后再修复。

Method: 通过激活修补分析发现两条规律：图像与文本token偏移模式不同（模态轴），层间偏移划分依赖架构但在数据集上稳定（层轴）。据此提出MoLaRx框架，用因果重要性分数（CIS）估计模态-层张量并划分功能区域，再用CIS引导的选择性修复（CISR）为各区域匹配MMD、LoRA或冻结算子，组合随架构自动适配。

Result: 在LFM2-VL-1.6B、Qwen2-VL-2B、PaliGemma2-3B三个架构不同的VLM及nuScenes与Argoverse 2两个跨城市基准上，MoLaRx在全部六个主表设置中取得最低跨城市轨迹误差差距，可训练参数减少50-60%，推理延迟降低8.4-10.9 ms；而统一MMD-only适配在六个设置中均崩溃。

Conclusion: 域偏移应被刻画为模态×层的二维结构并按位置精准修复，结构引导的定位-修复范式优于统一目标基线，验证了结构引导修复的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted as end-to-end driving planners, yet they suffer from cross-city domain shift. Existing methods treat this shift as a single quantity addressed by uniform alignment or single-site editing. We challenge this view on two counts: (i) domain shift in VLM trajectory planners is inherently structured across modality and layer, and (ii) a locate-then-repair recipe matched to this structure outperforms every uniform-objective baseline. We analyse domain shift via activation patching and uncover two regularities. (F1) Modality axis. Image and text tokens exhibit distinct shift patterns in each VLM. (F2) Layer axis. The layer-wise shift partition is architecture-dependent and dataset-stable. These findings recast domain shift as a two-dimensional tensor indexed by modality (image vs. text tokens) and layer, motivating a simple principle: locate where shift occurs, and repair only there. We instantiate this principle in MoLaRx (Modality-Layer Repair), a locate-then-repair framework. The Causal Importance Score (CIS) estimates the domain shift as a modality-layer tensor and partitions the layers into functional zones.
