CIS-Guided Selective Repair (CISR) then applies a matched operator (Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) / low-rank adaptation (LoRA) / freezing) to repair each zone, with the composition adapting automatically to each architecture. We evaluate MoLaRx on three architecturally distinct VLMs (LFM2-VL-1.6B, Qwen2-VL-2B, and PaliGemma2-3B) across two cross-city benchmarks (nuScenes and Argoverse 2). MoLaRx attains the lowest cross-city trajectory-error gap among adaptation methods in all six main-table settings, while using 50-60% fewer trainable parameters and 8.4-10.9 ms lower inference latency. In contrast, uniform MMD-only adaptation collapses in all six settings, highlighting the importance of structure-guided repair.

</details>


### [180] [PLS-GS: Probabilistic Lidar Scaffolds for Compact, Surface-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10699)
*Krishnan Harikumar,Alejandro Fontan,Thierry Peynot,Emili Hernández,Niko Sünderhauf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLS-GS 利用 LiDAR 与相机数据构建分层高斯混合模型作为概率脚手架，引导 3DGS 基元贴合实测表面，实现更紧凑且渲染更快的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 通常仅用图像优化，而机器人与建图场景同时提供相机与高精度 LiDAR 数据，可结合外观与几何；现有方法需离散分区（体素/八叉树），不够连续自适应。

Method: 对位置与颜色联合拟合分层高斯混合模型（HGMM），得到连续自适应脚手架；每个渲染高斯关联一个 GMM 分量并在其协方差诱导的局部表面坐标系中约束；用马氏距离支撑损失限制渲染高斯偏离 LiDAR 表面。

Result: 在五个大型室外场景中，相比图像与 LiDAR 初始化基线，基元更贴近 LiDAR 测量且数量显著更少，模型更紧凑、渲染更快；同等容量下更贴近测量且渲染质量相当。

Conclusion: PLS-GS 通过概率 LiDAR 脚手架有效利用多传感互补信息，构建紧凑、贴合实测表面的 3DGS 表示。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is typically optimized from images only, many robotics and mapping applications provide both camera and high-definition LiDAR data, combining rich visual appearance with accurate geometric structure. We introduce PLS-GS, a probabilistic LiDAR scaffold for Gaussian Splatting that exploits this complementary sensing setting to construct a compact 3DGS representation aligned to the measured surface. PLS-GS fits a Hierarchical Gaussian Mixture Model jointly over position and color, yielding a continuous and adaptive scaffold that eliminates the need for discrete scene partitioning such as voxel grids or octrees. The fitted mixture then guides the renderable 3DGS Gaussians: each rendering Gaussian is associated with a GMM component and constrained in the local surface frame induced by that component's covariance. A Mahalanobis support loss bounds the deviation of the rendering Gaussians from the corresponding LiDAR-derived surface. Across five large outdoor scenes, PLS-GS places primitives markedly closer to the LiDAR measurements than both image- and LiDAR-initialized baselines, while employing substantially fewer primitives, yielding a considerably more compact model and faster rendering. At matched capacity, PLS-GS places primitives closer to the LiDAR measurements, with comparable rendering quality. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [181] [Dynamics-Informed Action Learning for Goal-Conditioned Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10660)
*Yinuo Jiang,Guangzhi Wang,Yiran Wang,Junfu Pu,Cheng Cheng,Honghao Chen,Cheng Cheng,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DIAL方法，通过训练时展开对齐与多步逆动力学监督，并在测试时用逆动力学生成的动作初始化CEM规划，提升视觉控制中潜在世界模型的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在紧凑表示空间中规划动作序列进行视觉控制，但潜在动态的学习方式与规划使用方式之间存在两个差距：教师强制训练未暴露于规划时的递归预测潜在状态；基于采样的规划器通常从与目标无关的提议初始化动作搜索，未利用世界模型学到的动作条件动态。

Method: 训练阶段采用展开对齐使前向模型接触自回归潜在预测，并用多步逆监督训练轻量逆动态头从潜在状态转移推断动作；再将参数固定的逆动态头应用于模型生成转移，促使预测展开保留动作相关信息。测试时将逆动态与前向动态自回归耦合，构建面向目标的动态信息动作序列，用以初始化CEM提议分布的均值，随后由标准CEM搜索细化。

Result: 在四个视觉控制基准上，DIAL在标准与长时程场景中均优于现有潜在世界模型和规划基线。

Conclusion: DIAL弥合了潜在动态学习与规划使用之间的差距，验证了训练时展开对齐与逆动态引导规划初始化的有效性，提升了视觉控制中的规划表现。

Abstract: Latent world models enable visual control by planning action sequences in compact representation spaces, but a gap remains between how latent dynamics are learned and how they are used for planning. First, teacher-forced training does not expose the dynamics model to the recursively predicted latent states encountered during planning. Second, sampling-based planners typically initialize action search from goal-agnostic proposals, without exploiting the action-conditioned dynamics learned by the world model. We introduce Dynamics-Informed Action Learning (DIAL) to bridge these two gaps. During training, rollout alignment exposes the forward model to its own autoregressive latent predictions, while multi-step inverse supervision trains a lightweight inverse dynamics head to infer actions from latent-state transitions. We further apply the inverse head to model-generated transitions with its parameters held fixed, encouraging predicted rollouts to retain action-relevant information. At test time, DIAL autoregressively couples the inverse and forward dynamics to construct a dynamics-informed action sequence toward the goal, which initializes the mean of the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) proposal distribution. The subsequent CEM search then refines this initialization using the standard sampling and updating procedure. Experiments across four visual control benchmarks demonstrate that DIAL outperforms existing latent world models and planning baselines in both standard and long-horizon scenarios.

</details>


### [182] [How Many Demonstrations Does a VLA Need? On the Sample Efficiency of VLA Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09964)
*Rahul Chand,Miguel Villa Floran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在适应目标环境时的数据与计算效率，揭示了零样本或全数据微调比较所掩盖的模型间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常先在目标场景演示数据上后训练再部署，但模型比较多基于零样本或全数据微调成功率，无法反映适应所需的数据量和计算量，因此需要系统研究适应效率。

Method: 在四个LIBERO套件上，对六个VLA模型（π0、π0.5及GR00T N1-N1.7）改变演示数量和训练步数进行适应实验；并通过冻结VLM微调来探究预训练因素对适应效率的影响。

Result: 全数据微调下性能相近的模型在少样本时差异显著（如LIBERO-Spatial上50个演示时成功率相差达3倍）；π0.5比π0所需演示少2-5倍且在各预算下样本效率最高；GR00T中N1.5在四个套件中的三个上比N1.6/N1.7适应更高效；冻结VLM严重损害π模型但几乎不影响GR00T；验证损失能反映闭环成功率但低估π0.5的样本效率优势。

Conclusion: VLA模型的适应效率存在显著差异且无法由零样本或全数据微调性能推断，样本效率应作为模型评估的重要维度，预训练中VLM与动作专家的联合训练方式影响适应效率。

Abstract: Pretrained vision language action (VLA) models show strong zero-shot performance, but are rarely deployed directly and are often first post-trained on demonstrations from the target setting. Yet VLAs are typically compared by zero-shot or full-data fine-tuned success, revealing little about the data and compute required for adaptation. We study adaptation efficiency for six VLAs ($\pi_{0}$, $\pi_{0.5}$, and GR00T N1-N1.7) on four LIBERO suites, varying both demonstrations and training steps. This reveals differences hidden by full-data fine-tuning: models within two points on the full LIBERO-Spatial dataset differ by up to $3\times$ in success with 50 demonstrations. $\pi_{0.5}$ needs $2$--$5\times$ fewer demonstrations than $\pi_{0}$ and is the most sample-efficient model at every budget, with the largest gap on the long-horizon suite. Within GR00T, N1.5 adapts more efficiently than N1.6 and N1.7 on three of four suites, despite their stronger zero-shot performance. We study one pretraining factor behind these differences by freezing the VLM during finetuning: this severely degrades the $\pi$ models, whose VLM is jointly trained with the action expert during pretraining, but barely affects GR00T models. Finally, validation loss on held-out demonstrations tracks closed-loop success within a suite, but understates how much more sample-efficient $\pi_{0.5}$ is than $\pi_{0}$.

</details>
